namespace P2Objects;

public class AssayInputCheckTable : Window, IComponentConnector, IStyleConnector
{
	private ObservableCollection<AssayData> _AssayDataCollection = new ObservableCollection<AssayData>();

	public static readonly DependencyProperty PropertyNameProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PropertyName", typeof(string), typeof(AssayInputCheckTable), new UIPropertyMetadata(null));

	public bool isOkClicked;

	private AssayData _AssayData;

	private bool _IsIncrease;

	private string originStr = string.Empty;

	internal AssayInputCheckTable ThisWin;

	internal System.Windows.Controls.ListView assayListView;

	internal System.Windows.Controls.Button DeletePoint;

	internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox numText;

	internal System.Windows.Controls.Button addData;

	internal System.Windows.Controls.Button Ok;

	private bool _contentLoaded;

	public ObservableCollection<AssayData> AssayDataCollection => _AssayDataCollection;

	public string PropertyName
	{
		get
		{
			return (string)GetValue(PropertyNameProperty);
		}
		set
		{
			SetValue(PropertyNameProperty, value);
		}
	}

	public Dictionary<double, double> ReturnValues { get; set; }

	public bool IsCloseButtonClick { get; set; }

	public AssayInputCheckTable()
	{
		InitializeComponent();
	}

	public AssayInputCheckTable(SortedDictionary<double, double> values, string propertyName, bool isIncrease)
	{
		if (values == null || values.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		InitializeComponent();
		PropertyName = propertyName;
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
		{
			PropertyName = "Property";
		}
		ThisWin.Title = P2Globalization.GetString("Pro2AssayCheck");
		List<double> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.CheckOrder(values, isIncrease);
		_IsIncrease = isIncrease;
		foreach (KeyValuePair<double, double> value in values)
		{
			if (list.Contains(value.Key))
			{
				AssayDataCollection.Add(new AssayData(value.Key.ToString(), value.Value.ToString(), isError: true));
			}
			else
			{
				AssayDataCollection.Add(new AssayData(value.Key.ToString(), value.Value.ToString(), isError: false));
			}
		}
		if (AssayDataCollection.Count < 5)
		{
			int num = 5 - AssayDataCollection.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				AssayDataCollection.Add(new AssayData
				{
					Percent = "",
					IsError = false,
					PropertyValue = ""
				});
			}
		}
	}

	private void textBox_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
	{
		System.Windows.Controls.TextBox textBox = sender as System.Windows.Controls.TextBox;
		if (((e.Key >= Key.NumPad0 && e.Key <= Key.NumPad9) || (e.Key >= Key.D0 && e.Key <= Key.D9) || e.Key == Key.Decimal || e.Key == Key.OemPeriod) && Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.None)
		{
			e.Handled = false;
		}
		else
		{
			e.Handled = true;
		}
		if (textBox != null && (e.Key == Key.Decimal || e.Key == Key.OemPeriod) && textBox.Text.IndexOf(".") > -1)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
		}
	}

	private void addData_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
	{
		int.TryParse(numText.Text.Trim(), out var result);
		if (assayListView.SelectedItem is AssayData item)
		{
			int num = AssayDataCollection.IndexOf(item);
			for (int i = 0; i < result; i++)
			{
				AssayDataCollection.Insert(num + i + 1, new AssayData
				{
					Percent = "",
					IsError = false,
					PropertyValue = ""
				});
			}
		}
		else
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < result; j++)
			{
				AssayDataCollection.Add(new AssayData
				{
					Percent = "",
					IsError = false,
					PropertyValue = ""
				});
			}
		}
	}

	private bool DataIsOk()
	{
		bool result = true;
		SortedDictionary<double, double> sortedDictionary = new SortedDictionary<double, double>();
		foreach (AssayData item in AssayDataCollection)
		{
			double result2 = -1.0;
			double result3 = -1.0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Percent) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.PropertyValue))
			{
				double.TryParse(item.Percent, out result2);
				double.TryParse(item.PropertyValue, out result3);
				sortedDictionary.Add(result2, result3);
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Percent) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.PropertyValue))
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Percent) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.PropertyValue))
			{
				result = false;
			}
		}
		if (sortedDictionary.Count > 0)
		{
			List<double> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.CheckOrder(sortedDictionary, _IsIncrease);
			if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			foreach (AssayData item2 in AssayDataCollection)
			{
				double.TryParse(item2.Percent, out var result4);
				if (list != null && list.Contains(result4))
				{
					item2.IsError = true;
				}
				else
				{
					item2.IsError = false;
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void Ok_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
	{
		if (!DataIsOk())
		{
			if (System.Windows.MessageBox.Show("Data still have issues,are you sure to leave now? \n  Click \"No\" to check again,or click \"Yes\", the data will not be converted.", "Pro/II  Converter", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation, MessageBoxResult.No) == MessageBoxResult.Yes)
			{
				ReturnValues = null;
				isOkClicked = true;
				Close();
			}
			return;
		}
		ReturnValues = new Dictionary<double, double>();
		foreach (AssayData item in AssayDataCollection)
		{
			double result = -1.0;
			double result2 = -1.0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Percent) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.PropertyValue))
			{
				double.TryParse(item.Percent, out result);
				double.TryParse(item.PropertyValue, out result2);
				ReturnValues.Add(result, result2);
			}
		}
		if (ReturnValues.Count <= 2)
		{
			System.Windows.MessageBox.Show("At least two points are required, please check again!", "Pro/II  Converter", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation, MessageBoxResult.OK);
			return;
		}
		isOkClicked = true;
		Close();
	}

	private void Delete_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
	{
		if (assayListView.SelectedItem is AssayData item)
		{
			AssayDataCollection.Remove(item);
		}
	}

	private void SetDeleteButtonStatus()
	{
		if (assayListView != null)
		{
			if (assayListView.SelectedItem == null)
			{
				DeletePoint.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				DeletePoint.IsEnabled = true;
			}
		}
	}

	protected override void OnClosing(CancelEventArgs e)
	{
		if (!isOkClicked)
		{
			IsCloseButtonClick = true;
		}
		base.OnClosing(e);
	}

	private void TextBox_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
	{
		System.Windows.Controls.TextBox textBox = sender as System.Windows.Controls.TextBox;
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(textBox.Text))
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		foreach (AssayData item in AssayDataCollection)
		{
			if (textBox.Text == item.Percent)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		if (num >= 2)
		{
			textBox.Text = originStr;
			_AssayData.Percent = originStr;
		}
	}

	private void TextBox_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
	{
		originStr = (sender as System.Windows.Controls.TextBox).Text;
		foreach (AssayData item in AssayDataCollection)
		{
			if (originStr == item.Percent)
			{
				_AssayData = item;
			}
		}
	}

	private void TextBox_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
	{
		if (!(sender is System.Windows.Controls.TextBox textBox))
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			double.Parse(textBox.Text);
		}
		catch
		{
			textBox.Text = originStr;
		}
	}

	private void ThisWin_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
	{
		SetDeleteButtonStatus();
	}

	private void assayListView_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
	{
		SetDeleteButtonStatus();
	}

	[DebuggerNonUserCode]
	[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]

	{
		if (!_contentLoaded)
		{
			_contentLoaded = true;
			Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/P2Objects;component/assayinputchecktable.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
			System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
		}
	}

public class BoolToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
{
	public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		bool flag = bool.Parse(parameter as string);
		return ((bool)value != flag) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible;
	}

	public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		throw new NotImplementedException();
	}
}
public class BoolToRedConverter : IValueConverter
{
	public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		if (!(bool)value)
		{
			return new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Black);
		}
		return new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
	}

	public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		throw new NotImplementedException();
	}
}
public class AssayData : DependencyObject
{
	public static readonly DependencyProperty PercentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Percent", typeof(string), typeof(AssayData), new UIPropertyMetadata(null));

	public static readonly DependencyProperty PropertyValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PropertyValue", typeof(string), typeof(AssayData), new UIPropertyMetadata(null));

	public static readonly DependencyProperty IsErrorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsError", typeof(bool), typeof(AssayData), new UIPropertyMetadata(null));

	public string Percent
	{
		get
		{
			return (string)GetValue(PercentProperty);
		}
		set
		{
			SetValue(PercentProperty, value);
		}
	}

	public string PropertyValue
	{
		get
		{
			return (string)GetValue(PropertyValueProperty);
		}
		set
		{
			SetValue(PropertyValueProperty, value);
		}
	}

	public bool IsError
	{
		get
		{
			return (bool)GetValue(IsErrorProperty);
		}
		set
		{
			SetValue(IsErrorProperty, value);
		}
	}

	public AssayData(string percent, string propertyVal, bool isError)
	{
		Percent = percent;
		PropertyValue = propertyVal;
		IsError = isError;
	}

	public AssayData()
	{
	}
}
public class P2BaseObject : BaseObject
{
	public static IDictionary<string, P2BaseObject> g_Map { get; set; }

	public static string P2InputFileName { get; set; }

	public static UOM g_UOM { get; set; }

	public P2BaseObject Parent { get; set; }

	public IList<P2BaseObject> Children { get; set; }

	public VisualBaseNode VisualNode { get; set; }

	public static List<P2BaseObject> g_HCurves { get; set; }

	public bool bParsingError { get; set; }

	public bool bMappingError { get; set; }

	public P2BaseObject()
	{
		if (g_Map == null)
		{
			g_Map = new Dictionary<string, P2BaseObject>();
		}
		if (g_UOM == null)
		{
			g_UOM = new UOM();
		}
		if (g_HCurves == null)
		{
			g_HCurves = new List<P2BaseObject>();
		}
		Children = new List<P2BaseObject>();
		if (BaseObject._Report != null)
		{
			Report report = BaseObject._Report;
			int p2ObjectCount = report.P2ObjectCount + 1;
			report.P2ObjectCount = p2ObjectCount;
		}
	}

	public virtual HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		return null;
	}

	public virtual void SetConvertStatus()
	{
		if (VisualNode != null)
		{
			VisualNode._ConvertResult = VisualBaseNode.ConvertResult.CR_PASSED;
			VisualNode.Message = "";
		}
	}
}

	public static Dictionary<int, string> GetAllComponents()
	{
		Dictionary<int, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, string>();
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Component))
		{
			return null;
		}
		P2Component p2Component = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Component] as P2Component;
		if (p2Component.LIBIDs != null && p2Component.LIBIDs.LIBIDs != null)
		{
			foreach (LIBID.CompLIBID item in p2Component.LIBIDs.LIBIDs.Values.ToList())
			{
				dictionary.Add(item.nIndex, item.Name);
			}
		}
		if (p2Component.PETROLEUMs != null)
		{
			foreach (PETROLEUM pETROLEUM in p2Component.PETROLEUMs)
			{
				dictionary.Add(pETROLEUM.nIndex, pETROLEUM.Name.Trim() + "*");
			}
		}
		if (p2Component.NONLIBRARY != null)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, string> datum in p2Component.NONLIBRARY.Data)
			{
				dictionary.Add(datum.Key, datum.Value.Trim() + "*");
			}
		}
		if (p2Component.DuplicateNamedLibComp != null)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, ReNameLibComponent> item2 in p2Component.DuplicateNamedLibComp)
			{
				string value = string.Empty;
				if (item2.Value.Number == 1)
				{
					value = item2.Value.HYSYSName + "*";
				}
				else if (item2.Value.Number > 1)
				{
					string text = string.Empty;
					for (int i = 0; i < item2.Value.Number - 1; i++)
					{
						text += "-2";
					}
					value = item2.Value.HYSYSName + text + "*";
				}
				dictionary.Add(item2.Key, value);
			}
		}
		return dictionary;
	}

	public static string GetHYSYSCompName(int p2CompIndex)
	{
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Component))
		{
			return null;
		}
		P2Component p2Component = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Component] as P2Component;
		string text = string.Empty;
		Dictionary<int, string> allComponents = GetAllComponents();
		if (!allComponents.ContainsKey(p2CompIndex))
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (allComponents != null && p2Component._Map != null)
		{
			P2DataMapResult.P2Component p2Component2 = p2Component._Map.GetP2Component(allComponents[p2CompIndex]);
			if (p2Component2 != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(p2Component2.CASNumber) && string.Equals(p2Component2.CASNumber, "58-86-6"))
				{
					text = "D-XYLOSE";
				}
				else
				{
					text = p2Component._Map.GetP2Component(allComponents[p2CompIndex]).HYSYSComponent.PrintName;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text = p2Component._Map.GetP2Component(allComponents[p2CompIndex]).AspenComponent.Name;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text = allComponents[p2CompIndex];
			}
		}
		return text;
	}

	public static string GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(string hysysCompName)
	{
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Component))
		{
			return null;
		}
		string text = string.Empty;
		P2Component p2Component = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Component] as P2Component;
		Dictionary<int, string> allComponents = GetAllComponents();
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		foreach (KeyValuePair<int, string> item in allComponents)
		{
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			if (p2Component._Map.GetP2Component(item.Value) != null)
			{
				text2 = p2Component._Map.GetP2Component(item.Value).HYSYSComponent.PrintName;
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text2 = item.Value;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				dictionary.Add(text2, item.Value);
			}
		}
		if (p2Component.DuplicateNamedLibComp != null)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, ReNameLibComponent> item2 in p2Component.DuplicateNamedLibComp)
			{
				string key = string.Empty;
				if (item2.Value.Number == 1)
				{
					key = item2.Value.HYSYSName + "*";
				}
				else if (item2.Value.Number > 1)
				{
					string text3 = string.Empty;
					for (int i = 0; i < item2.Value.Number - 1; i++)
					{
						text3 += "-2";
					}
					key = item2.Value.HYSYSName + text3 + "*";
				}
				if (dictionary.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					dictionary[key] = item2.Value.P2Key;
				}
			}
		}
		if (dictionary.ContainsKey(hysysCompName))
		{
			text = dictionary[hysysCompName];
		}
		if (text.Length > 8)
		{
			text = text.Substring(0, 8);
		}
		return text;
	}

	public static bool HasNoIncludedCompInTraditionalCompoents()
	{
		bool flag = false;
		new Dictionary<string, string>();
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Component))
		{
			return false;
		}
		P2Component p2Component = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Component] as P2Component;
		UserCompoent userCompoent = new UserCompoent();
		if (userCompoent == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		_ = BaseObject.ApplicationFolder + "\\UserComponet.mdb";
		if (p2Component.LIBIDs != null)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, LIBID.CompLIBID> lIBID in p2Component.LIBIDs.LIBIDs)
			{
				LIBID.CompLIBID value = lIBID.Value;
				if (p2Component._Map.GetP2Component(value.Name) == null)
				{
					flag = ((!(value.Name == "D-XYLOSE") && !(value.Name == "DEXTROSE")) ? true : false);
				}
				else if (userCompoent.UserCompMapLib != null && userCompoent.UserCompMapLib.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (UserCompoent.NotConvertedComp item in userCompoent.UserCompMapLib)
					{
						if (item.Pro2Key == value.Name)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return flag;
	}

	public P2Component(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
			{
				if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.LIBID))
				{
					if (LIBIDs == null)
					{
						LIBIDs = new LIBID();
					}
					BuildLIBID(child);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.NONLIBRARY))
				{
					BuildNonlibrary(child);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.PETROLEUM))
				{
					if (PETROLEUMs == null)
					{
						PETROLEUMs = new List<PETROLEUM>();
					}
					string[] pairStringInBracket = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(child.Name);
					string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					if (child.Name.IndexOf('/') > -1)
					{
						string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
						for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
						{
							PETROLEUMs.Add(BuildPetroleum(array[i], pairStringInBracket));
						}
					}
					else
					{
						PETROLEUMs.Add(BuildPetroleum(text, pairStringInBracket));
					}
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.PHASE))
				{
					string text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && (text2.Contains("s=") || text2.Contains("s =") || text2.Contains("S=") || text2.Contains("S =")))
					{
						SolidComponentExists = true;
					}
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.ASSAY))
				{
					BuildAssay(child);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.CUTPOINTS) || child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.TBPCUTS))
				{
					if (CUTPOINTs == null)
					{
						CUTPOINTs = new List<Cutpoint>();
					}
					if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.TBPCUTS) && child.Name.IndexOf('=') < 0)
					{
						child.Name = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
						child.Name = CComponent.CUTPOINTS + " " + CComponent.TBPCUTS + "=" + child.Name;
					}
					else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.TBPCUTS) && child.Name.IndexOf('=') > 0)
					{
						child.Name = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, '=');
						child.Name = CComponent.CUTPOINTS + " " + CComponent.TBPCUTS + "=" + child.Name;
					}
					CUTPOINTs.Add(BuildCUTPOINT(child));
				}
				else
				{
					BuildInvariantProperties(child);
				}
			}
			if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Component))
			{
				P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(Categories.Component, this);
			}
			if (LIBIDs != null && LIBIDs.LIBIDs != null)
			{
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, LIBID.CompLIBID> lIBID in LIBIDs.LIBIDs)
				{
					_ = lIBID;
				}
			}
			if (PETROLEUMs != null)
			{
				int num = 0;
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PETROLEUM pETROLEUM in PETROLEUMs)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pETROLEUM.Name))
					{
						list.Add(pETROLEUM.Name);
					}
				}
				foreach (PETROLEUM pETROLEUM2 in PETROLEUMs)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pETROLEUM2.Name))
					{
						continue;
					}
					while (true)
					{
						pETROLEUM2.Name = "Petro" + num;
						if (!list.Contains(pETROLEUM2.Name))
						{
							break;
						}
						num++;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (NONLIBRARY == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, string> datum in NONLIBRARY.Data)
			{
				_ = datum;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseComp")));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bParsingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseComp")));
			}
		}
	}

	private Cutpoint BuildCUTPOINT(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		Cutpoint cutpoint = new Cutpoint();
		string str = obj.Name;
		ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
		List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
		{
			CComponent.DEFAULT,
			CComponent.BLEND,
			CComponent.CUTSET + "=",
			CComponent.TBPCUTS + "=",
			"TBPCUTS=",
			"TBPCUTS",
			"TBPC",
			"CUTSET=",
			"CUTSET",
			"CUTS"
		}, str.ToUpper());
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
		{
			list.Add(item.Content);
		}
		string[] array = list.ToArray();
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			try
			{
				if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.DEFAULT))
				{
					cutpoint.DEFAULT = true;
				}
				else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.BLEND))
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
					cutpoint.BLEND = array2[1];
				}
				else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.CUTSET))
				{
					string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
					cutpoint.CUTSET = array3[1];
				}
				else
				{
					if (!array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.TBPCUTS))
					{
						continue;
					}
					cutpoint.TBPCUTS = new Dictionary<double, double>();
					string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]));
					for (int j = 0; j < array4.Length; j++)
					{
						string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array4[j]);
						if (array5 != null && array5.Length != 0)
						{
							if (array5.Length == 3)
							{
								cutpoint.StartCutPoint = double.Parse(array5[0].Trim());
								cutpoint.TBPCUTS.Add(double.Parse(array5[1].Trim()), double.Parse(array5[2].Trim()));
							}
							else
							{
								cutpoint.TBPCUTS.Add(double.Parse(array5[0].Trim()), double.Parse(array5[1].Trim()));
							}
						}
					}
					continue;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
		return cutpoint;
	}

	private PETROLEUM BuildPetroleum(string name, string[] units)
	{
		PETROLEUM pETROLEUM = new PETROLEUM();
		try
		{
			string[] array = name.Split(',');
			string unit = units[0];
			string unit2 = units[1];
			if (array != null && array.Length >= 1 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[0]))
			{
				int.TryParse(array[0], out var result);
				if (array.Length >= 2)
				{
					pETROLEUM.nIndex = result;
					pETROLEUM.Name = array[1].Replace(" ", "");
					if (array.Length >= 3)
					{
						double.TryParse(array[2], out var result2);
						pETROLEUM.MW = result2;
						if (array.Length >= 4)
						{
							double.TryParse(array[3], out result2);
							pETROLEUM.StdLiqDensity = new DoubleValue(unit, result2);
							if (array.Length >= 5)
							{
								double.TryParse(array[4], out result2);
								pETROLEUM.NBP = new DoubleValue(unit2, result2);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bParsingError = true;
		}
		return pETROLEUM;
	}

	private void BuildNonlibrary(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			NONLIBRARY = new Nonlibrary();
			int slashLength = ParseUtilty.GetSlashLength(obj.Name, 2);
			string str = obj.Name.Substring(0, slashLength);
			ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(str);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array[i]);
				NONLIBRARY.Data.Add(int.Parse(array2[0]), array2[1]);
			}
			if (slashLength != obj.Name.Length)
			{
				array = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(obj.Name.Substring(slashLength + 1, obj.Name.Length - slashLength - 1));
				NONLIBRARY.Fill = array[1];
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bParsingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void BuildLIBID(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			string str = obj.Name;
			ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
			int num = str.LastIndexOf(CComponent.BANK);
			int num2 = str.LastIndexOf(CComponent.FILL);
			int length;
			if (num == -1 && num2 == -1)
			{
				length = str.Length;
			}
			else if (num == -1)
			{
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str.Substring(num2 + 1, str.Length - num2 - 1));
				LIBIDs.Fill = array[1];
				length = num2;
			}
			else if (num2 == -1)
			{
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str.Substring(num, str.Length - num).Trim())[1]);
				LIBIDs.Bank.LIBID = array2[0];
				if (array2.Length > 1)
				{
					LIBIDs.Bank.BankID = array2[1];
				}
				length = num;
			}
			else
			{
				string str2;
				string str3;
				if (num < num2)
				{
					str2 = str.Substring(num, num2 - num).Trim();
					str3 = str.Substring(num2, str.Length - num2).Trim();
				}
				else
				{
					str3 = str.Substring(num, num2 - num).Trim();
					str2 = str.Substring(num2, str.Length - num2).Trim();
				}
				string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str3);
				LIBIDs.Fill = array3[1];
				string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str2)[1]);
				LIBIDs.Bank.LIBID = array4[0];
				LIBIDs.Bank.BankID = array4[1];
				length = ((num < num2) ? num : num2);
			}
			string text = str.Substring(0, length).Trim();
			if (text[text.Length - 1] == ',')
			{
				text = text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1);
			}
			string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
			for (int i = 0; i < array5.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array5[i]);
				if (array6 == null || array6.Length < 1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				int nIndex = int.Parse(array6[0]);
				string text2 = ((array6.Length > 1) ? array6[1] : "");
				string number = string.Empty;
				string text3 = string.Empty;
				if (array6.Length == 3)
				{
					string[] array7 = array5[i].Split(',');
					if (array7.Length == 3)
					{
						number = array6[2];
					}
					else if (array7.Length == 4)
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(array7[2]))
						{
							text3 = array6[2];
						}
						else
						{
							number = array6[2];
						}
					}
				}
				else if (array6.Length == 4)
				{
					number = array6[2];
					text3 = array6[3];
				}
				if (text3 == "XYLOSE" && text2 == "DEXTROSE")
				{
					text2 = "D-XYLOSE";
					number = "58-86-6";
				}
				LIBID.CompLIBID compLIBID = new LIBID.CompLIBID
				{
					nIndex = nIndex,
					Name = text2,
					Number = number,
					Alias = text3
				};
				bool flag = false;
				int refIndex = 0;
				string text4 = string.Empty;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, LIBID.CompLIBID> lIBID in LIBIDs.LIBIDs)
				{
					P2DataMapResult.P2Component p2Component = _Map.GetP2Component(lIBID.Value.Name);
					P2DataMapResult.P2Component p2Component2 = _Map.GetP2Component(compLIBID.Name);
					if (p2Component != null && p2Component2 != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName) && p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName == p2Component2.HYSYSComponent.MenuName)
					{
						flag = true;
						refIndex = lIBID.Value.nIndex;
						text4 = p2Component.HYSYSComponent.PrintName;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					LIBIDs.LIBIDs.Add(array6[0], compLIBID);
					continue;
				}
				if (DuplicateNamedLibComp == null)
				{
					DuplicateNamedLibComp = new Dictionary<int, ReNameLibComponent>();
				}
				int num3 = 1;
				if (DuplicateNamedLibComp != null)
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<int, ReNameLibComponent> item in DuplicateNamedLibComp)
					{
						if (item.Value.HYSYSName == text4)
						{
							num3++;
						}
					}
				}
				DuplicateNamedLibComp.Add(compLIBID.nIndex, new ReNameLibComponent(compLIBID.Name, compLIBID.nIndex, refIndex, text4, num3));
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bParsingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void BuildAssay(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		string[] array;
		string[] array2;
		try
		{
			ASSAY = new Assay();
			ASSAY.SourceStr = obj.Name;
			string str = obj.Name;
			ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
			array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(str);
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			list2.Add(CComponent.FIT + "=");
			list2.Add(CComponent.CHARACTERIZE);
			list2.Add(CComponent.MW);
			list2.Add(CComponent.CONVERSION);
			list2.Add(CComponent.CURVEFIT + "=");
			list2.Add("CURV");
			list2.Add("CURVEFIT=");
			list2.Add(CComponent.GRAVITY);
			list2.Add(CComponent.TBPIP);
			list2.Add(CComponent.TBPEP);
			list2.Add(CComponent.NBP);
			list2.Add(CComponent.KVRECONCILE);
			list2.Add("KVRECONCILE=");
			list2.Add("KVRE=");
			list2.Add("KVRECONCILE");
			array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (text.StartsWith(CComponent.KVRECONCILE))
				{
					continue;
				}
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list2, text.ToUpper());
				List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
				{
					list3.Add(item.Content);
				}
				list.AddRange(list3);
			}
			array = list.ToArray();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bParsingError = true;
			return;
		}
		array2 = array;
		foreach (string text2 in array2)
		{
			try
			{
				BuildAssayInfo(text2.Trim());
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	private void BuildAssayInfo(string str)
	{
		if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.FIT))
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.Fit = array[1];
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.CHARACTERIZE))
		{
			string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.Characterize = array2[1];
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.MW))
		{
			string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.MW = array3[1];
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.CONVERSION))
		{
			string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.Conversion = array4[1];
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.CURVEFIT))
		{
			string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.Curvefit = array5[1];
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.GRAVITY))
		{
			string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.Gravity = array6[1];
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.TBPIP))
		{
			string[] array7 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.TBPIP = double.Parse(array7[1]);
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.TBPEP))
		{
			string[] array8 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.TBPEP = double.Parse(array8[1]);
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.NBP))
		{
			string[] array9 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.NBP = array9[1];
		}
		else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.KVRECONCILE))
		{
			string[] array10 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str);
			ASSAY.Kvreconcile = array10[1];
		}
	}

	private void BuildInvariantProperties(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		if (InvariantProperty == null)
		{
			InvariantProperty = new InvariantProperties();
		}
		try
		{
			string text = obj.Name.Trim();
			if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.MW))
			{
				if (InvariantProperty.MW == null)
				{
					InvariantProperty.MW = ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text);
				}
				else if (ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text) != null)
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text))
					{
						InvariantProperty.MW.Add(item.Key, item.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.SPGR))
			{
				if (InvariantProperty.SPGR == null)
				{
					InvariantProperty.SPGR = ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text);
				}
				else if (ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text) != null)
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item2 in ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text))
					{
						InvariantProperty.SPGR.Add(item2.Key, item2.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.API))
			{
				if (InvariantProperty.API == null)
				{
					InvariantProperty.API = ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text);
				}
				else if (ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text) != null)
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item3 in ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text))
					{
						InvariantProperty.API.Add(item3.Key, item3.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("NBP"))
			{
				if (InvariantProperty.NBP == null)
				{
					InvariantProperty.NBP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValueDic(text);
				}
				else if (ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValueDic(text) != null)
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<int, DoubleValue> item4 in ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValueDic(text))
					{
						InvariantProperty.NBP.Add(item4.Key, item4.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.ACENTRIC))
			{
				if (InvariantProperty.ACENTRIC == null)
				{

				}
				else if (ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text) != null)

					{
						InvariantProperty.HLTB.Add(item30.Key, item30.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.HLTM))
			{
				if (InvariantProperty.HLTM == null)
				{
					InvariantProperty.HLTM = ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text);
				}
				else if (ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text) != null)
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item31 in ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text))
					{
						InvariantProperty.HLTM.Add(item31.Key, item31.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CComponent.DENSITY))
			{
				if (InvariantProperty.DENSITY == null)
				{
					InvariantProperty.DENSITY = new Dictionary<int, DoubleValue>();
				}
				string unit = string.Empty;
				string[] stringsInBracket = ParseUtilty.GetStringsInBracket(text.ToUpper());
				if (stringsInBracket != null && stringsInBracket.Length == 4)
				{
					unit = stringsInBracket[2];
				}
				int num = text.IndexOf(CComponent.TABULAR);
				if (num > -1)
				{
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text.Substring(num));
					if (array.Length >= 2)
					{
						for (int i = 1; i < array.Length; i++)
						{
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array[i]);
							double result = 0.0;
							if (array2.Length >= 2)
							{
								int.TryParse(array2[0].Trim(), out var result2);
								double num2 = 0.0;
								for (int j = 1; j < array2.Length; j++)
								{
									double.TryParse(array2[j], out result);
									num2 += result;
								}
								num2 /= (double)(array2.Length - 1);
								InvariantProperty.DENSITY.Add(result2, new DoubleValue(unit, num2));
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			InvariantProperty = InvariantProperty;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bParsingError = true;
		}
	}

	private List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSPetrol> GetPETROLEUMs()
	{
		List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSPetrol> list = new List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSPetrol>();
		try
		{
			if (PETROLEUMs != null)
			{
				foreach (PETROLEUM pETROLEUM in PETROLEUMs)
				{
					HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSPetrol hYSYSPetrol = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSPetrol();
					hYSYSPetrol.Name = pETROLEUM.Name;
					hYSYSPetrol.MW = pETROLEUM.MW;
					hYSYSPetrol.nIndex = pETROLEUM.nIndex;
					hYSYSPetrol.StdLiqDensity = UnitConverter.GetHysysMassDensityValue(pETROLEUM.StdLiqDensity);
					hYSYSPetrol.NBP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, pETROLEUM.NBP);
					if (InvariantProperty != null)
					{
						if (InvariantProperty.MW != null && InvariantProperty.MW.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.MW = InvariantProperty.MW[pETROLEUM.nIndex];
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("MW");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.SPGR != null && InvariantProperty.SPGR.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.StdLiqDensity = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity, InvariantProperty.SPGR[pETROLEUM.nIndex], "SPGR");
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("SPGR");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.API != null && InvariantProperty.API.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.StdLiqDensity = UnitConverter.GetHysysMassDensityValue(new DoubleValue("API", InvariantProperty.API[pETROLEUM.nIndex]));
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("API");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.NBP != null && InvariantProperty.NBP.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.NBP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, InvariantProperty.NBP[pETROLEUM.nIndex]);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("NBP");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.ACENTRIC != null && InvariantProperty.ACENTRIC.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.ACENTRIC = InvariantProperty.ACENTRIC[pETROLEUM.nIndex];
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("ACENTRIC");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.VC != null && InvariantProperty.VC.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex) && InvariantProperty.VC[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Type == "M")
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.VC = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.MoleLiquidVolume, InvariantProperty.VC[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Value, InvariantProperty.VC[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Unit, string.Empty);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("VC");
						}
						else if (InvariantProperty.VC != null && InvariantProperty.VC.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  VC {1}", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_VCNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.TC != null && InvariantProperty.TC.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.TC = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, InvariantProperty.TC[pETROLEUM.nIndex]);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("TC");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.PC != null && InvariantProperty.PC.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.PC = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, InvariantProperty.PC[pETROLEUM.nIndex]);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("PC");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.RACKETT != null && InvariantProperty.RACKETT.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.RACKETT = InvariantProperty.RACKETT[pETROLEUM.nIndex];
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("RACKETT");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.DIPOLE != null && InvariantProperty.DIPOLE.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.DIPOLE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Dipolemoment, InvariantProperty.DIPOLE[pETROLEUM.nIndex]);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("DIPOLE");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.RADIUS != null && InvariantProperty.RADIUS.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.RADIUS = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, InvariantProperty.RADIUS[pETROLEUM.nIndex]);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("RADIUS");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.SOLUPARA != null && InvariantProperty.SOLUPARA.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.SOLUPARA = InvariantProperty.SOLUPARA[pETROLEUM.nIndex];
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("SOLUPARA");
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.MOLVOL != null && InvariantProperty.MOLVOL.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex) && InvariantProperty.MOLVOL[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Type == "M")
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.MOLVOL = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.MoleLiquidVolume, InvariantProperty.MOLVOL[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Value, InvariantProperty.MOLVOL[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Unit, string.Empty);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("MOLVOL");
						}
						else if (InvariantProperty.MOLVOL != null && InvariantProperty.MOLVOL.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  MOLVOL {1} ", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_MOLVOLNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.STDDENSITY != null && InvariantProperty.STDDENSITY.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.StdLiqDensity = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, InvariantProperty.STDDENSITY[pETROLEUM.nIndex]);
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST != null && InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							hYSYSPetrol.HCOMBUST = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Enthalpy, InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Value, InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST[pETROLEUM.nIndex].Unit, string.Empty);
							hYSYSPetrol.AddedProperties.Add("HCOMBUST");
						}
						else if (InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST != null && InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST.Keys.ToList().Contains(pETROLEUM.nIndex))
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  HCOMBUST {1} ", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_HCOMBUSTNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.ZC != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA DATA PETROLEUM {0}  ZC {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.CNUM != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  CNUM {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.ZNUM != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  ZNUM {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.HVAPORIZE != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  HVAPORIZE {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.HFUSION != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  HFUSION {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.NMP != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  NMP {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.PTP != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  PTP {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.TTP != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  TTP {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.GHV != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  GHV {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.LHV != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  LHV {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.SVTB != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  SVTB {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.SLTB != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  SLTB {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.SLTM != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  SLTM {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.HVTB != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  HVTB {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.HLTB != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  HLTB {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
						if (InvariantProperty.HLTM != null)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA PETROLEUM {0}  HLTM {1}.", hYSYSPetrol.Name, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
						}
					}
					list.Add(hYSYSPetrol);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				return list;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
		return null;
	}

	private List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary> CreateHYSYSNolibrary()
	{
		List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary> list = new List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary>();
		if (NONLIBRARY == null || NONLIBRARY.Data == null || NONLIBRARY.Data.Count == 0 || InvariantProperty == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		try
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, string> datum in NONLIBRARY.Data)
			{
				HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary noLibrary = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary();
				noLibrary.Name = datum.Value;
				bool flag = false;
				if (InvariantProperty.MW != null && InvariantProperty.MW.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.MW = InvariantProperty.MW[datum.Key];
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("MW");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.SPGR != null && InvariantProperty.SPGR.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.SPGR = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity, InvariantProperty.SPGR[datum.Key], "SPGR");
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("SPGR");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.API != null && InvariantProperty.API.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.API = UnitConverter.GetHysysMassDensityValue(new DoubleValue("API", InvariantProperty.API[datum.Key]));
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("API");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.NBP != null && InvariantProperty.NBP.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.NBP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, InvariantProperty.NBP[datum.Key]);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("NBP");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.ACENTRIC != null && InvariantProperty.ACENTRIC.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.ACENTRIC = InvariantProperty.ACENTRIC[datum.Key];
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("ACENTRIC");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.VC != null && InvariantProperty.VC.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key) && InvariantProperty.VC[datum.Key].Type == "M")
				{
					noLibrary.VC = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.MoleLiquidVolume, InvariantProperty.VC[datum.Key].Value, InvariantProperty.VC[datum.Key].Unit, string.Empty);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("VC");
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (InvariantProperty.VC != null && InvariantProperty.VC.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  VC {1} ", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_VCNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.TC != null && InvariantProperty.TC.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.TC = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, InvariantProperty.TC[datum.Key]);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("TC");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.PC != null && InvariantProperty.PC.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.PC = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, InvariantProperty.PC[datum.Key]);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("PC");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.RACKETT != null && InvariantProperty.RACKETT.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.RACKETT = InvariantProperty.RACKETT[datum.Key];
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("RACKETT");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.DIPOLE != null && InvariantProperty.DIPOLE.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.DIPOLE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Dipolemoment, InvariantProperty.DIPOLE[datum.Key]);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("DIPOLE");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.RADIUS != null && InvariantProperty.RADIUS.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.RADIUS = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, InvariantProperty.RADIUS[datum.Key]);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("RADIUS");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.SOLUPARA != null && InvariantProperty.SOLUPARA.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.SOLUPARA = InvariantProperty.SOLUPARA[datum.Key];
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("SOLUPARA");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.MOLVOL != null && InvariantProperty.MOLVOL.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key) && InvariantProperty.MOLVOL[datum.Key].Type == "M")
				{
					noLibrary.MOLVOL = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.MoleLiquidVolume, InvariantProperty.MOLVOL[datum.Key].Value, InvariantProperty.MOLVOL[datum.Key].Unit, string.Empty);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("MOLVOL");
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (InvariantProperty.MOLVOL != null && InvariantProperty.MOLVOL.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  MOLVOL {1}", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_MOLVOLNotConverted")));
				}
				if ((InvariantProperty.STDDENSITY == null || !InvariantProperty.STDDENSITY.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key)) && (InvariantProperty.SPGR == null || !InvariantProperty.SPGR.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key)) && (InvariantProperty.API == null || !InvariantProperty.API.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key)) && InvariantProperty.DENSITY != null && InvariantProperty.DENSITY.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.STDDENSITY = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, InvariantProperty.DENSITY[datum.Key]);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("STDDENSITY");
					flag = true;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST != null && InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					noLibrary.HCOMBUST = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Enthalpy, InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST[datum.Key].Value, InvariantProperty.HCOMBUST[datum.Key].Unit, string.Empty);
					noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("HCOMBUST");
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (InvariantProperty.MOLVOL != null && InvariantProperty.MOLVOL.Keys.ToList().Contains(datum.Key))
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  HCOMBUST {1}", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_HCOMBUSTNotConverted")));
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0} {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
					continue;
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.ZC != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  ZC {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.CNUM != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  CNUM {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.ZNUM != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  ZNUM {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.HVAPORIZE != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  HVAPORIZE {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.HFUSION != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  HFUSION {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.NMP != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  NMP {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.PTP != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  PTP {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.TTP != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  TTP {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.GHV != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  GHV {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.LHV != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  LHV {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.SVTB != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  SVTB {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.SLTB != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  SLTB {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.SLTM != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  SLTM {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.HVTB != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  HVTB {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.HLTB != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  HLTB {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (InvariantProperty.HLTM != null)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA NONLIBRARY {0}  HLTM {1}.", datum.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				list.Add(noLibrary);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
		return list;
	}

	private bool HasAssay()
	{
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Stream))
		{
			return false;
		}
		IList<P2Stream.BaseStream> streams = (P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Stream] as P2Stream).Streams;
		if (streams == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		foreach (P2Stream.BaseStream item in streams)
		{
			if (item.GetType() == typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.AssaySetting GetAssaySetting()
	{
		HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.AssaySetting assaySetting = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.AssaySetting();
		if (ASSAY == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		assaySetting.Characterize = ASSAY.Characterize;
		assaySetting.MW = ASSAY.MW;
		assaySetting.Conversion = ASSAY.Conversion;
		assaySetting.Curvefit = ASSAY.Curvefit;
		assaySetting.Gravity = ASSAY.Gravity;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ASSAY.Fit))
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA ASSAY FIT {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (ASSAY.TBPIP != 0.0)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA ASSAY TBPIP {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (ASSAY.TBPEP != 0.0)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA ASSAY TBPEP {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ASSAY.NBP))
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA ASSAY NBP {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ASSAY.Kvreconcile))
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format("COMPONENT DATA ASSAY KVRECONCILE {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		return assaySetting;
	}

	private List<string> CreateHYSYSTraditionalComponents()
	{
		notConvertedTraditionalComponents = new List<string>();
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		try
		{
			UserCompoent userCompoent = new UserCompoent();
			string text = (string)System.Windows.Application.Current.Properties["MainFolder"] + "\\UserComponet.mdb";
			int num = 0;
			if (LIBIDs != null)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int num2 = 0;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, LIBID.CompLIBID> lIBID in LIBIDs.LIBIDs)
				{
					LIBID.CompLIBID value = lIBID.Value;
					P2DataMapResult.P2Component p2Component = _Map.GetP2Component(value.Name);
					if (p2Component != null)
					{
						if (!list.Contains(p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName))
						{
							list.Add(p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName);
							dictionary.Add(p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName, value.Name);
						}
						else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName))
						{
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(p2Component.CASNumber) && string.Equals(p2Component.CASNumber, "58-86-6"))
							{
								list.Add("D-XYLOSE");
								dictionary.Add("D-XYLOSE", value.Name);
								continue;
							}
							string strMessage = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_ComponentNotIncludedInHYSYS"), value.Name);
							if (string.Compare(ApplicationName.appName.Trim(), "HYSYS", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
							{
								DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, strMessage);
							}
						}
						else if (userCompoent.AvailableCompNames.Contains(value.Name) || userCompoent.AvailableCompNames.Contains(dictionary[p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName]))
						{
							string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_DatabaseMappedCompDuplicated"), value.Name, dictionary[p2Component.HYSYSComponent.MenuName]);
							num++;
							userCompoent.Notes.Add(item);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (value.Name == "D-XYLOSE" || value.Name == "DEXTROSE")
						{
							continue;
						}
						userCompoent.Notes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_ComponentNotConverted"), value.Name));
						flag = false;
						notConvertedTraditionalComponents.Add(value.Name);
						bool flag2 = false;
						foreach (UserCompoent.NotConvertedComp item2 in userCompoent.UserCompMapNoLib)
						{
							if (item2.Pro2Key == value.Name)
							{
								num2++;
								flag2 = true;
								break;
							}
						}
						string strMessage2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_UnKnowLibraryComponent"), value.Name, text);
						if (!flag2)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, strMessage2);
						}
						foreach (UserCompoent.NotConvertedComp item3 in userCompoent.AvailableComp)
						{
							if (item3.Pro2Key == value.Name)
							{
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
						}
						if (!flag)
						{
							userCompoent.NotConvertedComps.Add(value.Name);
							userCompoent.Notes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_ComponentAddedToUserComponentTable"), value.Name));
						}
					}
				}
				if (notConvertedTraditionalComponents.Count + num > num2)
				{
					int num3 = notConvertedTraditionalComponents.Count - num2;
					string arg = "";
					if (num > 0)
					{
						arg = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_ComponentsNotMappedCorectly"), num);
					}
					string text2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_CountComponentNotConverted"), num3 + num);
					string description = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_ConvertFileAgain"), arg, text);
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, text2);
					string text3 = string.Empty;
					foreach (string note in userCompoent.Notes)
					{
						text3 = text3 + note + "\n";
					}
					text3 += "\n";
					text3 += string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_OperateDatabaseNote1"), text);
					text3 += "\n";
					text3 = text3 + string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_OperateDatabaseNote2")) + "\n";
					text3 = text3 + string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_OperateDatabaseNote3")) + "\n";
					new DetailMessageBox("Pro/II Converter", text2, description, text3).ShowDialog();
				}
				if (userCompoent.Notes.Count > 0)
				{
					string fileName = Path.GetFileName(P2BaseObject.P2InputFileName);
					userCompoent.WriteData(fileName);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
		return list;
	}

	private HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSKinematicViscosity CreateHYSYSAssayKivs(P2Stream.AssayStream assayStream)
	{
		HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSKinematicViscosity hYSYSKinematicViscosity = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSKinematicViscosity();
		if (assayStream.KVIS == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		hYSYSKinematicViscosity.KinematicViscosityUnit = UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.KinematicViscosity, assayStream.KVIS.KinematicViscosityUnit);
		hYSYSKinematicViscosity.Average1 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.KinematicViscosity, assayStream.KVIS.Average1);
		hYSYSKinematicViscosity.Average2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.KinematicViscosity, assayStream.KVIS.Average2);
		hYSYSKinematicViscosity.Temp1 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, assayStream.KVIS.Temp1);
		hYSYSKinematicViscosity.Temp2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, assayStream.KVIS.Temp2);
		hYSYSKinematicViscosity.Stream = assayStream.KVIS.Stream;
		double value = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, 1.0, string.Empty).Value;
		if (assayStream.KVIS.DataTemp1 != null && assayStream.KVIS.DataTemp2 != null)
		{
			hYSYSKinematicViscosity.DataTemp1 = new Dictionary<double, double>();
			hYSYSKinematicViscosity.DataTemp2 = new Dictionary<double, double>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<double, double> item in assayStream.KVIS.DataTemp1)
			{
				hYSYSKinematicViscosity.DataTemp1.Add(item.Key, item.Value * value);
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<double, double> item2 in assayStream.KVIS.DataTemp2)
			{
				hYSYSKinematicViscosity.DataTemp2.Add(item2.Key, item2.Value * value);
			}
		}
		return hYSYSKinematicViscosity;
	}

	private List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSUserProperty> CreateHYSYSAssayUserProperties(P2Stream.AssayStream assayStream)
	{
		List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSUserProperty> list = new List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSUserProperty>();
		if (assayStream == null || assayStream.SpetialProperties == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
		foreach (P2Stream.AssayStream.UserProperty spetialProperty in assayStream.SpetialProperties)
		{
			HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSUserProperty hYSYSUserProperty = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSUserProperty();
			string unit = string.Empty;
			string dataUnit = string.Empty;
			if (spetialProperty.MapInfo != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.MapInfo.HYSYSKey))
			{
				hYSYSUserProperty.Name = spetialProperty.MapInfo.HYSYSKey;
			}
			else
			{
				hYSYSUserProperty.Name = spetialProperty.Name;
			}
			if (hYSYSUserProperty.Name.Length >= 12)
			{
				hYSYSUserProperty.Name = hYSYSUserProperty.Name.Substring(0, 12);
			}
			if (list2.Contains(hYSYSUserProperty.Name))
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (string item in list2)
				{
					if (hYSYSUserProperty.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(item))
					{
						num++;
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSUserProperty.Name.Length >= 10)
				{
					hYSYSUserProperty.Name = hYSYSUserProperty.Name.Substring(0, 10);
				}
				hYSYSUserProperty.Name += num;
			}
			list2.Add(hYSYSUserProperty.Name);
			hYSYSUserProperty.Stream = spetialProperty.Stream;
			if (spetialProperty.MapInfo != null)
			{
				hYSYSUserProperty.VariableType = spetialProperty.MapInfo.Qualifier;
			}
			hYSYSUserProperty.Data = spetialProperty.Data;
			if (spetialProperty.MapInfo != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.MapInfo.Qualifier))
			{
				UnitClassEnum unitClass = (UnitClassEnum)Enum.Parse(typeof(UnitClassEnum), spetialProperty.MapInfo.Qualifier);
				unit = ((string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.AverageUnit) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.MapInfo.DefaultUnit)) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(unitClass, string.Empty) : ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.AverageUnit) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.MapInfo.DefaultUnit)) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(unitClass, spetialProperty.AverageUnit) : UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(unitClass, spetialProperty.MapInfo.DefaultUnit)));
				dataUnit = ((string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.DataUnit) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.MapInfo.DefaultUnit)) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(unitClass, string.Empty) : ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.DataUnit) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(spetialProperty.MapInfo.DefaultUnit)) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(unitClass, spetialProperty.DataUnit) : UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(unitClass, spetialProperty.MapInfo.DefaultUnit)));
			}
			hYSYSUserProperty.Average = new DoubleValue(unit, spetialProperty.Average);
			hYSYSUserProperty.DataUnit = dataUnit;
			if (hYSYSUserProperty.Data == null || hYSYSUserProperty.Data.Count <= 2)
			{
				Dictionary<double, double> dictionary = new Dictionary<double, double>();
				double num2 = 10.0;
				for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
				{
					dictionary.Add(num2, hYSYSUserProperty.Average.Value);
					num2 += 20.0;
				}
				hYSYSUserProperty.Data = dictionary;
			}
			list.Add(hYSYSUserProperty);
		}
		return list;
	}

	private List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay> GetHYSYSAssay()
	{
		bool num = HasAssay();
		HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.AssaySetting settings = null;
		if (!num && ASSAY != null)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_NoAssayStream"), ASSAY.SourceStr));
		}
		else
		{
			settings = GetAssaySetting();
		}
		List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay> list = new List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay>();
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Stream))
		{
			return null;
		}
		try
		{
			IList<P2Stream.BaseStream> streams = (P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Stream] as P2Stream).Streams;
			if (streams == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num2 = -1;
			int num3 = 1;
			foreach (P2Stream.BaseStream item in streams)
			{
				if (item.GetType() != typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream) || !(item is P2Stream.AssayStream assayStream))
				{
					continue;
				}
				num2++;
				HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay hYSYSAssay = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay();
				hYSYSAssay.AssayName = "A" + num2 + "_" + assayStream.Stream;
				if (hYSYSAssay.AssayName.Length >= 12)
				{
					hYSYSAssay.AssayName = hYSYSAssay.AssayName.Substring(0, 12);
				}
				hYSYSAssay.StreamName = assayStream.Stream;
				P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.BaseDistillation distillationData = assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData;
				if (distillationData != null && assayStream.DISTILLATION != null)
				{
					if (assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData != null && assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData.GetType() == typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.TBP))
					{
						if (distillationData is P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.TBP tBP)
						{
							if (tBP.Pressure != null)
							{
								hYSYSAssay.DistillationPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, tBP.Pressure);
							}
							hYSYSAssay.DistillationType = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.TBP;
						}
					}
					else if (assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData != null && assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData.GetType() == typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.D86))
					{
						if (distillationData is P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.D86 d)
						{
							if (d.Pressure != null)
							{
								hYSYSAssay.DistillationPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, d.Pressure);
							}
							hYSYSAssay.Cracking = d.Cracking;
							hYSYSAssay.DistillationType = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D86;
						}
					}
					else if (assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData != null && assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData.GetType() == typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.D1160))
					{
						if (distillationData is P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.D1160 d2)
						{
							if (d2.Pressure != null)
							{
								hYSYSAssay.DistillationPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, d2.Pressure);
							}
							hYSYSAssay.DistillationType = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D1160;
						}
					}
					else if (assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData != null && assayStream.DISTILLATION.DistillationData.GetType() == typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.D2887) && distillationData is P2Stream.AssayStream.Distillation.D2887)
					{
						hYSYSAssay.DistillationType = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D2887;
					}
					if (distillationData != null)
					{
						hYSYSAssay.AssayBoillingPercent = distillationData.Data.Keys.ToArray();
						hYSYSAssay.BoilingTempValues = distillationData.Data.Values.ToArray();
						hYSYSAssay.DistillationTempUnit = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, 1.0, distillationData.Temp).Unit;
					}
				}
				if (assayStream.GRAVITY != null && assayStream.GRAVITY.GetType() == typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream.Gravity))
				{
					P2Stream.AssayStream.Gravity gRAVITY = assayStream.GRAVITY;
					if (gRAVITY != null)
					{
						DoubleValue bulkMassDensity = null;
						if (gRAVITY.GravityType == P2Stream.AssayStream.GravityTypeEnum.API)
						{
							bulkMassDensity = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity, gRAVITY.Average, "API");
						}
						if (gRAVITY.GravityType == P2Stream.AssayStream.GravityTypeEnum.SPGR)
						{
							bulkMassDensity = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity, gRAVITY.Average, "SPGR");
						}
						if (gRAVITY.GravityType == P2Stream.AssayStream.GravityTypeEnum.WATSONK)
						{
							hYSYSAssay.IsWATSONK = true;
							bulkMassDensity = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, gRAVITY.Average);
						}
						hYSYSAssay.BulkMassDensity = bulkMassDensity;
						if (gRAVITY.Data != null && gRAVITY.Data.Count >= 2)
						{
							hYSYSAssay.DensityPercent = gRAVITY.Data.Keys.ToArray();
							hYSYSAssay.DensityValues = gRAVITY.Data.Values.ToArray();
						}
						else if (gRAVITY.Data != null && gRAVITY.Data.Count > 0)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.Convert, WarningLevel.Notes, $"PROPERTY STREAM \"{assayStream.Stream}\" " + string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_AssayGravityCurveNotConverted"), gRAVITY.GravityType.ToString()));
						}
					}
				}
				if (assayStream.LIGHTEND != null)
				{
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEnd();
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.COMPOSITION = assayStream.LIGHTEND.COMPOSITION;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis = assayStream.LIGHTEND.COMPOSITIONBasis;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit = assayStream.LIGHTEND.COMPOSITIONUnit;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.RATE = assayStream.LIGHTEND.RATE;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.FRACTION = assayStream.LIGHTEND.FRACTION;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.PERCENT = assayStream.LIGHTEND.PERCENT;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.MATCH = assayStream.LIGHTEND.MATCH;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.NOMATCH = assayStream.LIGHTEND.NOMATCH;
					hYSYSAssay.LightEndData.StreamName = assayStream.Stream;
				}
				if (assayStream.MW != null)
				{
					hYSYSAssay.MWAverage = assayStream.MW.Average;
					hYSYSAssay.MWData = assayStream.MW.Data;
				}
				hYSYSAssay.AssayBasis = assayStream.ASSAY;
				hYSYSAssay.UserProperties = CreateHYSYSAssayUserProperties(assayStream);
				hYSYSAssay.KVIS = CreateHYSYSAssayKivs(assayStream);
				if (ASSAY != null)
				{
					hYSYSAssay.Settings = settings;
				}
				list.Add(hYSYSAssay);
				num3++;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
		return list;
	}

	private List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSBlend> GetHysysBlend()
	{
		List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSBlend> list = new List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSBlend>();
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Stream))
		{
			return null;
		}
		IList<P2Stream.BaseStream> streams = (P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Stream] as P2Stream).Streams;
		if (streams == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		try
		{
			int num = -1;
			int num2 = 1;
			foreach (P2Stream.BaseStream item in streams)
			{
				if (item.GetType() != typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream) || !(item is P2Stream.AssayStream assayStream))
				{
					continue;
				}
				num++;
				HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSBlend hYSYSBlend = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSBlend();
				string text = assayStream.BLEND;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					text = assayStream.XBLEND;
				}
				Cutpoint cutpoint = null;
				if (CUTPOINTs != null)
				{
					Cutpoint cutpoint2 = null;
					foreach (Cutpoint cUTPOINT in CUTPOINTs)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && cUTPOINT.BLEND == text)
						{
							cutpoint = cUTPOINT;
							break;
						}
						if (cUTPOINT.DEFAULT || string.IsNullOrEmpty(cUTPOINT.BLEND))
						{
							cutpoint2 = cUTPOINT;
						}
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && cutpoint == null && cutpoint2 != null)
					{
						cutpoint = cutpoint2;
					}
				}
				if (cutpoint != null && cutpoint.TBPCUTS != null)
				{
					hYSYSBlend.IsUserRanges = true;
					hYSYSBlend.InitialCutPointValue = cutpoint.StartCutPoint;
					if (hYSYSBlend.InitialCutPointValue < 0.0)
					{
						hYSYSBlend.InitialCutPointValue = 0.0;
					}
					hYSYSBlend.NumberOfCutsInRangeValue = cutpoint.TBPCUTS.Values.ToArray();
					hYSYSBlend.EndRangeTemperatureValue = cutpoint.TBPCUTS.Keys.ToArray();
				}
				else
				{
					hYSYSBlend.IsUserRanges = false;
				}
				hYSYSBlend.TempUnit = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, 1.0, string.Empty).Unit;
				hYSYSBlend.BlendName = "B" + num + "_" + assayStream.Stream;
				if (hYSYSBlend.BlendName.Length >= 12)
				{
					hYSYSBlend.BlendName = hYSYSBlend.BlendName.Substring(0, 12);
				}
				hYSYSBlend.AssaysName = "A" + num + "_" + assayStream.Stream;
				if (hYSYSBlend.AssaysName.Length >= 12)
				{
					hYSYSBlend.AssaysName = hYSYSBlend.AssaysName.Substring(0, 12);
				}
				hYSYSBlend.StreamName = assayStream.Stream;
				list.Add(hYSYSBlend);
				num2++;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
		return list;
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent[] array = null;
		try
		{
			array = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent[1]
			{
				new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent()
			};
			array[0].TraditionalComponents = new List<string>();
			array[0].TraditionalComponents = CreateHYSYSTraditionalComponents();
			array[0].Petroleums = GetPETROLEUMs();
			array[0].Assays = GetHYSYSAssay();
			array[0].Blends = GetHysysBlend();
			array[0].NoLibraries = CreateHYSYSNolibrary();
			array[0].DuplicateNamedLibComp = DuplicateNamedLibComp;
			CheckAssayData(array[0].Assays);
			UserCompoent userCompoent = new UserCompoent();
			Dictionary<string, int> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, int>();
			if (LIBIDs != null && LIBIDs.LIBIDs != null && userCompoent.UserCompMapNoLib.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (LIBID.CompLIBID item in LIBIDs.LIBIDs.Values.ToList())
				{
					dictionary.Add(item.Name, item.nIndex);
				}
				foreach (UserCompoent.NotConvertedComp item2 in userCompoent.UserCompMapNoLib)
				{
					HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary noLibrary = new HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary();
