					if (notConvertedTraditionalComponents.Contains(item2.Pro2Key))
					{
						noLibrary.Name = item2.Pro2Key;
						noLibrary.MW = item2.MW;
						noLibrary.NBP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, item2.NBP);
						noLibrary.STDDENSITY = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, item2.Density);
						noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("MW");
						noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("NBP");
						noLibrary.AddedProperties.Add("STDDENSITY");
						if (array[0].NoLibraries == null)
						{
							array[0].NoLibraries = new List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary>();
						}
						array[0].NoLibraries.Add(noLibrary);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_Mapping")));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Comp_Mapping")));
			}
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void CheckAssayData(List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay> assays)
	{
		if (assays == null || assays.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay assay in assays)
		{
			if (assay.AssayBoillingPercent != null && assay.BoilingTempValues != null)
			{
				Dictionary<double, double> dictionary = new Dictionary<double, double>();
				for (int i = 0; i < assay.AssayBoillingPercent.Length; i++)
				{
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(assay.AssayBoillingPercent[i]))
					{
						dictionary.Add(assay.AssayBoillingPercent[i], assay.BoilingTempValues[i]);
					}
				}
				string caption = "Temperature [" + assay.DistillationTempUnit + "]";
				Dictionary<double, double> checkedValue = GetCheckedValue(dictionary, caption, isIncrease: true);
				if (checkedValue != null)
				{
					assay.AssayBoillingPercent = checkedValue.Keys.ToArray();
					assay.BoilingTempValues = checkedValue.Values.ToArray();
				}
				else
				{
					assay.AssayBoillingPercent = null;
					assay.BoilingTempValues = null;
				}
			}
			if (assay.MWData != null)
			{
				string caption2 = "MW";
				Dictionary<double, double> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<double, double>();
				foreach (KeyValuePair<double, double> mWDatum in assay.MWData)
				{
					dictionary2.Add(mWDatum.Key, mWDatum.Value);
				}
				assay.MWData = GetCheckedValue(dictionary2, caption2, isIncrease: true);
			}
			if (assay.DensityPercent != null && assay.DensityValues != null)
			{
				bool isIncrease = !assay.BulkMassDensity.Unit.StartsWith("API");
				string caption3 = "Density [" + assay.BulkMassDensity.Unit + "]";
				Dictionary<double, double> dictionary3 = new Dictionary<double, double>();
				for (int j = 0; j < assay.DensityPercent.Length; j++)
				{
					if (!dictionary3.ContainsKey(assay.DensityPercent[j]))
					{
						dictionary3.Add(assay.DensityPercent[j], assay.DensityValues[j]);
					}
				}
				Dictionary<double, double> checkedValue2 = GetCheckedValue(dictionary3, caption3, isIncrease);
				if (checkedValue2 != null)
				{
					assay.DensityPercent = checkedValue2.Keys.ToArray();
					assay.DensityValues = checkedValue2.Values.ToArray();
				}
				else
				{
					assay.DensityPercent = null;
					assay.DensityValues = null;
				}
			}
			if (assay.KVIS != null && assay.KVIS.DataTemp1 != null)
			{
				string caption4 = "Kinematic Viscosity [" + assay.KVIS.KinematicViscosityUnit + "]";
				assay.KVIS.DataTemp1 = GetCheckedValue(assay.KVIS.DataTemp1, caption4, isIncrease: true);
			}
			if (assay.KVIS != null && assay.KVIS.DataTemp2 != null)
			{
				string caption5 = "Kinematic Viscosity [" + assay.KVIS.KinematicViscosityUnit + "]";
				assay.KVIS.DataTemp2 = GetCheckedValue(assay.KVIS.DataTemp2, caption5, isIncrease: true);
			}
		}
	}

	private Dictionary<double, double> GetCheckedValue(Dictionary<double, double> inletValues, string caption, bool isIncrease)
	{
		if (inletValues != null)
		{
			SortedDictionary<double, double> sortedDictionary = new SortedDictionary<double, double>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<double, double> inletValue in inletValues)
			{
				sortedDictionary.Add(inletValue.Key, inletValue.Value);
			}
			List<double> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.CheckOrder(sortedDictionary, isIncrease);
			if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				do
				{
					flag = false;
					AssayInputCheckTable assayInputCheckTable = new AssayInputCheckTable(sortedDictionary, caption, isIncrease);
					if (assayInputCheckTable == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					assayInputCheckTable.ShowDialog();
					if (assayInputCheckTable.IsCloseButtonClick)
					{
						if (System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show("Data still have issues,are you sure to leave now? \n  Click \"No\" to check again,or click \"Yes\", the data will not be converted.", "Pro/II  Converter", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button2) == DialogResult.No)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						else
						{
							inletValues = null;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						inletValues = assayInputCheckTable.ReturnValues;
					}
				}
				while (flag);
			}
		}
		return inletValues;
	}
}
public class P2StreamCalculator : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class StreamProduct : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _dp = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public string V { get; set; }

		public string L { get; set; }

		public string W { get; set; }

		public string S { get; set; }

		public string M { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _dp;
			}
			set
			{
				_dp = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DT { get; set; }

		public bool DEWT { get; set; }

		public bool BUBT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DTAD { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DTBB { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEST { get; set; }

		public StreamProduct(string strSource)
		{
			string text = RetrieveProductString(strSource);
			ParseProduct(text);
			string operationExpress = strSource.Substring(text.Length);
			ParseOperation(operationExpress);
		}

		private string RetrieveProductString(string source)
		{
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			foreach (string item2 in new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.DT,
				CUnitOperation.DEWTEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.BUBBLETEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.DTAD,
				CUnitOperation.DTBB,
				"TEST"
			})
			{
				int item = source.IndexOf(item2);
				if (!item.Equals(-1))
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			list.Sort();
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				return source.Substring(0, list[0]);
			}
			return source;
		}

		private void ParseProduct(string productExpress)
		{
			List<string> propertyList = new List<string> { "V=", "L=", "W=", "S=", "M=" };
			productExpress = productExpress.Replace(" ", "");
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(propertyList, productExpress))
			{
				SetProduct(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetProduct(string source)
		{
			source = source.ToUpper();
			if (source.ToUpper().StartsWith("V"))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(source).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				V = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("V");
			}
			else if (source.ToUpper().StartsWith("L"))
			{
				string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(source).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				L = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("L");
			}
			else if (source.ToUpper().StartsWith("W"))
			{
				string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(source).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				W = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("W");
			}
			else if (source.ToUpper().StartsWith("S"))
			{
				string strName4 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(source).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				S = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("S");
			}
			else if (source.ToUpper().StartsWith("M"))
			{
				string strName5 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(source).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				M = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
			}
		}

		private void ParseOperation(string operationExpress)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.DT,
				CUnitOperation.DEWTEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.BUBBLETEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.DTAD,
				CUnitOperation.DTBB,
				"TEST"
			}, operationExpress);
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				SetValue(array[i]);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string source)
		{
			if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
			{
				PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(source);
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
			{
				if (source.Contains("="))
				{
					DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(source);
				}
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
			{
				TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(source);
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT))
			{
				DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(source);
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEWTEMPERATURE))
			{
				DEWT = true;
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BUBBLETEMPERATURE))
			{
				BUBT = true;
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DTAD))
			{
				DTAD = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(source);
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DTBB))
			{
				DTBB = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(source);
			}
			else if (source.StartsWith("TEST"))
			{
				TEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(source);
			}
		}
	}

	public class FeedBlend : PropertyItem
	{
		public Dictionary<string, double> FeedBlends { get; set; }

		public FeedBlend(string strSource)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(strSource);
			foreach (string str in array)
			{
				if (FeedBlends == null)
				{
					FeedBlends = new Dictionary<string, double>();
				}
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(str);
				if (!FeedBlends.ContainsKey(array2[0]))
				{
					if (array2.Length == 1)
					{
						FeedBlends.Add(array2[0], 1.0);
					}
					else
					{
						FeedBlends.Add(array2[0], Convert.ToDouble(array2[1]));
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class StreamCalculatorOperation : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _duty = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public string STOP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY
		{
			get
			{
				return _duty;
			}
			set
			{
				_duty = value;
			}
		}

		public StreamCalculatorOperation(string strSource)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.STOP,
				CUnitOperation.DUTY
			}, strSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STOP))
				{
					STOP = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
				}
				else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && array[i].Contains("="))
				{
					DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public List<Specification> OverheadSpecs = new List<Specification>();

	public List<Specification> BottomSpecs = new List<Specification>();

	public List<Specification> SynthesisSpecs = new List<Specification>();

	public FeedBlend BlendFeeds { get; set; }

	public StreamProduct OverheadProduct { get; set; }

	public StreamProduct BottomProduct { get; set; }

	public StreamProduct SynthesisProduct { get; set; }

	public StreamCalculatorOperation Operation { get; set; }

	public bool bConvert { get; set; }

	public P2StreamCalculator(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			if (OverheadProduct != null || BottomProduct != null)
			{
				bConvert = true;
			}
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.STREAMCALCULATOR + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "StreamCalculator", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			empty = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				BlendFeeds = new FeedBlend(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("FeedBlending");
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OVHD))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				OverheadProduct = new StreamProduct(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("OverheadProduct");
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BTMS))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				BottomProduct = new StreamProduct(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("BottomProduct");
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OVERHEADFRACTION) || empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OVERHEADRATE) || empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OVERHEADCOMP))
			{
				List<Specification> specs = new List<Specification>();
				string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				ParseSpecificationBody(text, out specs);
				string source = empty.Substring(0, empty.Length - text.Length).Trim();
				ParseSpecificationHead(source, specs);
				foreach (Specification item2 in specs)
				{
					OverheadSpecs.Add(item2);
				}
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BOTTOMFRACTION) || empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BOTTOMRATE) || empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BOTTOMCOMP))
			{
				List<Specification> specs2 = new List<Specification>();
				string text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				ParseSpecificationBody(text2, out specs2);
				string source2 = empty.Substring(0, empty.Length - text2.Length).Trim();
				ParseSpecificationHead(source2, specs2);
				foreach (Specification item3 in specs2)
				{
					BottomSpecs.Add(item3);
				}
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				SynthesisProduct = new StreamProduct(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("SynthesisProduct");
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCTFRACTION) || empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCTRATE) || empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCTCOMP))
			{
				List<Specification> specs3 = new List<Specification>();
				string text3 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				ParseSpecificationBody(text3, out specs3);
				string source3 = empty.Substring(0, empty.Length - text3.Length).Trim();
				ParseSpecificationHead(source3, specs3);
				foreach (Specification item4 in specs3)
				{
					SynthesisSpecs.Add(item4);
				}
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				Operation = new StreamCalculatorOperation(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Operation");
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty2, "STCA", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty2);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ParseSpecificationHead(string source, List<Specification> specs)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		string sTCASPECUNIT = string.Empty;
		string empty2 = string.Empty;
		string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(source);
		if (parentheses != null)
		{
			if (source.StartsWith("R"))
			{
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(parentheses);
				if (array.Length == 1)
				{
					empty = "M";
					sTCASPECUNIT = array[0];
				}
				else
				{
					empty = array[0];
					sTCASPECUNIT = array[1];
				}
			}
			else
			{
				empty = parentheses;
			}
			empty2 = source.Substring(0, source.Length - parentheses.Length - 2);
		}
		else
		{
			empty = "M";
			empty2 = source;
		}
		foreach (Specification spec in specs)
		{
			spec.STCASPECBASIS = empty;
			spec.STCASPECUNIT = sTCASPECUNIT;
			spec.STCASPECTYPE = empty2;
		}
	}

	private void ParseSpecificationBody(string source, out List<Specification> specs)
	{
		specs = new List<Specification>();
		string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(source);
		string value = string.Empty;
		string[] array2 = array;
		foreach (string str in array2)
		{
			Specification specification = new Specification();
			string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(str);
			if (array3.Length == 1)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					specification.STCASPECFIRSTCOMP = "1";
				}
				else
				{
					specification.STCASPECFIRSTCOMP = (Convert.ToInt32(value) + 1).ToString();
				}
				specification.STCASPECVALUE = array3[0];
				value = specification.STCASPECFIRSTCOMP;
			}
			else if (array3.Length == 2)
			{
				specification.STCASPECFIRSTCOMP = array3[0];
				specification.STCASPECVALUE = array3[1];
				value = specification.STCASPECFIRSTCOMP;
			}
			else
			{
				specification.STCASPECFIRSTCOMP = array3[0];
				specification.STCASPECLASTCOMP = array3[1];
				specification.STCASPECVALUE = array3[2];
				value = specification.STCASPECLASTCOMP;
			}
			specs.Add(specification);
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSComponentSplitter hYSYSComponentSplitter = new HYSYSComponentSplitter();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSComponentSplitter);
			hYSYSComponentSplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSComponentSplitter.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSComponentSplitter.Name = base.Name;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSComponentSplitter.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			ConvertFeedBlends(list, hYSYSComponentSplitter);
			if (OverheadProduct != null)
			{
				ConvertProducts(list, hYSYSComponentSplitter, OverheadProduct, "OVHD");
			}
			if (BottomProduct != null)
			{
				ConvertProducts(list, hYSYSComponentSplitter, BottomProduct, "BTMS");
			}
			if (SynthesisProduct != null)
			{
				hYSYSComponentSplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(P2Globalization.GetString("STCALNotConverted_1"));
			}
			hYSYSComponentSplitter.OVHDSpecs = OverheadSpecs;
			hYSYSComponentSplitter.BTMSSpecs = BottomSpecs;
			if (Operation != null)
			{
				hYSYSComponentSplitter.DUTY = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, Operation.DUTY);
			}
			if (Operation != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Operation.STOP))
			{
				hYSYSComponentSplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "STOP"));
			}
			SetOutletStreams(hYSYSComponentSplitter);
			hYSYSComponentSplitter.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSComponentSplitter.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSComponentSplitter.SeparateDefines();
			}
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "StreamCalculator", base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void ConvertFeedBlends(List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl, HYSYSComponentSplitter compsplitter)
	{
		if (BlendFeeds == null || BlendFeeds.FeedBlends == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		Dictionary<string, double> feedBlends = BlendFeeds.FeedBlends;
		foreach (string key in feedBlends.Keys)
		{
			compsplitter.InletStreams.Add(key);
			double scalingfactor = feedBlends[key];
			if (scalingfactor.Equals(1.0))
			{
				continue;
			}
			HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = HYSYSObjectHelper.AddSplitterForFeedBlending(compsplitter, scalingfactor, key);
			if (hYSYSSplitter != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
				{
					hYSYSSplitter.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
				}
				objColl.Add(hYSYSSplitter);
				hYSYSSplitter.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
		}
		if (compsplitter.InletStreams.Count > 1)
		{
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(compsplitter);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				objColl.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConvertProducts(List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl, HYSYSComponentSplitter compsplitter, StreamProduct product, string name)
	{
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		foreach (string addedProperty in product.AddedPropertyList)
		{
			string text = product.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
			if (name.Equals("OVHD"))
			{
				compsplitter.OVHDStreams.Add(text);
			}
			else
			{
				compsplitter.BTMStreams.Add(text);
			}
			dictionary.Add(addedProperty, text);
		}
		if (dictionary.Count > 1)
		{
			compsplitter.OutletStreams.Clear();
			if (name.Equals("OVHD"))
			{
				foreach (string oVHDStream in compsplitter.OVHDStreams)
				{
					compsplitter.OutletStreams.Add(oVHDStream);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (string bTMStream in compsplitter.BTMStreams)
				{
					compsplitter.OutletStreams.Add(bTMStream);
				}
			}
			List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, compsplitter);
			if (list != null)
			{
				foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item in list)
				{
					objColl.Add(item);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(compsplitter.FluidPackage))
					{
						item.FluidPackage = compsplitter.FluidPackage;
					}
					item.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
				}
			}
			if (name.Equals("OVHD"))
			{
				compsplitter.OVHDStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string outletStream in compsplitter.OutletStreams)
				{
					compsplitter.OVHDStreams.Add(outletStream);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				compsplitter.BTMStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string outletStream2 in compsplitter.OutletStreams)
				{
					compsplitter.BTMStreams.Add(outletStream2);
				}
			}
		}
		if (product.PRESSURE != null)
		{
			if (name.Equals("OVHD"))
			{
				compsplitter.OVHDPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, product.PRESSURE);
			}
			else
			{
				compsplitter.BTMSPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, product.PRESSURE);
			}
		}
		if (product.DP.Value != 0.0)
		{
			if (name.Equals("OVHD"))
			{
				compsplitter.OVHDDP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, product.DP);
			}
			else
			{
				compsplitter.BTMSDP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, product.DP);
			}
		}
		if (product.TEMPERATURE != null)
		{
			if (name.Equals("OVHD"))
			{
				compsplitter.OVHDTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, product.TEMPERATURE);
			}
			else
			{
				compsplitter.BTMSTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, product.TEMPERATURE);
			}
		}
		if (product.DT != null)
		{
			if (name.Equals("OVHD"))
			{
				compsplitter.OVHDDT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, product.DT);
			}
			else
			{
				compsplitter.BTMSDT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, product.DT);
			}
		}
		if (product.DEWT)
		{
			compsplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), name + " DEWT"));
		}
		if (product.BUBT)
		{
			compsplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), name + " BUBT"));
		}
		if (product.DTAD != null)
		{
			compsplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), name + " DTAD"));
		}
		if (product.DTBB != null)
		{
			compsplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), name + " DTBB"));
		}
		if (product.TEST != null)
		{
			compsplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), name + " Test"));
		}
	}

	private void SetOutletStreams(HYSYSComponentSplitter compsplitter)
	{
		compsplitter.OutletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (string oVHDStream in compsplitter.OVHDStreams)
		{
			compsplitter.OutletStreams.Add(oVHDStream);
		}
		foreach (string bTMStream in compsplitter.BTMStreams)
		{
			compsplitter.OutletStreams.Add(bTMStream);
		}
	}
}
public class P2Controller : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class SpecificationVariable
	{
		public List<string> DUTY { get; set; }

		public List<string> DNAME { get; set; }

		public List<string> DRAW { get; set; }

		public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

		public SpecificationVariable()
		{
		}

		public SpecificationVariable(string sSource)
		{
			DUTY = new List<string>();
			DNAME = new List<string>();
			DRAW = new List<string>();
			FEED = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.DUTY,
				CUnitOperation.DNAME,
				CUnitOperation.DRAW,
				CUnitOperation.FEED
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY))
				{
					DUTY.Add(text.Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DNAME))
				{
					DNAME.Add(text.Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DRAW))
				{
					DRAW.Add(text.Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
				{
					FEED.Add(text.Trim());
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Cparameter
	{
		private int _ITER = 10;

		private string _Statue = "SOLVE";

		public int ITER
		{
			get
			{
				return _ITER;
			}
			set
			{
				_ITER = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IPRINT { get; set; }

		public string Statue
		{
			get
			{
				return _Statue;
			}
			set
			{
				_Statue = value;
			}
		}

		public string RETURNUNIT { get; set; }

		public Cparameter(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.ITER,
				CUnitOperation.IPRINT,
				CUnitOperation.NOPRINT,
				CUnitOperation.SOLVE,
				CUnitOperation.STOP,
				CUnitOperation.CONTINUE,
				CUnitOperation.RETURNUNIT
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				try
				{
					if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ITER))
					{
						ITER = (int)ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text).Value;
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.IPRINT))
					{
						IPRINT = true;
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NOPRINT))
					{
						IPRINT = false;
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SOLVE))
					{
						Statue = "SOLVE";
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STOP))
					{
						Statue = "STOP";
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONTINUE))
					{
						Statue = "CONTINUE";
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RETURNUNIT))
					{
						RETURNUNIT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public Specification SPEC { get; set; }

	public SpecificationVariable VARY { get; set; }

	public Cparameter CPARAMETER { get; set; }

	public string VaryString { get; set; }

	public static List<P2Controller> GetControlList(string uid)
	{
		List<P2Controller> list = new List<P2Controller>();
		foreach (KeyValuePair<string, P2BaseObject> item in P2BaseObject.g_Map)
		{
			if (item.Value.GetType() == typeof(P2Controller) && item.Value is P2Controller p2Controller && p2Controller.SPEC.PrimaryID == uid)
			{
				list.Add(p2Controller);
			}
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			return list;
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static List<P2Controller> GetAllControllerByUnitOpType(string unitOpType)
	{
		List<P2Controller> list = new List<P2Controller>();
		foreach (KeyValuePair<string, P2BaseObject> item in P2BaseObject.g_Map)
		{
			if (item.Value.GetType() == typeof(P2Controller) && item.Value is P2Controller p2Controller && p2Controller.SPEC.PrimaryName == unitOpType)
			{
				list.Add(p2Controller);
			}
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			return list;
		}
		return null;
	}

	public P2Controller(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Notes, $"Build P2 P2Controller");
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.CONTROL + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, $"Build P2 P2Controller {ex.StackTrace}");
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
			if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPEC))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				SPEC = new Specification(text, "CONTROLLER", base.UID);
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VARY))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				VARY = new SpecificationVariable(text);
				VaryString = text;
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CPARAMETER))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				CPARAMETER = new Cparameter(text);
			}
		}
	}
}
public class P2CSTReactor : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Operation
	{
		private string _PHASE = "L";

		private bool _ISOTHERMAL = true;

		private DoubleValue _VMax = new DoubleValue("M3", 100.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMax = new DoubleValue("K", 3000.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMin = new DoubleValue("K", 1.0);

		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _DUTY = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public DoubleValue VOLUMN { get; set; }

		public string PHASE
		{
			get
			{
				return _PHASE;
			}
			set
			{
				_PHASE = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public bool ISOTHERMAL
		{
			get
			{
				return _ISOTHERMAL;
			}
			set
			{
				_ISOTHERMAL = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public bool ADIABATIC { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue VMax
		{
			get
			{
				return _VMax;
			}
			set
			{
				_VMax = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMax
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMax;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMax = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMin
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMin;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMin = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DUTY
		{
			get
			{
				return _DUTY;
			}
			set
			{
				_DUTY = value;
			}
		}

		public Operation()
		{
		}

		public Operation(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.VOLUMN,
				CUnitOperation.VMax,
				CUnitOperation.TMax,
				CUnitOperation.TMin,
				CUnitOperation.PHASE,
				CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.THERMAL,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC,
				CUnitOperation.DUTY
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VOLUMN))
					{
						VOLUMN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VMax))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							VMax = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMax))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							TMax = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMin))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							TMin = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHASE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							PHASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim();
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.THERMAL))
					{
						ISOTHERMAL = true;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC))
					{
						ISOTHERMAL = false;
						ADIABATIC = true;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && array[i].Contains('='))
					{
						DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Reaction
	{
		private DoubleValue _PEXP = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 1.0);

		private DoubleValue _ACTIVATION = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _TEXPONENT = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		public string RXID { get; set; }

		public int COMPONENT { get; set; }

		public string KPHASE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PEXP
		{
			get
			{
				return _PEXP;
			}
			set
			{
				_PEXP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue ACTIVATION
		{
			get
			{
				return _ACTIVATION;
			}
			set
			{
				_ACTIVATION = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEXPONENT
		{
			get
			{
				return _TEXPONENT;
			}
			set
			{
				_TEXPONENT = value;
			}
		}

		public Dictionary<int, double> KORDER { get; set; }

		public void SetKinetic(string sSource)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sSource))
			{
				return;
			}
			sSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.COMPONENT,
				CUnitOperation.PEXP,
				CUnitOperation.ACTIVATION,
				CUnitOperation.TEXPONENT
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COMPONENT))
					{
						COMPONENT = int.Parse(array[i].Trim());
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PEXP))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							PEXP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ACTIVATION))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							ACTIVATION = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEXPONENT) && array[i].Contains('='))
					{
						TEXPONENT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetKorder(string sSource)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sSource))
			{
				return;
			}
			sSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(sSource);
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array[i]);
					if (array2 != null && array2.Length == 2)
					{
						if (KORDER == null)
						{
							KORDER = new Dictionary<int, double>();
						}
						KORDER.Add(int.Parse(array2[0]), double.Parse(array2[1]));
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Nonvolatile
	{
		public Dictionary<int, DoubleValue> CHARGE { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<int, DoubleValue> FRACTION { get; set; }

		public Nonvolatile(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.CHARGE,
				CUnitOperation.FRACTION
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CHARGE))
					{
						CHARGE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValueDic(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRACTION))
					{
						CHARGE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValueDic(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Parameter
	{
		private double _BTRIAL = 3.0;

		private double _DERIV = 0.01;

		public double BTRIAL
		{
			get
			{
				return _BTRIAL;
			}
			set
			{
				_BTRIAL = value;

		{
			get
			{
				return _DERIV;
			}
			set
			{
				_DERIV = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue VESTIMATE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TESTIMATE { get; set; }

		public Parameter()
		{
		}

		public Parameter(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.BTRIAL,
				CUnitOperation.DERIV,
				CUnitOperation.VESTIMATE,
				CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BTRIAL))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							BTRIAL = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim());
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DERIV))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))

							DERIV = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim());

					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VESTIMATE))
					{
						VESTIMATE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
					{
						TESTIMATE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Tolerance

			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;

			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{

				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))

							TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);

					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Rxcalc
	{
		private RXBasis _ActivityBasisVapor;

		private RXBasis _ActivityBasisLiquid;

		private QualifierValue _KINETICS = new QualifierValue("", "POWERLAW");

		public RXBasis ActivityBasisVapor
		{
			get
			{
				return _ActivityBasisVapor;
			}
			set
			{
				_ActivityBasisVapor = value;
			}
		}

		public RXBasis ActivityBasisLiquid
		{
			get
			{
				return _ActivityBasisLiquid;
			}
			set
			{
				_ActivityBasisLiquid = value;
			}
		}

		public QualifierValue KINETICS
		{
			get
			{
				return _KINETICS;
			}
			set
			{
				_KINETICS = value;
			}
		}

		public Rxcalc()
		{
		}

		public Rxcalc(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.VAPOR,
				CUnitOperation.LIQUID,
				CUnitOperation.KINETICS
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VAPOR))
					{
						ActivityBasisVapor = (RXBasis)Enum.Parse(typeof(RXBasis), ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim());
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LIQUID))
					{
						ActivityBasisLiquid = (RXBasis)Enum.Parse(typeof(RXBasis), ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim());
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KINETICS))
					{
						KINETICS = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private static bool _isParsError;

	private HYSYSCSTReactor _HysysCSTReactor;

	private List<HYSYSBaseObject> _ObjectCollection;

	public string RXSTOIC_RXSET { get; set; }

	public string PRINT_PATH { get; set; }

	public Operation OPERATION { get; set; }

	public List<Reaction> Reactions { get; set; }

	public Rxcalc RXCALC { get; set; }

	public Nonvolatile NONVOLATILE { get; set; }

	public Parameter PARAMETER { get; set; }

	public Dictionary<int, double> FESTIMATE { get; set; }

	public Tolerance TOLERANCE { get; set; }

	public P2CSTReactor(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			_isParsError = false;
			TOLERANCE = new Tolerance();
			PARAMETER = new Parameter();
			RXCALC = new Rxcalc();
			OPERATION = new Operation();
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.CSTReactor + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "CSTR", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bParsingError || _isParsError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "CSTR", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		if (obj == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
		{
			return;
		}
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		SetHeadData(text.Trim().ToUpper());
		if (obj.Children == null || obj.Children.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < obj.Children.Count; i++)
		{
			try
			{
				P2BaseObject p2BaseObject = obj.Children[i];
				base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + p2BaseObject.ToString() + "\n";
				string text2 = p2BaseObject.Name.ToUpper();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
				{
					base.FEED = new List<string>();
					text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' ');
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(text2);
					for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
					{
						string strName = array[j].Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						base.FEED.Add(strName);
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
				{
					text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' ').Trim();
					base.PRODUCT = new Product(text2);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC))
				{
					RXSTOIC_RXSET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REACTION))
				{
					if (Reactions == null)
					{
						Reactions = new List<Reaction>();
					}
					Reaction reaction = new Reaction();
					reaction.RXID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
					if (p2BaseObject.Children != null && p2BaseObject.Children.Count > 0)
					{
						foreach (P2BaseObject child in p2BaseObject.Children)
						{
							string text3 = child.Name.ToUpper();
							if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KINETICS))
							{
								reaction.SetKinetic(text3.Trim());
							}
							else if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KORDER))
							{
								reaction.SetKorder(text3.Trim());
							}
							else if (text3.StartsWith(CReactionData.KPHASE))
							{
								reaction.KPHASE = text3;
							}
						}
					}
					Reactions.Add(reaction);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
				{
					OPERATION = new Operation(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXCALC))
				{
					RXCALC = new Rxcalc(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NONVOLATILE))
				{
					NONVOLATILE = new Nonvolatile(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PARAMETER))
				{
					PARAMETER = new Parameter(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FESTIMATE))
				{
					FESTIMATE = ParseUtilty.GetIntDoubleDic(text2);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TOLERANCE))
				{
					TOLERANCE = new Tolerance(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRINT))
				{
					PRINT_PATH = "PATH";
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
				{
					text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' ');
					if (!text2.StartsWith("PDATA"))
					{
						DEFINE item = new DEFINE(text2, "CSTR", base.UID);
						base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
				{
					base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			_HysysCSTReactor = new HYSYSCSTReactor();
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			_HysysCSTReactor.UID = base.UID;
			_HysysCSTReactor.Name = base.Name;
			_HysysCSTReactor.EnergyName = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			_ObjectCollection = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			_ObjectCollection.Add(_HysysCSTReactor);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PRINT_PATH))
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PRINT PATH"));
			}
			ConvertStreams();
			ConvertSetAndReactions();
			ConvertOperation();
			ConvertRXCALC();
			ConvertOtherKeyWords();
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				_HysysCSTReactor.SeparateDefines();
			}
			array = _ObjectCollection.ToArray();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.Convert, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "CSTR", base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void ConvertStreams()
	{
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		if (base.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (base.IsMultipleFeed && OPERATION.ISOTHERMAL)
		{
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOAdded"), "mixer", "multiple feeds"));
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(_HysysCSTReactor);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
				}
				_ObjectCollection.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(_ObjectCollection);
			}
		}
		if (base.PRODUCT == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
		{
			_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
			dictionary.Add(addedProperty, base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
		}
		if (dictionary.ContainsKey("S"))
		{
			string item = P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_ProductSolidStream") + " " + dictionary["S"] + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted");
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			if (_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Contains(dictionary["S"]))
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Remove(dictionary["S"]);
			}
			dictionary.Remove("S");
		}
		if (_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Count == 1 && base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Contains("M"))
		{
			string text = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
			_HysysCSTReactor.LquidOutlet = text;
			_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Add(text);
			string text2 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
			_HysysCSTReactor.VapourOutlet = text2;
			_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Add(text2);
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOAdded"), "mixer", "M stream"));
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductToMixer(_HysysCSTReactor, dictionary["M"]);
			if (hYSYSMixer2 != null)
			{
				_ObjectCollection.Add(hYSYSMixer2);
				hYSYSMixer2.SetCreateStreams(_ObjectCollection);
			}
		}
		else if ((dictionary.Count == 2 && dictionary.ContainsKey("L") && dictionary.ContainsKey("V")) || (dictionary.Count == 3 && dictionary.ContainsKey("L") && dictionary.ContainsKey("V") && dictionary.ContainsKey("S")))
		{
			if (dictionary.ContainsKey("S"))
			{
				string item2 = P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_ProductSolidStream") + " " + dictionary["S"] + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted");
				_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
			}
			_HysysCSTReactor.VapourOutlet = dictionary["V"];
			_HysysCSTReactor.LquidOutlet = dictionary["L"];
		}
		else
		{
			if (_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Count <= 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOAdded"), "Separator", P2Globalization.GetString("MultipleProducts")));
			List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, _HysysCSTReactor);
			_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Clear();
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			empty2 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
			_HysysCSTReactor.LquidOutlet = empty2;
			_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Add(empty2);
			empty = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
			_HysysCSTReactor.VapourOutlet = empty;
			_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreams.Add(empty);
			if (list == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item3 in list)
			{
				if (item3 is HYSYSSeparator hYSYSSeparator)
				{
					hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Clear();
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
					{
						hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Add(empty2);
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
					{
						hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Add(empty);
					}
				}
				_ObjectCollection.Add(item3);
				item3.SetCreateStreams(_ObjectCollection);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConvertSetAndReactions()
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(RXSTOIC_RXSET))
		{
			_HysysCSTReactor.ReactionSet = new HYSYSReactionSet(base.UID + "_CSTR_" + RXSTOIC_RXSET);
		}
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey("ReactionData") || (Reactions == null && Reactions.Count == 0))
		{
			return;
		}
		P2ReactionData p2ReactionData = (P2ReactionData)P2BaseObject.g_Map["ReactionData"];
		RXPhase rXPhase = RXPhase.V;
		bool flag = false;
		int num = 0;
		foreach (Reaction reaction in Reactions)
		{
			P2ReactionData.P2Reaction p2Reaction = p2ReactionData.P2RXSetsDic[RXSTOIC_RXSET].P2RXsDic[reaction.RXID];
			if (p2Reaction != null)
			{
				if (num == 0)
				{
					rXPhase = p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT;
				}
				if (rXPhase != p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			if (rXPhase == RXPhase.V)
			{
				OPERATION.PHASE = RXPhase.V.ToString();
			}
			else
			{
				OPERATION.PHASE = RXPhase.L.ToString();
			}
		}
		foreach (Reaction reaction2 in Reactions)
		{
			P2ReactionData.P2Reaction p2Reaction2 = p2ReactionData.P2RXSetsDic[RXSTOIC_RXSET].P2RXsDic[reaction2.RXID];
			if (p2Reaction2 == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			string iD = base.UID + "_" + RXSTOIC_RXSET + "_" + reaction2.RXID;
			HYSYSKineticReaction hYSYSKineticReaction = p2ReactionData.CreateHYSYSKineticRX(reaction2.RXID, p2Reaction2);
			hYSYSKineticReaction.ID = iD;
			if (hYSYSKineticReaction == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			if (reaction2.COMPONENT != 0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.BaseComponent = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(reaction2.COMPONENT);
			}
			if (reaction2.PEXP.Value != 1.0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.FrequencyFactor = reaction2.PEXP.Value;
			}
			if (reaction2.ACTIVATION.Value != 0.0 || hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy == null || hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy.Value == 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.MolarEnthalpy, reaction2.ACTIVATION);
			}
			if (reaction2.TEXPONENT.Value != 0.0 || hYSYSKineticReaction.ExtReacRateConst == 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.ExtReacRateConst = reaction2.TEXPONENT.Value;
			}
			if (reaction2.KORDER != null && reaction2.KORDER.Count > 0)
			{
				if (hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder == null)
				{
					hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder = new Dictionary<string, double>();
				}
				else
				{
					hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder.Clear();
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in reaction2.KORDER)
				{
					if (hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder == null)
					{
						hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder = new Dictionary<string, double>();
					}
					hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder.Add(P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(item.Key), item.Value);
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(reaction2.KPHASE))
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "REACTION KPHASE"));
			}
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase = OPERATION.PHASE;
			}
			if (RXCALC != null)
			{
				if (OPERATION.PHASE == "L")
				{
					hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis = RXCALC.ActivityBasisLiquid;
				}
				if (OPERATION.PHASE == "V")
				{
					hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis = RXCALC.ActivityBasisVapor;
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSKineticReaction != null)
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSKineticReaction);
			}
		}
		_ObjectCollection.Add(_HysysCSTReactor.ReactionSet);
	}

	private void ConvertOperation()
	{
		if (OPERATION == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		_HysysCSTReactor.DeltaPresure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
		_HysysCSTReactor.ProductPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRESSURE);
		double liquidVolumnPercent = 0.5;
		_HysysCSTReactor.IsSinglePhase = true;
		if (OPERATION.PHASE != null)
		{
			if (OPERATION.PHASE.StartsWith("BOIL"))
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.IsSinglePhase = false;
				if (PARAMETER.VESTIMATE != null && OPERATION.VMax == null)
				{
					OPERATION.VMax = new DoubleValue(PARAMETER.VESTIMATE.Unit, PARAMETER.VESTIMATE.Value * 2.0);
				}
				if (OPERATION.VMax != null && OPERATION.VOLUMN != null && OPERATION.VOLUMN.Value > 0.0)
				{
					liquidVolumnPercent = OPERATION.VOLUMN.Value / OPERATION.VMax.Value;
				}
				_HysysCSTReactor.Volumn = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume, OPERATION.VMax);
			}
			else if (OPERATION.PHASE.StartsWith("L") && OPERATION.VOLUMN != null)
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.Volumn = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume, OPERATION.VOLUMN);
				liquidVolumnPercent = 1.0;
			}
			else if (OPERATION.PHASE.StartsWith("V") && OPERATION.VOLUMN != null)
			{
				_HysysCSTReactor.Volumn = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume, OPERATION.VOLUMN);
				liquidVolumnPercent = 0.0;
			}
		}
		_HysysCSTReactor.LiquidVolumnPercent1 = liquidVolumnPercent;
		if (OPERATION.ISOTHERMAL)
		{
			if (OPERATION.TEMPERATURE == null)
			{
				try
				{
					string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "ISOTHERMAL");
					if (_HysysCSTReactor.InletStreams != null && _HysysCSTReactor.InletStreams.Count == 1)
					{
						IList<P2Stream.BaseStream> streams = (P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Stream] as P2Stream).Streams;
						if (streams != null)
						{
							foreach (P2Stream.BaseStream item2 in streams)
							{
								if (item2.Stream == _HysysCSTReactor.InletStreams[0] && item2.Temp != null)
								{
									_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreamTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, item2.Temp);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreamTemperature == null)
					{
						_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			CreateHYSYSHeater();
			_HysysCSTReactor.OutletStreamTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, OPERATION.TEMPERATURE);
		}
		else if (OPERATION.ADIABATIC)
		{
			_HysysCSTReactor.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, OPERATION.DUTY);
		}
	}

	private void CreateHYSYSHeater()
	{
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger();
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
		HYSYSObjectHelper.InsertObjectInStream(_HysysCSTReactor, _HysysCSTReactor.InletStreams[0], bInletStream: true, hYSYSHeatExchanger);
		if (hYSYSHeatExchanger != null)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater;
			if (OPERATION.TEMPERATURE != null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, OPERATION.TEMPERATURE);
			}
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.LoadStreams();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			_ObjectCollection.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(_ObjectCollection);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertRXCALC()
	{
		if (RXCALC != null && RXCALC.KINETICS.Value != "POWERLAW")
		{
			string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "KINETICS");
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertOtherKeyWords()
	{
		if (PARAMETER != null)
		{
			string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "BTRIAL");
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "DERIV");
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume, PARAMETER.VESTIMATE);
			if (hysysValue != null)
			{
				item = string.Format(" PARAMETER VESTIMATE={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue.Value, hysysValue.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
				_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			}
			hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume, PARAMETER.TESTIMATE);
			if (hysysValue != null)
			{
				item = string.Format(" PARAMETER TESTIMATE={0} {1}  {2}", hysysValue.Value, hysysValue.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
				_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			}
		}
		if (NONVOLATILE != null)
		{
			if (NONVOLATILE.CHARGE != null && NONVOLATILE.CHARGE.Count > 0)
			{
				string empty = string.Empty;
				_ = string.Empty;
				empty = $"NONVOLATILE CHARGE";
				using (Dictionary<int, DoubleValue>.Enumerator enumerator = NONVOLATILE.CHARGE.GetEnumerator())
				{
					if (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						KeyValuePair<int, DoubleValue> current = enumerator.Current;
						empty = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(current.Value.Unit)) ? (empty + current.Value.Unit) : (empty + "(M) "));
					}
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<int, DoubleValue> item3 in NONVOLATILE.CHARGE)
				{
					_ = $" {item3.Key},{item3.Value.Value}/";
				}
				_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(empty);
			}
			if (NONVOLATILE.FRACTION != null && NONVOLATILE.FRACTION.Count > 0)
			{
				string empty2 = string.Empty;
				string arg = string.Empty;
				empty2 = $"NONVOLATILE FRACTION";
				using (Dictionary<int, DoubleValue>.Enumerator enumerator = NONVOLATILE.FRACTION.GetEnumerator())
				{
					if (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						KeyValuePair<int, DoubleValue> current3 = enumerator.Current;
						empty2 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(current3.Value.Unit)) ? (empty2 + current3.Value.Unit) : (empty2 + "(M) "));
					}
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<int, DoubleValue> item4 in NONVOLATILE.FRACTION)
				{
					arg = $"{item4.Key},{item4.Value.Value}/";
				}
				empty2 = string.Format("{0} {1} " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"), empty2, arg);
				_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(empty2);
			}
		}
		if (FESTIMATE != null && FESTIMATE.Count > 0)
		{
			string empty3 = string.Empty;
			empty3 = $"FESTIMATE ";
			string arg2 = string.Empty;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item5 in FESTIMATE)
			{
				arg2 = $"{item5.Key},{item5.Value}/";
			}
			empty3 = string.Format("{0} {1} " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"), empty3, arg2);
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(empty3);
		}
		if (TOLERANCE != null)
		{
			UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, TOLERANCE.TEMP);
			string item2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TOLERANCE TEMP");
			_HysysCSTReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
		}
	}
}

	public class Side : PropertyItem
	{
		public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

		public List<string> M { get; set; }

		public string METHOD { get; set; }

		public Side(string source)
		{
			FEED = new List<string>();
			List<string> list = new List<string>
			{
				"FEED=",
				"M=",
				CUnitOperation.METHOD
			};
			string strSource = source.Replace(" ", "").ToUpper();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, strSource);
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			string text = string.Empty;
			int index = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				num++;
				if (item.Name == "FEED=")
				{
					flag = true;
					index = source.IndexOf("FEED=");
					if (index < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref index, source, "FEED");
						if (num == propertySplit.Count && index > 0)
						{
							text = source.Substring(0, index);
							source = source.Remove(index);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					num2 = source.IndexOf(item.Name);
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref num2, source, item.Name.Replace("=", ""));
					}
					if (index > -1 && num2 > index)
					{
						text = source.Substring(index, num2 - index);
						source = source.Remove(index, num2 - index);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ParseFeed(text);
			}
			propertySplit.Clear();
			list.Remove("FEED=");
			propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, source.Replace(" ", ""));
			foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
			{
				SetValue(item2.Content);
			}
		}

		private void ParseFeed(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string strName = array[i];
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				FEED.Add(strName);
			}
		}

		private void FindCorrectIndex(ref int index, string strSource, string keyName)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int startIndex = 0;
			while (!flag)
			{
				index = strSource.IndexOf(keyName, startIndex);
				for (int i = index + keyName.Length; i < strSource.Length; i++)
				{
					switch (strSource[i])
					{
					case '=':
						flag = true;
						break;
					default:
						startIndex = i;
						break;
					case ' ':
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				index = -1;
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith("M="))
			{
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					NameHelper.NotesName(text.Trim(), bIsStream: true);
					if (M == null)
					{
						M = new List<string>();
					}
					M.Add(NameHelper.ConvertName(text.Trim(), bIsStream: true));
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
		}
	}

				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty.Trim()))
				{
					num = startPar + i;
					RetrieveParameter(empty, num);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RetrieveParameter(string parameter, int index)
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue("", Convert.ToDouble(parameter));
			switch ((RealParameterTable)index)
