		public IntegerParameter(string source, int startPar)
		{
			string[] array = source.Split(',');
			string empty = string.Empty;
			int num = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < 34; i++)
			{
				empty = array[i];
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty.Trim()))
				{
					num = startPar + i;
					RetrieveParameter(empty, num);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RetrieveParameter(string parameter, int index)
		{
			int num = Convert.ToInt32(parameter);
			switch ((IntegerParameterTable)index)
			{
			case IntegerParameterTable.IfDesign:
				if (num.Equals(1))
				{
					IFDESIGN = true;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.IfHotAir:
				if (num.Equals(1))
				{
					IFHOTAIR = true;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.IfCoCurrent:
				if (num.Equals(1))
				{
					IFCOCURRENT = true;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iBWG:
				BWG = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iTubeSchedule:
				TUBESCHEDULE = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nTubes:
				TUBENUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nTubePass:
				PASSNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nTubePassMin:
				MINPASS = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nTubePassMax:
				MAXPASS = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iPattern:
				TUBEPATTERN = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nTubeRow:
				TUBEROWNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nTubeRowMin:
				MINTUBEROW = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nTubeRowMax:
				MAXTUBEROW = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.TubeMaterial:
				TUBEMATERIAL = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nBunSeries:
				SBUNDLENUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nBunParallel:
				PBUNDLENUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.FinMaterial:
				FINMATERIAL = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nBayParallel:
				PBAYNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.MinBayParallel:
				MINPBAYNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.MaxBayParallel:
				MAXPBAYNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iDraft:
				DRAFTTYPE = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nFan:
				FANNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.NozType:
				if (num.Equals(0))
				{
					HASNOZZLE = false;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nNozIn:
				NOZZLEINNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.nNozOut:
				NOZZLEOUTNUMBER = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.SpecType:
				SPECTYPE = num;
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iPrStdTema:
				if (num.Equals(0))
				{
					NOSTDTEMAPR = true;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iPrExtTema:
				if (num.Equals(0))
				{
					NOEXTTEMAPR = true;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iPrZone:
				if (num.Equals(0))
				{
					NOZONEPR = true;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.iPrMonitor:
				if (num.Equals(0))
				{
					NOMONITORPR = true;
				}
				break;
			case IntegerParameterTable.Dummy1:
			case IntegerParameterTable.Dummy2:
			case IntegerParameterTable.Dummy3:
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	public Side TUBE { get; set; }

	public Side AIR { get; set; }

	public RealParameter REALPARA { get; set; }

	public IntegerParameter INTPARA { get; set; }

	public bool USEDPCORRELATION { get; set; }

	public bool ADDVAVLE { get; set; }

	public P2AirCooledHeatExchanger(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.AIRCOOLEDHX + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "AirCooledHeatExchanger", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDE) && text.Contains('('))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				string strA = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(ParseUtilty.GetHead(text, ' ').Trim()).Trim();
				if (string.Compare(strA, CUnitOperation.TUBES, ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(strA, "1", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					TUBE = new Side(empty);
				}
				if (string.Compare(strA, CUnitOperation.AIRSIDE, ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(strA, "2", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					AIR = new Side(empty);
				}
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.INTEGER))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' '), ' ').Trim();
				int result = -1;
				if (int.TryParse(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text).Trim(), out result))
				{
					INTPARA = new IntegerParameter(empty, result);
					continue;
				}
				if (INTPARA == null)
				{
					INTPARA = new IntegerParameter();
				}
				SetIntData(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text).Trim(), int.Parse(empty));
			}
			else
			{
				if (!text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PARAMETER))
				{
					continue;
				}
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' '), ' ').Trim();
				int result2 = -1;
				if (int.TryParse(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text).Trim(), out result2))
				{
					REALPARA = new RealParameter(empty, result2);
					continue;
				}
				if (REALPARA == null)
				{
					REALPARA = new RealParameter();
				}
				SetParData(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text).Trim(), double.Parse(empty));
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSAirCooler hYSYSAirCooler = new HYSYSAirCooler();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSAirCooler);
			hYSYSAirCooler.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSAirCooler.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSAirCooler.Name = base.Name;
			ConvertSides(hYSYSAirCooler, list);
			ConvertParameters(hYSYSAirCooler, list);
			ConvertHCurves(hYSYSAirCooler, list);
			SetInletAndOutletStreams(hYSYSAirCooler);
			hYSYSAirCooler.SetCreateStreams(list);
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "AirCooledHeatExchanger", base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void ConvertSides(HYSYSAirCooler aircooler, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objCollection)
	{
		bool flag = false;
		if (TUBE != null)
		{
			if (TUBE.FEED != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in TUBE.FEED)
				{
					aircooler.TubeSideInletStreams.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (TUBE.M != null && TUBE.M.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string item2 in TUBE.M)
				{
					aircooler.TubeSideOutletStreams.Add(item2);
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBE.METHOD))
			{
				flag = true;
				aircooler.FluidPackage = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(TUBE.METHOD);
			}
			if (aircooler.TubeSideInletStreams.Count > 1)
			{
				aircooler.InletStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string tubeSideInletStream in aircooler.TubeSideInletStreams)
				{
					aircooler.InletStreams.Add(tubeSideInletStream);
				}
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(aircooler);
				if (hYSYSMixer != null)
				{
					objCollection.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					if (flag)
					{
						hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = aircooler.FluidPackage;
					}
					hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(objCollection);
				}
				aircooler.TubeSideInletStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string inletStream in aircooler.InletStreams)
				{
					aircooler.TubeSideInletStreams.Add(inletStream);
				}
			}
			if (aircooler.TubeSideOutletStreams.Count > 1)
			{
				aircooler.OutletStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string tubeSideOutletStream in aircooler.TubeSideOutletStreams)
				{
					aircooler.OutletStreams.Add(tubeSideOutletStream);
				}
				HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductInSamePhase(aircooler);
				if (hYSYSSplitter != null)
				{
					objCollection.Add(hYSYSSplitter);
					if (flag)
					{
						hYSYSSplitter.FluidPackage = aircooler.FluidPackage;
					}
					hYSYSSplitter.SetCreateStreams(objCollection);
				}
				aircooler.TubeSideOutletStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string outletStream in aircooler.OutletStreams)
				{
					aircooler.TubeSideOutletStreams.Add(outletStream);
				}
			}
		}
		if (AIR == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (AIR.FEED != null)
		{
			foreach (string item3 in AIR.FEED)
			{
				aircooler.AirSideInletStreams.Add(item3);
				aircooler.TobeDeletedStreams.Add(item3);
			}
		}
		if (AIR.M == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (string item4 in AIR.M)
		{
			aircooler.TobeDeletedStreams.Add(item4);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertParameters(HYSYSAirCooler aircooler, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objCollection)
	{
		if (INTPARA != null)
		{
			aircooler.IsCoCurrent = INTPARA.IFCOCURRENT;
			if (INTPARA.TUBEMATERIAL == 1)
			{
				aircooler.TubeMaterial = "Carbon Steel";
			}
			else
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeMaterial"));
			}
			aircooler.IsInlineBundle = INTPARA.TUBEPATTERN == 0;
			aircooler.TubeNumber = INTPARA.TUBENUMBER;
			aircooler.TubePassNumber = INTPARA.PASSNUMBER;
			aircooler.TubeRowNumber = INTPARA.TUBEROWNUMBER;
			aircooler.BundleNumberPerBay = INTPARA.PBUNDLENUMBER;
			aircooler.BayNumberPerUnit = INTPARA.PBAYNUMBER;
			if (INTPARA.FINMATERIAL == 1)
			{
				aircooler.FinMaterial = "Carbon Steel";
			}
			else
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FinMaterial"));
			}
			aircooler.IsFanForced = INTPARA.DRAFTTYPE == 0;
			aircooler.FanNumber = INTPARA.FANNUMBER;
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "IfDesign"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "IfHotAir"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iBWG"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iTubeSchedule"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nTubePassMin"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nTubePassMax"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nTubeRowMin"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nTubeRowMax"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nBunSeries"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "MinBayParallel"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "MaxBayParallel"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NozzlePresent"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nNozIn"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nNozOut"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iPrStdTema"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iPrExtTema"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iPrZone"));
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iPrMonitor"));
		}
		if (REALPARA != null)
		{
			if (REALPARA.TubeLengeh != null)
			{
				aircooler.TubeLength = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, REALPARA.TubeLengeh);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TubeID != null)
			{
				aircooler.TubeID = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.TubeID);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TubeOD != null)
			{
				aircooler.TubeOD = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.TubeOD);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TubeThick != null)
			{
				aircooler.TubeThickness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.TubeThick);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TransPitch != null)
			{
				aircooler.TransPitch = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.TransPitch);
			}
			if (REALPARA.LongPitch != null)
			{
				aircooler.LongPitch = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.LongPitch);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TubeTCond != null)
			{
				aircooler.TubeTCond = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.ThermalConductivity, REALPARA.TubeTCond);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TubeFoul != null)
			{
				aircooler.TubeFoul = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FoulingCoefficient, REALPARA.TubeFoul);
			}
			if (REALPARA.FinThick != null)
			{
				aircooler.FinThickness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.FinThick);
			}
			if (REALPARA.FinTCond != null)
			{
				aircooler.FinTCond = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.ThermalConductivity, REALPARA.FinTCond);
			}
			if (REALPARA.AirFouling != null)
			{
				aircooler.AirFouling = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FoulingCoefficient, REALPARA.AirFouling);
			}
			if (REALPARA.FanDiameter != null)
			{
				aircooler.FanDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, REALPARA.FanDiameter);
			}
			aircooler.FanEfficiency = REALPARA.FanEff;
			if (REALPARA.NozInID != null)
			{
				aircooler.NozInID = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.NozInID);
			}
			if (REALPARA.NozOutID != null)
			{
				aircooler.NozOutID = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, REALPARA.NozOutID);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TTempSpec != null)
			{
				aircooler.TTempSpec = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, REALPARA.TTempSpec);
			}
			if (REALPARA.HOCISpec != null)
			{
				aircooler.HICOSpec = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, REALPARA.HOCISpec);
			}
			if (REALPARA.HIHOSpec != null)
			{
				aircooler.HIHOSpec = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, REALPARA.HIHOSpec);
			}
			if (REALPARA.TLFracSpec != null)
			{
				aircooler.TLFracSpec = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, REALPARA.TLFracSpec);
			}
			if (REALPARA.ATempSpec != null)
			{
				aircooler.ATempSpec = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, REALPARA.ATempSpec);

			if (REALPARA.COCISpec != null)

				aircooler.COCISpec = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, REALPARA.COCISpec);

			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (REALPARA.DPUnit != null)

			}
			if (REALPARA.AirLayerThick != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AFoulLayer"));
			}
			if (REALPARA.AirFilmCoef != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AFilmCoef"));
			}
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AFilmScale"));
			if (REALPARA.AirDPBundle != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AdPBun"));
			}
			if (REALPARA.MinAirDPBundle != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AdPBunMin"));
			}
			if (REALPARA.MaxAirDPBundle != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AdPBunMax"));
			}
			aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AdPBundleScale"));
			if (REALPARA.MinAirVelo != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AveloMin"));
			}
			if (REALPARA.MaxAirVelo != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AveloMax"));
			}
			if (REALPARA.FanPower != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FanPower"));
			}
			if (REALPARA.DutySpec != null)
			{
				aircooler.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "DutySpec"));
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHCurves(HYSYSAirCooler aircooler, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objCollection)
	{
		P2HCURVE p2HCURVE = HYSYSObjectHelper.RetrieveHCurveObject(aircooler.UID);
		if (p2HCURVE == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < p2HCURVE.UnitCurves.Count; i++)
		{
			if (string.Compare(p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i].UID, aircooler.UID, ignoreCase: true) != 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			P2HCURVE.UOCurve uOCurve = p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i];
			string connectSide = uOCurve.ConnectSide;
			if (string.Compare("air", connectSide, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = HYSYSObjectHelper.AddHeaterToUO(aircooler.UID, aircooler.TubeSideInletStreams[0], aircooler.TubeSideOutletStreams[0]);
			if (hYSYSHeatExchanger != null)
			{
				p2HCURVE.LinkedIDs.Remove(aircooler.UID);
				p2HCURVE.LinkedIDs.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID);
				uOCurve.UID = hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID;
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.HcurvePlotPoint = uOCurve.POINTS;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(aircooler.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.FluidPackage = aircooler.FluidPackage;
				}
				objCollection.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(objCollection);
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetInletAndOutletStreams(HYSYSAirCooler aircooler)
	{
		aircooler.InletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (string tubeSideInletStream in aircooler.TubeSideInletStreams)
		{
			aircooler.InletStreams.Add(tubeSideInletStream);
		}
		aircooler.OutletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (string tubeSideOutletStream in aircooler.TubeSideOutletStreams)
		{
			aircooler.OutletStreams.Add(tubeSideOutletStream);
		}
	}

	private void SetIntData(string sID, int value)
	{
		string text = sID.ToUpper();
		if (text == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		switch (text.Length)
		{
		case 8:
			switch (text[2])
			{
			case 'D':
				if (text == "IFDESIGN")
				{
					INTPARA.IFDESIGN = value == 1;
				}
				break;
			case 'H':
				if (text == "IFHOTAIR")
				{
					INTPARA.IFHOTAIR = value == 1;
				}
				break;
			case 'A':
				if (text == "IPATTERN")
				{
					INTPARA.TUBEPATTERN = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'U':
				if (text == "NTUBEROW")
				{
					INTPARA.TUBEROWNUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'E':
				if (text == "SPECTYPE")
				{
					INTPARA.SPECTYPE = value;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 11:
			switch (text[10])
			{
			case 'T':
				if (text == "IFCOCURRENT")
				{
					INTPARA.IFCOCURRENT = value == 1;
				}
				break;
			case 'N':
				if (text == "NTUBEROWMIN")
				{
					INTPARA.MINTUBEROW = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'X':
				if (text == "NTUBEROWMAX")
				{
					INTPARA.MAXTUBEROW = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'L':
				if (text == "FINMATERIAL")
				{
					INTPARA.FINMATERIAL = value;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 4:
			switch (text[0])
			{
			case 'I':
				if (text == "IBWG")
				{
					INTPARA.BWG = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'N':
				if (text == "NFAN")
				{
					INTPARA.FANNUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 6:
			switch (text[1])
			{
			case 'T':
				if (text == "NTUBES")
				{
					INTPARA.TUBENUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'D':
				if (text == "IDRAFT")
				{
					INTPARA.DRAFTTYPE = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'N':
				if (text == "NNOZIN")
				{
					INTPARA.NOZZLEINNUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 12:
			switch (text[3])
			{
			case 'B':
				if (!(text == "NTUBEPASSMIN"))
				{
					if (text == "NTUBEPASSMAX")
					{
						INTPARA.MAXPASS = value;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					INTPARA.MINPASS = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'E':
				if (text == "TUBEMATERIAL")
				{
					INTPARA.TUBEMATERIAL = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'N':
				if (text == "NBUNPARALLEL")
				{
					INTPARA.PBUNDLENUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'Y':
				if (text == "NBAYPARALLEL")
				{
					INTPARA.PBAYNUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 10:
			switch (text[3])
			{
			case 'N':
				if (text == "NBUNSERIES")
				{
					INTPARA.SBUNDLENUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'S':
				if (text == "IPRSTDTEMA")
				{
					INTPARA.NOSTDTEMAPR = value == 0;
				}
				break;
			case 'E':
				if (text == "IPREXTTEMA")
				{
					INTPARA.NOEXTTEMAPR = value == 0;
				}
				break;
			case 'M':
				if (text == "IPRMONITOR")
				{
					INTPARA.NOMONITORPR = value == 0;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 14:
			switch (text[1])
			{
			case 'I':
				if (text == "MINBAYPARALLEL")
				{
					INTPARA.MINPBAYNUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'A':
				if (text == "MAXBAYPARALLEL")
				{
					INTPARA.MAXPBAYNUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 7:
			switch (text[1])
			{
			case 'O':
				if (text == "NOZTYPE")
				{
					INTPARA.HASNOZZLE = value == 1;
				}
				break;
			case 'N':
				if (text == "NNOZOUT")
				{
					INTPARA.NOZZLEOUTNUMBER = value;
				}
				break;
			case 'P':
				if (text == "IPRZONE")
				{
					INTPARA.NOZONEPR = value == 0;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 13:
			if (text == "ITUBESCHEDULE")
			{
				INTPARA.TUBESCHEDULE = value;
			}
			break;
		case 9:
			if (text == "NTUBEPASS")
			{
				INTPARA.PASSNUMBER = value;
			}
			break;
		case 5:
			break;
		}
	}

			break;

			break;
		case "HIHOSPEC":
			SetRealParameterValue(REALPARA.HIHOSpec, value);
			break;
		case "HICOSPEC":
			SetRealParameterValue(REALPARA.HICOSpec, value);
			break;
		case "TLFRACSPEC":
			SetRealParameterValue(REALPARA.TLFracSpec, value);
			break;
		}
	}

	private void SetRealParameterValue(DoubleValue dVal, double value)
	{
		if (dVal == null)
		{
			dVal = new DoubleValue("", value);
		}
		else
		{
			dVal.Value = value;
		}
	}
}
public class P2PlugFlowReactor : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Operation : PropertyItem
	{
		private string _PHASE = "V";

		private bool _ISOTHERMAL = true;

		private int _TUBES = 1;

		private int _POINTS = 10;

		private DoubleValue _DPIN = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _DUTY = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public DoubleValue Length { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DIAMETER { get; set; }

		public string PHASE
		{
			get
			{
				return _PHASE;
			}
			set
			{
				_PHASE = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PIN { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public bool ISOTHERMAL
		{
			get
			{
				return _ISOTHERMAL;
			}
			set
			{
				_ISOTHERMAL = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public bool ADIABATIC { get; set; }

		public bool COCURRENT { get; set; }

		public bool COUNTERCURRENT { get; set; }

		public int TUBES
		{
			get
			{
				return _TUBES;
			}
			set
			{
				_TUBES = value;
			}
		}

		public int POINTS
		{
			get
			{
				return _POINTS;
			}
			set
			{
				_POINTS = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DPIN
		{
			get
			{
				return _DPIN;
			}
			set
			{
				_DPIN = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DUTY
		{
			get
			{
				return _DUTY;
			}
			set
			{
				_DUTY = value;
			}
		}

		public Operation()
		{
		}

		public Operation(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.LENGTH,
				CUnitOperation.DIAMETER,
				CUnitOperation.TUBES,
				CUnitOperation.PHASE,
				CUnitOperation.DPIN,
				CUnitOperation.PIN,
				CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.THERMAL,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC,
				CUnitOperation.DUTY,
				CUnitOperation.COCURRENT,
				CUnitOperation.COUNTERCURRENT,
				CUnitOperation.POINTS
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LENGTH))
					{
						Length = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DIAMETER))
					{
						DIAMETER = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TUBES))
					{
						TUBES = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim());
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHASE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							PHASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim();
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPIN))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							DPIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPIN))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							POINTS = (int)ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PIN))
					{
						PIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.THERMAL))
					{
						ISOTHERMAL = true;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC))
					{
						ISOTHERMAL = false;
						ADIABATIC = true;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COCURRENT))
					{
						COCURRENT = true;
						ISOTHERMAL = false;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COUNTERCURRENT))
					{
						COUNTERCURRENT = true;
						ISOTHERMAL = false;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Rxcalc
	{
		private RXBasis _ActivityBasis;

		private IntegrationMethodEnum _IntegrationMethod;

		private int _NSTEPS = 20;

		private DoubleValue _TOLERANCE = new DoubleValue("PCT", 0.1);

		private QualifierValue _KINETICS = new QualifierValue("", "POWERLAW");

		public RXBasis ActivityBasis
		{
			get
			{
				return _ActivityBasis;
			}
			set
			{
				_ActivityBasis = value;
			}
		}

		public IntegrationMethodEnum IntegrationMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return _IntegrationMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				_IntegrationMethod = value;
			}
		}

		public int NSTEPS
		{
			get
			{
				return _NSTEPS;
			}
			set
			{
				_NSTEPS = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue STEPSIZE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TOLERANCE
		{
			get
			{
				return _TOLERANCE;
			}
			set
			{
				_TOLERANCE = value;
			}
		}

		public QualifierValue KINETICS
		{
			get
			{
				return _KINETICS;
			}
			set
			{
				_KINETICS = value;
			}
		}

		public Rxcalc()
		{
		}

		public Rxcalc(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.CONCENTRATION,
				CUnitOperation.PARTIALPRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.ACTIVITY,
				CUnitOperation.RUNGEKUTTA,
				CUnitOperation.GEAR,
				CUnitOperation.LSODA,
				CUnitOperation.NSTEPS,
				CUnitOperation.STEPSIZE,
				CUnitOperation.TOLERANCE,
				CUnitOperation.KINETICS
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONCENTRATION))
					{
						ActivityBasis = RXBasis.CONCENTRATION;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PARTIALPRESSURE))
					{
						ActivityBasis = RXBasis.PARTIALPRESSURE;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ACTIVITY))
					{
						ActivityBasis = RXBasis.ACTIVITY;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RUNGEKUTTA))
					{
						IntegrationMethod = IntegrationMethodEnum.RUNGEKUTTA;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.GEAR))
					{
						IntegrationMethod = IntegrationMethodEnum.GEAR;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LSODA))
					{
						IntegrationMethod = IntegrationMethodEnum.LSODA;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NSTEPS))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							NSTEPS = int.Parse(array[i].Trim());
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STEPSIZE))
					{
						STEPSIZE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TOLERANCE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							TOLERANCE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KINETICS))
					{
						KINETICS = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Catalyst
	{
		private double _SPHE = 1.0;

		public DoubleValue PDIAM { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PORO { get; set; }

		public double SPHE
		{
			get
			{
				return _SPHE;
			}
			set
			{
				_SPHE = value;
			}
		}

		public string PSHAPE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PLENGTH { get; set; }

		public Catalyst(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.PDIAM,
				CUnitOperation.PORO,
				CUnitOperation.SPHE,
				CUnitOperation.PSHAPE,
				CUnitOperation.PLENGTH
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PDIAM))
					{
						PDIAM = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PORO))
					{
						PORO = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPHE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							SPHE = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]));
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PSHAPE))
					{
						PSHAPE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLENGTH))
					{
						PLENGTH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Openpipe
	{
		private string _DPCORR = "BBM";

		public string DPCORR
		{
			get
			{
				return _DPCORR;
			}
			set
			{
				_DPCORR = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DPTOL { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue FLOWEFF { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue FRICTION { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue AROUGH { get; set; }

		public string RROUGH { get; set; }

		public string NOACCEL { get; set; }

		public string SourceString { get; set; }

		public Openpipe(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			SourceString = obj.Name;
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.DPCORR,
				CUnitOperation.DPTOL,
				CUnitOperation.FLOWEFF,
				CUnitOperation.FRICTION,
				CUnitOperation.AROUGH,
				CUnitOperation.RROUGH,
				CUnitOperation.NOACCEL
			}, text.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPCORR))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							DPCORR = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPTOL))
					{
						DPTOL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLOWEFF))
					{
						FLOWEFF = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRICTION))
					{
						FRICTION = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.AROUGH))
					{
						AROUGH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RROUGH))
					{
						RROUGH = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NOACCEL))
					{
						NOACCEL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Hot
	{
		private DoubleValue _DPIN = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		public string FEED_ProductStrs { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PIN { get; set; }

		public string SourceString { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DPIN
		{
			get
			{
				return _DPIN;
			}
			set
			{
				_DPIN = value;
			}
		}

		public Hot(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			SourceString = obj.Name;
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim());
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PIN))
					{
						PIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPIN))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							DPIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].Length > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(FEED_ProductStrs))
					{
						FEED_ProductStrs = FEED_ProductStrs + ", " + array[i];
					}
					else if (array[i].Length > 0)
					{
						FEED_ProductStrs = array[i];
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Cold
	{
		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		public DoubleValue POUT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TOUT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue U { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TIN { get; set; }

		public string HTCO { get; set; }

		public string TSTR { get; set; }

		public string SourceString { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public Cold(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
			{
				return;
			}
			SourceString = obj.Name;
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim());
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POUTLET))
					{
						POUT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TOUT))
					{
						TOUT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.U))
					{
						U = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TIN))
					{
						TIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTCO))
					{
						HTCO = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TSTR))
					{
						TSTR = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Reaction
	{
		private DoubleValue _PEXP = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 1.0);

		private DoubleValue _ACTIVATION = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _TEXPONENT = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		public string RXID { get; set; }

		public int COMPONENT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PEXP
		{
			get
			{
				return _PEXP;
			}
			set
			{
				_PEXP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue ACTIVATION
		{
			get
			{
				return _ACTIVATION;
			}
			set
			{
				_ACTIVATION = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEXPONENT
		{
			get
			{
				return _TEXPONENT;
			}
			set
			{
				_TEXPONENT = value;
			}
		}

		public Dictionary<int, double> KORDER { get; set; }

		public void SetKinetic(string sSource)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sSource))
			{
				return;
			}
			sSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.COMPONENT,
				CUnitOperation.PEXP,
				CUnitOperation.ACTIVATION,
				CUnitOperation.TEXPONENT
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COMPONENT))
					{
						COMPONENT = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]).Trim());
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PEXP))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							PEXP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ACTIVATION))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							ACTIVATION = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEXPONENT) && array[i].Contains('='))
					{
						TEXPONENT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetKorder(string sSource)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sSource))
			{
				return;
			}
			sSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(sSource);
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array[i]);
					if (array2 != null && array2.Length == 2)
					{
						if (KORDER == null)
						{
							KORDER = new Dictionary<int, double>();
						}
						KORDER.Add(int.Parse(array2[0]), double.Parse(array2[1]));
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_isParsError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Profile
	{
		public string[] Units { get; set; }

		public string Content { get; set; }

		public Profile(string[] strs, string content)
		{
			Units = strs;
			Content = content;
		}
	}

	public enum IntegrationMethodEnum
	{
		RUNGEKUTTA,
		GEAR,
		LSODA
	}

	private static bool _isParsError;

	private string _PACKING_DPCORR = "ERGUN";

	private HYSYSPlugFlowReactor _HysysPlugFlowReactor;

	private List<HYSYSBaseObject> _ObjectCollection;

	public string RXSTOIC_RXSET { get; set; }

	public Profile TPROFILE { get; set; }

	public Profile PPROFILE { get; set; }

	public Profile PROFILE { get; set; }

	public string PRINT_PATH { get; set; }

	public Operation OPERATION { get; set; }

	public Rxcalc RXCALC { get; set; }

	public Catalyst CATALYST { get; set; }

	public Openpipe OPENPIPE { get; set; }

	public List<Reaction> Reactions { get; set; }

	public string PACKING_DPCORR
	{
		get
		{
			return _PACKING_DPCORR;
		}
		set
		{
			_PACKING_DPCORR = value;
		}
	}

	public Hot HOT { get; set; }

	public Cold COLD { get; set; }

	public P2PlugFlowReactor(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			_isParsError = false;
			RXCALC = new Rxcalc();
			OPERATION = new Operation();
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.EQUILIBRIUMREACTOR + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "PLUGFLOW", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bParsingError || _isParsError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "PLUGFLOW", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		if (obj == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.Name))
		{
			return;
		}
		base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		SetHeadData(text.Trim().ToUpper());
		if (obj.Children == null || obj.Children.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < obj.Children.Count; i++)
		{
			try
			{
				P2BaseObject p2BaseObject = obj.Children[i];
				base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + p2BaseObject.ToString() + "\n";
				string text2 = p2BaseObject.Name.ToUpper();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
				{
					base.FEED = new List<string>();
					text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' ');
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(text2);
					for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
					{
						string strName = array[j].Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						base.FEED.Add(strName);
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
				{
					text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' ').Trim();
					base.PRODUCT = new Product(text2);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC))
				{
					RXSTOIC_RXSET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REACTION))
				{
					if (Reactions == null)
					{
						Reactions = new List<Reaction>();
					}
					Reaction reaction = new Reaction();
					reaction.RXID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
					if (p2BaseObject.Children != null && p2BaseObject.Children.Count > 0)
					{
						foreach (P2BaseObject child in p2BaseObject.Children)
						{
							string text3 = child.Name.ToUpper();
							if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KINETICS))
							{
								reaction.SetKinetic(text3.Trim());
							}
							else if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KORDER))
							{
								reaction.SetKorder(text3.Trim());
							}
						}
					}
					Reactions.Add(reaction);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
				{
					OPERATION = new Operation(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXCALC))
				{
					RXCALC = new Rxcalc(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TPROFILE))
				{
					TPROFILE = new Profile(ParseUtilty.GetStringsInBracket(text2), ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PPROFILE))
				{
					PPROFILE = new Profile(ParseUtilty.GetStringsInBracket(text2), ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PROFILE))
				{
					PROFILE = new Profile(ParseUtilty.GetStringsInBracket(text2), ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRINT))
				{
					PRINT_PATH = "PATH";
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPENPIPE))
				{
					OPENPIPE = new Openpipe(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PACKING))
				{
					if (text2.Contains('='))
					{
						PACKING_DPCORR = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CATALYST))
				{
					CATALYST = new Catalyst(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HOT))
				{
					HOT = new Hot(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COLD))
				{
					COLD = new Cold(p2BaseObject);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
				{
					text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' ');
					if (!text2.StartsWith("PDATA"))
					{
						DEFINE item = new DEFINE(text2, "PLUG", base.UID);
						base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
				{
					base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text2, ' '));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor = new HYSYSPlugFlowReactor();
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.UID = base.UID;
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.Name = base.Name;
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.EnergyName = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			_ObjectCollection = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			_ObjectCollection.Add(_HysysPlugFlowReactor);
			ConvertStreams();
			ConvertSetAndReactions();
			ConvertOperation();
			ConvertRXCALC();
			ConvertProfile();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PRINT_PATH))
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PRINT PATH"));
			}
			ConvertOpenPipe();
			if (PACKING_DPCORR.StartsWith("ERGU"))
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.IsEngun = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PACKING DPCORR");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			}
			ConvertCatalyst();
			if (HOT != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(HOT.SourceString))
			{
				string item2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "HOT");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
			}
			if (COLD != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(COLD.SourceString))
			{
				string item3 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "COLD");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item3);
			}
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.SeparateDefines();
			}
			array = _ObjectCollection.ToArray();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.Convert, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "PLUGFLOW", base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void ConvertStreams()
	{
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		if (base.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
		{
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOAdded"), "mixer", "multiple feeds"));
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(_HysysPlugFlowReactor);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
				}
				_ObjectCollection.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(_ObjectCollection);
			}
		}
		if (base.PRODUCT == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
		{
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
			dictionary.Add(addedProperty, base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
		}
		if (_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Count == 1)
		{
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.Product = _HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams[0];
		}
		else if (_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Count == 2 && dictionary.ContainsKey("S"))
		{
			string item = P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_ProductSolidStream") + " " + dictionary["S"] + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			if (_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Contains(dictionary["S"]))
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Remove(dictionary["S"]);
			}
			dictionary.Remove("S");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.Product = _HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams[0];
		}
		else
		{
			if (_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Count <= 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (dictionary.ContainsKey("S"))
			{
				string item2 = P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_ProductSolidStream") + " " + dictionary["S"] + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
				if (_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Contains(dictionary["S"]))
				{
					_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Remove(dictionary["S"]);
				}
				dictionary.Remove("S");
			}
			List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, _HysysPlugFlowReactor);
			if (list != null)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOAdded"), "Separator", P2Globalization.GetString("MultipleProducts")));
			}
			if (_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams != null && _HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams.Count > 0)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.Product = _HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreams[0];
			}
			if (list == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item3 in list)
			{
				_ObjectCollection.Add(item3);
				item3.SetCreateStreams(_ObjectCollection);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConvertSetAndReactions()
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(RXSTOIC_RXSET))
		{
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ReactionSet = new HYSYSReactionSet(base.UID + "_PFR_" + RXSTOIC_RXSET);
		}
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey("ReactionData") || (Reactions == null && Reactions.Count == 0))
		{
			return;
		}
		P2ReactionData p2ReactionData = (P2ReactionData)P2BaseObject.g_Map["ReactionData"];
		RXPhase rXPhase = RXPhase.V;
		bool flag = false;
		int num = 0;
		foreach (Reaction reaction in Reactions)
		{
			P2ReactionData.P2Reaction p2Reaction = p2ReactionData.P2RXSetsDic[RXSTOIC_RXSET].P2RXsDic[reaction.RXID];
			if (p2Reaction != null)
			{
				if (num == 0)
				{
					rXPhase = p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT;
				}
				if (rXPhase != p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			if (rXPhase == RXPhase.V)
			{
				OPERATION.PHASE = RXPhase.V.ToString();
			}
			else
			{
				OPERATION.PHASE = RXPhase.L.ToString();
			}
		}
		foreach (Reaction reaction2 in Reactions)
		{
			P2ReactionData.P2Reaction p2Reaction2 = p2ReactionData.P2RXSetsDic[RXSTOIC_RXSET].P2RXsDic[reaction2.RXID];
			if (p2Reaction2 == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			string iD = base.UID + "_" + RXSTOIC_RXSET + "_" + reaction2.RXID;
			HYSYSKineticReaction hYSYSKineticReaction = p2ReactionData.CreateHYSYSKineticRX(reaction2.RXID, p2Reaction2);
			hYSYSKineticReaction.ID = iD;
			if (hYSYSKineticReaction == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			if (reaction2.COMPONENT != 0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.BaseComponent = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(reaction2.COMPONENT);
			}
			if (reaction2.PEXP.Value != 1.0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.FrequencyFactor = reaction2.PEXP.Value;
			}
			if (reaction2.ACTIVATION.Value != 0.0 || hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy == null || hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy.Value == 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.MolarEnthalpy, reaction2.ACTIVATION);
			}
			if (reaction2.TEXPONENT.Value != 0.0 || hYSYSKineticReaction.ExtReacRateConst == 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.ExtReacRateConst = reaction2.TEXPONENT.Value;
			}
			if (reaction2.KORDER != null && reaction2.KORDER.Count > 0)
			{
				if (hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder == null)
				{
					hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder = new Dictionary<string, double>();
				}
				else
				{
					hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder.Clear();
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in reaction2.KORDER)
				{
					if (hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder == null)
					{
						hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder = new Dictionary<string, double>();
					}
					hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder.Add(P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(item.Key), item.Value);
				}
			}
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase = OPERATION.PHASE;
			}
			if (RXCALC != null)
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis = RXCALC.ActivityBasis;
			}
			if (hYSYSKineticReaction != null)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSKineticReaction);
			}
		}
		_ObjectCollection.Add(_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ReactionSet);
	}

	private void ConvertOperation()
	{
		if (OPERATION == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.TubleLength = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, OPERATION.Length);
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.TubleDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, OPERATION.DIAMETER);
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.NumberOfTubes = OPERATION.TUBES;
		if (OPERATION.PIN != null)
		{
			CreateHYSYSValve().ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PIN);
		}
		else if (OPERATION.DPIN != null && OPERATION.DPIN.Value != 0.0)
		{
			CreateHYSYSValve().ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.DPIN);
		}
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.DeltaPresure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.DP);
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ProductPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRESSURE);
		string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "POINTS");
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
		if (OPERATION.ISOTHERMAL)
		{
			if (OPERATION.TEMPERATURE != null)
			{
				CreateHYSYSHeater();
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.OutletStreamTemperature = OPERATION.TEMPERATURE;
			}
			else
			{
				item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "THERMAL");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			}
		}
		else if (OPERATION.ADIABATIC)
		{
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, OPERATION.DUTY);
		}
		else if (OPERATION.COCURRENT)
		{
			item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "COCURRENT");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
		}
		else if (OPERATION.COUNTERCURRENT)
		{
			item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "COUNTERCURRENT");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertRXCALC()
	{
		if (RXCALC == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (RXCALC.IntegrationMethod == IntegrationMethodEnum.RUNGEKUTTA)
		{
			string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "RUNGEKUTTA");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NSTEPS");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			if (RXCALC.STEPSIZE != null)
			{
				item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "STEPSIZE");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			}
		}
		else if (RXCALC.IntegrationMethod == IntegrationMethodEnum.GEAR)
		{
			string item2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "GEAR");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
			item2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "GEAR TORLERANCE(PCT)");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
		}
		else if (RXCALC.IntegrationMethod == IntegrationMethodEnum.LSODA)
		{
			string item3 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "LSODA");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item3);
			item3 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "LSODA TORLERANCE(PCT)");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item3);
		}
		if (RXCALC.KINETICS.Value != "POWERLAW")
		{
			string item4 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "KINETICS");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item4);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertProfile()
	{
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		if (TPROFILE != null)
		{
			string[] array = new string[4];
			if (TPROFILE.Units != null && TPROFILE.Units.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < TPROFILE.Units.Length; i++)
				{
					array[i] = TPROFILE.Units[i];
				}
			}
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, array[0]));
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Length, array[1]));
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, array[2]));
			if (list != null && list.Count == 3)
			{
				string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TPROFILE");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			}
		}
		else if (PPROFILE != null)
		{
			string[] array2 = new string[4];
			if (PPROFILE.Units != null && PPROFILE.Units.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < PPROFILE.Units.Length; j++)
				{
					array2[j] = PPROFILE.Units[j];
				}
			}
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, array2[0]));
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Length, array2[1]));
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, array2[2]));
			if (list != null && list.Count == 3)
			{
				string item2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PPROFILE");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (PROFILE == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string[] array3 = new string[4];
			if (PROFILE.Units != null && PROFILE.Units.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int k = 0; k < PROFILE.Units.Length; k++)
				{
					array3[k] = PROFILE.Units[k];
				}
			}
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, array3[0]));
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Length, array3[1]));
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, array3[2]));
			list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, array3[2]));
			if (list != null && list.Count == 3)
			{
				string item3 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PROFILE");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item3);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConvertOpenPipe()
	{
		if (OPENPIPE != null)
		{
			string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PPROFILE");
			_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
		}
	}

	private HYSYSValve CreateHYSYSValve()
	{
		if (_HysysPlugFlowReactor == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = new HYSYSValve();
		hYSYSValve.InletStreams = _HysysPlugFlowReactor.InletStreams;
		string item = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
		hYSYSValve.OutletStreams.Add(item);
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.InletStreams.Clear();
		_HysysPlugFlowReactor.InletStreams.Add(item);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
		{
			hYSYSValve.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
		}
		return hYSYSValve;
	}

	private void CreateHYSYSHeater()
	{
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger();
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
		HYSYSObjectHelper.InsertObjectInStream(_HysysPlugFlowReactor, _HysysPlugFlowReactor.InletStreams[0], bInletStream: true, hYSYSHeatExchanger);
		if (hYSYSHeatExchanger != null)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater;
			if (OPERATION.TEMPERATURE != null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, OPERATION.TEMPERATURE);
			}
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.LoadStreams();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			_ObjectCollection.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(_ObjectCollection);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertCatalyst()
	{
		if (CATALYST != null)
		{
			if (CATALYST.PDIAM != null)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ParticleDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, CATALYST.PDIAM);
			}
			if (CATALYST.SPHE != 0.0)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ParticleSphericity = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, CATALYST.SPHE);
			}
			else if (CATALYST.PSHAPE.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPHERICAL))
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ParticleSphericity = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 1.0);
			}
			if (CATALYST.PLENGTH != null && CATALYST.PSHAPE.StartsWith("CYLI"))
			{
				DoubleValue p2Value = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, CATALYST.PLENGTH.Value, CATALYST.PLENGTH.Unit, "");
				DoubleValue p2Value2 = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, CATALYST.PDIAM.Value, CATALYST.PDIAM.Unit, "");
				double x = 1.5 * Math.Pow(p2Value2.Value, 2.0) * p2Value.Value;
				double num = 0.5 * Math.Pow(p2Value2.Value, 2.0) + p2Value2.Value * p2Value.Value;
				double val = Math.Pow(x, 2.0 / 3.0) / num;
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ParticleSphericity = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, val);
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ParticleDiameter = new DoubleValue(p2Value2.Unit, Math.Pow(x, 1.0 / 3.0));
			}
			else
			{
				string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "CATALYST PSHAPE");
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
			}
			if (CATALYST.PORO != null)
			{
				_HysysPlugFlowReactor.PackedBedVoidFraction = CATALYST.PORO;
			}
		}
	}
}
public class P2ReactionData : P2BaseObject
{
	public class P2ReactionSet
	{
		public string ID { get; set; }

		public string NAME { get; set; }

		public KineticsType KineticsType { get; set; }

		public List<P2Reaction> P2Reactions { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, P2Reaction> P2RXsDic { get; set; }

		public P2ReactionSet()
		{
			P2Reactions = new List<P2Reaction>();
		}
	}

	public class P2Reaction
	{
		public string ID { get; set; }

		public string NAME { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, double> STOICHIOMETRY { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<string, double> STOI_HYSYSName { get; set; }

		public HORX HORX { get; set; }

		public KINETIC KINETIC { get; set; }

		public KPHASE KPHASE { get; set; }

		public KBASIS KBASIS { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, double> KORDER { get; set; }

		public EPHASE EPHASE { get; set; }

		public EBASIS EBASIS { get; set; }

		public EQUILIBRIUM EQUILIBRIUM { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, double> EEXPONENT { get; set; }

		public bool IsKPhaseDefaultSet { get; set; }

		public bool IsEPhaseDefaultSet { get; set; }

		public P2Reaction()
		{
			STOICHIOMETRY = new Dictionary<int, double>();
			STOI_HYSYSName = new Dictionary<string, double>();
			KORDER = new Dictionary<int, double>();
			EEXPONENT = new Dictionary<int, double>();
			KPHASE = new KPHASE();
			EPHASE = new EPHASE();
		}
	}

	public class HORX
	{
		private DoubleValue _REFTEMP = new DoubleValue("C", 25.0);

		public DoubleValue HEAT { get; set; }

		public int REFCOMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue REFTEMP
		{
			get
			{
				return _REFTEMP;
			}
			set
			{
				_REFTEMP = value;
			}
		}

		public string REFPHASE { get; set; }

		public HORX()
		{
			HEAT = new DoubleValue();
			REFTEMP = new DoubleValue();
		}
	}

	public class KINETIC
	{
		private double _PEXP = 1.0;

		private DoubleValue _ACTIVATION = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		private double _TEXPONENT;

		public double PEXP
		{
			get
			{
				return _PEXP;
			}
			set
			{
				_PEXP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue ACTIVATION
		{
			get
			{
				return _ACTIVATION;
			}
			set
			{
				_ACTIVATION = value;
			}
		}

		public double TEXPONENT
		{
			get
			{
				return _TEXPONENT;
			}
			set
			{
				_TEXPONENT = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public class KPHASE
	{
		public RXPhase DEFAULT { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, RXPhase> COMPONENT { get; set; }

		public KPHASE()
		{
			COMPONENT = new Dictionary<int, RXPhase>();
			DEFAULT = RXPhase.L;
		}
	}

	public class KBASIS
	{
		private RXBasis _VAPOR;

		private RXBasis _LIQUID;

		public RXBasis VAPOR
		{
			get
			{
				return _VAPOR;
			}
			set
			{
				_VAPOR = value;
			}
		}

		public RXBasis LIQUID
		{
			get
			{
				return _LIQUID;
			}
			set
			{
				_LIQUID = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public class EQUILIBRIUM
	{
		public double A { get; set; }

		public double B { get; set; }

		public double C { get; set; }

		public double D { get; set; }

		public double E { get; set; }

		public double F { get; set; }

		public double G { get; set; }

		public double H { get; set; }
	}

	public class EPHASE
	{
		public RXPhase DEFAULT { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, RXPhase> COMPONENT { get; set; }

		public EPHASE()
		{
			COMPONENT = new Dictionary<int, RXPhase>();
			DEFAULT = RXPhase.L;
		}
	}

	public class EBASIS
	{
		private RXBasis _VAPOR;

		private RXBasis _LIQUID;

		public RXBasis VAPOR
		{
			get
			{
				return _VAPOR;
			}
			set
			{
				_VAPOR = value;
			}
		}

		public RXBasis LIQUID
		{
			get
			{
				return _LIQUID;
			}
			set
			{
				_LIQUID = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public List<P2ReactionSet> P2ReactionSets;

	public const string Key = "ReactionData";

	public IDictionary<string, P2ReactionSet> P2RXSetsDic;

	public P2ReactionData(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			P2ReactionSets = new List<P2ReactionSet>();
			SetValue(obj);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseReactionData")));
		}
		P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add("ReactionData", this);
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		if (obj.Children != null)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				P2ReactionSet p2ReactionSet = new P2ReactionSet();
				p2ReactionSet.ID = SetHeadData(empty)[0];
				p2ReactionSet.NAME = SetHeadData(empty)[1];
				P2ReactionSets.Add(p2ReactionSet);
				p2ReactionSet.P2RXsDic = new Dictionary<string, P2Reaction>();
				if (child.Children == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (P2BaseObject child2 in child.Children)
				{
					try
					{
						empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' ');
						P2Reaction p2Reaction = new P2Reaction();
						p2Reaction.ID = SetHeadData(empty)[0];
						p2Reaction.NAME = SetHeadData(empty)[1];
						p2ReactionSet.P2Reactions.Add(p2Reaction);
						if (child2.Children != null)
						{
							string empty2 = string.Empty;
							foreach (P2BaseObject child3 in child2.Children)
							{
								if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.STOICHIOMETRY))
								{
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(empty2);
									for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
									{
										int num = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array[i])[0]);
										double value = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array[i])[1]);
										string hYSYSCompName = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(num);
										p2Reaction.STOICHIOMETRY.Add(num, value);
										if (hYSYSCompName == null || hYSYSCompName == string.Empty)
										{
											string strMessage = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ReactantNotFound"), p2ReactionSet.ID, p2Reaction.ID, num);
											if (string.Compare(ApplicationName.appName.Trim(), "HYSYS", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
											{
												DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Warning, strMessage);
											}
											continue;
										}
										if (!p2Reaction.STOI_HYSYSName.Keys.Contains(hYSYSCompName))
										{
											p2Reaction.STOI_HYSYSName.Add(hYSYSCompName, value);
											continue;
										}
										p2Reaction.STOI_HYSYSName.Add(hYSYSCompName + "1", value);
										string strMessage2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ReactantDuplicate"), p2ReactionSet.ID, p2Reaction.ID, hYSYSCompName);
										if (string.Compare(ApplicationName.appName.Trim(), "HYSYS", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
										{
											DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Warning, strMessage2);
										}
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.HORX))
								{
									p2Reaction.HORX = new HORX();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
									{
										CReactionData.HEAT,
										CReactionData.REFCOMP,
										CReactionData.REFTEMP,
										CReactionData.REFPHASE
									}, empty2.ToUpper());
									List<string> list = new List<string>();
									foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
									{
										list.Add(item.Content);
									}
									string[] array2 = list.ToArray();
									for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
									{
										if (array2[j].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.HEAT))
										{
											p2Reaction.HORX.HEAT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array2[j]);
										}
										else if (array2[j].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.REFCOMP))
										{
											p2Reaction.HORX.REFCOMP = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array2[j]));
										}
										else if (array2[j].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.REFTEMP))
										{
											p2Reaction.HORX.REFTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array2[j]);
										}
										else if (array2[j].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.REFPHASE))
										{
											p2Reaction.HORX.REFPHASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array2[j]);
										}
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.KINETIC))
								{
									p2Reaction.KINETIC = new KINETIC();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									List<PropertyPair> propertySplit2 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
									{
										CReactionData.PEXP,
										CReactionData.ACTIVATION,
										CReactionData.TEXPONENT
									}, empty2.ToUpper());
									List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
									foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit2)
									{
										list2.Add(item2.Content);
									}
									string[] array3 = list2.ToArray();
									for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
									{
										if (array3[k].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.PEXP))
										{
											p2Reaction.KINETIC.PEXP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]).Value;
										}
										else if (array3[k].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.ACTIVATION))
										{
											p2Reaction.KINETIC.ACTIVATION = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
										}
										else if (array3[k].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.TEXPONENT))
										{
											p2Reaction.KINETIC.TEXPONENT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]).Value;
										}
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.KPHASE))
								{
									p2Reaction.IsKPhaseDefaultSet = true;
									p2Reaction.KPHASE = new KPHASE();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									string[] array4 = new string[2];
									if (empty2.ToUpper().Contains(CReactionData.COMPONENT))
									{
										array4 = empty2.Split(new char[1] { ',' }, 2);
									}
									else
									{
										array4[0] = empty2;
									}
									if (array4[0] != null)
									{
										if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[0]).ToUpper() == "V")
										{
											p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.V;
										}
										else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[0]).ToUpper() == "L")
										{
											p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.L;
										}
										else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[0]).ToUpper() == "L1")
										{
											p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.L1;
										}
										else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[0]).ToUpper() == "L2")
										{
											p2Reaction.KPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.L2;
										}
									}
									if (array4[1] == null)
									{
										continue;
									}
									string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[1].Trim()));
									for (int l = 0; l < array5.Length; l++)
									{
										int key = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array5[l])[0]);
										RXPhase value2 = RXPhase.L;
										switch (ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array5[l])[1].ToUpper())
										{
										case "V":
											value2 = RXPhase.V;
											break;
										case "L":
											value2 = RXPhase.L;
											break;
										case "L1":
											value2 = RXPhase.L1;
											break;
										case "L2":
											value2 = RXPhase.L2;
											break;
										}
										p2Reaction.KPHASE.COMPONENT.Add(key, value2);
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.KBASIS))
								{
									p2Reaction.KBASIS = new KBASIS();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									List<PropertyPair> propertySplit3 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
									{
										CReactionData.VAPOR,
										CReactionData.LIQUID
									}, empty2.ToUpper());
									List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
									foreach (PropertyPair item3 in propertySplit3)
									{
										list3.Add(item3.Content);
									}
									string[] array6 = list3.ToArray();
									for (int m = 0; m < array6.Length; m++)
									{
										if (array6[m].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.VAPOR))
										{
											switch (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array6[m]).Trim())
											{
											case "CONCENTRATION":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.CONCENTRATION;
												break;
											case "PARTIALPRESSURE":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.PARTIALPRESSURE;
												break;
											case "FUGACITY":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.FUGACITY;
												break;
											case "MOLEFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.MOLEFRACTION;
												break;
											case "MASSFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.MASSFRACTION;
												break;
											}
										}
										else if (array6[m].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.LIQUID))
										{
											switch (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array6[m]).Trim())
											{
											case "CONCENTRATION":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.CONCENTRATION;
												break;
											case "PARTIALPRESSURE":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.FUGACITY;
												break;
											case "FUGACITY":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.ACTIVITY;
												break;
											case "MOLEFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.MOLEFRACTION;
												break;
											case "MASSFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.KBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.MASSFRACTION;
												break;
											}
										}
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.KORDER))
								{
									p2Reaction.KORDER = new Dictionary<int, double>();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									string[] array7 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(empty2);
									for (int n = 0; n < array7.Length; n++)
									{
										int key2 = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array7[n])[0].Trim());
										double value3 = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array7[n])[1].Trim());
										p2Reaction.KORDER.Add(key2, value3);
									}
								}
