								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.EQUILIBRIUM))
								{
									p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM = new EQUILIBRIUM();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									string[] array8 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(empty2);
									for (int num2 = 0; num2 < array8.Length; num2++)
									{
										string text = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array8[num2])[0];
										double num3 = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array8[num2])[1]);
										switch (text)
										{
										case "A":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.A = num3;
											break;
										case "B":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.B = num3;
											break;
										case "C":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.C = num3;
											break;
										case "D":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.D = num3;
											break;
										case "E":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.E = num3;
											break;
										case "F":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.F = num3;
											break;
										case "G":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.G = num3;
											break;
										case "H":
											p2Reaction.EQUILIBRIUM.H = num3;
											break;
										}
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.EPHASE))
								{
									p2Reaction.IsEPhaseDefaultSet = true;
									p2Reaction.EPHASE = new EPHASE();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									string[] array9 = new string[2];
									if (empty2.ToUpper().Contains(CReactionData.COMPONENT))
									{
										array9 = empty2.Split(new char[1] { ',' }, 2);
									}
									else
									{
										array9[0] = empty2;
									}
									if (array9[0] != null)
									{
										if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array9[0]).ToUpper() == "V")
										{
											p2Reaction.EPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.V;
										}
										else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array9[0]).ToUpper() == "L")
										{
											p2Reaction.EPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.L;
										}
										else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array9[0]).ToUpper() == "L1")
										{
											p2Reaction.EPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.L1;
										}
										else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array9[0]).ToUpper() == "L2")
										{
											p2Reaction.EPHASE.DEFAULT = RXPhase.L2;
										}
									}
									if (array9[1] == null)
									{
										continue;
									}
									string[] array10 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array9[1].Trim()));
									for (int num4 = 0; num4 < array10.Length; num4++)
									{
										int key3 = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array10[num4])[0]);
										RXPhase value4 = RXPhase.L;
										switch (ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array10[num4])[1].ToUpper())
										{
										case "V":
											value4 = RXPhase.V;
											break;
										case "L":
											value4 = RXPhase.L;
											break;
										case "L1":
											value4 = RXPhase.L1;
											break;
										case "L2":
											value4 = RXPhase.L2;
											break;
										}
										p2Reaction.EPHASE.COMPONENT.Add(key3, value4);
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.EBASIS))
								{
									p2Reaction.EBASIS = new EBASIS();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									List<PropertyPair> propertySplit4 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
									{
										CReactionData.VAPOR,
										CReactionData.LIQUID
									}, empty2.ToUpper());
									List<string> list4 = new List<string>();
									foreach (PropertyPair item4 in propertySplit4)
									{
										list4.Add(item4.Content);
									}
									string[] array11 = list4.ToArray();
									for (int num5 = 0; num5 < array11.Length; num5++)
									{
										if (array11[num5].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.VAPOR))
										{
											switch (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array11[num5]).Trim())
											{
											case "CONCENTRATION":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.CONCENTRATION;
												break;
											case "PARTIALPRESSURE":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.PARTIALPRESSURE;
												break;
											case "FUGACITY":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.FUGACITY;
												break;
											case "MOLEFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.MOLEFRACTION;
												break;
											case "MASSFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.VAPOR = RXBasis.MASSFRACTION;
												break;
											}
										}
										else if (array11[num5].ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.LIQUID))
										{
											switch (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array11[num5]).Trim())
											{
											case "CONCENTRATION":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.CONCENTRATION;
												break;
											case "PARTIALPRESSURE":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.FUGACITY;
												break;
											case "FUGACITY":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.ACTIVITY;
												break;
											case "MOLEFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.MOLEFRACTION;
												break;
											case "MASSFRACTION":
												p2Reaction.EBASIS.LIQUID = RXBasis.MASSFRACTION;
												break;
											}
										}
									}
								}
								else if (child3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.EEXPONENT))
								{
									p2Reaction.EEXPONENT = new Dictionary<int, double>();
									empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child3.Name, ' ');
									string[] array12 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(empty2);
									for (int num6 = 0; num6 < array12.Length; num6++)
									{
										int key4 = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array12[num6])[0].Trim());
										double value5 = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array12[num6])[1].Trim());
										p2Reaction.EEXPONENT.Add(key4, value5);
									}
								}
							}
						}
						p2ReactionSet.P2RXsDic.Add(p2Reaction.ID, p2Reaction);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseReactionError"), child.Name, child2.Name));
					}
				}
			}
		}
		P2RXSetsDic = new Dictionary<string, P2ReactionSet>();
		for (int num7 = 0; num7 < P2ReactionSets.Count; num7++)
		{
			P2RXSetsDic.Add(P2ReactionSets[num7].ID, P2ReactionSets[num7]);
		}
	}

	public string[] SetHeadData(string head)
	{
		string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(head);
		string[] array2 = new string[2];
		foreach (string text in array)
		{
			if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.ID))
			{
				array2[0] = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CReactionData.NAME))
			{
				array2[1] = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
		}
		return array2;
	}

	public HYSYSEquilibriumReaction CreateHYSYSEquilibriumRX(string RXID, P2Reaction P2RX)
	{
		HYSYSEquilibriumReaction hYSYSEquilibriumReaction = new HYSYSEquilibriumReaction();
		ConvertBasicRXInfo(RXID, hYSYSEquilibriumReaction, P2RX);
		DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, P2Globalization.GetString("KineticDataIgnored4EquilRxn"));
		if (P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM != null)
		{
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams = new HYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParameters();
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.IsKeqSourceLnEuqation = true;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.A = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.A;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.B = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.B;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.C = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.C;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.D = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.D;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.E = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.E;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.F = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.F;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.G = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.G;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.H = P2RX.EQUILIBRIUM.H;
		}
		if (P2RX.EPHASE != null)
		{
			if (P2RX.EPHASE.DEFAULT == RXPhase.L)
			{
				hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.Phase = EquilibriumReactionPhase.LiquidPhase;
			}
			else if (P2RX.EPHASE.DEFAULT == RXPhase.V)
			{
				hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.Phase = EquilibriumReactionPhase.VapourPhase;
			}
		}
		return hYSYSEquilibriumReaction;
	}

	public HYSYSConversionReaction CreateHYSYSConversionRX(string RXID, P2Reaction P2RX)
	{
		HYSYSConversionReaction hYSYSConversionReaction = new HYSYSConversionReaction();
		ConvertBasicRXInfo(RXID, hYSYSConversionReaction, P2RX);
		DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, P2Globalization.GetString("KineticDataIgnored4ConvRxn"));
		if (P2RX.IsKPhaseDefaultSet)
		{
			if (hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis == null)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis = new HYSYSConversionReaction.HYSYSConversionReactionBasis();
			}
			if (P2RX.KPHASE.DEFAULT == RXPhase.L)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.LiquidPhase;
			}
			else if (P2RX.KPHASE.DEFAULT == RXPhase.V)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.VapourPhase;
			}
		}
		if (P2RX.IsEPhaseDefaultSet)
		{
			if (hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis == null)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis = new HYSYSConversionReaction.HYSYSConversionReactionBasis();
			}
			if (P2RX.EPHASE.DEFAULT == RXPhase.L)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.LiquidPhase;
			}
			else if (P2RX.EPHASE.DEFAULT == RXPhase.V)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.VapourPhase;
			}
		}
		return hYSYSConversionReaction;
	}

	public HYSYSKineticReaction CreateHYSYSKineticRX(string rxID, P2Reaction p2RX)
	{
		HYSYSKineticReaction hYSYSKineticReaction = new HYSYSKineticReaction();
		ConvertBasicRXInfo(rxID, hYSYSKineticReaction, p2RX);
		if (p2RX.KPHASE != null)
		{
			hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase = p2RX.KPHASE.DEFAULT.ToString();
		}
		if (p2RX.KBASIS != null)
		{
			if (hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase == "L")
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis = p2RX.KBASIS.LIQUID;
			}
			else if (hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase == "V")
			{
				hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis = p2RX.KBASIS.VAPOR;
			}
		}
		if (p2RX.KINETIC != null)
		{
			hYSYSKineticReaction.FrequencyFactor = p2RX.KINETIC.PEXP;
			hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.MolarEnthalpy, p2RX.KINETIC.ACTIVATION);
			hYSYSKineticReaction.ExtReacRateConst = p2RX.KINETIC.TEXPONENT;
		}
		if (p2RX.KORDER != null && p2RX.KORDER.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in p2RX.KORDER)
			{
				if (hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder == null)
				{
					hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder = new Dictionary<string, double>();
				}
				hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder.Add(P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(item.Key), item.Value);
			}
		}
		return hYSYSKineticReaction;
	}

	public void ConvertBasicRXInfo(string RXID, HYSYSReaction HYSYSRX, P2Reaction P2RX)
	{
		DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Notes, P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertP2Rxn"));
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(RXID))
		{
			HYSYSRX.ID = RXID;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(P2RX.ID) && string.Compare(ApplicationName.appName.Trim(), "HYSYS", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertP2Rxn2HYSYSRxn"), P2RX.ID, RXID));
		}
		if (P2RX.STOICHIOMETRY != null)
		{
			HYSYSRX.Stoichiometry = P2RX.STOI_HYSYSName;
		}
		if (P2RX.HORX != null)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, P2Globalization.GetString("HORXDataNotConverted"));
		}
	}
}
public enum KineticsType
{
	POWERLAW,
	PROCEDURE,
	SUBROUTINE
}
public enum RXPhase
{
	L,
	V,
	L1,
	L2
}
public class P2Stream : P2BaseObject
{
	public class BaseStream
	{
		public string Stream { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue Temp { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue Pressure { get; set; }

		public string REFSTREAM { get; set; }

		public string P2NodeInfor { get; set; }

		public string SET { get; set; }
	}

	public class StreamComposition
	{
		public string CompositionUOM { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, double> Composition { get; set; }

		public StreamComposition()
		{
			Composition = new Dictionary<int, double>();
		}

		public StreamComposition Clone()
		{
			StreamComposition streamComposition = new StreamComposition();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in Composition)
			{
				streamComposition.Composition.Add(item);
			}
			return streamComposition;
		}
	}

	public class PureComponentStream : BaseStream
	{
		public string Phase { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue LFRAC { get; set; }

		public StreamComposition CompositionInfo { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue Rate { get; set; }

		public bool Normalize { get; set; }

		public bool IsRateSet { get; set; }

		public P2BasisTypes P2BasisType { get; set; }

		public string P2CompositionUOM { get; set; }

		public PureComponentStream(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			base.P2NodeInfor = obj.ToString() + "\n";
			CompositionInfo = new StreamComposition();
			Parse(obj.Name);
		}

		private void Parse(string str)
		{
			try
			{
				string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
				while (str.Length > 0)
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ',');
					if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("STR"))
					{
						string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
						base.Stream = array[1];
						NameHelper.NotesName(base.Stream, bIsStream: true);
						base.Stream = NameHelper.ConvertName(base.Stream, bIsStream: true);
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("TEMP"))
					{
						DoubleValue value = new DoubleValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value);
						base.Temp = value;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("PRES"))
					{
						DoubleValue value2 = new DoubleValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value2);
						base.Pressure = value2;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("PHAS"))
					{
						string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
						Phase = array2[1];
						if (Phase.StartsWith("V"))
						{
							LFRAC = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
						}
						else if (Phase.StartsWith("L"))
						{
							LFRAC = new DoubleValue("", 1.0);
						}
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("LFRA"))
					{
						DoubleValue value3 = new DoubleValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value3);
						LFRAC = value3;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.REFSTREAM))
					{
						base.REFSTREAM = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.ToUpper());
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.SET))
					{
						base.SET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.ToUpper());
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("RATE"))
					{
						IsRateSet = true;
						if (text.IndexOf('=') == -1)
						{
							string text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ',');
							text += ",";
							text += text2;
						}
						TypeUnitValue value4 = new TypeUnitValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(text, ref value4);
						if (!(value4.Type == "M") && !(value4.Type == "LV") && !(value4.Type == "V") && !(value4.Type == "G") && !(value4.Type == "GV") && !(value4.Type == "W") && !(value4.Type == "WT") && string.IsNullOrEmpty(value4.Unit) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value4.Type))
						{
							value4.Unit = value4.Type.Trim();
							value4.Type = string.Empty;
						}
						Rate = value4;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("COMP"))
					{
						text += ",";
						text += str;
						int slashLength = ParseUtilty.GetSlashLength(text, 2);
						str = ((slashLength != text.Length) ? text.Substring(slashLength + 1, text.Length - slashLength - 1).Trim() : "");
						text = text.Substring(0, slashLength);
						string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text));
						if (array3 == null || array3[0] == "M")
						{
							P2BasisType = P2BasisTypes.M;
						}
						else if (array3[0] == "WT" || array3[0] == "W")
						{
							P2BasisType = P2BasisTypes.WT;
						}
						else if (array3[0] == "LV" || array3[0] == "V")
						{
							P2BasisType = P2BasisTypes.LV;
						}
						else if (array3[0] == "GV")
						{
							P2BasisType = P2BasisTypes.GV;
						}
						if (array3 != null && array3.Length > 1 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(array3[1]))
						{
							P2CompositionUOM = array3[1];
						}
						string[] array5;
						if (text.Contains('='))
						{
							string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
							array5 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(array4[1]);
						}
						else
						{
							array5 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' '));
						}
						for (int i = 0; i < array5.Length; i++)
						{
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(array5[i]))
							{
								continue;
							}
							try
							{
								string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array5[i]);
								if (array4.Length == 1)
								{
									int key = 1;
									if (CompositionInfo.Composition.Count > 0)
									{
										key = CompositionInfo.Composition.Keys.ToList()[CompositionInfo.Composition.Count - 1] + 1;
									}
									CompositionInfo.Composition.Add(key, double.Parse(array4[0]));
									CompositionInfo.CompositionUOM = P2CompositionUOM;
								}
								else if (array4.Length == 2)
								{
									int result = 0;
									double result2 = 0.0;
									int.TryParse(array4[0], out result);
									try
									{
										result2 = double.Parse(array4[1]);
									}
									catch
									{
										double.TryParse(array4[0], out result2);
										result = ((CompositionInfo.Composition.Count <= 0) ? 1 : (CompositionInfo.Composition.Keys.ToList()[CompositionInfo.Composition.Count - 1] + 1));
									}
									if (!result.Equals(0) && !result2.Equals(0.0))
									{
										CompositionInfo.Composition.Add(result, result2);
									}
									CompositionInfo.CompositionUOM = P2CompositionUOM;
								}
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
								_BHasError = true;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("NORMALIZE"))
					{
						Normalize = true;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				_BHasError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class AssayStream : BaseStream
	{
		public class Distillation
		{
			public class BaseDistillation
			{
				public IDictionary<double, double> Data { get; set; }

				public string Temp { get; set; }

				public string Stream { get; set; }

				public BaseDistillation(P2BaseObject obj)
				{
					Data = new Dictionary<double, double>();
					Parse(obj.Name);
				}

				private void Parse(string str)
				{
					ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
					while (str.Length > 0)
					{
						try
						{
							if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith("DATA"))
							{
								int slashLength = ParseUtilty.GetSlashLength(str, 2);
								string str2 = str.Substring(0, slashLength);
								str = ((slashLength < str.Length) ? str.Substring(slashLength + 1, str.Length - slashLength - 1).Trim() : string.Empty);
								string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(str2);
								string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(array[1]);
								for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
								{
									array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array2[i]);
									Data.Add(double.Parse(array[0]), double.Parse(array[1]));
								}
							}
							else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith("TEMP"))
							{
								string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ','));
								Temp = array3[1];
							}
							else if (str.ToUpper().StartsWith("STRE"))
							{
								string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ','));
								Stream = array4[1];
								NameHelper.NotesName(Stream, bIsStream: true);
								Stream = NameHelper.ConvertName(Stream, bIsStream: true);
							}
							else
							{
								ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ',');
							}
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							_BHasError = true;
						}
					}
				}
			}

			public class D86 : BaseDistillation
			{
				private DoubleValue _Pressure = new DoubleValue("MMHG", 760.0);

				public DoubleValue Pressure
				{
					get
					{
						return _Pressure;
					}
					set
					{
						_Pressure = value;
					}
				}

				public bool Cracking { get; set; }

				public D86(P2BaseObject obj)
					: base(obj)
				{
					try
					{
						if (obj.Name.IndexOf("CRACKING") != -1)
						{
							Cracking = true;
						}
						int num = obj.Name.IndexOf("PRESSURE");
						if (num != -1)
						{
							string str = obj.Name.Substring(num, obj.Name.Length - num);
							string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ',');
							if (text.Contains('='))
							{
								DoubleValue value = new DoubleValue();
								ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value);
								Pressure = value;
							}
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						_BHasError = true;
					}
				}
			}

			public class TBP : BaseDistillation
			{
				public DoubleValue Pressure { get; set; }

				public TBP(P2BaseObject obj)
					: base(obj)
				{
					try
					{
						int num = obj.Name.IndexOf("PRESSURE");
						if (num != -1)
						{
							string str = obj.Name.Substring(num, obj.Name.Length - num);
							string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ',');
							if (text.Contains('='))
							{
								DoubleValue value = new DoubleValue();
								ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value);
								Pressure = value;
							}
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						_BHasError = true;
					}
				}
			}

			public class D1160 : BaseDistillation
			{
				private DoubleValue _Pressure = new DoubleValue("MMHG", 760.0);

				public DoubleValue Pressure
				{
					get
					{
						return _Pressure;
					}
					set
					{
						_Pressure = value;
					}
				}

				public D1160(P2BaseObject obj)
					: base(obj)
				{
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(obj.Name);
					if (array == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					string[] array2 = array;
					foreach (string text in array2)
					{
						try
						{
							if (text.StartsWith(CStream.PRESSURE) && text.Contains('='))
							{
								Pressure = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
							}
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							_BHasError = true;
						}
					}
				}
			}

			public class D2887 : BaseDistillation
			{
				public D2887(P2BaseObject obj)
					: base(obj)
				{
				}
			}

			public BaseDistillation DistillationData { get; set; }

			public Distillation(P2BaseObject obj)
			{
				if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("D86"))
				{
					DistillationData = new D86(obj);
				}
				else if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("TBP"))
				{
					DistillationData = new TBP(obj);
				}
				else if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("D1160"))
				{
					DistillationData = new D1160(obj);
				}
				else if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("D2887"))
				{
					DistillationData = new D2887(obj);
				}
			}
		}

		public class Gravity : DataBase
		{
			public GravityTypeEnum GravityType { get; set; }

			public Gravity(P2BaseObject obj)
				: base(obj)
			{
				if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("API"))
				{
					GravityType = GravityTypeEnum.API;
				}
				else if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("SPGR"))
				{
					GravityType = GravityTypeEnum.SPGR;
				}
				else if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("WATSONK"))
				{
					GravityType = GravityTypeEnum.WATSONK;
				}
			}
		}

		public enum GravityTypeEnum
		{
			API,
			SPGR,
			WATSONK
		}

		public class DataBase
		{
			public double Average { get; set; }

			public string AverageUnit { get; set; }

			public string Stream { get; set; }

			public IDictionary<double, double> Data { get; set; }

			public string DataUnit { get; set; }

			public DataBase(P2BaseObject obj)
			{
				try
				{
					string str = obj.Name;
					ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
					string text = str;
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(str);
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith("AVER") || array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith("AVG"))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							Average = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
							AverageUnit = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Unit;
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.STREAM))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
							Stream = array2[1];
							NameHelper.NotesName(Stream, bIsStream: true);
							Stream = NameHelper.ConvertName(Stream, bIsStream: true);
						}
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						return;
					}
					DataUnit = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(text)[0];
					Data = new Dictionary<double, double>();
					text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
					string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					for (int j = 0; j < array3.Length; j++)
					{
						array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array3[j]);
						if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
						{
							Data.Add(double.Parse(array[0].Trim()), double.Parse(array[1].Trim()));
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_BHasError = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public class LightendsData
		{
			private string _MATCH = "MATCH";

			public IDictionary<int, double> COMPOSITION { get; set; }

			public string COMPOSITIONBasis { get; set; }

			public string COMPOSITIONUnit { get; set; }

			public QualifierValue RATE { get; set; }

			public QualifierValue FRACTION { get; set; }

			public QualifierValue PERCENT { get; set; }

			public string MATCH
			{
				get
				{
					return _MATCH;
				}
				set
				{
					_MATCH = value;
				}
			}

			public string NOMATCH { get; set; }

			public string STREAM { get; set; }

			public string NORMALIZE { get; set; }

			public LightendsData(P2BaseObject obj)
			{
				string str = obj.Name;
				if (str.ToUpper().Contains(CStream.NORMALIZE))
				{
					NORMALIZE = "NORMALIZE";
					int num = str.ToUpper().IndexOf(CStream.NORMALIZE);
					int num2 = str.IndexOf(",", num);
					str = ((num2 != -1) ? str.Remove(num, num2 - num) : str.Remove(num));
				}
				ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
				string text = str;
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(str);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					try
					{
						if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.FRACTION))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
							if (FRACTION == null)
							{
								FRACTION = new QualifierValue();
							}
							FRACTION.Qualifier = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(array2[0])[0];
							FRACTION.Value = array2[1];
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.RATE))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
							if (RATE == null)
							{
								RATE = new QualifierValue();
							}
							RATE.Qualifier = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(array3[0])[0];
							RATE.Value = array3[1];
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.PERCENT))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
							if (PERCENT == null)
							{
								PERCENT = new QualifierValue();
							}
							PERCENT.Qualifier = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(array4[0])[0];
							PERCENT.Value = array4[1];
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.STREAM))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
							STREAM = array5[1];
							NameHelper.NotesName(STREAM, bIsStream: true);
							STREAM = NameHelper.ConvertName(STREAM, bIsStream: true);
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.MATCH))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							MATCH = "MATCH";
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.NOMATCH))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							NOMATCH = "NOMATCH";
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.NORMALIZE))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							NORMALIZE = "NORMALIZE";
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						_BHasError = true;
					}
				}
				try
				{
					COMPOSITION = new Dictionary<int, double>();
					COMPOSITIONBasis = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(text)[0];
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(COMPOSITIONBasis))
					{
						COMPOSITIONBasis = "M";
					}
					COMPOSITIONUnit = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(text)[1];
					text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
					string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					for (int j = 0; j < array6.Length; j++)
					{
						array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array6[j]);
						if (array.Length > 1)
						{
							COMPOSITION.Add(int.Parse(array[0].Trim()), double.Parse(array[1].Trim()));
						}
						else
						{
							COMPOSITION.Add(j, double.Parse(array6[j].Trim()));
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_BHasError = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public class KinematicViscosity
		{
			public DoubleValue Average1 { get; set; }

			public DoubleValue Average2 { get; set; }

			public DoubleValue Temp1 { get; set; }

			public DoubleValue Temp2 { get; set; }

			public string Stream { get; set; }

			public IDictionary<double, double> DataTemp1 { get; set; }

			public IDictionary<double, double> DataTemp2 { get; set; }

			public string KinematicViscosityUnit { get; set; }

			public KinematicViscosity(P2BaseObject obj)
			{
				string str = obj.Name;
				ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
				string text = str;
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(str);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					try
					{
						if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.AVERAGE))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							Average1 = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
							i++;
							if (i < array.Length)
							{
								double.TryParse(array[i], out var result);
								Average2 = new DoubleValue(Average1.Unit, result);
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.STREAM))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
							Stream = array2[1];
							NameHelper.NotesName(Stream, bIsStream: true);
							Stream = NameHelper.ConvertName(Stream, bIsStream: true);
						}
						if (array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.TEMP))
						{
							text = text.Replace(array[i], "");
							Temp1 = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
							i++;
							if (i < array.Length)
							{
								double.TryParse(array[i], out var result2);
								Temp2 = new DoubleValue(Temp1.Unit, result2);
							}
						}
						if (!array[i].ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.DATA))
						{
							continue;
						}
						int num = 0;
						if (Temp1 == null)
						{
							Temp1 = new DoubleValue(ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(array[i])[0], ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value);
							num = 1;
						}
						KinematicViscosityUnit = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(array[i])[num];
						i++;
						if (i < array.Length)
						{
							double.TryParse(ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(array[i])[0], out var result3);
							if (Temp2 == null)
							{
								Temp2 = new DoubleValue(Temp1.Unit, result3);
							}
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						_BHasError = true;
					}
				}
				try
				{
					DataTemp1 = new Dictionary<double, double>();
					DataTemp2 = new Dictionary<double, double>();
					text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
					string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					Dictionary<int, List<string>> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, List<string>>();
					for (int j = 0; j < array3.Length; j++)
					{
						array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array3[j]);
						if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						List<string> list = new List<string>();
						for (int k = 0; k < array.Length; k++)
						{
							if (array[k].Trim().Contains(' '))
							{
								string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[k].Trim());
								for (int l = 0; l < array4.Length; l++)
								{
									if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array4[l].Trim()))
									{
										list.Add(array4[l].Trim());
									}
								}
							}
							else
							{
								list.Add(array[k]);
							}
						}
						dictionary.Add(j, list);
					}
					foreach (int key in dictionary.Keys)
					{
						if (dictionary[key].Count == 3)
						{
							DataTemp1.Add(double.Parse(dictionary[key][0].Trim()), double.Parse(dictionary[key][1].Trim()));
							DataTemp2.Add(double.Parse(dictionary[key][0].Trim()), double.Parse(dictionary[key][2].Trim()));
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_BHasError = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public class UserProperty : DataBase
		{
			public string Name { get; set; }

			public AssayUserProperty MapInfo { get; set; }

			public UserProperty(P2BaseObject obj)
				: base(obj)
			{
			}
		}

		private string _ASSAY = "LV";

		private string _Phase = "M";

		public TypeUnitValue Rate { get; set; }

		public string ASSAY
		{
			get
			{
				return _ASSAY;
			}
			set
			{
				_ASSAY = value;
			}
		}

		public string BLEND { get; set; }

		public string XBLEND { get; set; }

		public string Phase
		{
			get
			{
				return _Phase;
			}
			set
			{
				_Phase = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue LFRAC { get; set; }

		public Distillation DISTILLATION { get; set; }

		public DataBase MW { get; set; }

		public Gravity GRAVITY { get; set; }

		public LightendsData LIGHTEND { get; set; }

		public List<UserProperty> SpetialProperties { get; set; }

		public KinematicViscosity KVIS { get; set; }

		public AssayStream(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			base.P2NodeInfor = obj.ToString() + "\n";
			Parse(obj.Name);
			foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
			{
				try
				{
					base.P2NodeInfor = base.P2NodeInfor + child.ToString() + "\n";
					if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("D86") || child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("TBP") || child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("D1160") || child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("D2887"))
					{
						DISTILLATION = new Distillation(child);
						continue;
					}
					if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("API") || child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("SPGR") || child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("WATSONK"))
					{
						GRAVITY = new Gravity(child);
						continue;
					}
					if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("MW"))
					{
						MW = new DataBase(child);
						continue;
					}
					if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("LIGH"))
					{
						LIGHTEND = new LightendsData(child);
						continue;
					}
					if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("KVIS"))
					{
						KVIS = new KinematicViscosity(child);
						continue;
					}
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (string item3 in P2ToHYSYSssayUserPropertyMap.Data.Keys.ToList())
					{
						string text = item3;
						int num = text.IndexOf('(');
						if (num > 0)
						{
							text = text.Substring(0, num);
						}
						if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(text))
						{
							if (SpetialProperties == null)
							{
								SpetialProperties = new List<UserProperty>();
							}
							UserProperty item = new UserProperty(child)
							{
								Name = item3,
								MapInfo = P2ToHYSYSssayUserPropertyMap.Data[item3]
							};
							SpetialProperties.Add(item);
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						string[] array = child.Name.Split(' ');
						if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
						{
							string name = array[0];
							if (SpetialProperties == null)
							{
								SpetialProperties = new List<UserProperty>();
							}
							UserProperty item2 = new UserProperty(child)
							{
								Name = name
							};
							SpetialProperties.Add(item2);
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.Convert, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ASSAYStreamNotConverted"), child.Name));
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_BHasError = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void Parse(string str)
		{
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ' ');
			while (str.Length > 0)
			{
				try
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ',');
					if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.STREAM))
					{
						string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
						base.Stream = array[1];
						NameHelper.NotesName(base.Stream, bIsStream: true);
						base.Stream = NameHelper.ConvertName(base.Stream, bIsStream: true);
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.TEMP))
					{
						DoubleValue value = new DoubleValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value);
						base.Temp = value;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.PRESSURE))
					{
						DoubleValue value2 = new DoubleValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value2);
						base.Pressure = value2;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.PHASE))
					{
						if (text.Contains('='))
						{
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
							Phase = array2[1];
						}
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.LFRAC))
					{
						DoubleValue value3 = new DoubleValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text, ref value3);
						LFRAC = value3;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.RATE))
					{
						if (text.IndexOf('=') == -1)
						{
							string text2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, ',');
							text += ",";
							text += text2;
						}
						TypeUnitValue value4 = new TypeUnitValue();
						ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(text, ref value4);
						Rate = value4;
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.ASSAY))
					{
						string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
						ASSAY = array3[1];
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.BLEND))
					{
						string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
						BLEND = array4[1];
					}
					else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.XBLEND))
					{
						string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(text);
						XBLEND = array5[1];
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_BHasError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private static bool _BHasError;

	public BaseStream BaseStreamInfo { get; set; }

	public StreamComposition StreamCompositionInfo { get; set; }

	public string Phase { get; set; }

	public TypeUnitValue Rate { get; set; }

	public IList<BaseStream> Streams { get; set; }

	public P2Stream(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			_BHasError = false;
			Streams = new List<BaseStream>();
			foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
			{
				ParseStream(child);
			}
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(Categories.Stream, this);
			if (BaseObject._Report != null)
			{
				Report report = BaseObject._Report;
				int p2StreamCount = report.P2StreamCount + 1;
				report.P2StreamCount = p2StreamCount;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseStream")));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bParsingError || _BHasError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseStream")));
			}
		}
	}

	private void ParseStream(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CStream.PROP))
		{
			if (obj.Name.IndexOf(CStream.ASSAY) == -1)
			{
				Streams.Add(new PureComponentStream(obj));
			}
			else
			{
				Streams.Add(new AssayStream(obj));
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		try
		{
			IList<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			foreach (BaseStream stream in Streams)
			{
				if (stream.GetType() == typeof(PureComponentStream))
				{
					PureComponentStream pureComponentStream = (PureComponentStream)stream;
					HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = new HYSYSStream();
					hYSYSStream.ConvertNotes = stream.P2NodeInfor;
					hYSYSStream.FluidPackage = stream.SET;
					hYSYSStream.Pressure = ((pureComponentStream.Pressure == null) ? null : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, pureComponentStream.Pressure.Value, pureComponentStream.Pressure.Unit));
					hYSYSStream.Stream = pureComponentStream.Stream;
					hYSYSStream.Temp = ((pureComponentStream.Temp == null) ? null : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, pureComponentStream.Temp.Value, pureComponentStream.Temp.Unit));
					hYSYSStream.VaporFrac = ((pureComponentStream.LFRAC == null) ? null : new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 1.0 - pureComponentStream.LFRAC.Value));
					hYSYSStream.RefStream = pureComponentStream.REFSTREAM;
					if (pureComponentStream.CompositionInfo.Composition != null)
					{
						if (!pureComponentStream.IsRateSet)
						{
							foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in pureComponentStream.CompositionInfo.Composition)
							{
								TypeUnitValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, item.Value, pureComponentStream.CompositionInfo.CompositionUOM, pureComponentStream.P2BasisType.ToString());
								string hYSYSCompName = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(item.Key);
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName))
								{
									hYSYSStream.Composition.Data.Add(hYSYSCompName, hysysValue.Value);
									hYSYSStream.Composition.CompositionUOM = hysysValue.Unit;
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item2 in pureComponentStream.CompositionInfo.Composition)
							{
								string hYSYSCompName2 = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(item2.Key);
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName2))
								{
									hYSYSStream.Composition.Data.Add(hYSYSCompName2, item2.Value);
								}
							}
						}
						if (pureComponentStream.P2BasisType == P2BasisTypes.M)
						{
							if (!pureComponentStream.IsRateSet)
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFlow;
							}
							else
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFrac;
							}
						}
						else if (pureComponentStream.P2BasisType == P2BasisTypes.WT)
						{
							if (!pureComponentStream.IsRateSet)
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MassFlow;
							}
							else
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MassFrac;
							}
						}
						else if (pureComponentStream.P2BasisType == P2BasisTypes.LV || pureComponentStream.P2BasisType == P2BasisTypes.L)
						{
							if (!pureComponentStream.IsRateSet)
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.LVFlow;
							}
							else
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.LVFrac;
							}
						}
						else if (pureComponentStream.P2BasisType == P2BasisTypes.GV)
						{
							if (!pureComponentStream.IsRateSet)
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.GVFlow;
							}
							else
							{
								hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.GVFrac;
							}
						}
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pureComponentStream.Phase) && pureComponentStream.Phase.StartsWith("L"))
					{
						hYSYSStream.VaporFrac = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);
					}
					else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pureComponentStream.Phase) && pureComponentStream.Phase.StartsWith("V"))
					{
						hYSYSStream.VaporFrac = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 1.0);
					}
					if (pureComponentStream.Rate != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(pureComponentStream.Rate.Type) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(pureComponentStream.Rate.Unit))
					{
						pureComponentStream.Rate.Type = "M";
					}
					hYSYSStream.Rate = ((pureComponentStream.Rate == null) ? null : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, pureComponentStream.Rate.Value, pureComponentStream.Rate.Unit, pureComponentStream.Rate.Type));
					list.Add(hYSYSStream);
				}
				else if (stream.GetType() == typeof(AssayStream))
				{
					AssayStream assayStream = (AssayStream)stream;
					HYSYSStream hYSYSStream2 = new HYSYSStream();
					hYSYSStream2.ConvertNotes = stream.P2NodeInfor;
					hYSYSStream2.FluidPackage = stream.SET;
					hYSYSStream2.IsAssayStream = true;
					hYSYSStream2.Pressure = ((assayStream.Pressure == null) ? null : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, assayStream.Pressure.Value, assayStream.Pressure.Unit));
					hYSYSStream2.Stream = ((assayStream.Stream == null) ? null : assayStream.Stream);
					hYSYSStream2.Temp = ((assayStream.Temp == null) ? null : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, assayStream.Temp.Value, assayStream.Temp.Unit));
					if (assayStream.Rate != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(assayStream.Rate.Type))
					{
						assayStream.Rate.Type = UnitConverter.GetHysysRateType(UnitClassEnum.Rate, assayStream.Rate.Value, assayStream.Rate.Unit, assayStream.Rate.Type);
					}
					hYSYSStream2.Rate = ((assayStream.Rate == null) ? null : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, assayStream.Rate.Value, assayStream.Rate.Unit, assayStream.Rate.Type));
					hYSYSStream2.VaporFrac = ((assayStream.LFRAC == null) ? null : new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 1.0 - assayStream.LFRAC.Value));
					hYSYSStream2.RefStream = assayStream.REFSTREAM;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(assayStream.Phase) && assayStream.Phase.StartsWith("L"))
					{
						hYSYSStream2.VaporFrac = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);
					}
					else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(assayStream.Phase) && assayStream.Phase.StartsWith("V"))
					{
						hYSYSStream2.VaporFrac = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 1.0);
					}
					list.Add(hYSYSStream2);
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertStream")));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertStream")));
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	private void SetArray(double[] a, IDictionary<int, double> Dic)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < a.Length; i++)
		{
			a[i] = 0.0;
		}
		foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in Dic)
		{
			if (item.Key - 1 < a.Length)
			{
				a[item.Key - 1] = item.Value;
			}
		}
	}
}
public enum P2BasisTypes
{
	M,
	WT,
	LV,
	L,
	GV
}
public class P2ThermoDynamic : P2BaseObject
{
	private List<string> stringsForAcitivity;

	private List<string> stringsForAllThermoProps;

	public List<P2SystemMethod> SystemMethodes { get; set; }

	public List<P2SystemMethod> IndividualMethodes { get; set; }

	public bool HasAspenPlusActivityMethod { get; set; }

	public P2ThermoDynamic(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			GetActivityMethodInfo(obj);
			SystemMethodes = new List<P2SystemMethod>();
			IndividualMethodes = new List<P2SystemMethod>();
			if (obj.Children == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
			{
				if (child.Name.ToUpper().Contains(CThermo.SYSTEM))
				{
					P2SystemMethod p2SystemMethod = new P2SystemMethod();
					bool flag = false;
					p2SystemMethod.Setting = new P2MethodSetting(child.Name, ref flag);
					base.bParsingError = flag;
					foreach (P2BaseObject child2 in child.Children)
					{
						p2SystemMethod.Setting.P2SubStatementStr.Add(child2.Name);
						P2MethodSetting setting = p2SystemMethod.Setting;
						setting.P2StatementStr = setting.P2StatementStr + "\n  " + child2.Name;
						if (child.Name.StartsWith(CThermo.WATER))
						{
							p2SystemMethod.SpecificWater = new P2ThomoSpecificWater(child2.Name);
						}
					}
					SystemMethodes.Add(p2SystemMethod);
					continue;
				}
				P2SystemMethod p2SystemMethod2 = new P2SystemMethod();
				bool flag2 = false;
				p2SystemMethod2.Setting = new P2MethodSetting(child.Name, ref flag2);
				base.bParsingError = flag2;
				foreach (P2BaseObject child3 in child.Children)
				{
					p2SystemMethod2.Setting.P2SubStatementStr.Add(child3.Name);
					P2MethodSetting setting2 = p2SystemMethod2.Setting;
					setting2.P2StatementStr = setting2.P2StatementStr + "\n  " + child3.Name;
					if (child.Name.StartsWith(CThermo.WATER))
					{
						p2SystemMethod2.SpecificWater = new P2ThomoSpecificWater(child3.Name);
					}
				}
				IndividualMethodes.Add(p2SystemMethod2);
			}
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(Categories.Thermodynamic, this);
		}
		catch (P2CException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseThermo")));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bParsingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseThermo")));
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSFluidPackages[] array = new HYSYSFluidPackages[1]
		{
			new HYSYSFluidPackages()
		};
		try
		{
			foreach (P2SystemMethod systemMethode in SystemMethodes)
			{
				try
				{
					array[0].AllPropertyNames.Add(systemMethode.Setting.SET);
					QualifierValue sYSTEM = systemMethode.Setting.SYSTEM;
					if (systemMethode.Setting.DEFAULT)
					{
						array[0].DefaultMehodeName = systemMethode.Setting.SET;
					}
					QualifierValue enthalpy = null;
					QualifierValue density = null;
					string hysysPropertyKey = P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.GetHysysPropertyKey(CThermo.SYSTEM_key, sYSTEM.Value);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysPropertyKey))
					{
						if (P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.HYSYSActivityProperties.ContainsKey(sYSTEM.Value))
						{
							string text = P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.HYSYSActivityProperties[sYSTEM.Value];
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(systemMethode.Setting.KVALUE_VLE) && text == "UNIFAC")
							{
								text = "UNIFAC VLE";
							}
							else if (text == "UNIFAC")
							{
								text = "UNIFAC LLE";
							}
							HYSYSFluidPackage hYSYSFluidPackage = new HYSYSFluidPackage(systemMethode.Setting.SET, isIndividual: false, systemMethode.Setting.DEFAULT, text, enthalpy, density, sYSTEM.Value);
							CreateConvertNotes(systemMethode, hYSYSFluidPackage, isEOS: false, sYSTEM.Value);
							array[0].ActivityFluidPackages.Add(hYSYSFluidPackage);
						}
						continue;
					}
					if (P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.EnthalpyThermoList.Contains(sYSTEM.Value))
					{
						if (systemMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY != null && systemMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY.Value == "LK")
						{
							enthalpy = systemMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY;
						}
						else if (systemMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY2 != null && systemMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Value == "LK")
						{
							enthalpy = systemMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY2;
						}
					}
					if (P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.DensityThermoList.Contains(sYSTEM.Value))
					{
						if (systemMethode.Setting.DENSITY != null && systemMethode.Setting.DENSITY.Value == "RACKETT")
						{
							density = systemMethode.Setting.DENSITY;
						}
						else if (systemMethode.Setting.DENSITY2 != null && systemMethode.Setting.DENSITY2.Value == "RACKETT")
						{
							density = systemMethode.Setting.DENSITY2;
						}
					}
					HYSYSFluidPackage hYSYSFluidPackage2 = new HYSYSFluidPackage(systemMethode.Setting.SET, isIndividual: false, systemMethode.Setting.DEFAULT, hysysPropertyKey, enthalpy, density, sYSTEM.Value);
					CreateConvertNotes(systemMethode, hYSYSFluidPackage2, isEOS: true, sYSTEM.Value);
					array[0].EOSFluidPackages.Add(hYSYSFluidPackage2);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					base.bMappingError = true;
				}
			}
			foreach (P2SystemMethod individualMethode in IndividualMethodes)
			{
				try
				{
					array[0].AllPropertyNames.Add(individualMethode.Setting.SET);
					string text2 = individualMethode.Setting.KVALUE_VLE;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						goto IL_0333;
					}
					text2 = individualMethode.Setting.KVALUE_VLLE;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						goto IL_0333;
					}
					text2 = individualMethode.Setting.KVALUE_LLE;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						goto IL_0333;
					}
					goto end_IL_02d0;
					IL_0333:
					if (individualMethode.Setting.DEFAULT)
					{
						array[0].DefaultMehodeName = individualMethode.Setting.SET;
					}
					QualifierValue enthalpy2 = null;
					QualifierValue density2 = null;
					string hysysPropertyKey2 = P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.GetHysysPropertyKey(CThermo.KVALUE_key, text2);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysPropertyKey2))
					{
						if (P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.HYSYSActivityProperties.ContainsKey(text2))
						{
							string text3 = P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.HYSYSActivityProperties[text2];
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(individualMethode.Setting.KVALUE_VLE) && text3 == "UNIFAC")
							{
								text3 = "UNIFAC VLE";
							}
							else if (text3 == "UNIFAC")
							{
								text3 = "UNIFAC LLE";
							}
							if (P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.HYSYSActivityProperties.ContainsKey(text2))
							{
								HYSYSFluidPackage hYSYSFluidPackage3 = new HYSYSFluidPackage(individualMethode.Setting.SET, isIndividual: false, individualMethode.Setting.DEFAULT, text3, enthalpy2, density2, text2);
								CreateConvertNotes(individualMethode, hYSYSFluidPackage3, isEOS: false, text2);
								array[0].ActivityFluidPackages.Add(hYSYSFluidPackage3);
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					if (P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.EnthalpyThermoList.Contains(text2))
					{
						if (individualMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY != null && individualMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY.Value == "LK")
						{
							enthalpy2 = individualMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY;
						}
						else if (individualMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY2 != null && individualMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Value == "LK")
						{
							enthalpy2 = individualMethode.Setting.ENTHALPY2;
						}
					}
					if (P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.DensityThermoList.Contains(text2))
					{
						if (individualMethode.Setting.DENSITY != null && individualMethode.Setting.DENSITY.Value == "RACKETT")
						{
							density2 = individualMethode.Setting.DENSITY;
						}
						else if (individualMethode.Setting.DENSITY2 != null && individualMethode.Setting.DENSITY2.Value == "RACKETT")
						{
							density2 = individualMethode.Setting.DENSITY2;
						}
					}
					HYSYSFluidPackage hYSYSFluidPackage4 = new HYSYSFluidPackage(individualMethode.Setting.SET, isIndividual: false, individualMethode.Setting.DEFAULT, hysysPropertyKey2, enthalpy2, density2, text2);
					CreateConvertNotes(individualMethode, hYSYSFluidPackage4, isEOS: true, text2);
					array[0].EOSFluidPackages.Add(hYSYSFluidPackage4);
					continue;
					end_IL_02d0:;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					base.bMappingError = true;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			bool num = P2Component.HasNoIncludedCompInTraditionalCompoents();
			array[0].HasAspenPlusActivityProperty = HasAspenPlusActivityMethod;
			if (num)
			{
				array[0].HasAspenPlusActivityProperty = false;
			}
			if (!Options.IsEosHYSYSProp)
			{
				array[0].StringsForAspenPlus = stringsForAllThermoProps;
				array[0].IsEOSUsingAspenPlus = true;
			}
			else
			{
				array[0].StringsForAspenPlus = stringsForAcitivity;
				array[0].IsEOSUsingAspenPlus = false;
			}
		}
		catch (P2CException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertThermo")));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertThermo")));
			}
		}
		return array;
	}

	private List<string> CreateConvertNotes(P2SystemMethod method, HYSYSFluidPackage package0, bool isEOS, string kval)
	{
		List<string> result = new List<string>();
		package0.ConvertNotes.Add(method.Setting.P2StatementStr + "\n");
		if (isEOS)
		{
			if (method.Setting.ENTHALPY != null && (!P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.EnthalpyThermoList.Contains(kval) || method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Value != "LK"))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY= {0} {1}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Qualifier, method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (method.Setting.ENTHALPY2 != null && (!P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.EnthalpyThermoList.Contains(kval) || method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Value != "LK"))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY={0} {1}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Qualifier, method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (method.Setting.DENSITY != null && (!P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.DensityThermoList.Contains(kval) || method.Setting.DENSITY.Value != "RACKETT"))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.DENSITY.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY= {0} {1}", method.Setting.DENSITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.DENSITY.Qualifier, method.Setting.DENSITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (method.Setting.DENSITY2 != null && (!P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.DensityThermoList.Contains(kval) || method.Setting.DENSITY2.Value != "RACKETT"))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.DENSITY2.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY={0} {1}", method.Setting.DENSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.DENSITY2.Qualifier, method.Setting.DENSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (method.Setting.ENTHALPY != null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY= {0} {1}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Qualifier, method.Setting.ENTHALPY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (method.Setting.ENTHALPY2 != null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY={0} {1}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTHALPY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Qualifier, method.Setting.ENTHALPY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (method.Setting.DENSITY != null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.DENSITY.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY= {0} {1}", method.Setting.DENSITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.DENSITY.Qualifier, method.Setting.DENSITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (method.Setting.DENSITY2 != null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.DENSITY2.Qualifier))
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY={0} {1}", method.Setting.DENSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				else
				{
					package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DENSITY({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.DENSITY2.Qualifier, method.Setting.DENSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.L1KEY))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("L1KEY={0} {1}", method.Setting.L1KEY, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.L2KEY))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("L2KEY ={0} {1}", method.Setting.L2KEY, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (method.Setting.VISCOSITY != null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.VISCOSITY.Qualifier))
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("VISCOSITY= {0} {1}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			else
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("VISCOSITY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY.Qualifier, method.Setting.VISCOSITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
		}
		if (method.Setting.VISCOSITY2 != null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Qualifier))
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("VISCOSITY={0} {1}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			else
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("VISCOSITY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Qualifier, method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
		}
		if (method.Setting.ENTROPY != null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.ENTROPY.Qualifier))
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTROPY= {0} {1}", method.Setting.ENTROPY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			else
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTROPY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.ENTROPY.Qualifier, method.Setting.ENTROPY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
		}
		if (method.Setting.VISCOSITY2 != null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Qualifier))
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTROPY={0} {1}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			else
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("ENTROPY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Qualifier, method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.RVPMETHOD))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("RVPMETHOD ={0} {1}", method.Setting.RVPMETHOD, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.TVPMETHOD))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("TVPMETHOD ={0} {1}", method.Setting.TVPMETHOD, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.PHI))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("PHI ={0} {1}", method.Setting.PHI, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.HENRY))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HENRY ={0} {1}", method.Setting.HENRY, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.TRANSPORT))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("TRANSPORT ={0} {1}", method.Setting.TRANSPORT, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.FLPO))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("FLPO ={0} {1}", method.Setting.FLPO, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		foreach (string item in method.Setting.P2SubStatementStr)
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("{0} {1}", item, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (method.Setting.CONDUCTIVITY != null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.CONDUCTIVITY.Qualifier))
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("CONDUCTIVITY= {0} {1}", method.Setting.CONDUCTIVITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			else
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("CONDUCTIVITY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.CONDUCTIVITY.Qualifier, method.Setting.CONDUCTIVITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
		}
		if (method.Setting.VISCOSITY2 != null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Qualifier))
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("CONDUCTIVITY={0} {1}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			else
			{
				package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("CONDUCTIVITY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Qualifier, method.Setting.VISCOSITY2.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method.Setting.SURFACE))
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("SURFACE ={0} {1}", method.Setting.SURFACE, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		if (method.Setting.DIFFUSIVITY != null)
		{
			package0.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("DIFFUSIVITY ({0})={1} {2}", method.Setting.DIFFUSIVITY.Qualifier, method.Setting.DIFFUSIVITY.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
		}
		return result;
	}

	public bool IsNeedEnableEOSSelection()
	{
		bool flag = P2Component.HasNoIncludedCompInTraditionalCompoents();
		bool flag2 = HasAssay();
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = Convert2HYSYSObj();
		bool flag3 = true;
		if (array != null && array.Count() >= 1)
		{
			HYSYSFluidPackages hYSYSFluidPackages = array[0] as HYSYSFluidPackages;
			if (hYSYSFluidPackages.EOSFluidPackages != null && hYSYSFluidPackages.EOSFluidPackages.Count == 0)
			{
				flag3 = false;
			}
		}
		if (flag3)
		{
			_ = flag2 || flag;
		}
		if (flag3 && !flag2)
		{
			return !flag;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private bool HasAssay()
	{
		if (!P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey(Categories.Stream))
		{
			return false;
		}
		IList<P2Stream.BaseStream> streams = (P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Stream] as P2Stream).Streams;
		if (streams == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		foreach (P2Stream.BaseStream item in streams)
		{
			if (item.GetType() == typeof(P2Stream.AssayStream))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void GetActivityMethodInfo(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		stringsForAcitivity = new List<string>();
		stringsForAllThermoProps = new List<string>();
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Parent.Children)
		{
			if (child.Name.StartsWith(Categories.Title))
			{
				stringsForAcitivity.Add(child.Name);
				stringsForAllThermoProps.Add(child.Name);
				foreach (P2BaseObject child2 in child.Children)
				{
					stringsForAcitivity.Add("  " + child2.Name);
					stringsForAllThermoProps.Add(child2.Name);
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.StartsWith(Categories.Component))
			{
				stringsForAcitivity.Add(child.Name);
				stringsForAllThermoProps.Add(child.Name);
				foreach (P2BaseObject child3 in child.Children)
				{
					stringsForAcitivity.Add("  " + child3.Name);
					stringsForAllThermoProps.Add(child3.Name);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!child.Name.StartsWith(Categories.Thermodynamic))
				{
					continue;
				}
				stringsForAcitivity.Add(child.Name);
				stringsForAllThermoProps.Add(child.Name);
				foreach (P2BaseObject child4 in child.Children)
				{
					stringsForAllThermoProps.Add(child4.Name);
					foreach (P2BaseObject child5 in child4.Children)
					{
						stringsForAllThermoProps.Add(child5.Name);
					}
					string[] array2;
					if (child4.Name.Contains(CThermo.SYSTEM))
					{
						string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child4.Name, ' '));
						string text = string.Empty;
						array2 = array;
						foreach (string text2 in array2)
						{
							if (text2.StartsWith(CThermo.SYSTEM))
							{
								text = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text2).Value;
								break;
							}
						}
						foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> activityProperty in P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.ActivityProperties)
						{
							string key = activityProperty.Key;
							if (!text.Contains(key))
							{
								continue;
							}
							stringsForAcitivity.Add("  " + child4.Name);
							if (child4.Children != null)
							{
								foreach (P2BaseObject child6 in child4.Children)
								{
									stringsForAcitivity.Add(child6.Name);
								}
							}
							ParseUtilty.IsAspenPlusInstalled();
							HasAspenPlusActivityMethod = true;
							break;
						}
						continue;
					}
					string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child4.Name, ' '));
					string text3 = string.Empty;
					array2 = array3;
					foreach (string text4 in array2)
					{
						if (text4.StartsWith(CThermo.KVALUE))
						{
							QualifierValue qualifierValue = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text4);
							if (qualifierValue.Qualifier == CThermo.VLE || string.IsNullOrEmpty(qualifierValue.Qualifier))
							{
								text3 = qualifierValue.Value;
								break;
							}
							if (qualifierValue.Qualifier == CThermo.VLLE || string.IsNullOrEmpty(qualifierValue.Qualifier))
							{
								text3 = qualifierValue.Value;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> activityProperty2 in P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.ActivityProperties)
					{
						string key2 = activityProperty2.Key;
						if (!text3.Contains(key2))
						{
							continue;
						}
						stringsForAcitivity.Add("  " + child4.Name);
						foreach (P2BaseObject child7 in child4.Children)
						{
							stringsForAcitivity.Add(child7.Name);
						}
						ParseUtilty.IsAspenPlusInstalled();
						HasAspenPlusActivityMethod = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		stringsForAcitivity.Add("STREAM DATA");
		stringsForAcitivity.Add("$ *** NO STREAM OBJECTS ***");
		stringsForAcitivity.Add("UNIT OPERATIONS");
		stringsForAcitivity.Add("$ *** NO UNITS ***");
		stringsForAcitivity.Add("END");
		stringsForAllThermoProps.Add("STREAM DATA");
		stringsForAllThermoProps.Add("$ *** NO STREAM OBJECTS ***");
		stringsForAllThermoProps.Add("UNIT OPERATIONS");
		stringsForAllThermoProps.Add("$ *** NO UNITS ***");
		stringsForAllThermoProps.Add("END");
	}
}
public class P2SystemMethod
{
	public P2MethodSetting Setting { get; set; }

	public P2ThomoSpecificWater SpecificWater { get; set; }
}
public class P2MethodSetting
{
	public QualifierValue SYSTEM { get; private set; }

	public string SET { get; private set; }

	public bool DEFAULT { get; private set; }

	public string KVALUE_SLLE { get; private set; }

	public string L1KEY { get; private set; }

	public string L2KEY { get; private set; }

	public string KVALUE_VLE { get; private set; }

	public string KVALUE_LLE { get; private set; }

	public string KVALUE_VLLE { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue ENTHALPY { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue DENSITY { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue VISCOSITY { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue ENTROPY { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue ENTHALPY2 { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue DENSITY2 { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue VISCOSITY2 { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue ENTROPY2 { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue CONDUCTIVITY { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue CONDUCTIVITY2 { get; private set; }

	public QualifierValue DIFFUSIVITY { get; private set; }

	public string SURFACE { get; private set; }

	public string RVPMETHOD { get; private set; }

	public string TVPMETHOD { get; private set; }

	public string PHI { get; private set; }

	public string HENRY { get; private set; }

	public string TRANSPORT { get; private set; }

	public string FLPO { get; private set; }

	public string P2StatementStr { get; set; }

	public List<string> P2SubStatementStr { get; set; }

	public P2MethodSetting(string sSource, ref bool bParsingError)
	{
		P2SubStatementStr = new List<string>();
		P2StatementStr = sSource;
		sSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
		string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(sSource);
		foreach (string text in array)
		{
			try
			{
				if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.SYSTEM))
				{
					SYSTEM = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.SET))
				{
					SET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.SURFACE))
				{
					SURFACE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.DEFAULT))
				{
					DEFAULT = true;
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.L1KEY))
				{
					L1KEY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.L2KEY))
				{
					L2KEY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.KVALUE))
				{
					QualifierValue qualifierValue = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					if (qualifierValue.Qualifier == CThermo.SLLE)
					{
						KVALUE_SLLE = qualifierValue.Value;
					}
					else if (qualifierValue.Qualifier == CThermo.VLE)
					{
						KVALUE_VLE = qualifierValue.Value;
					}
					else if (qualifierValue.Qualifier == CThermo.LLE)
					{
						KVALUE_LLE = qualifierValue.Value;
					}
					else if (qualifierValue.Qualifier == CThermo.VLLE)
					{
						KVALUE_VLLE = qualifierValue.Value;
					}
					else
					{
						KVALUE_VLE = qualifierValue.Value;
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.DIFFUSIVITY))
				{
					DIFFUSIVITY = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.ENTHALPY))
				{
					if (ENTHALPY == null)
					{
						ENTHALPY = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
					else
					{
						ENTHALPY2 = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.DENSITY))
				{
					if (DENSITY == null)
					{
						DENSITY = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
					else
					{
						DENSITY2 = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.CONDUCTIVITY))
				{
					if (CONDUCTIVITY == null)
					{
						CONDUCTIVITY = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
					else
					{
						CONDUCTIVITY2 = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.VISCOSITY))
				{
					if (VISCOSITY == null)
					{
						VISCOSITY = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
					else
					{
						VISCOSITY2 = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.ENTROPY))
				{
					if (ENTROPY == null)
					{
						ENTROPY = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
					else
					{
						ENTROPY2 = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.RVPMETHOD))
				{
					RVPMETHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.TVPMETHOD))
				{
					TVPMETHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.PHI))
				{
					PHI = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.HENRY))
				{
					HENRY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.TRANSPORT))
				{
					TRANSPORT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.FLPO))
				{
					FLPO = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseThermo")));
				bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}
}
public class P2ThomoSpecificWater
{
	public string DECANT { get; private set; }

	public string SOLUBILITY { get; private set; }

	public string PROPERTY { get; private set; }

	public string ENTHALPY { get; private set; }

	public P2ThomoSpecificWater(string sSource)
	{
		sSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
		string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(sSource);
		foreach (string text in array)
		{
			if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.DECANT))
			{
				DECANT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.SOLUBILITY))
			{
				SOLUBILITY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.PROPERTY))
			{
				PROPERTY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CThermo.ENTHALPY))
			{
				ENTHALPY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
		}
	}
}
public class P2Title : P2BaseObject
{
	public string Dimension { get; set; }

	public Dimensional DefaultDimensional { get; set; }

	public IList<StringValue> SpecialDefine { get; set; }

	public string RVPBasis { get; set; }

	public DoubleValue TVPBasis { get; set; }

	public P2Title(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		DefaultDimensional = Dimensional.English;
		try
		{
			SpecialDefine = new List<StringValue>();
			foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
			{
				if (!child.Name.StartsWith(CTitle.Dimension))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' '));
				if (array.Length != 0)
				{
					Dimension = array[0];
					switch (Dimension)
					{
					case "ENGLISH":
						DefaultDimensional = Dimensional.English;
						break;
					case "METRIC":
						DefaultDimensional = Dimensional.Metric;
						break;
					case "SI":
						DefaultDimensional = Dimensional.SI;
						break;
					}
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(array[i]);
						if (array2 != null && array2.Length == 2)
						{
							StringValue item = new StringValue
							{
								Name = array2[0],
								Value = array2[1]
							};
