							SpecialDefine.Add(item);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (!child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("CALC"))
					{
						continue;
					}
					string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' '));
					foreach (string text in array3)
					{
						if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("RVP"))
						{
							RVPBasis = text;
						}
						else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("TVP"))
						{
							TVPBasis = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(Categories.Title, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseTitle")));
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSTitle[] array = null;
		try
		{
			array = new HYSYSTitle[1]
			{
				new HYSYSTitle()
			};
			array[0].Dimension = Dimension;
			array[0].SpecialDefine = new List<StringValue>();
			array[0].SpecialDefine.AddRange(SpecialDefine);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertTitle")));
		}
		finally
		{
			foreach (VisualBaseNode child in base.VisualNode.Children)
			{
				child._ConvertResult = VisualBaseNode.ConvertResult.CR_PASSED;
			}
		}
		return array;
	}
}
public class P2UnitOperation : P2BaseObject
{
	public P2UnitOperation(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		base.Name = "P2UnitOperation";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.Children.Add(CreateUnitOperations(child));
		}
	}

	public P2BaseObject CreateUnitOperations(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLASH))
		{
			return new P2Flash(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PUMP))
		{
			return new P2Pump(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VALVE))
		{
			return new P2Valve(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MIXER))
		{
			return new P2Mixer(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COMPRESSOR))
		{
			return new P2Compressor(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EXPANDER))
		{
			return new P2Expander(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONVERSIONREACTOR))
		{
			return new P2ConversionReactor(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EQUILIBRIUMREACTOR))
		{
			return new P2EquilibriumReactor(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.GIBBSREACTOR))
		{
			return new P2GibbsReactor(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSTReactor))
		{
			return new P2CSTReactor(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RIGOROUSHEATEXCHANGER))
		{
			return new P2RigorousHeatExchanger(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLUGFLOW))
		{
			return new P2PlugFlowReactor(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIMPLEHEATEXCHANGER))
		{
			return new P2SimpleHeatExchanger(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LNGHEATEXCHANGER))
		{
			return new P2LNGHeatExchanger(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPLITTER))
		{
			return new P2Splitter(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FURNACE))
		{
			return new P2Furnace(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COLUMN) || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDESTRIPPER) || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDERECTIFIER))
		{
			return new P2RigorousDistillation(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PIPE))
		{
			return new P2Pipe(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.AIRCOOLEDHX))
		{
			return new P2AirCooledHeatExchanger(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHASE))
		{
			return new P2PhaseEnvelopeUtility(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BVLE))
		{
			return new P2BVLEUtility(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HYDRATE))
		{
			return new P2HydrateUtility(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STREAMCALCULATOR))
		{
			return new P2StreamCalculator(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEPRESSURE))
		{
			return new P2DEPRESSUREUtility(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HCURVE))
		{
			return new P2HCURVE(obj);
		}
		if (obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RESET))
		{
			return new P2Reset(obj);
		}
		if ((obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("BLEN") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("RESE") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("CREP") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("CAPE") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("MEMB") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("FUEL") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("DRYE") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("RFIL") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("FCEN") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DISSOLVER) || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CRYSTALLIZER) || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MELTER) || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("FREE") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("OPTI") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("HEXT") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("MVC") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("UAUO") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONTROL) || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("SHOR") || obj.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("CALC")) && obj is VisualBaseNode visualBaseNode)
		{
			visualBaseNode._ConvertResult = VisualBaseNode.ConvertResult.CR_IGNORED;
			visualBaseNode.Message = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UONotConverted"), obj.Name);
			if (string.Compare(ApplicationName.appName.Trim(), "HYSYS", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.Parse, WarningLevel.Warning, visualBaseNode.Message);
			}
		}
		return null;
	}
}
internal class P2RigorousDistillation : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class ColumnParameter
	{
		private int _MaxInteration;

		private double _ERRINC = 1.0;

		private double _DAMP = 0.2;

		private int _STOP = 5;

		private int _CAVE;

		private double _CUTOFF = 1E-08;

		private CVAROption _CVAR;

		public ColumnAlgorithmType ALGORITHM { get; set; }

		public int MaxInteration
		{
			get
			{
				return _MaxInteration;
			}
			set
			{
				_MaxInteration = value;
			}
		}

		public int StageNumber { get; set; }

		public double ERRINC
		{
			get
			{
				return _ERRINC;
			}
			set
			{
				_ERRINC = value;
			}
		}

		public double DAMP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DAMP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DAMP = value;
			}
		}

		public int STOP
		{
			get
			{
				return _STOP;
			}
			set
			{
				_STOP = value;
			}
		}

		public int CAVE
		{
			get
			{
				return _CAVE;
			}
			set
			{
				_CAVE = value;
			}
		}

		public string KEY { get; set; }

		public bool FREEWATER { get; set; }

		public double CUTOFF
		{
			get
			{
				return _CUTOFF;
			}
			set
			{
				_CUTOFF = value;
			}
		}

		public CVAROption CVAR
		{
			get
			{
				return _CVAR;
			}
			set
			{
				_CVAR = value;
			}
		}

		public int SEGMENTS { get; set; }

		public ColumnParameter()
		{
		}

		public ColumnParameter(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.IO,
				CUnitOperation.SURE,
				CUnitOperation.CHEMDIST,
				CUnitOperation.ENHANCEDIO,
				CUnitOperation.RATEFRAC,
				CUnitOperation.LLEX,
				CUnitOperation.ERRINC,
				CUnitOperation.DAMP,
				CUnitOperation.TRAY,
				CUnitOperation.STOP,
				CUnitOperation.CAVE,
				CUnitOperation.KEY,
				"FREE",
				CUnitOperation.CUTOFF,
				CUnitOperation.CVAR,
				CUnitOperation.SEGMENTS
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.IO))
				{
					ALGORITHM = ColumnAlgorithmType.IO;
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_MaxInteration = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SURE))
				{
					ALGORITHM = ColumnAlgorithmType.SURE;
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_MaxInteration = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CHEMDIST))
				{
					ALGORITHM = ColumnAlgorithmType.CHEMDIST;
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_MaxInteration = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ENHANCEDIO))
				{
					ALGORITHM = ColumnAlgorithmType.ENHANCEDIO;
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_MaxInteration = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATEFRAC))
				{
					ALGORITHM = ColumnAlgorithmType.RATEFRAC;
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_MaxInteration = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LLEX))
				{
					ALGORITHM = ColumnAlgorithmType.LLEX;
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_MaxInteration = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ERRINC))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_ERRINC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DAMP))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_DAMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TRAY))
				{
					StageNumber = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STOP))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_STOP = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CAVE))
				{
					_CAVE = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KEY))
				{
					KEY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FREEWATER))
				{
					FREEWATER = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CUTOFF))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						_CUTOFF = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CVAR))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						string text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim().ToUpper();
						if (text.StartsWith("LINE"))
						{
							_CVAR = CVAROption.LINEAR;
						}
						else if (text.StartsWith("ADAP"))
						{
							_CVAR = CVAROption.ADAPTIVE;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SEGMENTS))
				{
					SEGMENTS = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ColumnFeed
	{
		public string FeedID { get; set; }

		public int FeedTrayNumber { get; set; }

		public ColumnFeed()
		{
		}

		public ColumnFeed(string sSource)
		{
			string[] array = ((!sSource.Contains(",")) ? ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(sSource) : ParseUtilty.CutByComma(sSource));
			string strName = array[0].Trim();
			NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
			FeedID = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
			FeedTrayNumber = int.Parse(array[1].Trim());
		}
	}

	public class ColumnProduct
	{
		private string _PRODUCTSUPERCEDE = "ON";

		private int _StageNumber;

		public TrayFlowRate OVHD { get; set; }

		public string OVHDProductID { get; set; }

		public TrayFlowRate BTMS { get; set; }

		public string BTMSProductID { get; set; }

		public string PRODUCTSUPERCEDE
		{
			get
			{
				return _PRODUCTSUPERCEDE;
			}
			set
			{
				_PRODUCTSUPERCEDE = value;
			}
		}

		public Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> LDRAW { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> VDRAW { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> WATER { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> L1DRAW { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> L2DRAW { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> L1PART { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> L2PART { get; set; }

		public ColumnProduct()
		{
		}

		public ColumnProduct(string sSource, int stages, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			LDRAW = new Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate>();
			VDRAW = new Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate>();
			WATER = new Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate>();
			L1DRAW = new Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate>();
			L2DRAW = new Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate>();
			L1PART = new Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate>();
			L2PART = new Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate>();
			List<string> propertyList = new List<string>
			{
				"OVHD(",
				"OVHD=",
				"OVHD =",
				"BTMS(",
				"BTMS=",
				"LDRAW(",
				"LDRAW=",
				"LDRA(",
				"LDRA=",
				"VDRAW(",
				"VDRAW=",
				"VDRA(",
				"VDRA=",
				"L1DRAW(",
				"L1DRAW=",
				"L1DR(",
				"L1DR=",
				"L2DRAW(",
				"L2DRAW=",
				"L2DR(",
				"L2DR=",
				"WATER(",
				"WATER=",
				"WATE(",
				"WATE=",
				"L1PART(",
				"L1PART=",
				"L1PA(",
				"L1PA=",
				"L2PART(",
				"L2PART=",
				"L2PA(",
				"L2PA=",
				CUnitOperation.SUPERCEDE
			};
			_StageNumber = stages;
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(propertyList, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content.Trim(), ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				string strPhase = string.Empty;
				string flowBasis = "M";
				string unit = string.Empty;
				string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(strProperty);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parentheses))
				{
					GetQualifier(parentheses, ref strPhase, ref flowBasis, ref unit);
				}
				string text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				string empty = string.Empty;
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OVHD))
				{
					OVHD = new TrayFlowRate();
					OVHD.TrayNumber = 1;
					int num = text.IndexOf(",");
					double value = -1.0;
					if (num < 0)
					{
						string strName = text.Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						OVHDProductID = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					}
					else
					{
						string strName2 = text.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
						text = text.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						value = double.Parse(text);
						OVHDProductID = empty;
					}
					OVHD.FlowRate.Value = value;
					OVHD.FlowRate.Unit = unit;
					OVHD.FlowRate.Name = strPhase;
					OVHD.FlowRate.Type = flowBasis;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BTMS))
				{
					BTMS = new TrayFlowRate();
					BTMS.TrayNumber = _StageNumber;
					double value2 = -1.0;
					strPhase = "L";
					int num = text.IndexOf(",");
					if (num < 0)
					{
						string strName3 = text.Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
						BTMSProductID = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
					}
					else
					{
						string strName4 = text.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
						text = text.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						value2 = double.Parse(text);
						BTMSProductID = empty;
					}
					BTMS.FlowRate.Value = value2;
					BTMS.FlowRate.Unit = unit;
					BTMS.FlowRate.Name = strPhase;
					BTMS.FlowRate.Type = flowBasis;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LDRAW))
				{
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					strPhase = "L";
					string[] array2 = array;
					foreach (string obj in array2)
					{
						int num = obj.IndexOf(",");
						string strName5 = obj.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
						text = obj.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						if (obj.Contains("TOTAL"))
						{
							strPhase = "TL";
							text = text.Substring(0, text.LastIndexOf(",") - 1).Trim();
						}
						TrayFlowRate value3 = new TrayFlowRate(text, flowBasis, strPhase, unit);
						LDRAW.Add(empty, value3);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VDRAW))
				{
					string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					strPhase = "V";
					string[] array2 = array3;
					foreach (string obj2 in array2)
					{
						int num = obj2.IndexOf(",");
						string strName6 = obj2.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName6, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName6, bIsStream: true);
						text = obj2.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						if (obj2.Contains("TOTAL"))
						{
							strPhase = "TV";
							text = text.Substring(0, text.LastIndexOf(",") - 1).Trim();
						}
						TrayFlowRate value4 = new TrayFlowRate(text, flowBasis, strPhase, unit);
						VDRAW.Add(empty, value4);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WATER))
				{
					string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					strPhase = "W";
					string[] array2 = array4;
					foreach (string obj3 in array2)
					{
						int num = obj3.IndexOf(",");
						string strName7 = obj3.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName7, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName7, bIsStream: true);
						text = obj3.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						TrayFlowRate value5 = new TrayFlowRate(text, flowBasis, strPhase, unit);
						WATER.Add(empty, value5);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.L1DRAW))
				{
					string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					strPhase = "L1";
					string[] array2 = array5;
					foreach (string obj4 in array2)
					{
						int num = obj4.IndexOf(",");
						string strName8 = obj4.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName8, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName8, bIsStream: true);
						text = obj4.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						TrayFlowRate value6 = new TrayFlowRate(text, flowBasis, strPhase, unit);
						L1DRAW.Add(empty, value6);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.L2DRAW))
				{
					string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					strPhase = "L2";
					string[] array2 = array6;
					foreach (string obj5 in array2)
					{
						int num = obj5.IndexOf(",");
						string strName9 = obj5.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName9, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName9, bIsStream: true);
						text = obj5.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						TrayFlowRate value7 = new TrayFlowRate(text, flowBasis, strPhase, unit);
						L2DRAW.Add(empty, value7);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.L1PART))
				{
					string[] array7 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					strPhase = "L1";
					string[] array2 = array7;
					foreach (string obj6 in array2)
					{
						int num = obj6.IndexOf(",");
						string strName10 = obj6.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName10, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName10, bIsStream: true);
						text = obj6.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						TrayFlowRate value8 = new TrayFlowRate(text, flowBasis, strPhase, unit);
						L1PART.Add(empty, value8);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.L2PART))
				{
					string[] array8 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text);
					strPhase = "L2";
					string[] array2 = array8;
					foreach (string obj7 in array2)
					{
						int num = obj7.IndexOf(",");
						string strName11 = obj7.Substring(0, num).Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName11, bIsStream: true);
						empty = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName11, bIsStream: true);
						text = obj7.Substring(num + 1).Trim();
						TrayFlowRate value9 = new TrayFlowRate(text, flowBasis, strPhase, unit);
						L2PART.Add(empty, value9);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SUPERCEDE))
				{
					_PRODUCTSUPERCEDE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}

		private void GetQualifier(string strQual, ref string strPhase, ref string flowBasis, ref string unit)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(strQual);
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "L1")
				{
					strPhase = "L1";
				}
				else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "L2")
				{
					strPhase = "L2";
				}
				else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "L")
				{
					strPhase = "L";
				}
				else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "M")
				{
					flowBasis = "M";
				}
				else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "WT")
				{
					flowBasis = "WT";
				}
				else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "LV")
				{
					flowBasis = "LV";
				}
				else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "GV")
				{
					flowBasis = "GV";
				}
				else
				{
					unit = text.Trim();
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class TrayFlowRate
	{
		public TypeUnitValue FlowRate { get; set; }

		public int TrayNumber { get; set; }

		public TrayFlowRate()
		{
			FlowRate = new TypeUnitValue();
		}

		public TrayFlowRate(string sSource, string flowBase, string phase, string unit)
		{
			FlowRate = new TypeUnitValue();
			FlowRate.Name = phase;
			FlowRate.Type = flowBase;
			FlowRate.Unit = unit;
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(sSource);
			if (array.Length > 1)
			{
				TrayNumber = int.Parse(array[0].Trim());
				FlowRate.Value = double.Parse(array[1].Trim());
			}
			else if (array.Length == 1)
			{
				TrayNumber = int.Parse(array[0].Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	public class ColumnPseudoProduct
	{
		public int SourceTrayNumber { get; set; }

		public int TargetTrayNumber { get; set; }

		public QualifierValue PseudoStream { get; set; }

		public ColumnPseudoProduct()
		{
		}

		public ColumnPseudoProduct(string qual, string strContent, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			SetValue(qual, strContent, ref bParseError);
		}

		private void SetValue(string qual, string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(strProperty);
				PseudoStream = new QualifierValue(qual, array[0]);
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					SourceTrayNumber = int.Parse(array[1]);
				}
				else if (array.Length == 3)
				{
					SourceTrayNumber = int.Parse(array[1]);
					TargetTrayNumber = int.Parse(array[2]);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ColumnCondenser
	{
		public CondenserType TYPE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TESTIMATE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DT { get; set; }

		public ColumnCondenser()
		{
		}

		public ColumnCondenser(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.TYPE,
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.DT + "="
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TYPE))
				{
					if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper().Trim()
						.StartsWith("PART"))
					{
						TYPE = CondenserType.PART;
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper().Trim()
						.StartsWith("MIX"))
					{
						TYPE = CondenserType.MIXED;
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper().Trim()
						.StartsWith("BUBB"))
					{
						TYPE = CondenserType.BUBB;
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper().Trim()
						.StartsWith("TFIX"))
					{
						TYPE = CondenserType.TFIX;
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper().Trim()
						.StartsWith("DTB"))
					{
						TYPE = CondenserType.DTBB;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
				{
					PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
				{
					TESTIMATE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
				{
					TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT))
				{
					DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ColumnDuty
	{
		public int ID { get; set; }

		public int TrayNumber { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DutyValue { get; set; }

		public string Name { get; set; }

		public string Phase { get; set; }

		public ColumnDuty()
		{
		}

		public ColumnDuty(string sSource, string uom, string phase, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(phase))
				{
					Phase = phase;
				}
				string[] array = sSource.Split(',');
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					if (array[i].Trim().Contains(' '))
					{
						string[] array2 = array[i].Trim().Split(' ');
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array2[j]))
							{
								list.Add(array2[j].Trim());
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						list.Add(array[i].Trim());
					}
				}
				switch (list.Count)
				{
				case 1:
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[0].Trim()))
					{
						ID = int.Parse(list[0].Trim());
					}
					break;
				case 2:
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[0].Trim()))
					{
						ID = int.Parse(list[0].Trim());
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[1].Trim()))
					{
						TrayNumber = int.Parse(list[1].Trim());
					}
					break;
				case 3:
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[0].Trim()))
					{
						ID = int.Parse(list[0].Trim());
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[1].Trim()))
					{
						TrayNumber = int.Parse(list[1].Trim());
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[2].Trim()))
					{
						DutyValue = new DoubleValue(uom, double.Parse(list[2].Trim()));
					}
					break;
				case 4:
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[0].Trim()))
					{
						ID = int.Parse(list[0].Trim());
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[1].Trim()))
					{
						TrayNumber = int.Parse(list[1].Trim());
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[2].Trim()))
					{
						DutyValue = new DoubleValue(uom, double.Parse(list[2].Trim()));
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list[3].Trim()))
					{
						Name = list[3].Trim();
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ColumnReboiler
	{
		private ReboilerType _TYPE;

		public ReboilerType TYPE
		{
			get
			{
				return _TYPE;
			}
			set
			{
				_TYPE = value;
			}
		}

		public bool HasBaffle { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue RATE { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue LFRAC { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue VFRAC { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DT { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue LFEST { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue VFEST { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue RTEST { get; set; }

		public ColumnReboiler()
		{
		}

		public ColumnReboiler(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.TYPE,
				CUnitOperation.BAFFLE,
				CUnitOperation.RATE,
				CUnitOperation.LFRAC,
				CUnitOperation.VFRAC,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.DT,
				CUnitOperation.LFEST,
				CUnitOperation.VFEST,
				CUnitOperation.RTEST
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TYPE))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("=") && !ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).StartsWith("KETT"))
					{
						_TYPE = ReboilerType.THERMOSIPHON;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BAFFLE))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("=") && ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).StartsWith("YES"))
					{
						HasBaffle = true;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATE))
				{
					RATE = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(RATE.Type.ToString()))
					{
						RATE.Type = "M";
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LFRAC))
				{
					LFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(LFRAC.Type.ToString()))
					{
						LFRAC.Type = "M";
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VFRAC))
				{
					VFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(VFRAC.Type.ToString()))
					{
						VFRAC.Type = "M";
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
				{
					TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT))
				{
					DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LFEST))
				{
					LFEST = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(LFEST.Type.ToString()))
					{
						LFEST.Type = "M";
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VFEST))
				{
					VFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(VFEST.Type.ToString()))
					{
						VFEST.Type = "M";
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RTEST))
				{
					RTEST = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					RTEST.Type = "M";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ColumnHeatLossGain
	{
		public List<QSpecification> QSPEC { get; set; }

		public QColumn QCOLUMN { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue QTRAY { get; set; }

		public ColumnHeatLossGain()
		{
			QSPEC = new List<QSpecification>();
		}

		public ColumnHeatLossGain(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				QSPEC = new List<QSpecification>();
				if (sSource.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QTRAY))
				{
					QTRAY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(sSource);
				}
				else if (sSource.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QSPEC))
				{
					string head = ParseUtilty.GetHead(sSource.Trim(), ' ');
					string unit = string.Empty;
					if (head.Contains("("))
					{
						unit = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head);
					}
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' '));
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						QSpecification item = new QSpecification(array[i], unit);
						QSPEC.Add(item);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					string sSource2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
					QCOLUMN = new QColumn(sSource2);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class QColumn

		public DoubleValue QCOL { get; set; }

		public QColumn()

			{

				CUnitOperation.QREBO,
				CUnitOperation.QCOL

			}

		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QCOND))
			{
				QCondenser = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QREBO))
			{
				QReboiler = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QCOL))
			{
				QCOL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
		}
	}

	public class ColumnPumpAroundAndBypass
	{
		private string _PHASE = "L";

		public int paId { get; set; }

		public string NAME { get; set; }

		public string DNAME { get; set; }

		public int SourceTray { get; set; }

		public int TargetTray { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public string PHASE
		{
			get
			{
				return _PHASE;
			}
			set
			{
				_PHASE = value;
			}
		}

		public TypeUnitValue RATE { get; set; }

		public bool AllPumpAround { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue LFRAC { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DT { get; set; }

		public ColumnPumpAroundAndBypass()
		{
		}

		public ColumnPumpAroundAndBypass(string sSource, ref bool bParseError, int index)
		{
			paId = index;
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.FROM + "=",
				CUnitOperation.TO + "=",
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.PHASE,
				CUnitOperation.RATE,
				CUnitOperation.TOTAL,
				CUnitOperation.LFRAC,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.DT + "=",
				CUnitOperation.NAME,
				"DNAME"
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NAME))
				{
					NAME = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DNAME))
				{
					DNAME = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FROM))
				{
					SourceTray = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				}
				else if (strProperty.Replace(" ", "").StartsWith("TO="))
				{
					TargetTray = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
				{
					PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHASE))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						PHASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATE))
				{
					RATE = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(RATE.Type))
					{
						RATE.Type = "M";
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TOTAL))
				{
					AllPumpAround = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LFRAC))
				{
					LFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(strProperty);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(LFRAC.Type))
					{
						LFRAC.Type = "M";
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
				{
					TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT))
				{
					DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class PressureProfile
	{
		public List<TrayPressure> PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public PSpecification PSPEC { get; set; }

		public PressureProfile()
		{
		}

		public PressureProfile(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (sSource.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
				{
					PRESSURE = new List<TrayPressure>();
					string head = ParseUtilty.GetHead(sSource, ' ');
					string unit = string.Empty;
					if (head.Contains("("))
					{
						unit = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head);
					}
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' '));
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						TrayPressure item = new TrayPressure(array[i], unit);
						PRESSURE.Add(item);
					}
				}
				else if (sSource.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PSPEC))
				{
					string sSource2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(sSource, ' ');
					PSPEC = new PSpecification(sSource2, ref bParseError);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class TBPCutpointSpecification
	{
		public string Stream { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue CutPoint { get; set; }

		public string ReferenceStream { get; set; }

		public List<string> PORDER { get; set; }

		public bool bGlobalSpec { get; set; }

		public TBPCutpointSpecification()
		{
		}

		public TBPCutpointSpecification(string SpecExpress, string ID, List<string> Feeds, List<string> Products, List<string> SubBlocks, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			PORDER = new List<string>();
			CutPoint = new DoubleValue();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CStream.STREAM,
				CComponent.CUTPOINTS,
				CUnitOperation.REFSTRM,
				CUnitOperation.VALUE
			}, SpecExpress))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
			bGlobalSpec = GlobalSpec(ReferenceStream, ID, Feeds, Products, SubBlocks);
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CStream.STREAM))
				{
					Stream = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CComponent.CUTPOINTS))
				{
					CutPoint = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REFSTRM))
				{
					ReferenceStream = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VALUE))
				{
					CutPoint.Value = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}

		private bool GlobalSpec(string strExpress, string ID, List<string> Feeds, List<string> Products, List<string> SubBlocks)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(strExpress))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (strExpress.Contains("CALC=") || strExpress.Contains("CALCULATOR="))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (strExpress != ID && !Feeds.Contains(strExpress) && !Products.Contains(strExpress) && !SubBlocks.Contains(strExpress))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	public class PSpecification
	{
		public DoubleValue PTOP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DPCOL { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DPTRAY { get; set; }

		public PSpecification()
		{
		}

		public PSpecification(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				"TOP=",
				CUnitOperation.PTOP + "=",
				CUnitOperation.DPCOL,
				CUnitOperation.DPTRAY,
				"DP"
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PTOP) || strProperty.StartsWith("TOP"))
				{
					PTOP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPCOL))
				{
					DPCOL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPTRAY))
				{
					DPTRAY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DP="))
				{
					DPTRAY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class InitialEstimateGenerator
	{
		private EstimateModel _MODEL;

		public EstimateModel MODEL
		{
			get
			{
				return _MODEL;
			}
			set
			{
				_MODEL = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue CTEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TTEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue BTEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue RTEMP { get; set; }

		public QualifierValue RATIO { get; set; }

		public QualifierValue REFLUX { get; set; }

		public InitialEstimateGenerator()
		{
		}

		public InitialEstimateGenerator(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.MODEL,
				"CONV",
				"REFI",
				"CHEM",
				CUnitOperation.CTEMP,
				CUnitOperation.TTEMP,
				CUnitOperation.BTEMP,
				CUnitOperation.RTEMP,
				CUnitOperation.RRATIO,
				CUnitOperation.REFLUX
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MODEL))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
						if (stringValue.StartsWith("CONV"))
						{
							_MODEL = EstimateModel.CONV;
						}
						else if (stringValue.StartsWith("REFI"))
						{
							_MODEL = EstimateModel.REFIN;
						}
						else if (stringValue.StartsWith("CHEM"))
						{
							_MODEL = EstimateModel.CHEM;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CTEMP))
				{
					CTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TTEMP))
				{
					TTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BTEMP))
				{
					BTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RTEMP))
				{
					RTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RRATIO))
				{
					RATIO = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REFLUX))
				{
					REFLUX = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class TrayComposition
	{
		public int TrayNumber { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<int, double> composition { get; set; }

		public string Phase { get; set; }

		public TrayComposition()
		{
			composition = new Dictionary<int, double>();
		}

		public TrayComposition(string sSource, string phase)
		{
			composition = new Dictionary<int, double>();
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(sSource);
			Phase = phase;
			TrayNumber = int.Parse(array[0].Trim());
			for (int i = 1; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				composition.Add(i, double.Parse(array[i].Trim()));
			}
		}
	}

	public class SpecificationVariable
	{
		public List<string> DUTY { get; set; }

		public List<string> DNAME { get; set; }

		public List<string> DRAW { get; set; }

		public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

		public SpecificationVariable()
		{
		}

		public SpecificationVariable(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			DUTY = new List<string>();
			DNAME = new List<string>();
			DRAW = new List<string>();
			FEED = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				"DUTY=",
				"HEAT=",
				CUnitOperation.DNAME,
				"DRAW=",
				"FEED="
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				try
				{
					if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) || strProperty.StartsWith("HEAT"))
					{
						DUTY.Add(text.Trim());
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DNAME))
					{
						DNAME.Add(text.Trim());
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DRAW))
					{
						DRAW.Add(text.Trim());
					}
					else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
					{
						FEED.Add(text.Trim());
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					bParseError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class TrayEfficiency
	{
		public EfficiencyType TYPE { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<int, double> EFFData { get; set; }

		public TrayEfficiency()
		{
		}

		public TrayEfficiency(string sSource, string typeEff)
		{
			if (typeEff.StartsWith("MURP"))
			{
				TYPE = EfficiencyType.MURPHREE;
			}
			else if (typeEff.StartsWith("EQUI"))
			{
				TYPE = EfficiencyType.EQUILIBRIUM;
			}
			else if (typeEff.StartsWith("VAPO"))
			{
				TYPE = EfficiencyType.VAPORIZATION;
			}
			EFFData = new Dictionary<int, double>();
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(sSource);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array[i].Trim());
				EFFData.Add(int.Parse(array2[0].Trim()), double.Parse(array2[1].Trim()));
			}
		}
	}

	public class ComponentEfficiency
	{
		public EfficiencyType TYPE { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<int, Dictionary<int, double>> EFFData { get; set; }

		public ComponentEfficiency()
		{
		}

		public ComponentEfficiency(string sSource, string typeEff)
		{
			if (typeEff.StartsWith("MURP"))
			{
				TYPE = EfficiencyType.MURPHREE;
			}
			else if (typeEff.StartsWith("EQUI"))
			{
				TYPE = EfficiencyType.EQUILIBRIUM;
			}
			else if (typeEff.StartsWith("VAPO"))
			{
				TYPE = EfficiencyType.VAPORIZATION;
			}
			EFFData = new Dictionary<int, Dictionary<int, double>>();
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(sSource);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array[i].Trim());
				Dictionary<int, double> value = new Dictionary<int, double> { 
				{
					int.Parse(array2[1].Trim()),
					double.Parse(array2[2].Trim())
				} };
				EFFData.Add(int.Parse(array2[0].Trim()), value);
			}
		}
	}

	public class HomotopyAnalysis
	{
		private int _ITERATIONS = 10;

		private int _HVARYFLAG;

		public int SPECIFICATION { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue INITIAL { get; set; }

		public int ITERATIONS
		{
			get
			{
				return _ITERATIONS;
			}
			set
			{
				_ITERATIONS = value;
			}
		}

		public int HVARYFLAG
		{
			get
			{
				return _HVARYFLAG;
			}
			set
			{
				_HVARYFLAG = value;
			}
		}

		public HomotopyAnalysis()
		{
		}

		public HomotopyAnalysis(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.INITIAL,
				CUnitOperation.ITERATIONS,
				CUnitOperation.HVARYFLAG,
				CUnitOperation.SPECIFICATION
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.INITIAL))
			{
				INITIAL = new DoubleValue("", double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim()));
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ITERATIONS))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					_ITERATIONS = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HVARYFLAG))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					_HVARYFLAG = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPECIFICATION))
			{
				SPECIFICATION = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
			}
			else
			{
				SPECIFICATION = int.Parse(strProperty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	public class PrintOption
	{
		private ITERReportLevel _ITER = ITERReportLevel.PARTIAL;

		private PropertyReportLevel _PROPTABLES = PropertyReportLevel.PART;

		private ProfileReportLevel _PROFILE;

		public ITERReportLevel ITER
		{
			get
			{
				return _ITER;
			}
			set
			{
				_ITER = value;
			}
		}

		public PropertyReportLevel PROPTABLES
		{
			get
			{
				return _PROPTABLES;
			}
			set
			{
				_PROPTABLES = value;
			}
		}

		public bool bPrintRecovery { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintTEFF { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintTLoad { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintDiagram { get; set; }

		public ProfileReportLevel PROFILE
		{
			get
			{
				return _PROFILE;
			}
			set
			{
				_PROFILE = value;
			}
		}

		public bool bPrintCompositionMBasis { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintCompositionWTBasis { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintCompositionLVBasis { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintCompositionGVBasis { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintKValue { get; set; }

		public ComponentPair LightKeyComp { get; set; }

		public ComponentPair HeavyKeyComp { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintSummaryOnWTBasis { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintSummaryOnLVBasis { get; set; }

		public bool bPrintSummaryOnGVBasis { get; set; }

		public bool bGenerateFRIData { get; set; }

		public PrintOption()
		{
		}

		public PrintOption(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.ITER,
				CUnitOperation.PROPTABLES,
				CUnitOperation.RECOVERY,
				CUnitOperation.TEFF,
				CUnitOperation.TLOAD,
				CUnitOperation.DIAGRAM,
				CUnitOperation.PROFILE,
				CUnitOperation.COMPONENT,
				CThermo.KVALUE,
				CUnitOperation.KEYL,
				CUnitOperation.KEYH,
				CUnitOperation.SUMMARY,
				CUnitOperation.FRIDATA
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ITER))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						string text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper();
						if (text.StartsWith("NONE"))
						{
							_ITER = ITERReportLevel.NONE;
						}
						else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ESTIMATE))
						{
							_ITER = ITERReportLevel.ESTIMATE;
						}
						else if (text.StartsWith("ALL"))
						{
							_ITER = ITERReportLevel.ALL;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PROPTABLES))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						string text2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper();
						if (text2.StartsWith("BRIE"))
						{
							_PROPTABLES = PropertyReportLevel.BRIEF;
						}
						else if (text2.StartsWith("ALL"))
						{
							_PROPTABLES = PropertyReportLevel.ALL;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RECOVERY))
				{
					bPrintRecovery = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEFF))
				{
					bPrintTEFF = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TLOAD))
				{
					bPrintTLoad = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DIAGRAM))
				{
					bPrintDiagram = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PROFILE))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains("="))
					{
						string text3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper();
						if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ESTIMATE))
						{
							_PROFILE = ProfileReportLevel.ESTIMATE;
						}
						else if (text3.StartsWith("ALL"))
						{
							_PROFILE = ProfileReportLevel.ALL;
						}
						else if (text3.StartsWith("FINA"))
						{
							_PROFILE = ProfileReportLevel.FINAL;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COMPONENT))
				{
					switch (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper())
					{
					case "M":
						bPrintCompositionMBasis = true;
						break;
					case "WT":
						bPrintCompositionWTBasis = true;
						break;
					case "LV":
						bPrintCompositionLVBasis = true;
						break;
					case "GV":
						bPrintCompositionGVBasis = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CThermo.KVALUE))
				{
					bPrintKValue = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KEYL))
				{
					LightKeyComp = new ComponentPair();
					string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(stringValue);
					if (array.Length < 2)
					{
						array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(stringValue);
					}
					if (array.Length == 1)
					{
						LightKeyComp.StartComponent = int.Parse(array[0]);
						LightKeyComp.EndComponent = int.Parse(array[0]);
					}
					else if (array.Length == 2)
					{
						LightKeyComp.StartComponent = int.Parse(array[0]);
						LightKeyComp.EndComponent = int.Parse(array[1]);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KEYH))
				{
					HeavyKeyComp = new ComponentPair();
					string stringValue2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(stringValue2);
					if (array2.Length < 2)
					{
						array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(stringValue2);
					}
					if (array2.Length == 1)
					{
						HeavyKeyComp.StartComponent = int.Parse(array2[0]);
						HeavyKeyComp.EndComponent = int.Parse(array2[0]);
					}
					else if (array2.Length == 2)
					{
						HeavyKeyComp.StartComponent = int.Parse(array2[0]);
						HeavyKeyComp.EndComponent = int.Parse(array2[1]);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SUMMARY))
				{
					switch (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).ToUpper())
					{
					case "WT":
						bPrintSummaryOnWTBasis = true;
						break;
					case "LV":
						bPrintSummaryOnLVBasis = true;
						break;
					case "GV":
						bPrintSummaryOnGVBasis = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRIDATA))
				{
					bGenerateFRIData = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

		{

			{

				CUnitOperation.RFCALC,
				CUnitOperation.SPLITFRAC,
				CUnitOperation.BULK,
				CUnitOperation.HETP,

				CUnitOperation.MTRATE,
				CUnitOperation.DCOEF,
				CUnitOperation.HTCOEF,
				CUnitOperation.MTCOEF
			}, sSource))
			{
				if (item.Content.Contains("="))
				{
					SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RFIEG))
				{
					_RFIEG = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RFCALC))
				{
					_RFCALC = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPLITFRAC))
				{
					_SPLITFRAC = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BULK))
				{
					_BULK = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HETP))
				{
					_HETP = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BUBBLE))
				{
					_BUBB = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEW))
				{
					_DEW = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.INTERFACE))
				{
					_INTERFACE = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPFLOOD))
				{
					_DPFLOOD = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTRATE))
				{
					_HTRATE = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MTRATE))
				{
					_MTRATE = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DCOEF))
				{
					_DCOFF = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTCOEF))
				{
					_HTCOEF = true;
				}

				{
					_MTCOEF = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class PlotResult
	{
		public bool bPlotProfile { get; set; }

		public List<ComponentPair> XCOMP { get; set; }

		public List<ComponentPair> YCOMP { get; set; }

		public bool bPlotLogComposition { get; set; }

		public bool bPlotSFactor { get; set; }

		public PlotResult()
		{
		}

		public PlotResult(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			XCOMP = new List<ComponentPair>();
			YCOMP = new List<ComponentPair>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.PROFILE,
				CUnitOperation.XCOMP,
				CUnitOperation.YCOMP,
				CUnitOperation.LOG,
				CUnitOperation.SFACTOR
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PROFILE))
				{
					bPlotProfile = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.XCOMP))
				{
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						ComponentPair item = new ComponentPair(array[i]);
						XCOMP.Add(item);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.YCOMP))
				{
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						ComponentPair item2 = new ComponentPair(array[i]);
						YCOMP.Add(item2);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LOG))
				{
					bPlotLogComposition = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SFACTOR))
				{
					bPlotSFactor = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ComponentPair
	{
		public int StartComponent { get; set; }

		public int EndComponent { get; set; }

		public ComponentPair()
		{
		}

		public ComponentPair(string sSource)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(sSource);
			if (array.Length != 0)
			{
				StartComponent = int.Parse(array[0].Trim());
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					EndComponent = int.Parse(array[1].Trim());
				}
				else
				{
					EndComponent = int.Parse(array[0].Trim());
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class TraySizing : TrayCommon
	{
		private TrayType _TYPE;

		private DoubleValue _DMIN = new DoubleValue("IN", 15.0);

		private double _FFACTOR = 100.0;

		public TrayType TYPE
		{
			get
			{
				return _TYPE;
			}
			set
			{
				_TYPE = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DMIN
		{
			get
			{
				return _DMIN;
			}
			set
			{
				_DMIN = value;
			}
		}

		public string BASESEG { get; set; }

		public double FFACTOR
		{
			get
			{
				return _FFACTOR;
			}
			set
			{
				_FFACTOR = value;
			}
		}

		public TraySizing()
		{
		}

		public TraySizing(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			ParseSection(ref sSource);
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.VALVE,
				CUnitOperation.SIEVE,
				CUnitOperation.CAP,
				CUnitOperation.DMIN,
				CUnitOperation.BASESEGMENT,
				"FF",
				"SPAC=",
				"SPACING=",
				"SPACING(",
				"SPAC(",
				CUnitOperation.V1,
				CUnitOperation.V4,
				"PASS",
				"DPCA",
				"SF"
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetSpecialValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
			setDependentDefaultValue();
		}

		private void SetSpecialValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VALVE))
				{
					_TYPE = TrayType.VALVE;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIEVE))
				{
					_TYPE = TrayType.SIEVE;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CAP))
				{
					_TYPE = TrayType.CAP;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DMIN))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						_DMIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BASESEGMENT))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						BASESEG = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith("FF"))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						_FFACTOR = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else
				{
					SetValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}

		private void setDependentDefaultValue()
		{
		}
	}

	public class TrayRating : TrayCommon
	{

		private string _DCWExpress = string.Empty;

		public string USID { get; set; }

		public string CASE { get; set; }

		public TrayRating()
		{
		}

		public TrayRating(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			DCW = new Dictionary<int, DoubleValue>();
			ParseSection(ref sSource);
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				"DCW",
				"VALVE,",
				"VALV,",
				"VALVE ",
				"VALV ",
				"V1 ",
				"V1,",
				"V4 ",
				"V4,",
				"SIEVE ",
				"SIEVE,",
				"SIEV ",
				"SIEV,",
				"CAP ",
				"CAP,",
				"PASS",
				"SPAC=",
				"SPACE=",
				"SPACING=",
				"SPAC(",
				"SPACE(",
				"SPACING(",
				"SPACING(T",
				"SPAC(T",
				"SPACE(T",
				"SPACING(CAP,",
				"SPAC(CAP,",
				"SPACE(CAP,",
				"DPCA",
				"DIAM=",
				"DIAMETER=",
				"DIAM(",
				"DIAMETER(",
				"DIAM(T",
				"DIAM(VALVE,",
				"DIAM(VALV,",
				"DIAM(SIEVE,",
				"DIAM(SIEVEHOLE,",
				"DIAM(SIEV,",
				"DIAM(CAP,",
				"DIAMETER(T",
				"DIAMETER(VALVE,",
				"DIAMETER(VALV,",
				"DIAMETER(SIEVE,",
				"DIAMETER(SIEVEHOLE,",
				"DIAMETER(SIEV,",
				"DIAMETER(CAP,",
				"THICKNESS(D",
				"THICKNESS(DECK)",
				"THICKNESS(V",
				"THICKNESS(VALVE)",
				"THICKNESS(VALVE,",
				"THICKNESS(DECK,",
				"THICKNESS(VALV,",
				"THICKNESS(",
				"THIC(D",
				"THIC(",
				"THIC(VALVE,",
				"THIC(VALV,",
				"THICK(D",
				"THICK(",
				"THICK(VALVE,",
				"THICK(VALV,",
				CUnitOperation.NUMBER,
				"SF",
				CUnitOperation.FLAPPROACH,
				"HOLEAREA=",
				"HOLE=",
				"HOLEAREA(",
				"HOLE(",
				CUnitOperation.MATERIAL,
				CUnitOperation.WEIRHEIGHT,
				CUnitOperation.DCC,
				CUnitOperation.USID,
				CUnitOperation.CASE
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetSpecialValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
			setDependentDefaultValue();
			transferThicknessfromGauge(ref _DECKThickness);
			transferThicknessfromGauge(ref _VALVETHICKNESS);
			setDCWValue();
		}

		private void SetSpecialValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DIAMETER))
				{
					string unit = string.Empty;
					bool flag = true;
					string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(strProperty);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parentheses))
					{
						string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(parentheses.Trim());
						foreach (string text in array)
						{
							if (text.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VAL") || text.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("V1") || text.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("V4") || text.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SIE") || text.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CAP"))
							{
								flag = false;
							}
							else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() != "TRAY")
							{
								unit = text.Trim();
							}
						}
					}
					double val = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					if (flag)
					{
						TrayDiameter = new TypeUnitValue(unit, "", val);
					}
					else
					{
						InternalDiameter = new TypeUnitValue(unit, "", val);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.THICKNESS))
				{
					if (!strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						return;
					}
					string unit2 = string.Empty;
					bool flag2 = false;
					string parentheses2 = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(strProperty);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parentheses2))
					{
						string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(parentheses2.Trim());
						foreach (string text2 in array)
						{
							if (text2.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VAL"))
							{
								flag2 = true;
							}
							else if (!text2.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DEC"))
							{
								unit2 = text2.Trim();
							}
						}
					}
					double val2 = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					if (flag2)
					{
						VALVETHICKNESS = new TypeUnitValue(unit2, "", val2);
					}
					else
					{
						DECKTHICKNESS = new TypeUnitValue(unit2, "", val2);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DCW))
				{
					_DCWExpress = strProperty.Trim();
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NUMBER))
				{
					NUMBER = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPACING) && strProperty.Contains("CAP"))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(strProperty).Trim());
						string unit3 = string.Empty;
						if (array2.Length == 2)
						{
							unit3 = ((!(array2[0].ToUpper().Trim() != "CAP")) ? array2[1] : array2[0]);
						}
						double val3 = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
						CAPSPACING = new DoubleValue(unit3, val3);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLAPPROACH))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						FLAPPROACH = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HOLEAREA))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						_HOLEAREA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MATERIAL))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						_MATERIAL = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(strProperty);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WEIRHEIGHT))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						_WEIR = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DCC))
				{
					DCC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.USID))
				{
					USID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CASE))
				{
					CASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else
				{
					switch (strProperty)
					{
					case "VALVE":
						_TYPE = TrayType.VALVE;
						break;
					case "V1":
						_TYPE = TrayType.V1;
						break;
					case "V4":
						_TYPE = TrayType.V4;
						break;
					case "SIEVE":
						_TYPE = TrayType.SIEVE;
						break;
					case "CAP":
						_TYPE = TrayType.CAP;
						break;
					default:
						SetValue(strProperty);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}

		private void setDCWValue()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_DCWExpress))
			{
				return;
			}
			string head = ParseUtilty.GetHead(_DCWExpress, '=');
			string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(_DCWExpress);
			string unit = string.Empty;
			if (head.Contains("("))
			{
				unit = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head).Trim();
			}
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(stringValue);
			int pASSES = base.PASSES;
			if (array.Length <= 2 && pASSES == 1)
			{
				for (int i = 1; i <= array.Length; i++)
				{
					string text = array[i - 1];
					DoubleValue value = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(text.Trim()));
					DCW.Add((i - 1) * 4 + 1, value);
				}
				return;
			}
			switch (pASSES)
			{
			case 2:
			{
				for (int m = 1; m <= array.Length; m++)
				{
					string text5 = array[m - 1];
					DoubleValue value5 = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(text5.Trim()));
					int num3 = ((m > 2) ? 2 : 0);
					DCW.Add(m + num3, value5);
				}
				break;
			}
			case 3:
			{
				for (int k = 1; k <= array.Length; k++)
				{
					string text3 = array[k - 1];
					DoubleValue value3 = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(text3.Trim()));
					DCW.Add(k * 2 - 1, value3);
				}
				break;
			}
			case 4:
			{
				for (int l = 1; l <= array.Length; l++)
				{
					string text4 = array[l - 1];
					DoubleValue value4 = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(text4.Trim()));
					int num2 = ((l > 3) ? 1 : 0);
					DCW.Add(l + num2, value4);
				}
				break;
			}
			case 5:
			{
				for (int j = 1; j <= array.Length; j++)
				{
					string text2 = array[j - 1];
					DoubleValue value2 = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(text2.Trim()));
					int num = 0;
					if (j > 1 && j < 5)
					{
						num = 1;
					}
					else if (j >= 5)
					{
						num = 2;
					}
					DCW.Add(j + num, value2);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		private void setDependentDefaultValue()
		{
			_ = TYPE;
			_ = 2;
		}

		private void transferThicknessfromGauge(ref TypeUnitValue ThickValue)
		{
			if (ThickValue.Unit.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("GAUG"))
			{
				double value = ThickValue.Value;
				if (value == 3.0)
				{
					ThickValue.Value = 0.25;
				}
				else if (value == 10.0)
				{
					ThickValue.Value = 0.134;
				}
				else if (value == 12.0)
				{
					ThickValue.Value = 0.104;
				}
				else if (value == 14.0)
				{
					ThickValue.Value = 0.074;
				}
				else if (value == 16.0)
				{
					ThickValue.Value = 0.06;
				}
				else if (value == 18.0)
				{
					ThickValue.Value = 0.05;
				}
				else if (value == 20.0)
				{
					ThickValue.Value = 0.037;
				}
				ThickValue.Unit = "IN";
			}
		}
	}

	public class Packing : Hydraulics
	{

		public Packing(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{

			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.TYPE,
				CUnitOperation.SIZE,
				CUnitOperation.FACTOR,
				CUnitOperation.HEIGHT,
				CUnitOperation.HETP,

				CUnitOperation.CSMAX,
