				CUnitOperation.CASE,
				CUnitOperation.ESTDIAM,
				CUnitOperation.BASESEGMENT,
				CUnitOperation.VOIDFRACTION,
				CUnitOperation.LIQMIX,
				CUnitOperation.VAPMIX,
				CUnitOperation.CSURFACETENSION,
				CUnitOperation.SULZER,
				CUnitOperation.FLEXE,
				CUnitOperation.FLEXI,
				CUnitOperation.INTALOX,
				CUnitOperation.KGWIREGAUZE,
				CUnitOperation.CAPACITY
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TYPE))
				{
					TYPE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIZE))
				{
					SIZE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FACTOR))
				{
					FACTOR = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HEIGHT))
				{
					HEIGHT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HETP))
				{
					HETP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HMETHOD))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						_HMETHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPMETHOD))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						_DPMETHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPSECTION))
				{
					DPSECTION = ParseUtilty.GetTwoUnitsDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPPACK))
				{
					DPPACK = ParseUtilty.GetTwoUnitsDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DIAMETER))
				{
					DIAMETER = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLOOD))
				{
					FLOOD = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					_bFLMETHOD = false;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DESIGN))
				{
					DesignVelocity = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					_bFLMETHOD = false;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLAPPROACH))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						FLAPPROACH = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
						_bFLMETHOD = false;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSMETHOD))
				{
					CSMETHOD = true;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSMAX))
				{
					CSMAX = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSDESIGN))
				{
					CSDESIGN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSAPPROACH))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						CSAPPROACH = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.USID))
				{
					USID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CASE))
				{
					CASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ESTDIAM))
				{
					ESTDIAM = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BASESEGMENT))
				{
					BASESEG = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VOIDFRACTION))
				{
					VOIDFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LIQMIX))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						string text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim().ToUpper();
						if (text.StartsWith("COMP"))
						{
							_LIQMIX = LiqVapMixType.COMPLETE;
						}
						else if (text.StartsWith("LINE"))
						{
							_LIQMIX = LiqVapMixType.LINEAR;
						}
						else if (text.StartsWith("LOG"))
						{
							_LIQMIX = LiqVapMixType.LOG;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VAPMIX))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						string text2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim().ToUpper();
						if (text2.StartsWith("COMP"))
						{
							_VAPMIX = LiqVapMixType.COMPLETE;
						}
						else if (text2.StartsWith("LINE"))
						{
							_VAPMIX = LiqVapMixType.LINEAR;
						}
						else if (text2.StartsWith("LOG"))
						{
							_VAPMIX = LiqVapMixType.LOG;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSURFACETENSION))
				{
					CSURFACETENSION = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SULZER))
				{
					SULZER = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
					TYPE = CUnitOperation.SULZER;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLEXE))
				{
					FLEXE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
					TYPE = CUnitOperation.FLEXE;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLEXI))
				{
					FLEXI = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
					TYPE = CUnitOperation.FLEXI;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.INTALOX))
				{
					INTALOX = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
					TYPE = CUnitOperation.INTALOX;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KGWIREGAUZE))
				{
					KGWIREGAUZE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					TYPE = CUnitOperation.KGWIREGAUZE;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CAPACITY))
				{
					CAPACITY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class FlashZoneCalculation
	{
		private double _HEATEREFFI = 0.85;

		private double _LBYPASSFRC = 0.99;

		private double _VBYPASSFRC = 0.99;

		public int TrayNumber { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TLDT { get; set; }

		public double HEATEREFFI
		{
			get
			{
				return _HEATEREFFI;
			}
			set
			{
				_HEATEREFFI = value;
			}
		}

		public double LBYPASSFRC
		{
			get
			{
				return _LBYPASSFRC;
			}
			set
			{
				_LBYPASSFRC = value;
			}
		}

		public double VBYPASSFRC
		{
			get
			{
				return _VBYPASSFRC;
			}
			set
			{
				_VBYPASSFRC = value;
			}
		}

		public FlashZoneCalculation()
		{
		}

		public FlashZoneCalculation(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.TRAY,
				CUnitOperation.TLDT,
				CUnitOperation.HEATEREFF,
				CUnitOperation.LBYPASSFRAC,
				CUnitOperation.VBYPASSFRAC
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TRAY))
				{
					TrayNumber = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TLDT))
				{
					TLDT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HEATEREFF))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						HEATEREFFI = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LBYPASSFRAC))
				{
					if (strProperty.Contains('='))
					{
						LBYPASSFRC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VBYPASSFRAC) && strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					VBYPASSFRC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ToleranceSetting
	{
		private double _EQUILBRIUM = 0.001;

		private double _ENTHALPY = 0.001;

		private double _COMP = 0.001;

		private double _KVALUE = 0.001;

		public double EQUILBRIUM
		{
			get
			{
				return _EQUILBRIUM;
			}
			set
			{
				_EQUILBRIUM = value;
			}
		}

		public double ENTHALPY
		{
			get
			{
				return _ENTHALPY;
			}
			set
			{
				_ENTHALPY = value;
			}
		}

		public double COMP
		{
			get
			{
				return _COMP;
			}
			set
			{
				_COMP = value;
			}
		}

		public double KVALUE
		{
			get
			{
				return _KVALUE;
			}
			set
			{
				_KVALUE = value;
			}
		}

		public ToleranceSetting()
		{
		}

		public ToleranceSetting(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.EQUILBRIUM,
				CThermo.ENTHALPY,
				CUnitOperation.COMPONENT,
				CThermo.KVALUE
			}, sSource))
			{
				if (item.Content.Contains('='))
				{
					SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EQUILBRIUM))
				{
					_EQUILBRIUM = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CThermo.ENTHALPY))
				{
					_ENTHALPY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COMPONENT))
				{
					_COMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CThermo.KVALUE))
				{
					_KVALUE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty).Value;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class Hydraulics
	{
		public RangeValue SECTION { get; set; }

		public void ParseSection(ref string sSource)
		{
			if (sSource.Contains(CUnitOperation.SECTION))
			{
				int num = sSource.IndexOf(CUnitOperation.SECTION);
				int num2 = sSource.IndexOf(",", sSource.IndexOf(",", num) + 1);
				if (num2 > 0)
				{
					string str = sSource.Substring(num, num2 - num);
					SECTION = ParseUtilty.GetRangeValue(str);
					sSource = sSource.Remove(num, num2 - num + 1);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class TrayCommon : Hydraulics
	{
		private int _PASSES = 1;

		private TypeUnitValue _TRAYSPACING = new TypeUnitValue("IN", "TRAY", 24.0);

		private double _DPCALC = 1.0;

		private double _SFACTOR = 1.0;

		private int _TRAYSPERSEGMENT = 1;

		private LiqVapMixType _LIQMIX;

		private LiqVapMixType _VAPMIX;

		public int PASSES
		{
			get
			{
				return _PASSES;
			}
			set
			{
				_PASSES = value;
			}
		}

		public TypeUnitValue TRAYSPACING
		{
			get
			{
				return _TRAYSPACING;
			}
			set
			{
				_TRAYSPACING = value;
			}
		}

		public double DPCALC
		{
			get
			{
				return _DPCALC;
			}
			set
			{
				_DPCALC = value;
			}
		}

		public double SFACTOR
		{
			get
			{
				return _SFACTOR;
			}
			set
			{
				_SFACTOR = value;
			}
		}

		public int TRAYSPERSEGMENT
		{
			get
			{
				return _TRAYSPERSEGMENT;
			}
			set
			{
				_TRAYSPERSEGMENT = value;
			}
		}

		public LiqVapMixType LIQMIX
		{
			get
			{
				return _LIQMIX;
			}
			set
			{
				_LIQMIX = value;
			}
		}

		public LiqVapMixType VAPMIX
		{
			get
			{
				return _VAPMIX;
			}
			set
			{
				_VAPMIX = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DCRESTIME { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue ACTAREA { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue WLEN { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DCAREA { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue ESTDIAM { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue VAPVOLPCT { get; set; }

		public void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PASSES))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					_PASSES = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPACING))
			{
				if (!strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					return;
				}
				string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(strProperty);
				string unit = string.Empty;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parentheses))
				{
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(parentheses.Trim());
					foreach (string text in array)
					{
						if (!(text.ToUpper().Trim() == "TRAY"))
						{
							unit = text.Trim();
						}
					}
				}
				double val = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				_TRAYSPACING = new TypeUnitValue(unit, "", val);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPCALC))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					_DPCALC = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("SF"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					_SFACTOR = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TRAYSPERSEGMENT))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					_TRAYSPERSEGMENT = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LIQMIX))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					string text2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim().ToUpper();
					if (text2.StartsWith("COMP"))
					{
						_LIQMIX = LiqVapMixType.COMPLETE;
					}
					else if (text2.StartsWith("LINE"))
					{
						_LIQMIX = LiqVapMixType.LINEAR;
					}
					else if (text2.StartsWith("LOG"))
					{
						_LIQMIX = LiqVapMixType.LOG;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VAPMIX))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					string text3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim().ToUpper();
					if (text3.StartsWith("COMP"))
					{
						_VAPMIX = LiqVapMixType.COMPLETE;
					}
					else if (text3.StartsWith("LINE"))
					{
						_VAPMIX = LiqVapMixType.LINEAR;
					}
					else if (text3.StartsWith("LOG"))
					{
						_VAPMIX = LiqVapMixType.LOG;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DCRESTIME))
			{
				DCRESTIME = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ACTAREA))
			{
				ACTAREA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WLEN))
			{
				WLEN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DCAREA))
			{
				DCAREA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ESTDIAM))
			{
				ESTDIAM = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VAPVOLPCT))
			{
				VAPVOLPCT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
		}
	}

	public class VLLECheck
	{
		public bool CHECKON { get; set; }

		public int TRAYS { get; set; }

		public string SET { get; set; }

		public VLLECheck()
		{
		}

		public VLLECheck(string sSource, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.CHECK,
				CThermo.SET,
				CUnitOperation.TRAY
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content, ref bParseError);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty, ref bool bParseError)
		{
			try
			{
				if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CHECK))
				{
					string text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
					if (text == "ON")
					{
						CHECKON = true;
					}
					else if (text == "OFF")
					{
						CHECKON = false;
					}
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TRAY))
				{
					TRAYS = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
				}
				else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CThermo.SET))
				{
					SET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				bParseError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	private string _FEEDSUPERCEDE = "ON";

	private bool _TwoLiquidPhaseExisted;

	private bool _FreeWaterInCondenser;

	private bool _FreeWaterInAllStages;

	private bool bParseError;

	private int _paIndex;

	public P2RigorousDistillation(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		Feeds = new List<ColumnFeed>();
		FeedNames = new List<string>();
		ProductNames = new List<string>();
		SubObjNames = new List<string>();
		TSIZE = new List<TraySizing>();
		TRATE = new List<TrayRating>();
		PACKING = new List<Packing>();
		DUTY = new List<ColumnDuty>();
		SegmentMethod = new Dictionary<int, string>();
		VARY = new List<SpecificationVariable>();
		PseudoProducts = new List<ColumnPseudoProduct>();
		PA = new List<ColumnPumpAroundAndBypass>();
		TempProfileEstimate = new List<TrayTemperature>();
		VaporProfileEstimate = new List<TrayFlowRate>();
		LiquidProfileEstimate = new List<TrayFlowRate>();
		CompositionProfileEstimate = new List<TrayComposition>();
		FreewaterRatioEstimate = new List<TrayFreewaterRatio>();
		PORDER = new List<string>();
		base.VisualNode = (VisualBaseNode)obj;
		try
		{
			SetData(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(IDENTIFICATION + base.UID, this);
			SetDependentDefaultValues();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bParsingError = true;
		}
		finally
		{
			string arg = ((IDENTIFICATION.Length > 4) ? IDENTIFICATION : "Column");
			if (base.bParsingError || bParseError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), arg, base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetData(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			string str = obj.Name.ToUpper();
			string head = ParseUtilty.GetHead(str, ' ');
			if (head.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COLUMN))
			{
				IDENTIFICATION = CUnitOperation.COLUMN;
			}
			else if (head.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDESTRIPPER))
			{
				IDENTIFICATION = CUnitOperation.SIDESTRIPPER;
			}
			else if (head.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDERECTIFIER))
			{
				IDENTIFICATION = CUnitOperation.SIDERECTIFIER;
			}
			head = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
			SetHeadData(head);
			base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bParsingError = true;
		}
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			try
			{
				base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
				string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
				string empty = string.Empty;
				if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PARAMETER))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					PARAMETER = new ColumnParameter(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
				{
					empty = ((!text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED + " ")) ? child.Name.Remove(0, 4) : ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' '));
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(empty);
					int num = 1;
					string empty2 = string.Empty;
					bool flag = false;
					if (empty.Contains(CUnitOperation.SEPARATE) || empty.Contains(CUnitOperation.SUPERCEDE))
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						empty2 = array[i];
						if (empty2.Contains(CUnitOperation.SEPARATE) || empty2.Contains(CUnitOperation.SUPERCEDE))
						{
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(empty2);
							foreach (string text2 in array2)
							{
								if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SEPARATE))
								{
									SeparateFeed = true;
								}
								else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NOTSEPARATE))
								{
									SeparateFeed = false;
								}
								else if (text2.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SUPERCEDE) && text2.Contains('='))
								{
									_FEEDSUPERCEDE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
								}
							}
							if (flag)
							{
								empty2 = empty2.Remove(empty2.IndexOf(",", empty2.IndexOf(",") + 1));
							}
						}
						ColumnFeed item = new ColumnFeed(empty2);
						Feeds.Add(item);
						num++;
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
				{
					empty = (text.Contains(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT + " ") ? ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ') : (text.StartsWith("PRODUCT") ? child.Name.Remove(0, 7) : (text.StartsWith("PRODUC") ? child.Name.Remove(0, 6) : ((!text.StartsWith("PRODU")) ? child.Name.Remove(0, 4) : child.Name.Remove(0, 5)))));
					Products = new ColumnProduct(empty, PARAMETER.StageNumber, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TFLOW))
				{
					empty = ((text.Contains(CUnitOperation.TFLOW + " ") || text.Contains("TFLOW ")) ? ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ') : ((!text.StartsWith("TFLOW")) ? child.Name.Remove(0, 4) : child.Name.Remove(0, 5)));
					List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
					{
						"NET(L)",
						"NET(V)",
						"TOT(L)",
						"TOT(V)",
						"TOTA(L)",
						"TOTA(V)",
						"TOTAL(L)",
						"TOTAL(V)",
						"PA(L)",
						"PA(V)",
						CUnitOperation.TSFEED,
						CUnitOperation.TSLIQUID,
						CUnitOperation.TSVAPOR
					}, empty);
					for (int k = 0; k < propertySplit.Count; k++)
					{
						PropertyPair propertyPair = propertySplit[k];
						if (k < propertySplit.Count - 1)
						{
							propertyPair.Content = empty.Substring(empty.IndexOf(propertyPair.Content), empty.IndexOf(propertySplit[k + 1].Content) - empty.IndexOf(propertyPair.Content)).Trim();
						}
						string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(propertyPair.Content);
						string empty3 = string.Empty;
						if (array3.Length == 1)
						{
							empty3 = propertyPair.Name;
							string text3 = array3[0].Remove(0, propertyPair.Name.Length).Trim();
							if (text3.Contains("/"))
							{
								string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(text3);
								foreach (string strContent in array2)
								{
									ColumnPseudoProduct item2 = new ColumnPseudoProduct(empty3, strContent, ref bParseError);
									PseudoProducts.Add(item2);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								ColumnPseudoProduct item3 = new ColumnPseudoProduct(empty3, text3, ref bParseError);
								PseudoProducts.Add(item3);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (array3.Length != 2)
							{
								continue;
							}
							empty3 = array3[0];
							if (array3[1].Contains("/"))
							{
								string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(array3[1]);
								foreach (string strContent2 in array2)
								{
									ColumnPseudoProduct item4 = new ColumnPseudoProduct(empty3, strContent2, ref bParseError);
									PseudoProducts.Add(item4);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								ColumnPseudoProduct item5 = new ColumnPseudoProduct(empty3, array3[1], ref bParseError);
								PseudoProducts.Add(item5);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONDENSER))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					Condenser = new ColumnCondenser(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) || text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HEAT))
				{
					string head2 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name.Trim(), ' ');
					string text4 = string.Empty;
					string phase = string.Empty;
					if (head2.Contains("("))
					{
						text4 = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head2);
						if (text4.Contains(","))
						{
							string[] array4 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(text4);
							phase = array4[0].Trim();
							text4 = array4[1].Trim();
						}
					}
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(empty);
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						ColumnDuty item6 = new ColumnDuty(array2[j], text4, phase, ref bParseError);
						DUTY.Add(item6);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REBOILER))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					Reboiler = new ColumnReboiler(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QSPEC) || text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QCOLUMN) || text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QTRAY))
				{
					ColumnHeatLG = new ColumnHeatLossGain(child.Name, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PA) && !text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PACKING))
				{
					_paIndex++;
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					ColumnPumpAroundAndBypass item7 = new ColumnPumpAroundAndBypass(empty, ref bParseError, _paIndex);
					PA.Add(item7);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE) || text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PSPEC))
				{
					PressureData = new PressureProfile(child.Name, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ESTIMATE))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					ESTIMATE = new InitialEstimateGenerator(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
				{
					TempProfileEstimate = new List<TrayTemperature>();
					string head3 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string strSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					CorrectUserEstimateParseIssue(ref head3, ref strSource);
					string unit = string.Empty;
					if (head3.Contains("("))
					{
						unit = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head3);
					}
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(strSource);
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						TrayTemperature item8 = new TrayTemperature(array2[j], unit);
						TempProfileEstimate.Add(item8);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VAPOR))
				{
					if (PARAMETER.ALGORITHM != ColumnAlgorithmType.LLEX)
					{
						VaporProfileEstimate = new List<TrayFlowRate>();
						string head4 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
						string strSource2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
						CorrectUserEstimateParseIssue(ref head4, ref strSource2);
						string flowBase = "M";
						if (head4.Contains("("))
						{
							flowBase = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head4);
						}
						string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(strSource2);
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							TrayFlowRate item9 = new TrayFlowRate(array2[j], flowBase, "", "");
							VaporProfileEstimate.Add(item9);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LIQUID))
				{
					LiquidProfileEstimate = new List<TrayFlowRate>();
					string head5 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string strSource3 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					CorrectUserEstimateParseIssue(ref head5, ref strSource3);
					string phase2 = string.Empty;
					string flowBase2 = "M";
					if (PARAMETER.ALGORITHM == ColumnAlgorithmType.CHEMDIST || PARAMETER.ALGORITHM == ColumnAlgorithmType.SURE)
					{
						phase2 = "L";
					}
					if (head5.Contains("("))
					{
						if (head5.Contains(","))
						{
							string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head5));
							phase2 = array5[0].Trim();
							flowBase2 = array5[1].Trim();
						}
						else
						{
							string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head5);
							switch (parentheses)
							{
							case "L":
							case "L1":
							case "L2":
								phase2 = parentheses;
								break;
							default:
								flowBase2 = parentheses;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(strSource3);
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						TrayFlowRate item10 = new TrayFlowRate(array2[j], flowBase2, phase2, "");
						LiquidProfileEstimate.Add(item10);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CESTIMATE))
				{
					string head6 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string strSource4 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					CorrectUserEstimateParseIssue(ref head6, ref strSource4);
					string phase3 = string.Empty;
					if (PARAMETER.ALGORITHM != ColumnAlgorithmType.CHEMDIST)
					{
						phase3 = "L";
					}
					if (head6.Contains("("))
					{
						phase3 = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head6);
					}
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(strSource4);
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						TrayComposition item11 = new TrayComposition(array2[j], phase3);
						CompositionProfileEstimate.Add(item11);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FREEWATER))
				{
					FreewaterRatioEstimate = new List<TrayFreewaterRatio>();
					string head7 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string strSource5 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					CorrectUserEstimateParseIssue(ref head7, ref strSource5);
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(strSource5);
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						TrayFreewaterRatio item12 = new TrayFreewaterRatio(array2[j]);
						FreewaterRatioEstimate.Add(item12);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					DEFINE item13 = new DEFINE(empty, "COLUMN", base.UID);
					base.GlobalDefine.Add(item13);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPECIFICATION))
				{
					string head8 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					if (empty.Contains(CComponent.CUTPOINTS))
					{
						TBP = new TBPCutpointSpecification(empty, base.UID, FeedNames, ProductNames, SubObjNames, ref bParseError);
						continue;
					}
					Specification specification = new Specification(empty, "COLUMN", base.UID);
					if (head8.Contains("("))
					{
						specification.bInactiveSpec = true;
					}
					base.GlobalSpec.Add(specification);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PORDER))
				{
					PORDER = new List<string>();
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(empty);
					foreach (string item14 in array2)
					{
						PORDER.Add(item14);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VARY))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					SpecificationVariable item15 = new SpecificationVariable(empty, ref bParseError);
					VARY.Add(item15);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEFF))
				{
					string head9 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string typeEff = string.Empty;
					if (head9.Contains("("))
					{
						typeEff = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head9);
					}
					TrayEff = new TrayEfficiency(empty, typeEff);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CEFF))
				{
					string head10 = ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Name, ' ');
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					string typeEff2 = string.Empty;
					if (head10.Contains("("))
					{
						typeEff2 = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(head10);
					}
					TrayEff = new TrayEfficiency(empty, typeEff2);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EFACTOR))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ').Trim();
					EFactor = new DoubleValue("", double.Parse(empty));
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HOMOTOPY))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					Homotopy = new HomotopyAnalysis(empty);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRINT))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					PRINT = new PrintOption(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RFPRINT))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					RateFracPrint = new RateFracPrintOption(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLOT))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					PLOT = new PlotResult(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TSIZE))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					TraySizing item16 = new TraySizing(empty, ref bParseError);
					TSIZE.Add(item16);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TRATE))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					TrayRating item17 = new TrayRating(empty, ref bParseError);
					TRATE.Add(item17);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PACKING))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					Packing item18 = new Packing(empty, ref bParseError);
					PACKING.Add(item18);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TOLERANCE))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					TOLERANCE = new ToleranceSetting(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FZONE))
				{
					empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					FZONE = new FlashZoneCalculation(empty, ref bParseError);
				}
				else
				{
					if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
					{
						empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (empty.Contains(","))
						{
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(empty);
							for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
							{
								string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array2[j]);
								SegmentMethod.Add(int.Parse(array6[1].Trim()), array6[0].Trim());
							}
							base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(SegmentMethod.Values.First());
						}
						else
						{
							base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
						}
						continue;
					}
					if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VLLECHECK))
					{
						empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
						VLLECHECK = new VLLECheck(empty, ref bParseError);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetDependentDefaultValues()
	{
		SetOverheadProductPhase();
	}

	private void SetOverheadProductPhase()
	{
		if (Products != null)
		{
			if (Condenser == null)
			{
				Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Name = "V";
			}
			else if (Condenser.TYPE == CondenserType.MIXED || Condenser.TYPE == CondenserType.PART)
			{
				Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Name = "V";
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Name))
			{
				Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Name = "L";
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = new HYSYSColumn();
		list.Add(hYSYSColumn);
		hYSYSColumn.UID = base.UID;
		hYSYSColumn.Name = base.Name;
		hYSYSColumn.IDENTIFICATION = IDENTIFICATION;
		hYSYSColumn.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
		try
		{
			if (PARAMETER != null)
			{
				ConvertColumnParameter(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (Feeds.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertFeeds(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (Products != null)
			{
				ConvertProduct(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (PseudoProducts.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertPseudoProduct(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (Condenser != null)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist = true;
				ConvertCondenser(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (DUTY.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertDuty(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (Reboiler != null)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist = true;
				ConvertReboiler(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (ColumnHeatLG != null)
			{
				ConvertHeatLossGain(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (PA.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertPumpAround(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (PressureData != null)
			{
				ConvertPressureSetting(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (ESTIMATE != null)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.CheckProgramGenerateEstimates = true;
				ConvertEstimateGenerator(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (TempProfileEstimate.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertTempProfileEst(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (VaporProfileEstimate.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertVapFlowProfileEst(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (LiquidProfileEstimate.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertLiqFlowProfileEst(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (CompositionProfileEstimate.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertCompositionProfileEst(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (FreewaterRatioEstimate.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("FreeWaterEstNotConvert"), base.UID));
			}
			if (base.GlobalSpec.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.GlobalSpec = base.GlobalSpec;
			}
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
			}
			if (base.LocalSpec.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.LocalSpec = base.LocalSpec;
			}
			if (base.LocalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.LocalDefine = base.LocalDefine;
			}
			if (VARY.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertSpecVary(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (TrayEff != null)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TrayEffEstNotConvert"), base.UID));
			}
			if (CompEff != null)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompEffEstNotConvert"), base.UID));
			}
			if (EFactor != null)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EffScalingFactorNotConvert"), base.UID));
			}
			_ = Homotopy;
			_ = PRINT;
			_ = RateFracPrint;
			_ = PLOT;
			if (TRATE.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertTRate(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (TSIZE.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertTSize(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (PACKING.Count > 0)
			{
				ConvertPacking(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			_ = FZONE;
			_ = TBP;
			_ = PORDER.Count;
			_ = 0;
			if (TOLERANCE != null)
			{
				ConvertTolerance(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSColumn.FluidPackage = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(base.METHOD);
			}
			_ = SegmentMethod.Count;
			_ = 0;
			if (VLLECHECK != null)
			{
				ConvertVLLECheck(hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (_FreeWaterInCondenser || _TwoLiquidPhaseExisted)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.bDrawWaterFromCondenser = true;
			}
			if (PARAMETER != null && PARAMETER.ALGORITHM != ColumnAlgorithmType.LLEX)
			{
				if (_TwoLiquidPhaseExisted)
				{
					hYSYSColumn.PhaseType = ColumnPhaseEnum.VLL;
				}
				else if (_FreeWaterInAllStages)
				{
					hYSYSColumn.PhaseType = ColumnPhaseEnum.VLW;
				}
				else
				{
					hYSYSColumn.PhaseType = ColumnPhaseEnum.VL;
				}
			}
			hYSYSColumn.SetCreateStreams(list);
			ConvertHCurves(hYSYSColumn);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "Column", base.UID));
			}
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}

	private void ConvertColumnParameter(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		ColumnAlgorithmType aLGORITHM = PARAMETER.ALGORITHM;
		try
		{
			if (aLGORITHM.ToString().Length > 0)
			{
				switch (aLGORITHM)
				{
				case ColumnAlgorithmType.IO:
				case ColumnAlgorithmType.SURE:
				case ColumnAlgorithmType.ENHANCEDIO:
					column.SolvingMethod = SolveMethodTypeEnum.MODIFIEDHYSIMIO;
					break;
				case ColumnAlgorithmType.CHEMDIST:
					column.SolvingMethod = SolveMethodTypeEnum.SPARSECONTINUATIONSOLVER;
					break;
				case ColumnAlgorithmType.LLEX:
					column.PhaseType = ColumnPhaseEnum.LL;
					break;
				case ColumnAlgorithmType.RATEFRAC:
					column.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("RateFracNotConvert"), base.UID));
					break;
				}
			}
			if (PARAMETER.MaxInteration > 0)
			{
				column.MaxInterationNumber = PARAMETER.MaxInteration;
			}
			if (PARAMETER.StageNumber > 0)
			{
				column.stageNumber = PARAMETER.StageNumber;
			}
			if (PARAMETER.FREEWATER)
			{
				_FreeWaterInAllStages = true;
			}
			column.fixingDampFactor = new DoubleValue("", PARAMETER.DAMP);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertFeeds(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (ColumnFeed feed in Feeds)
			{
				column.InletStreams.Add(feed.FeedID);
				column.ColumnFeeds.Add(feed.FeedID, feed.FeedTrayNumber);
			}
			column.bFlashFeed = SeparateFeed;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertProduct(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			bool bLDrawFromCondenser = false;
			bool bVDrawFromCondenser = false;
			if (Products.LDRAW.Count > 0)
			{
				SetSideDrawProduct(Products.LDRAW, column, ref bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bVDrawFromCondenser, "L");
			}
			if (Products.VDRAW.Count > 0)
			{
				SetSideDrawProduct(Products.VDRAW, column, ref bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bVDrawFromCondenser, "V");
			}
			if (Products.WATER.Count > 0)
			{
				CheckWaterPhase(Products.WATER);
				SetSideDrawProduct(Products.WATER, column, ref bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bVDrawFromCondenser, "W");
			}
			if (Products.L1DRAW.Count > 0)
			{
				_TwoLiquidPhaseExisted = true;
				SetSideDrawProduct(Products.L1DRAW, column, ref bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bVDrawFromCondenser, "L1");
			}
			if (Products.L2DRAW.Count > 0)
			{
				_TwoLiquidPhaseExisted = true;
				SetSideDrawProduct(Products.L2DRAW, column, ref bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bVDrawFromCondenser, "L2");
			}
			if (Products.L1PART.Count > 0)
			{
				_TwoLiquidPhaseExisted = true;
				SetSideDrawProduct(Products.L1PART, column, ref bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bVDrawFromCondenser, "L1");
			}
			if (Products.L2PART.Count > 0)
			{
				_TwoLiquidPhaseExisted = true;
				SetSideDrawProduct(Products.L2PART, column, ref bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bVDrawFromCondenser, "L2");
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Products.OVHDProductID))
			{
				if (bLDrawFromCondenser)
				{
					if (Products.OVHD.FlowRate != null)
					{
						Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Name = "V";
					}
					Condenser.TYPE = CondenserType.MIXED;
				}
				else if (bVDrawFromCondenser)
				{
					if (Products.OVHD.FlowRate != null)
					{
						Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Name = "L";
					}
					Condenser.TYPE = CondenserType.MIXED;
				}
				column.OutletStreams.Add(Products.OVHDProductID);
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue>();
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = null;
				if (Products.OVHD.FlowRate != null)
				{
					if (Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Name == "V" && Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Type.ToUpper().Contains("V"))
					{
						typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysStdGasVRate(new DoubleValue(Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Unit, Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Value), Products.OVHD.FlowRate.Type);
						typeUnitValue.Name = "V";
					}
					else
					{
						typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, Products.OVHD.FlowRate);
					}
				}
				dictionary.Add(1, typeUnitValue);
				column.ColumnProducts.Add(Products.OVHDProductID, dictionary);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Products.BTMSProductID))
			{
				column.OutletStreams.Add(Products.BTMSProductID);
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue>();
				TypeUnitValue value = null;
				if (Products.BTMS.FlowRate != null)
				{
					value = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, Products.BTMS.FlowRate);
				}
				dictionary2.Add(PARAMETER.StageNumber, value);
				column.ColumnProducts.Add(Products.BTMSProductID, dictionary2);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void CheckWaterPhase(Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> dict)
	{
		foreach (TrayFlowRate value in dict.Values)
		{
			if (value.TrayNumber == 1)
			{
				_FreeWaterInCondenser = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetSideDrawProduct(Dictionary<string, TrayFlowRate> dict, HYSYSColumn column, ref bool bLDrawFromCondenser, ref bool bVDrawFromCondenser, string phase)
	{
		foreach (string key in dict.Keys)
		{
			column.OutletStreams.Add(key);
			Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue>();
			TrayFlowRate trayFlowRate = new TrayFlowRate();
			trayFlowRate.FlowRate = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, dict[key].FlowRate);
			trayFlowRate.TrayNumber = dict[key].TrayNumber;
			if (trayFlowRate.TrayNumber == 1)
			{
				if (phase.Equals("L") || phase.Equals("L1"))
				{
					bLDrawFromCondenser = true;
				}
				else if (phase.Equals("V"))
				{
					bVDrawFromCondenser = true;
				}
			}
			dictionary.Add(trayFlowRate.TrayNumber, trayFlowRate.FlowRate);
			column.ColumnProducts.Add(key, dictionary);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertPseudoProduct(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (ColumnPseudoProduct pseudoProduct in PseudoProducts)
			{
				if (pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NET))
				{
					if (ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.Trim()) == "L")
					{
						column.ExportNetLiquidFlow.Add(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value, pseudoProduct.SourceTrayNumber);
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.Trim()) == "V")
					{
						column.ExportNetVaporFlow.Add(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value, pseudoProduct.SourceTrayNumber);
					}
				}
				else if (pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.StartsWith("TOT"))
				{
					if (ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.Trim()) == "L")
					{
						column.ExportTotalLiquidFlow.Add(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value, pseudoProduct.SourceTrayNumber);
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.Trim()) == "V")
					{
						column.ExportTotalVaporFlow.Add(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value, pseudoProduct.SourceTrayNumber);
					}
				}
				else if (pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PA))
				{
					RangeValue rangeValue = new RangeValue();
					rangeValue.StartValue = pseudoProduct.SourceTrayNumber.ToString();
					rangeValue.EndValue = pseudoProduct.TargetTrayNumber.ToString();
					if (ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.Trim()) == "L")
					{
						column.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow.Add(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value, rangeValue);
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.Trim()) == "V")
					{
						column.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow.Add(pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value, rangeValue);
					}
				}
				else if (pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TSFEED))
				{
					column.ExportFeedToReboiler = pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value;
				}
				else if (pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TSLIQUID))
				{
					column.ExportLiquidFromReboiler = pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value;
				}
				else if (pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Qualifier.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TSVAPOR))
				{
					column.ExportVaporFromReboiler = pseudoProduct.PseudoStream.Value;
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertCondenser(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			if (Condenser.TYPE == CondenserType.PART)
			{
				column.CondenserType = HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.FULLREFLUX;
			}
			else if (Condenser.TYPE == CondenserType.MIXED)
			{
				column.CondenserType = HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.PARTIAL;
			}
			else if (Condenser.TYPE == CondenserType.BUBB)
			{
				column.CondenserType = HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.TOTAL;
			}
			else if (Condenser.TYPE == CondenserType.DTBB)
			{
				column.CondenserType = HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.TOTAL;
			}
			else if (Condenser.TYPE == CondenserType.TFIX)
			{
				column.CondenserType = HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.TOTAL;
			}
			if (Condenser.PRESSURE != null)
			{
				column.CondenserPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, Condenser.PRESSURE);
			}
			if (Condenser.TESTIMATE != null)
			{
				column.CondenserEstimateTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, Condenser.TESTIMATE);
			}
			if (Condenser.TEMPERATURE != null)
			{
				column.CondenserTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, Condenser.TEMPERATURE);
			}
			if (Condenser.DT != null)
			{
				column.CondenserSubcoolDT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, Condenser.DT);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertDuty(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (ColumnDuty item in DUTY)
			{
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = new TypeUnitValue();
				if (item.DutyValue != null)
				{
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, item.DutyValue.Value, item.DutyValue.Unit, "");
				}
				typeUnitValue.Name = item.Name;
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue>();
				dictionary.Add(item.TrayNumber, typeUnitValue);
				column.DutySpec.Add(item.ID, dictionary);
				column.DutyNO.Add(item.ID, item.ID);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertReboiler(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			if (Reboiler.TYPE == ReboilerType.KETTLE)
			{
				column.ReboilerType = HYSYSReboilerTypeEnum.KETTLE;
			}
			else if (Reboiler.TYPE == ReboilerType.THERMOSIPHON)
			{
				if (Reboiler.HasBaffle)
				{
					column.ReboilerType = HYSYSReboilerTypeEnum.THERMOSIPHONWITHBAFFLE;
				}
				else
				{
					column.ReboilerType = HYSYSReboilerTypeEnum.THERMOSIPHONWITHOUTBAFFLE;
				}
			}
			if (Reboiler.RATE != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerCirculationRate = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, Reboiler.RATE);
			}
			if (Reboiler.LFRAC != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerReturnStreamLiquidFrac = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, Reboiler.LFRAC);
			}
			if (Reboiler.VFRAC != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerReturnStreamVaporFrac = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, Reboiler.VFRAC);
			}
			if (Reboiler.TEMP != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, Reboiler.TEMP);
			}
			if (Reboiler.DT != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerTemperatureRise = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, Reboiler.DT);
			}
			if (Reboiler.LFEST != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerLiqProdToReboilerFeedRatioEstimate = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, Reboiler.LFEST);
			}
			if (Reboiler.VFEST != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerVapProdToTeboilerFeedRatioEstimate = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, Reboiler.VFEST);
			}
			if (Reboiler.RTEST != null)
			{
				column.ReboilerMolarFeedFlowrateEstimate = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, Reboiler.RTEST);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHeatLossGain(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		int stageNumber = PARAMETER.StageNumber;
		try
		{
			if (ColumnHeatLG.QSPEC.Count > 0)
			{
				SortedDictionary<int, DoubleValue> sortedDictionary = new SortedDictionary<int, DoubleValue>();
				foreach (QSpecification item in ColumnHeatLG.QSPEC)
				{
					sortedDictionary.Add(item.TrayNumber, item.QValue);
				}
				LinearInterpolationHeatLossGain(sortedDictionary);
				int startTray = GetStartTray();
				int endTray = GetEndTray();
				for (int i = startTray; i <= endTray; i++)
				{
					DoubleValue val = sortedDictionary[i];
					SetDutySpecForHeatLossGain(val, column, i);
				}
			}
			else if (ColumnHeatLG.QCOLUMN != null)
			{
				DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue();
				int num = stageNumber;
				if (Condenser != null)
				{
					num--;
				}
				if (Reboiler != null)
				{
					num--;
				}
				int startTray2 = GetStartTray();
				int endTray = GetEndTray();
				doubleValue.Value = ColumnHeatLG.QCOLUMN.QCOL.Value / (double)num;
				doubleValue.Unit = ColumnHeatLG.QCOLUMN.QCOL.Unit;
				for (int j = startTray2; j <= endTray; j++)
				{
					SetDutySpecForHeatLossGain(doubleValue, column, j);
				}
			}
			else if (ColumnHeatLG.QTRAY != null)
			{
				int startTray3 = GetStartTray();
				int endTray = GetEndTray();
				for (int k = startTray3; k <= endTray; k++)
				{
					SetDutySpecForHeatLossGain(ColumnHeatLG.QTRAY, column, k);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private int GetStartTray()
	{
		if (Condenser == null)
		{
			return 1;
		}
		return 2;
	}

	private int GetEndTray()
	{
		if (Reboiler == null)
		{
			return PARAMETER.StageNumber;
		}
		return PARAMETER.StageNumber - 1;
	}

	private void LinearInterpolationHeatLossGain(SortedDictionary<int, DoubleValue> dutySDInput)
	{
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		List<int> list = dutySDInput.Keys.ToList();
		DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
		DoubleValue doubleValue2 = null;
		foreach (int item in list)
		{
			if (item == num)
			{
				num = item;
				doubleValue = dutySDInput[num];
				continue;
			}
			num2 = item;
			doubleValue2 = dutySDInput[num2];
			for (int i = num + 1; i < num2; i++)
			{
				DoubleValue doubleValue3 = new DoubleValue();
				doubleValue3.Unit = doubleValue.Unit;
				doubleValue3.Value = doubleValue.Value + (doubleValue2.Value - doubleValue.Value) * (double)(i - num) / (double)(num2 - num);
				dutySDInput.Add(i, doubleValue3);
			}
			num = num2;
			doubleValue = doubleValue2;
		}
	}

	private void SetDutySpecForHeatLossGain(DoubleValue val, HYSYSColumn column, int Stage)
	{
		int num = 0;
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, val.Value, val.Unit, "");
		num = column.getDutyNO();
		Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue>();
		dictionary.Add(Stage, hysysValue);
		column.HeatLossGainSpec.Add(num, dictionary);
	}

	private void ConvertPumpAround(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (ColumnPumpAroundAndBypass item in PA)
			{
				PumpAround pumpAround = new PumpAround();
				pumpAround.SourceTrayNo = item.SourceTray;
				pumpAround.TargetTrayNo = item.TargetTray;
				pumpAround.DrawPhase = item.PHASE;
				if (item.PRESSURE != null)
				{
					int sourceTray = item.SourceTray;
					if (Condenser != null)
					{
						sourceTray--;
					}
					column.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("PAReturnPNotConvert"), base.UID, item.NAME));
				}
				pumpAround.paId = item.paId;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.NAME))
				{
					pumpAround.Name = item.NAME;
				}
				if (item.RATE != null)
				{
					pumpAround.PumparoundRate = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, item.RATE);
				}
				if (item.LFRAC != null)
				{
					pumpAround.ReturnStreamLiqFrac = item.LFRAC;
				}
				if (item.TEMP != null)
				{
					pumpAround.ReturnStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, item.TEMP);
				}
				if (item.DT != null)
				{
					pumpAround.ReturnStreamTempChange = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, item.DT);
				}
				if (item.AllPumpAround)
				{
					int sourceTray2 = item.SourceTray;
					if (Condenser != null)
					{
						sourceTray2--;
					}
					column.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AllPANotConvert"), base.UID, item.NAME));
				}
				column.PA.Add(pumpAround);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertPressureSetting(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			if (PressureData.PRESSURE != null && PressureData.PRESSURE.Count > 0)
			{
				int num = 1;
				int num2 = 0;
				int num3 = 0;
				if (Condenser != null && column.CondenserPressure != null)
				{
					column.PressureProfile.Add(1, column.CondenserPressure);
				}
				foreach (TrayPressure item3 in PressureData.PRESSURE)
				{
					int trayNumber = item3.TrayNumber;
					DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, item3.PressureValue);
					if (!column.PressureProfile.Keys.Contains(trayNumber))
					{
						column.PressureProfile.Add(trayNumber, hysysValue);
					}
					if (num == 2)
					{
						num3 = trayNumber;
					}
					if (num == PressureData.PRESSURE.Count - 1)
					{
						num2 = trayNumber;
					}
					num++;
				}
				if (column.ColumnTopPressure == null)
				{
					string text = string.Empty;
					double val;
					if (Condenser == null)
					{
						val = column.PressureProfile[1].Value;
					}
					else if (column.PressureProfile.Keys.Contains(2))
					{
						val = column.PressureProfile[2].Value;
						text = column.PressureProfile[2].Unit;
					}
					else
					{
						val = column.PressureProfile[1].Value + (column.PressureProfile[num3].Value - column.PressureProfile[1].Value) / (double)(num3 - 1);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text = column.PressureProfile[1].Unit;
					}
					column.ColumnTopPressure = new DoubleValue(text, val);
				}
				if (column.ColumnBottomPressure == null)
				{
					column.ColumnBottomPressure = new DoubleValue(val: (Reboiler != null) ? (column.PressureProfile[column.stageNumber].Value - (column.PressureProfile[column.stageNumber].Value - column.PressureProfile[num2].Value) / (double)(column.stageNumber - num2)) : column.PressureProfile[column.stageNumber].Value, unit: column.PressureProfile[column.stageNumber].Unit);
				}
				if (Condenser != null)
				{
					DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, column.PressureProfile[1], "bar");
					DoubleValue convertedValue2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, column.ColumnTopPressure, "bar");
					column.CondenserPressureDrop = new DoubleValue("bar", convertedValue2.Value - convertedValue.Value);
				}
				if (Reboiler != null)
				{
					DoubleValue convertedValue3 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, column.ColumnBottomPressure, "bar");
					DoubleValue convertedValue4 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, column.PressureProfile[column.stageNumber], "bar");
					column.ReboilerPressureDrop = new DoubleValue("bar", convertedValue4.Value - convertedValue3.Value);
				}
				return;
			}
			if (PressureData.PSPEC != null)
			{
				if (PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP != null)
				{
					column.ColumnTopPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit))
					{
						PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit = UnitConverter.GetP2GlobalUnit(UnitClassEnum.Pressure);
					}
					if (Condenser != null && Condenser.PRESSURE != null)
					{
						double valueIn = PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value - Condenser.PRESSURE.Value;
						if (PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit != Condenser.PRESSURE.Unit)
						{
							DoubleValue p2Value = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, Condenser.PRESSURE.Value, Condenser.PRESSURE.Unit, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit);
							valueIn = PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value - p2Value.Value;
						}
						column.CondenserPressureDrop = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, valueIn, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit);
					}
				}
				int num4 = PARAMETER.StageNumber;
				if (Condenser != null)
				{
					num4--;
				}
				if (Reboiler != null)
				{
					num4--;
				}
				if (PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY != null)
				{
					column.ColumnPressureDrop = new DoubleValue(PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY.Unit, PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY.Value * (double)(num4 - 1));
					if (PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP == null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY);
						string item = "Tray pressure drop(value=" + hysysValue2.Value + ",unit=" + hysysValue2.Unit + ") column top pressure is empty";
						column.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						return;
					}
					DoubleValue doubleValue = PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY;
					string text2 = string.Empty;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY.Unit))
					{
						text2 = PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY.Unit;
					}
					double valueIn2;
					if (PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit.Contains(text2))
					{
						valueIn2 = PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value + PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY.Value * (double)(num4 - 1);
					}
					else
					{
						doubleValue = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY.Value, text2, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit);
						valueIn2 = PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value + doubleValue.Value * (double)(num4 - 1);
					}
					if (Reboiler == null)
					{
						column.ColumnBottomPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, valueIn2, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit);
					}
					else
					{
						column.ColumnBottomPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value + doubleValue.Value * (double)(num4 - 1), PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit);
					}
					if (Condenser != null && column.CondenserPressureDrop == null)
					{
						column.CondenserPressureDrop = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY);
					}
					if (Reboiler != null && column.ReboilerPressureDrop == null)
					{
						column.ReboilerPressureDrop = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, PressureData.PSPEC.DPTRAY);
					}
				}
				else if (PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL != null)
				{
					column.ColumnPressureDrop = PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL;
					if (PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP == null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue3 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL);
						string item2 = "Column pressure drop(value=" + hysysValue3.Value + ",unit=" + hysysValue3.Unit + ") column top pressure is empty";
						column.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
						return;
					}
					double num5 = PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value + PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL.Value;
					DoubleValue doubleValue2 = PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL;
					string text3 = string.Empty;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL.Unit))
					{
						text3 = PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL.Unit;
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit.Contains(text3))
					{
						num5 = PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value + PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL.Value;
					}
					else
					{
						doubleValue2 = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, PressureData.PSPEC.DPCOL.Value, text3, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit);
						num5 = PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Value + doubleValue2.Value;
					}
					column.ColumnBottomPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, num5, PressureData.PSPEC.PTOP.Unit);
					double num6 = doubleValue2.Value / (double)num4;
					if (Reboiler != null)
					{
						column.ColumnBottomPressure.Value = column.ColumnBottomPressure.Value - num6;
					}
					if (Condenser != null && column.CondenserPressureDrop == null)
					{
						column.CondenserPressureDrop = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, num6, doubleValue2.Unit);
					}
					if (Reboiler != null && column.ReboilerPressureDrop == null)
					{
						column.ReboilerPressureDrop = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, num6, doubleValue2.Unit);
					}
				}
				else if (column.ColumnBottomPressure == null)
				{
					column.ColumnBottomPressure = column.ColumnTopPressure;
				}
			}
			if (column.bCondenserExist && column.CondenserPressureDrop == null)
			{
				column.CondenserPressureDrop = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
			}
			if (column.bReboilerExist && column.ReboilerPressureDrop == null)
			{
				column.ReboilerPressureDrop = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertEstimateGenerator(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			if (ESTIMATE.CTEMP != null && Condenser != null)
			{
				column.TempProfileEstimate.Add(1, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ESTIMATE.CTEMP));
			}
			if (ESTIMATE.TTEMP != null)
			{
				if (Condenser != null)
				{
					column.TempProfileEstimate.Add(2, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ESTIMATE.TTEMP));
				}
				else
				{
					column.TempProfileEstimate.Add(1, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ESTIMATE.TTEMP));
				}
			}
			if (ESTIMATE.BTEMP != null)
			{
				if (Reboiler != null)
				{
					column.TempProfileEstimate.Add(PARAMETER.StageNumber - 1, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ESTIMATE.BTEMP));
				}
				else
				{
					column.TempProfileEstimate.Add(PARAMETER.StageNumber, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ESTIMATE.BTEMP));
				}
			}
			if (ESTIMATE.RTEMP != null && Reboiler != null)
			{
				column.TempProfileEstimate.Add(PARAMETER.StageNumber, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ESTIMATE.RTEMP));
			}
			if (ESTIMATE.RATIO != null)
			{
				column.bRROrRefluxEstExist = true;
				double value = 3.0;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ESTIMATE.RATIO.Value))
				{
					value = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ESTIMATE.RATIO.Value));
				}
				string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(ESTIMATE.RATIO.Qualifier);
				column.EstRRatio = new TypeUnitValue();
				column.EstRRatio.Value = value;
				column.EstRRatio.Type = "M";
				column.EstRRatio.Name = "L";
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parentheses))
				{
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(parentheses);
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						string text = array[i].ToUpper();
						switch (text)
						{
						case "L":
							column.EstRRatio.Name = text;
							break;
						case "LD":
							column.EstRRatio.Name = text;
							break;
						case "L1":
							column.EstRRatio.Name = text;
							break;
						case "L2":
							column.EstRRatio.Name = text;
							break;
						case "LF":
							column.EstRRatio.Name = text;
							break;
						case "M":
							column.EstRRatio.Type = text;
							break;
						case "WT":
							column.EstRRatio.Type = text;
							break;
						case "LV":
							column.EstRRatio.Type = text;
							break;
						case "GV":
							column.EstRRatio.Type = text;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (ESTIMATE.REFLUX != null)
			{
				column.bRROrRefluxEstExist = true;
				double val = double.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ESTIMATE.REFLUX.Value));
				string parentheses2 = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(ESTIMATE.REFLUX.Qualifier);
				column.EstReflux = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, new TypeUnitValue(parentheses2, "M", val));
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertTempProfileEst(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (TrayTemperature item in TempProfileEstimate)
			{
				if (column.TempProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(item.TrayNumber))
				{
					column.TempProfileEstimate[item.TrayNumber] = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, item.TemperatureValue);
				}
				else
				{
					column.TempProfileEstimate.Add(item.TrayNumber, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, item.TemperatureValue));
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertVapFlowProfileEst(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (TrayFlowRate item in VaporProfileEstimate)
			{
				column.VaporFlowProfileEstimate.Add(item.TrayNumber, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, item.FlowRate));
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertLiqFlowProfileEst(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (TrayFlowRate item in LiquidProfileEstimate)
			{
				column.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate.Add(item.TrayNumber, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, item.FlowRate));
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertCompositionProfileEst(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (TrayComposition item in CompositionProfileEstimate)
			{
				if (item.Phase == "L")
				{
					column.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Add(item.TrayNumber, item.composition);
				}
				else if (item.Phase == "L1")
				{
					column.L1PhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Add(item.TrayNumber, item.composition);
				}
				else if (item.Phase == "L2")
				{
					column.L2PhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Add(item.TrayNumber, item.composition);
				}
				else if (item.Phase == "V")
				{
					column.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Add(item.TrayNumber, item.composition);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertSpecVary(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (SpecificationVariable item in VARY)
			{
				if (item.DUTY.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (string item2 in item.DUTY)
					{
						column.VaryDutyIDs.Add(item2);
					}
				}
				if (item.DNAME.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (string item3 in item.DNAME)
					{
						column.VaryDutyNames.Add(item3);
					}
				}
				if (item.DRAW.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (string item4 in item.DRAW)
					{
						column.VaryDrawStreamIDs.Add(item4);
					}
				}
				if (item.FEED.Count <= 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (string item5 in item.FEED)
				{
					column.VaryFeedStreamIDs.Add(item5);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertTolerance(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		column.EquilTolerance = TOLERANCE.EQUILBRIUM;
		column.HeatSpecTolerance = TOLERANCE.ENTHALPY;
	}

	private void ConvertVLLECheck(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		column.bVLLECheckOn = VLLECHECK.CHECKON;
		column.VLLECheckTrays = VLLECHECK.TRAYS;
		column.VLLECheckSet = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(VLLECHECK.SET);
	}

	private void CorrectUserEstimateParseIssue(ref string head, ref string strSource)
	{
		if (!head.Contains(","))
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		bool flag = false;
		string text = head;
		for (int i = 0; i < text.Length; i++)
		{
			if (char.IsDigit(text[i]))
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
			num++;
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			strSource = head.Substring(num) + strSource;
			head = head.Substring(0, num);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHCurves(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			P2HCURVE p2HCURVE = HYSYSObjectHelper.RetrieveHCurveObject(column.UID);
			if (p2HCURVE == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < p2HCURVE.UnitCurves.Count; i++)
			{
				string key = p2HCURVE.UID + "_" + (i + 1);
				List<DoubleValue> list = new List<DoubleValue>();
				if (string.Compare(p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i].UID, column.UID, ignoreCase: true) != 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				P2HCURVE.UOCurve uOCurve = p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i];
				if (uOCurve.POINTS > 0)
				{
					column.HCurvePoints.Add(key, uOCurve.POINTS);
				}
				int num = Convert.ToInt16(uOCurve.ConnectSide);
				column.HCurveDutyNO.Add(key, num);
				if (!column.DutyHCurvePlotPoints.Keys.Contains(num) && uOCurve.POINTS > 0)
				{
					column.DutyHCurvePlotPoints.Add(num, uOCurve.POINTS);
				}
				if (uOCurve.PRESSURE != null && uOCurve.PRESSURE.Values.Length != 0)
				{
					string unit = uOCurve.PRESSURE.Unit;
					double[] values = uOCurve.PRESSURE.Values;
					foreach (double valueIn in values)
					{
						list.Add(UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, valueIn, unit));
					}
					column.HCurvePressureColl.Add(key, list);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertTRate(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (TrayRating item in TRATE)
			{
				ColumnTray columnTray = new ColumnTray();
				columnTray.CalculationMode = HydraulicsCalulationModeEnum.RATING;
				if (item.SECTION != null)
				{
					columnTray.StartStage = Convert.ToInt16(item.SECTION.StartValue);
					columnTray.EndStage = Convert.ToInt16(item.SECTION.EndValue);
				}
				columnTray.TrayType = TrayTypeEnum.VALVE;
				if (item.TYPE == TrayType.CAP)
				{
					columnTray.TrayType = TrayTypeEnum.CAP;
				}
				else if (item.TYPE == TrayType.SIEVE)
				{
					columnTray.TrayType = TrayTypeEnum.SIEVE;
				}
				if (item.TRAYSPACING != null)
				{
					columnTray.TraySpacing = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.TRAYSPACING.Value, item.TRAYSPACING.Unit);
				}
				if (item.TrayDiameter != null)
				{
					columnTray.SectionDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.TrayDiameter.Value, item.TrayDiameter.Unit);
				}
				if (item.PASSES > 0)
				{
					columnTray.NumberOfFlowPaths = item.PASSES;
				}
				if (item.DECKTHICKNESS != null)
				{
					columnTray.TrayThickness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DECKTHICKNESS.Value, item.DECKTHICKNESS.Unit);
				}
				if (item.VALVETHICKNESS != null)
				{
					columnTray.ValveThickness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.VALVETHICKNESS.Value, item.VALVETHICKNESS.Unit);
				}
				if (item.HOLEAREA != null)
				{
					columnTray.HoleArea = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, item.HOLEAREA);
				}
				if (item.DCC != null)
				{
					columnTray.DowncomerClearaness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCC);
				}
				if (item.InternalDiameter != null && item.TYPE == TrayType.SIEVE)
				{
					columnTray.SieveHoleDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.InternalDiameter.Value, item.InternalDiameter.Unit);
				}
				if (item.WEIR != null)
				{
					columnTray.WeirHeight = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.WEIR);
				}
				if (item.DCW.Count > 0)
				{
					if (item.PASSES == 1)
					{
						columnTray.SideDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[1]);
					}
					else if (item.PASSES == 2)
					{
						columnTray.SideDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[1]);
						columnTray.CenterDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[2]);
					}
					else if (item.PASSES == 3)
					{
						columnTray.SideDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[1]);
						columnTray.OffCenterDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[3]);
					}
					else if (item.PASSES == 4)
					{
						columnTray.SideDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[1]);
						columnTray.CenterDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[2]);
						columnTray.OffCenterDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[3]);
					}
					else if (item.PASSES == 5)
					{
						columnTray.SideDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[1]);
						columnTray.OffCenterDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[3]);
						columnTray.OffSideDCTopWidth = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DCW[4]);
					}
				}
				column.colTrays.Add(columnTray);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertTSize(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (TraySizing item in TSIZE)
			{
				ColumnTray columnTray = new ColumnTray();
				columnTray.CalculationMode = HydraulicsCalulationModeEnum.SIZING;
				if (item.SECTION != null)
				{
					columnTray.StartStage = Convert.ToInt16(item.SECTION.StartValue);
					columnTray.EndStage = Convert.ToInt16(item.SECTION.EndValue);
				}
				columnTray.TrayType = TrayTypeEnum.VALVE;
				if (item.TYPE == TrayType.CAP)
				{
					columnTray.TrayType = TrayTypeEnum.CAP;
				}
				else if (item.TYPE == TrayType.SIEVE)
				{
					columnTray.TrayType = TrayTypeEnum.SIEVE;
				}
				if (item.TRAYSPACING != null)
				{
					columnTray.TraySpacing = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.TRAYSPACING.Value, item.TRAYSPACING.Unit);
				}
				if (item.PASSES > 0)
				{
					columnTray.NumberOfFlowPaths = item.PASSES;
				}
				if (item.FFACTOR > 0.0)
				{
					columnTray.MaxFlooding = item.FFACTOR;
				}
				column.colTrays.Add(columnTray);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertPacking(HYSYSColumn column)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (Packing item in PACKING)
			{
				columnPack columnPack = new columnPack();
				if (item.SECTION != null)
				{
					columnPack.StartStage = Convert.ToInt16(item.SECTION.StartValue);
					columnPack.EndStage = Convert.ToInt16(item.SECTION.EndValue);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.TYPE))
				{
					string text = item.TYPE.ToUpper().Trim();
					bool flag = true;
					string text2 = text;
					for (int i = 0; i < text2.Length; i++)
					{
						if (!char.IsDigit(text2[i]))
						{
							flag = false;
							break;
						}
					}
					columnPack.IsRandomPack = flag;
					if (flag)
					{
						columnPack.RandomPackType = Convert.ToInt16(text);
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("FLEXE"))
					{
						columnPack.StructurePackType = StructuredPackTypeEnum.FLEXE;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("FLEXI"))
					{
						columnPack.StructurePackType = StructuredPackTypeEnum.FLEXI;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("INTA"))
					{
						columnPack.StructurePackType = StructuredPackTypeEnum.INTALOX;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("KGWI"))
					{
						columnPack.StructurePackType = StructuredPackTypeEnum.KGWIGEGAUZE;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("SULZ"))
					{
						columnPack.StructurePackType = StructuredPackTypeEnum.SULZER;
					}
				}
				if (item.SIZE != null)
				{
					columnPack.RandomPackSize = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.SIZE);
				}
				if (item.DIAMETER != null)
				{
					columnPack.CalculationMode = HydraulicsCalulationModeEnum.RATING;
					columnPack.SectionDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, item.DIAMETER);
				}
				column.colPacks.Add(columnPack);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}
}
