internal class P2Compressor : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Operation : PropertyItem
	{
		private double _WTOL = 0.001;

		private DoubleValue _EFF = new DoubleValue("PCT", 100.0);

		private DoubleValue _POLY = new DoubleValue("PCT", 100.0);

		private string _CALC = "ASME";

		private double _PRSWITCH = 1.15;

		private double _FANH = 2.0;

		private double _FANE = 1.0;

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue WORK { get; set; }

		public double PRATIO { get; set; }

		public double WTOL
		{
			get
			{
				return _WTOL;
			}
			set
			{
				_WTOL = value;
			}
		}

		public double PDESIGN { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue EFF
		{
			get
			{
				return _EFF;
			}
			set
			{
				_EFF = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue POLY
		{
			get
			{
				return _POLY;
			}
			set
			{
				_POLY = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEMP { get; set; }

		public string CALC
		{
			get
			{
				return _CALC;
			}
			set
			{
				_CALC = value;
			}
		}

		public double PRSWITCH
		{
			get
			{
				return _PRSWITCH;
			}
			set
			{
				_PRSWITCH = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PIN { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEST { get; set; }

		public double RPM { get; set; }

		public double REFRPM { get; set; }

		public double FANH
		{
			get
			{
				return _FANH;
			}
			set
			{
				_FANH = value;
			}
		}

		public double FANE
		{
			get
			{
				return _FANE;
			}
			set
			{
				_FANE = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public P2Compressor(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.COMPRESSOR + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "COMPRESSOR", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		List<string> list = null;
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(empty);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
					if (array2.Length != 0)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							string strName = array2[j];
							NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							base.FEED.Add(strName);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				OPERATION = new Operation();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
					"POUT",
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRATIO,
					CUnitOperation.WORK,
					CUnitOperation.WTOL,
					CUnitOperation.PDESIGN,
					CUnitOperation.EFF,
					CUnitOperation.POLY,
					CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
					CUnitOperation.CALC,
					CUnitOperation.PRSWITCH,
					CUnitOperation.PIN,
					CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE,
					CUnitOperation.RPM,
					CUnitOperation.REFRPM,
					CUnitOperation.FANH,
					CUnitOperation.FANE,
					"REFRPM"
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
				{
					list2.Add(item2.Content);
				}
				string[] array3 = list2.ToArray();
				for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
				{
					array3[k] = array3[k].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE) || array3[k].StartsWith("POUT"))
					{
						OPERATION.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						OPERATION.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRATIO))
					{
						OPERATION.PRATIO = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WORK))
					{
						OPERATION.WORK = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WTOL) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsWTOLSet = true;
						OPERATION.WTOL = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PDESIGN))
					{
						OPERATION.PDESIGN = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EFF) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsEFFSet = true;
						OPERATION.EFF = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POLY) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsPOLYSet = true;
						OPERATION.POLY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						IsTempSet = true;
						OPERATION.TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CALC) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsCALCSet = true;
						OPERATION.CALC = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRSWITCH) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsPRSWITCHSet = true;
						OPERATION.PRSWITCH = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PIN))
					{
						OPERATION.PIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
					{
						OPERATION.TEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RPM))
					{
						OPERATION.RPM = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REFRPM))
					{
						OPERATION.REFRPM = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FANH) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsFANHSet = true;
						OPERATION.FANH = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FANE) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsFANESet = true;
						OPERATION.FANE = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
			{
				PRESSURE = new Pressure();
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRATIO))
			{
				PRATIO = new PRatio();
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WORK))
			{
				WORK = new Work();
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HEAD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, '=');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.HEAD);
				Dictionary<double, double> tableValues = ParseUtilty.GetTableValues(empty);
				HEAD = new Head();
				HEAD.Rate.Values = new double[tableValues.Keys.Count];
				HEAD.HeadValue.Values = new double[tableValues.Keys.Count];
				HEAD.Rate.Values = tableValues.Keys.ToArray();
				HEAD.HeadValue.Values = tableValues.Values.ToArray();
				HEAD.Rate.Unit = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(child.Name)[0];
				HEAD.HeadValue.Unit = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(child.Name)[1];
				if (child.Name.ToUpper().IndexOf("ADIA") > -1)
				{
					HEAD.ADIA_POLY_ACTUAL = "ADIA";
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().IndexOf("POLY") > -1)
				{
					HEAD.ADIA_POLY_ACTUAL = "POLY";
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().IndexOf("ACTU") > -1)
				{
					HEAD.ADIA_POLY_ACTUAL = "ACTUAL";
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EFFICIENCY))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, '=');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.EFFICIENCY);
				Dictionary<double, double> tableValues2 = ParseUtilty.GetTableValues(empty);
				EFFICIENCY = new Efficiency();
				EFFICIENCY.Rate.Values = new double[tableValues2.Keys.Count];
				EFFICIENCY.EfficiencyValue.Values = new double[tableValues2.Keys.Count];
				EFFICIENCY.Rate.Values = tableValues2.Keys.ToArray();
				EFFICIENCY.EfficiencyValue.Values = tableValues2.Values.ToArray();
				EFFICIENCY.Rate.Unit = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(child.Name)[0];
				EFFICIENCY.EfficiencyValue.Unit = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(child.Name)[1];
				if (child.Name.ToUpper().IndexOf("ADIA") > -1)
				{
					EFFICIENCY.ADIA_POLY = "ADIA";
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().IndexOf("POLY") > -1)
				{
					EFFICIENCY.ADIA_POLY = "POLY";
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PINLET))
			{
				PINLET = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(child.Name));
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POUTLET))
			{
				POUTLET = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(child.Name));
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COOLER))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.COOLER);
				COOLER = new Cooler();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit2 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string> { "ACTE", "ACDP", "TEMP", "DP" }, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item3 in propertySplit2)
				{
					list3.Add(item3.Content);
				}
				string[] array4 = list3.ToArray();
				for (int l = 0; l < array4.Length; l++)
				{
					array4[l] = array4[l].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array4[l].StartsWith("ACTE") || array4[l].StartsWith("TEMP"))
					{
						COOLER.ACTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array4[l]);
					}
					else if ((array4[l].StartsWith("ACDP") || array4[l].StartsWith("DP")) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						COOLER.ACDP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array4[l]);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "Compressor", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CThermo.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CThermo.METHOD);
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty.Trim());
			}
		}
		if (list != null)
		{
			HYSYSBaseObject.g_mapDefine.Add(base.UID, list);
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSCompressor hYSYSCompressor = new HYSYSCompressor();
			hYSYSCompressor.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSCompressor.Name = base.Name;
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSCompressor);
			hYSYSCompressor.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
				{
					hYSYSCompressor.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
				}
			}
			if (base.PRODUCT != null)
			{
				foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
				{
					hYSYSCompressor.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
					dictionary.Add(addedProperty, base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				}
			}
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSCompressor);
				if (hYSYSMixer != null)
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompressor.FluidPackage))
					{
						hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSCompressor.FluidPackage;
					}
					hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
				}
			}
			if (base.IsMultiplePRODUCT)
			{
				List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, hYSYSCompressor);
				if (list2 != null)
				{
					foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item in list2)
					{
						list.Add(item);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompressor.FluidPackage))
						{
							item.FluidPackage = hYSYSCompressor.FluidPackage;
						}
						item.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
			}
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				ConvertOperationData(hYSYSCompressor);
			}
			ConvertCurveData(hYSYSCompressor);
			if (COOLER != null)
			{
				ConvertCoolerData(hYSYSCompressor, list);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSCompressor.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSCompressor.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSCompressor.SeparateDefines();
			}
			hYSYSCompressor.SetCreateStreams(list);
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), CUnitOperation.COMPRESSOR, base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void ConvertOperationData(HYSYSCompressor compressor)
	{
		if (OPERATION.WORK != null)
		{
			compressor.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Work, OPERATION.WORK);
		}
		else if (OPERATION.PRESSURE != null)
		{
			compressor.AttachedPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRESSURE);
		}
		else if (OPERATION.DP != null)
		{
			compressor.AttachedDP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
		}
		else if (OPERATION.PRATIO != 0.0)
		{
			compressor.AttachedPRatio = OPERATION.PRATIO;
		}
		if (IsWTOLSet)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "WTOL"));
		}
		if (OPERATION.PDESIGN != 0.0)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PDESIGN"));
		}
		if ((OPERATION.EFF != null && IsEFFSet) || (!IsTempSet && !IsPOLYSet && !IsEFFSet))
		{
			compressor.AdiabaticEfficiency = OPERATION.EFF.Value;
		}
		else if (OPERATION.POLY != null && IsPOLYSet)
		{
			compressor.PolytropicEfficiency = OPERATION.POLY.Value;
		}
		else if (OPERATION.TEMP != null)
		{
			compressor.AttachedTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, OPERATION.TEMP);
		}
		if (IsCALCSet)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "CALC"));
		}
		if (IsPRSWITCHSet)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PRSWITCH"));
		}
		if (OPERATION.PIN != null)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PIN"));
		}
		if (OPERATION.TEST != null)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TEST"));
		}
		if (OPERATION.RPM != 0.0)
		{
			compressor.RPM = OPERATION.RPM;
		}
		if (OPERATION.REFRPM != 0.0)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "REFRPM"));
		}
		if (IsFANHSet)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FANH"));
		}
		if (IsFANESet)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FANE"));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertCurveData(HYSYSCompressor compressor)
	{
		if (PRESSURE != null)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorCurveNotConverted"), "PRESSURE"));
		}
		if (PRATIO != null)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorCurveNotConverted"), "PRATIO"));
		}
		if (WORK != null)
		{
			compressor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorCurveNotConverted"), "WORK"));
		}
		if (HEAD != null)
		{
			compressor.CurveRate = new DoubleValues();
			compressor.CurveRate.Values = new double[HEAD.Rate.Values.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < HEAD.Rate.Values.Length; i++)
			{
				compressor.CurveRate.Values[i] = UnitConverter.GetHysysActGasVRate(new DoubleValue(HEAD.Rate.Unit, HEAD.Rate.Values[i])).Value;
				compressor.CurveRate.Unit = UnitConverter.GetHysysActGasVRate(new DoubleValue(HEAD.Rate.Unit, HEAD.Rate.Values[i])).Unit;
				compressor.CurveHead = UnitConverter.GetHysysValues(UnitClassEnum.Length, HEAD.HeadValue);
			}
		}
		if (EFFICIENCY == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		compressor.CurveEfficiencyType = EFFICIENCY.ADIA_POLY;
		if (HEAD != null)
		{
			compressor.CurveEfficiency = new DoubleValues();
			compressor.CurveEfficiency.Values = new double[HEAD.Rate.Values.Length];
			for (int j = 0; j < HEAD.Rate.Values.Length; j++)
			{
				compressor.CurveEfficiency.Values[j] = CurveInterpolation.GetInterpolationDatum(EFFICIENCY.Rate.Values, EFFICIENCY.EfficiencyValue.Values, HEAD.Rate.Values[j]);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConvertCoolerData(HYSYSCompressor compressor, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl)
	{
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger();
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
		HYSYSObjectHelper.InsertObjectInStream(compressor, compressor.OutletStreams[0], bInletStream: false, hYSYSHeatExchanger);
		if (hYSYSHeatExchanger != null)
		{
			if (COOLER.ACTEMP != null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.HotSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, COOLER.ACTEMP);
			}
			if (COOLER.ACDP != null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.HotSideDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, COOLER.ACDP);
			}
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.LoadStreams();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(compressor.FluidPackage))
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.FluidPackage = compressor.FluidPackage;
			}
			objColl.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
		}
	}
}
internal class P2Expander : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Operation : PropertyItem
	{
		private double _WTOL = 0.001;

		private DoubleValue _EFF = new DoubleValue("PCT", 100.0);

		public DoubleValue Pressure { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP { get; set; }

		public double PRatio { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue Work { get; set; }

		public double WTOL
		{
			get
			{
				return _WTOL;
			}
			set
			{
				_WTOL = value;
			}
		}

		public double PDESIGN { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue EFF
		{
			get
			{
				return _EFF;
			}
			set
			{
				_EFF = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PIN { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEST { get; set; }
	}

	public Operation OPERATION { get; set; }

	public bool IsEFFSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsWTOLSet { get; set; }

	public P2Expander(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.EXPANDER + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "EXPANDER", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		List<string> list = null;
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(empty);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
					if (array2.Length != 0)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							string strName = array2[j];
							NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							base.FEED.Add(strName);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				OPERATION = new Operation();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
					"POUT",
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRATIO,
					CUnitOperation.WORK,
					CUnitOperation.WTOL,
					CUnitOperation.PDESIGN,
					CUnitOperation.EFF,
					CUnitOperation.PIN,
					CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE
				}, empty);
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
				{
					list2.Add(item2.Content);
				}
				string[] array3 = list2.ToArray();
				for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
				{
					array3[k] = array3[k].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE) || array3[k].StartsWith("POUT"))
					{
						OPERATION.Pressure = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						OPERATION.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRATIO))
					{
						OPERATION.PRatio = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WORK))
					{
						OPERATION.Work = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WTOL) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsWTOLSet = true;
						OPERATION.WTOL = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PDESIGN))
					{
						OPERATION.PDESIGN = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]));
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EFF) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						OPERATION.EFF = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PIN))
					{
						OPERATION.PIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
					{
						OPERATION.TEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "Expander", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CThermo.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CThermo.METHOD);
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty.Trim());
			}
		}
		if (list != null)
		{
			HYSYSBaseObject.g_mapDefine.Add(base.UID, list);
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSExpander hYSYSExpander = new HYSYSExpander();
			hYSYSExpander.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSExpander.Name = base.Name;
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSExpander);
			hYSYSExpander.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			if (OPERATION.Work != null)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Work, OPERATION.Work);
			}
			else if (OPERATION.Pressure != null)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.AttachedPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.Pressure);
			}
			else if (OPERATION.DP != null)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.AttachedDP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
			}
			else if (OPERATION.PRatio != 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.AttachedPRatio = OPERATION.PRatio;
			}
			if (IsWTOLSet)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "WTOL"));
			}
			if (OPERATION.PDESIGN != 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PDESIGN"));
			}
			if ((OPERATION.EFF != null && IsEFFSet) || !IsEFFSet)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.AdiabaticEfficiency = OPERATION.EFF.Value;
			}
			if (OPERATION.PIN != null)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PIN"));
			}
			if (OPERATION.TEST != null)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TEST"));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSExpander.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
				{
					hYSYSExpander.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
				}
			}
			if (base.PRODUCT != null)
			{
				foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
				{
					hYSYSExpander.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
					dictionary.Add(addedProperty, base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				}
			}
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSExpander);
				if (hYSYSMixer != null)
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSExpander.FluidPackage))
					{
						hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSExpander.FluidPackage;
					}
					hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
				}
			}
			if (base.IsMultiplePRODUCT)
			{
				List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, hYSYSExpander);
				if (list2 != null)
				{
					foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item in list2)
					{
						list.Add(item);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSExpander.FluidPackage))
						{
							item.FluidPackage = hYSYSExpander.FluidPackage;
						}
						item.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
			}
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSExpander.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSExpander.SeparateDefines();
			}
			hYSYSExpander.SetCreateStreams(list);
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), CUnitOperation.EXPANDER, base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}
}
public class P2Flash : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Isothermal
	{
		public DoubleValue TEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP { get; set; }

		public Isothermal(string sSource)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_ParsingError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Adiabatic : FlashUnitValues
	{
		private DoubleValue _DUTY = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		public bool IsAdiabatic { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY
		{
			get
			{
				return _DUTY;
			}
			set
			{
				_DUTY = value;
			}
		}

		public Adiabatic(string strSource)
		{
			IsAdiabatic = true;
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.PESTIMATE,
				CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE,
				CUnitOperation.DUTY
			}, strSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						base.TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						base.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						base.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PESTIMATE))
					{
						base.PEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
					{
						base.TEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && array[i].Contains('='))
					{
						DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_ParsingError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class FlashUnitValues
	{
		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue(string.Empty, 0.0);

		public DoubleValue TEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PEST { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEST { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public FlashUnitValues()
		{
		}

		public FlashUnitValues(string sSource)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.PESTIMATE,
				CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PESTIMATE))
					{
						PEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
					{
						TEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_ParsingError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class FlashSpec
	{
		public SpecStream STREAM { get; set; }

		public bool DRY { get; set; }

		public SpecOperations OperParameters { get; set; }

		public double VALUE { get; set; }

		public double ATOL { get; set; }

	}

	{
		public string StreamName { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue Property1 { get; set; }
	}

	{
		public string Operator { get; set; }

		public string Reference { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue Property2 { get; set; }
	}

	public class Entrainment
	{
		public string FROM { get; set; }

		public string TO { get; set; }

		public double FRACTION { get; set; }

		public double PERCENT { get; set; }

		public bool HasSetPERCENT { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue RATE { get; set; }

		public Entrainment(string sSource)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.FROM,
				CUnitOperation.TO,
				CUnitOperation.FRACTION,
				CUnitOperation.PERCENT,
				CUnitOperation.RATE
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FROM))
					{
						FROM = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TO))
					{
						TO = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRACTION))
					{
						FRACTION = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PERCENT))
					{
						PERCENT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
						HasSetPERCENT = true;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATE))
					{
						RATE = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_ParsingError = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private static bool _ParsingError;

	private HYSYSSeparator _Flash;

	private List<HYSYSBaseObject> _HysysObjects;

	private List<string> _RecordedConvertedNotes = new List<string>();

	public Specification SPEC { get; set; }

	public string KPRINT { get; set; }

	public string OPERATION_PHASE { get; set; }

	public Isothermal ISOTHERMAL { get; set; }

	public Adiabatic ADIABATIC { get; set; }

	public FlashUnitValues DEW { get; set; }

	public FlashUnitValues DEWHC { get; set; }

	public FlashUnitValues DEWWATER { get; set; }

	public FlashUnitValues BUBBLE { get; set; }

	public FlashUnitValues ISENTROPIC { get; set; }

	public UpperDewPoint UPPERDEWPOINT { get; set; }

	public FlashUnitValues TPSPEC { get; set; }

	public List<Entrainment> ENTRAINMENTs { get; set; }

	public P2Flash(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.FLASH + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "FLASH", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bParsingError || _ParsingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "FLASH", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
		{
			CUnitOperation.UID,
			CUnitOperation.NAME,
			CUnitOperation.KPRINT
		}, text.ToUpper());
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
		{
			list.Add(item2.Content);
		}
		string[] array = list.ToArray();
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			try
			{
				string text2 = array[i];
				text2 = text2.ToUpper();
				if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UID))
				{
					base.UID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				}
				else if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NAME))
				{
					base.Name = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				}
				else if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KPRINT))
				{
					KPRINT = "KPRINT";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			try
			{
				base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
				if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
				{
					base.FEED = new List<string>();
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' '));
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						string strName = array2[j].Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						base.FEED.Add(strName);
					}
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION_PHASE))
				{
					ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(child.Name);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
				{
					string text3 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					base.PRODUCT = new Product(text3.ToUpper());
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL))
				{
					string sSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					ISOTHERMAL = new Isothermal(sSource);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC))
				{
					string strSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					ADIABATIC = new Adiabatic(strSource);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEW))
				{
					string sSource2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					DEW = new FlashUnitValues(sSource2);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEWHC))
				{
					string sSource3 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					DEWHC = new FlashUnitValues(sSource3);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEWWATER))
				{
					string sSource4 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					DEWWATER = new FlashUnitValues(sSource4);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BUBBLE))
				{
					string sSource5 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					BUBBLE = new FlashUnitValues(sSource5);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISENTROPIC))
				{
					string sSource6 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					ISENTROPIC = new FlashUnitValues(sSource6);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UPPERDEWPOINT))
				{
					string sSource7 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					UPPERDEWPOINT = new UpperDewPoint(sSource7);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TPSPEC))
				{
					string sSource8 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					TPSPEC = new FlashUnitValues(sSource8);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPEC))
				{
					string specExpress = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ').Trim();
					SPEC = new Specification(specExpress, "CONTROLLER", base.UID);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
				{
					DEFINE item = new DEFINE(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' '), "FLASH", base.UID);
					base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ENTRAINMENT))
				{
					if (ENTRAINMENTs == null)
					{
						ENTRAINMENTs = new List<Entrainment>();
					}
					string sSource9 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
					ENTRAINMENTs.Add(new Entrainment(sSource9));
				}
				else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
				{
					base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(child.Name.ToUpper());
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		_HysysObjects = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		try
		{
			_Flash = new HYSYSSeparator();
			_Flash.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			_HysysObjects.Add(_Flash);
			_Flash.UID = base.UID;
			_Flash.Name = base.Name;
			_Flash.EnergyStream = base.UID + "_Energy";
			_Flash.SPEC = SPEC;
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
				{
					_Flash.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
				}
			}
			SetProductAndSeparatorType();
			SetTempAndPres();
			SetSpec();
			SetEntrainment();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(OPERATION_PHASE))
			{
				_Flash.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("PHSASE {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				_Flash.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			_Flash.SetCreateStreams(_HysysObjects);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				_Flash.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				_Flash.SeparateDefines();
			}
			SelectEquipmentForReplace(_Flash, ref _HysysObjects);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "FLASH", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "FLASH", base.UID));
			}
		}
		return Enumerable.ToArray(_HysysObjects);
	}

	private void SetProductAndSeparatorType()
	{
		if (_Flash == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			if (base.PRODUCT == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				_Flash.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.S))
			{
				if (_Flash.OutletStreams.Contains(base.PRODUCT.S))
				{
					_Flash.OutletStreams.Remove(base.PRODUCT.S);
				}
				if (base.PRODUCT != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.S))
				{
					string item = P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_ProductSolidStream") + " " + base.PRODUCT.S + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted");
					_Flash.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					_RecordedConvertedNotes.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (_Flash.OutletStreams.Count > 2 || (string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.V) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.W)))
			{
				_Flash.SeparatorType = HYSYSSeparatorType.ThreePhaseSep;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.V) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.M))
				{
					_Flash.VapourOutletStream = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, base.UID);
				}
				else
				{
					_Flash.VapourOutletStream = base.PRODUCT.V;
				}
				_Flash.LiquidOutletStream = base.PRODUCT.L;
				_Flash.HeavyLiquidtStream = base.PRODUCT.W;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.M))
				{
					_Flash.HasMixedProduct = true;
					_Flash.OutletStreams.Clear();
					_Flash.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.M);
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.AddMixerToSeparator(_Flash);
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
					_HysysObjects.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(_HysysObjects);
					string item2 = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_MixtureStreamToMixcer"), base.PRODUCT.M.ToString());
					_Flash.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
					_Flash.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.W);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_Flash.SeparatorType = HYSYSSeparatorType.Separator;
				_Flash.VapourOutletStream = base.PRODUCT.V;
				_Flash.LiquidOutletStream = base.PRODUCT.L;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.L) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.W))
				{
					_Flash.LiquidOutletStream = base.PRODUCT.W;
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void SetTempAndPres()
	{
		if (_Flash == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			if (DEW != null)
			{
				_Flash.IsDew = true;
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, DEW.TEMP);
				_Flash.OutletStreamPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, DEW.PRESSURE);
				_Flash.InletDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, DEW.DP);
			}
			else if (DEWHC != null)
			{
				_Flash.IsDew = true;
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, DEWHC.TEMP);
				_Flash.OutletStreamPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, DEWHC.PRESSURE);
				_Flash.InletDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, DEWHC.DP);
			}
			else if (DEWWATER != null)
			{
				_Flash.IsDew = true;
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, DEWWATER.TEMP);
				_Flash.OutletStreamPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, DEWWATER.PRESSURE);
				_Flash.InletDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, DEWWATER.DP);
			}
			else if (UPPERDEWPOINT != null)
			{
				_Flash.IsDew = true;
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, UPPERDEWPOINT.TEMP);
			}
			else if (BUBBLE != null)
			{
				_Flash.IsBubble = true;
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, BUBBLE.TEMP);
				_Flash.OutletStreamPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, BUBBLE.PRESSURE);
				_Flash.InletDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, BUBBLE.DP);
			}
			else if (ISENTROPIC != null)
			{
				string item = CUnitOperation.P2Head_Flash + base.UID + " ISENTROPIC " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted");
				_Flash.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
				_RecordedConvertedNotes.Add(item);
			}
			else if (ADIABATIC != null && ADIABATIC.IsAdiabatic)
			{
				_Flash.IsAdiabatic = true;
				if (base.GlobalDefine != null && base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (DEFINE item2 in base.GlobalDefine)
					{
						if (item2.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item2.SecondRefID))
						{
							_Flash.Duty = null;
						}
						else
						{
							_Flash.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, ADIABATIC.DUTY);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_Flash.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, ADIABATIC.DUTY);
				}
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ADIABATIC.TEMP);
				_Flash.OutletStreamPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, ADIABATIC.PRESSURE);
				_Flash.InletDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, ADIABATIC.DP);
			}
			else if (ISOTHERMAL != null)
			{
				_Flash.IsIsothermal = true;
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, ISOTHERMAL.TEMP);
				_Flash.OutletStreamPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, ISOTHERMAL.PRESSURE);
				_Flash.InletDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, ISOTHERMAL.DP);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void SetSpec()
	{
		try
		{
			if (TPSPEC != null)
			{
				_Flash.HasSpec = true;
				_Flash.OutLetStreamTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, TPSPEC.TEMP);
				_Flash.OutletStreamPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, TPSPEC.PRESSURE);
				_Flash.InletDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, TPSPEC.DP);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void SetEntrainment()
	{
		if (ENTRAINMENTs == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			foreach (Entrainment eNTRAINMENT in ENTRAINMENTs)
			{
				if (_Flash.Entrainments == null)
				{
					_Flash.Entrainments = new List<HYSYSSeparator.HYSYSEntrainment>();
				}
				HYSYSSeparator.HYSYSEntrainment hYSYSEntrainment = new HYSYSSeparator.HYSYSEntrainment();
				hYSYSEntrainment.FROM = eNTRAINMENT.FROM;
				hYSYSEntrainment.TO = eNTRAINMENT.TO;
				hYSYSEntrainment.FRACTION = eNTRAINMENT.FRACTION;
				if (eNTRAINMENT.HasSetPERCENT)
				{
					hYSYSEntrainment.FRACTION = eNTRAINMENT.PERCENT / 100.0;
				}
				if (eNTRAINMENT.RATE != null)
				{
					hYSYSEntrainment.RATE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, eNTRAINMENT.RATE.Value, eNTRAINMENT.RATE.Unit, eNTRAINMENT.RATE.Type);
				}
				_Flash.Entrainments.Add(hYSYSEntrainment);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			base.bMappingError = true;
		}
	}

	private void SelectEquipmentForReplace(HYSYSSeparator logicFlash, ref List<HYSYSBaseObject> hysysObjects)
	{
		if (logicFlash == null || (base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Count != 1 && (base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Count != 2 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.S))))
		{
			return;
		}
		hysysObjects = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		if (logicFlash != null)
		{
			if (logicFlash.IsAdiabatic && (logicFlash.Duty == null || logicFlash.Duty.Value == 0.0))
			{
				CreateValveToReplaceFlash(logicFlash, ref hysysObjects);
			}
			else
			{
				CreateHeaterToReplaceFlash(logicFlash, ref hysysObjects);
			}
		}
	}

	private void CreateHeaterToReplaceFlash(HYSYSSeparator logicFlash, ref List<HYSYSBaseObject> hysysObjects)
	{
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger();
		hysysObjects.Clear();
		hysysObjects.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = base.UID;
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater;
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.FluidPackage = logicFlash.FluidPackage;
		if (base.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Count == 1)
		{
			foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
			}
		}
		else if (base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Count == 2 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.S))
		{
			foreach (string addedProperty2 in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				if (addedProperty2 != "S")
				{
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty2).ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (logicFlash.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletTemperature = logicFlash.OutLetStreamTemp;
		}
		else if (logicFlash.Duty != null)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.Duty = logicFlash.Duty;
		}
		if (_Flash.IsBubble)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.CLFRAC = new DoubleValue("", 1.0);
		}
		else if (_Flash.IsDew)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.CLFRAC = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
		}
		if (logicFlash.OutletStreamPressure != null)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletPressure = logicFlash.OutletStreamPressure;
		}
		else if (logicFlash.InletDeltaPressure != null)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideDeltaPressure = logicFlash.InletDeltaPressure;
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideDeltaPressure = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
		}
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.LoadStreams();
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_Heater"), base.UID));
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
		if (hYSYSHeatExchanger.InletStreams != null && hYSYSHeatExchanger.InletStreams.Count > 1)
		{
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			_HysysObjects.Add(hYSYSMixer);
			hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(_HysysObjects);
		}
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.AddRange(_RecordedConvertedNotes);
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(_HysysObjects);
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Clear();
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.AddRange(hYSYSHeatExchanger.InletStreams);
		SetSpec(logicFlash, hYSYSHeatExchanger);
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.GlobalDefine = _Flash.GlobalDefine;
	}

	private void CreateValveToReplaceFlash(HYSYSSeparator logicFlash, ref List<HYSYSBaseObject> hysysObjects)
	{
		HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = new HYSYSValve();
		hysysObjects.Clear();
		hysysObjects.Add(hYSYSValve);
		hYSYSValve.UID = base.UID;
		if (base.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				hYSYSValve.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Count == 1)
		{
			foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				hYSYSValve.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
			}
		}
		else if (base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Count == 2 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.PRODUCT.S))
		{
			foreach (string addedProperty2 in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				if (addedProperty2 != "S")
				{
					hYSYSValve.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty2).ToString());
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		hYSYSValve.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_Valve"), base.UID));
		hYSYSValve.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
		if (hYSYSValve.InletStreams != null && hYSYSValve.InletStreams.Count > 1)
		{
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSValve);
			hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			_HysysObjects.Add(hYSYSMixer);
			hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(_HysysObjects);
		}
		if (logicFlash.OutletStreamPressure != null)
		{
			hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = logicFlash.OutletStreamPressure;
		}
		else if (logicFlash.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
		{
			hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_Temperature = logicFlash.OutLetStreamTemp;
		}
		else if (logicFlash.InletDeltaPressure != null)
		{
			hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = logicFlash.InletDeltaPressure;
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
		}
		hYSYSValve.ConvertNotes.AddRange(_RecordedConvertedNotes);
		SetSpec(logicFlash, hYSYSValve);
		hYSYSValve.FluidPackage = logicFlash.FluidPackage;
		hYSYSValve.SetCreateStreams(_HysysObjects);
	}

	private void SetSpec(HYSYSSeparator logicFlash, HYSYSUnitOperationBase hyObj)
	{
		HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = null;
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = null;
		if (hyObj.GetType() == typeof(HYSYSValve))
		{
			hYSYSValve = hyObj as HYSYSValve;
		}
		else if (hyObj.GetType() == typeof(HYSYSHeatExchanger))
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger = hyObj as HYSYSHeatExchanger;
		}
		if (logicFlash.SPEC == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(logicFlash.SPEC.PrimaryID) || logicFlash.SPEC.SpecType != SpecificationType.STREAM)
		{
			return;
		}
		Specification sPEC = logicFlash.SPEC;
		string flowbasis = "M";
		string unit = string.Empty;
		double refValue = -1.0;
		if (sPEC.Operator != Specification.OperatorType.none)
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add("Global specification \"" + sPEC.P2SpecExpression + "\"  was not converted");
			return;
		}
		ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis, ref unit, ref refValue);
		if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RATE) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") == -1)
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add("SPEC " + sPEC.P2SpecExpression + "\"  was not converted");
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RATE) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.FRACTION) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.PERCENT) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.PPM) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.GRAINS))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.TEMP))
		{
			if (hYSYSHeatExchanger != null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, sPEC.Value);
			}
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.PRESSURE))
		{
			if (hYSYSHeatExchanger != null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, sPEC.Value);
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideDeltaPressure = null;
			}
			else if (hYSYSValve != null)
			{
				hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, sPEC.Value);
			}
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.MOISTURE))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.LFACT))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.VFRAC))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.WFRAC))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.MW))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.SPGR))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.API))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.DENSITY))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.MOLVOL))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.HOTVOL))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.ENTHALPY))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.TBP))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.D86))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.D1160))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RVP))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.TVP))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.VISCOSITY))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.CONDUCTIVITY))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.SURFACE))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.SPROPERTY))
		{
			hyObj.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, logicFlash.UID));
		}
	}
}
internal class P2Furnace : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public enum RealParameterTable
	{
		TTEMP = 1,
		TOUT,
		TWALL,
		PWQLO,
		BTEMP,
		TMAX,
		DTMAX,
		TAMB,
		PRESSURE,
		DP,
		Dummy1,
		BTES,
		QLES,
		TTES,
		TEST,
		Dummy2,
		Dummy3,
		Dummy4,
		Dummy5,
		UAGT,
		UATU,
		UAGW,
		UAWA,
		RFDP,
		RFDF,
		C1,
		C2,
		C3,
		Dummy6,
		Dummy7,
		DUTY,
		WQLOSS
	}

	public class Side : PropertyItem
	{
		public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

		public string M { get; set; }

		public string METHOD { get; set; }

		public Side(string source)
		{
			FEED = new List<string>();
			List<string> list = new List<string>
			{
				"FEED=",
				"M=",
				CUnitOperation.METHOD
			};
			string strSource = source.Replace(" ", "").ToUpper();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, strSource);
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			string text = string.Empty;
			int index = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				num++;
				if (item.Name == "FEED=")
				{
					flag = true;
					index = source.IndexOf("FEED=");
					if (index < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref index, source, "FEED");
						if (num == propertySplit.Count && index > 0)
						{
							text = source.Substring(0, index);
							source = source.Remove(index);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					num2 = source.IndexOf(item.Name);
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref num2, source, item.Name.Replace("=", ""));
					}
					if (index > -1 && num2 > index)
					{
						text = source.Substring(index, num2 - index);
						source = source.Remove(index, num2 - index);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ParseFeed(text);
			}
			propertySplit.Clear();
			list.Remove("FEED=");
			propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, source.Replace(" ", ""));
			foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
			{
				SetValue(item2.Content);
			}
		}

		private void ParseFeed(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string strName = array[i];
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				FEED.Add(strName);
			}
		}

		private void FindCorrectIndex(ref int index, string strSource, string keyName)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int startIndex = 0;
			while (!flag)
			{
				index = strSource.IndexOf(keyName, startIndex);
				for (int i = index + keyName.Length; i < strSource.Length; i++)
				{
					switch (strSource[i])
					{
					case '=':
						flag = true;
						break;
					default:
						startIndex = i;
						break;
					case ' ':
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				index = -1;
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith("M="))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				M = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
		}
	}

	public class Parameter : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _dp = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public DoubleValue TTEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TOUT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TWALL { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PWQLO { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue BTES { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue QLES { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TTES { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue UAGT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue UATU { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue UAGW { get; set; }

		public double C2 { get; set; }

		public double C3 { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue WQLOSS { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get

				return _dp;
			}
			set
			{
				_dp = value;
			}
		}

		public Parameter(string source, int startPar)
		{
			string[] array = source.Split(',');
			string empty = string.Empty;
			int num = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				empty = array[i];
				if (empty.Length != 0)
				{
					num = startPar + i;
					RetrieveParameter(empty, num);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RetrieveParameter(string parameter, int index)
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue("", Convert.ToDouble(parameter));
			switch ((RealParameterTable)index)
			{
			case RealParameterTable.TTEMP:
				TTEMP = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.TTEMP.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.TOUT:
				TOUT = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.TOUT.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.TWALL:
				TWALL = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.TWALL.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.PWQLO:
				PWQLO = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.PWQLO.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.BTEMP:
				BTEMP = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.BTEMP.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.TMAX:
				TMAX = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.TMAX.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.DTMAX:
				DTMAX = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.DTMAX.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.TAMB:
				TAMB = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.TAMB.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.PRESSURE:
				PRESSURE = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.PRESSURE.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.DP:
				DP = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.DP.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.BTES:
				BTES = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.BTES.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.QLES:
				QLES = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.QLES.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.TTES:
				TTES = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.TTES.ToString());

			case RealParameterTable.TEST:
				TEST = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.TEST.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.UAGT:
				UAGT = doubleValue;

				break;
			case RealParameterTable.UATU:
				UATU = doubleValue;

			case RealParameterTable.UAGW:

			case RealParameterTable.UAWA:
				UAWA = doubleValue;

			case RealParameterTable.RFDP:
				RFDP = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.RFDP.ToString());
				break;
			case RealParameterTable.RFDF:
				RFDF = doubleValue;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.RFDF.ToString());
				break;

				C1 = Convert.ToDouble(parameter);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.C1.ToString());

			case RealParameterTable.C2:
				C2 = Convert.ToDouble(parameter);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(RealParameterTable.C2.ToString());
