	public Side TUBE { get; set; }

	public Side COMB { get; set; }

	public Parameter REALPARA { get; set; }

	public bool USEDPCORRELATION { get; set; }

	public bool ADDVAVLE { get; set; }

	public P2Furnace(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.FURNACE + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "FURNACE", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDE) && text.Contains('('))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				string strA = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(ParseUtilty.GetHead(text, ' ').Trim()).Trim();
				if (string.Compare(strA, CUnitOperation.PROCESSSIDE, ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(strA, "1", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					TUBE = new Side(empty);
				}
				if (string.Compare(strA, CUnitOperation.COMBUSTIONSIDE, ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(strA, "2", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					COMB = new Side(empty);
				}
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.INTEGER))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' '), ' ').Trim();
				if (string.Compare(empty, "1", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					USEDPCORRELATION = true;
				}
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PARAMETER))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' '), ' ').Trim();
				int startPar = Convert.ToInt32(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text).Trim());
				REALPARA = new Parameter(empty, startPar);
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSFiredHeater hYSYSFiredHeater = new HYSYSFiredHeater();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSFiredHeater);
			hYSYSFiredHeater.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSFiredHeater.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSFiredHeater.Name = base.Name;
			AddAirSideStream(hYSYSFiredHeater, list);
			ConvertSides(hYSYSFiredHeater, list);
			ConvertParameters(hYSYSFiredHeater, list);
			SetInletStreams(hYSYSFiredHeater);
			hYSYSFiredHeater.SetCreateStreams(list);
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "FURNACE", base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void AddAirSideStream(HYSYSFiredHeater furnace, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objCollection)
	{
		string text = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
		furnace.AIRSIDESTREAMS.Add(text);
		HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = new HYSYSStream();
		hYSYSStream.Stream = text;
		hYSYSStream.Source = null;
		hYSYSStream.Destination = furnace;
		hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFrac;
		hYSYSStream.Composition.Data.Add("Oxygen", 0.2);
		hYSYSStream.Composition.Data.Add("Nitrogen", 0.8);
		objCollection.Add(hYSYSStream);
	}

	private void ConvertSides(HYSYSFiredHeater furnace, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objCollection)
	{
		bool flag = false;
		if (TUBE != null)
		{
			if (TUBE.FEED != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in TUBE.FEED)
				{
					furnace.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBE.M))
			{
				furnace.TUBESIDEOUTLETSTREAMS.Add(TUBE.M);
				furnace.OutletStreams.Add(TUBE.M);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBE.METHOD))
			{
				flag = true;
				furnace.FluidPackage = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(TUBE.METHOD);
			}
			if (furnace.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS.Count > 1)
			{
				furnace.InletStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string tUBESIDEINLETSTREAM in furnace.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS)
				{
					furnace.InletStreams.Add(tUBESIDEINLETSTREAM);
				}
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(furnace);
				if (hYSYSMixer != null)
				{
					objCollection.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(furnace.FluidPackage))
					{
						hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = furnace.FluidPackage;
					}
					hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(objCollection);
				}
				furnace.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS.Clear();
				foreach (string inletStream in furnace.InletStreams)
				{
					furnace.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS.Add(inletStream);
				}
			}
		}
		if (COMB == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (COMB.FEED != null)
		{
			foreach (string item2 in COMB.FEED)
			{
				furnace.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS.Add(item2);
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(COMB.M))
		{
			furnace.COMBSIDEOUTLETSTREAMS.Add(COMB.M);
			furnace.OutletStreams.Add(COMB.M);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(COMB.METHOD) && !flag)
		{
			furnace.FluidPackage = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(COMB.METHOD);
		}
		if (furnace.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS.Count <= 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		furnace.InletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (string cOMBSIDEINLETSTREAM in furnace.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS)
		{
			furnace.InletStreams.Add(cOMBSIDEINLETSTREAM);
		}
		HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(furnace);
		if (hYSYSMixer2 != null)
		{
			objCollection.Add(hYSYSMixer2);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(furnace.FluidPackage))
			{
				hYSYSMixer2.FluidPackage = furnace.FluidPackage;
			}
			hYSYSMixer2.SetCreateStreams(objCollection);
		}
		furnace.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS.Clear();
		foreach (string inletStream2 in furnace.InletStreams)
		{
			furnace.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS.Add(inletStream2);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertParameters(HYSYSFiredHeater furnace, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objCollection)
	{
		if (REALPARA.TOUT != null)
		{
			furnace.TUBESIDETOUT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, REALPARA.TOUT);
		}
		if (REALPARA.DP.Value != 0.0 || REALPARA.PRESSURE != null)
		{
			HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = new HYSYSValve();
			hYSYSValve.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
			HYSYSObjectHelper.InsertObjectInStream(furnace, furnace.TUBESIDEOUTLETSTREAMS[0], bInletStream: true, hYSYSValve);
			if (REALPARA.DP.Value != 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, REALPARA.DP);
			}
			if (REALPARA.PRESSURE != null)
			{
				hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, REALPARA.PRESSURE);
			}
			objCollection.Add(hYSYSValve);
			hYSYSValve.SetCreateStreams(objCollection);
		}
		if (REALPARA.BTEMP != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter BTEMP"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.BTES != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter BTES"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.DTMAX != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter DTMAX"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.DUTY != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter DUTY"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.PWQLO != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter PWQLO"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.QLES != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter QLES"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.RFDF != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter RFDF"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.RFDP != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter RFDP"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.TAMB != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter TAMB"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.TEST != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter TEST"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.TMAX != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter TMAX"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.TTEMP != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter TTEMP"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.TTES != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter TTES"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.TWALL != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter TWALL"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.UAGT != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter UAGT"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.UAGW != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter UAGW"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.UATU != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter UATU"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.UAWA != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter UAWA"));
		}
		if (REALPARA.WQLOSS != null)
		{
			furnace.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Parameter WQLOSS"));
		}
	}

	private void SetInletStreams(HYSYSFiredHeater firedHeater)
	{
		firedHeater.InletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (string cOMBSIDEINLETSTREAM in firedHeater.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS)
		{
			firedHeater.InletStreams.Add(cOMBSIDEINLETSTREAM);
		}
		foreach (string tUBESIDEINLETSTREAM in firedHeater.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS)
		{
			firedHeater.InletStreams.Add(tUBESIDEINLETSTREAM);
		}
	}
}
internal class P2LNGHeatExchanger : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Cell : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _dp = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

		public string V { get; set; }

		public string L { get; set; }

		public string W { get; set; }

		public string S { get; set; }

		public string M { get; set; }

		public string METHOD { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public int NUMBER { get; set; }

		public string CELL { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _dp;
			}
			set
			{
				_dp = value;
			}
		}

		public Cell(string source)
		{
			FEED = new List<string>();
			List<string> list = new List<string>
			{
				"FEED=",
				"V=",
				"L=",
				"W=",
				"S=",
				"M=",
				"DUTY",
				"TEMP",
				"NUMBER",
				"CELL=",
				"DP",
				CUnitOperation.METHOD
			};
			string strSource = source.Replace(" ", "").ToUpper();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, strSource);
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			string text = string.Empty;
			int index = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				num++;
				if (item.Name == "FEED=")
				{
					flag = true;
					index = source.IndexOf("FEED=");
					if (index < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref index, source, "FEED");
						if (num == propertySplit.Count && index > 0)
						{
							text = source.Substring(0, index);
							source = source.Remove(index);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					num2 = source.IndexOf(item.Name);
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref num2, source, item.Name.Replace("=", ""));
					}
					if (index > -1 && num2 > index)
					{
						text = source.Substring(index, num2 - index);
						source = source.Remove(index, num2 - index);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ParseFeed(text);
			}
			propertySplit.Clear();
			list.Remove("FEED=");
			propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, source.Replace(" ", ""));
			foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
			{
				SetValue(item2.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith("V="))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				V = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("V");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("L="))
			{
				string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				L = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("L");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("W="))
			{
				string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				W = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("W");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("S="))
			{
				string strName4 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				S = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("S");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("M="))
			{
				string strName5 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				M = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("DUTY"))
			{
				DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("DUTY");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("TEMP"))
			{
				TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("TEMP");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("NUMBER"))
			{
				NUMBER = Convert.ToInt32(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("CELL"))
			{
				CELL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("DP"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
		}

		private void ParseFeed(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string strName = array[i];
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				FEED.Add(strName);
			}
		}

		private void FindCorrectIndex(ref int index, string sSource, string keyName)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int startIndex = 0;
			while (!flag)
			{
				index = sSource.IndexOf(keyName, startIndex);
				for (int i = index + keyName.Length; i < sSource.Length; i++)
				{
					switch (sSource[i])
					{
					case '=':
						flag = true;
						break;
					default:
						startIndex = i;
						break;
					case ' ':
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				index = -1;
			}
		}
	}

	public class LNGHXOperation
	{
		public DoubleValue HLEAK { get; set; }
	}

	private QualifierValue _zones = new QualifierValue("OUTPUT", "5");

	private Dictionary<int, int> _HCellNumbers = new Dictionary<int, int>();

	private Dictionary<int, int> _CCellNumbers = new Dictionary<int, int>();

	public QualifierValue ZONES
	{
		get
		{
			return _zones;
		}
		set
		{
			_zones = value;
		}
	}

	public List<Cell> HOTS { get; set; }

	public List<Cell> COLDS { get; set; }

	public LNGHXOperation OPERATION { get; set; }

	public string PLOT { get; set; }

	public P2LNGHeatExchanger(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.LNGHEATEXCHANGER + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "LNGHeatExchanger", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
		{
			CUnitOperation.UID,
			CUnitOperation.NAME,
			CUnitOperation.ZONES
		}, text.ToUpper());
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		foreach (PropertyPair item4 in propertySplit)
		{
			list.Add(item4.Content);
		}
		string[] array = list.ToArray();
		foreach (string text2 in array)
		{
			if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UID))
			{
				base.UID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				NameHelper.NotesName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
				base.UID = NameHelper.ConvertName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.UID);
			}
			else if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NAME))
			{
				base.Name = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.NAME);
			}
			else if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ZONES))
			{
				ZONES = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(text2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.ZONES);
			}
		}
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string text3 = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text3, ' ').Trim();
				OPERATION.HLEAK = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(empty);
			}
			else if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HOT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text3, ' ').Trim();
				if (HOTS == null)
				{
					HOTS = new List<Cell>();
				}
				Cell item = new Cell(empty);
				HOTS.Add(item);
			}
			else if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COLD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text3, ' ').Trim();
				if (COLDS == null)
				{
					COLDS = new List<Cell>();
				}
				Cell item2 = new Cell(empty);
				COLDS.Add(item2);
			}
			else if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLOT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text3, ' ').Trim();
				PLOT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
			}
			else if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item3 = new DEFINE(empty, "LNG Heat Exchanger", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item3);
			}
			else if (text3.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text3, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger = new HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger();
			list.Add(hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.Name = base.Name;
			hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.ZONES = ZONES;
			if (PLOT != null && PLOT.Length > 0)
			{
				hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Plotting Results"));
			}
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.IsHeatLeak = true;
			}
			ConvertHotAndColdCell(hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger, list);
			ConvertHCurves(hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger, list);
			SetInletAndOutletStreams(hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.SeparateDefines();
			}
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "LNGHeatExchanger", base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void ConvertHotAndColdCell(HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger LNGHX, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl)
	{
		if (HOTS != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < HOTS.Count; i++)
			{
				Cell cell = HOTS[i];
				if (cell.FEED != null)
				{
					LNGHX.HotSidesInletStreams.Add(i, null);
					List<string> list = new List<string>();
					foreach (string item in cell.FEED)
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
					if (list.Count > 1)
					{
						LNGHX.InletStreams.Clear();
						foreach (string item2 in list)
						{
							LNGHX.InletStreams.Add(item2);
						}
						HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(LNGHX);
						if (hYSYSMixer != null)
						{
							objColl.Add(hYSYSMixer);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.METHOD))
							{
								hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = cell.METHOD;
							}
							hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
						}
						list.Clear();
						foreach (string inletStream in LNGHX.InletStreams)
						{
							list.Add(inletStream);
						}
					}
					LNGHX.HotSidesInletStreams[i] = list;
				}
				LNGHX.HotSidesOutletStreams.Add(i, null);
				Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.V))
				{
					list2.Add(cell.V);
					dictionary.Add("V", cell.V);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.L))
				{
					list2.Add(cell.L);
					dictionary.Add("L", cell.L);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.M))
				{
					list2.Add(cell.M);
					dictionary.Add("M", cell.M);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.W))
				{
					list2.Add(cell.W);
					dictionary.Add("W", cell.W);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.S))
				{
					list2.Add(cell.S);
					dictionary.Add("S", cell.S);
				}
				if (list2.Count > 1)
				{
					LNGHX.OutletStreams.Clear();
					foreach (string item3 in list2)
					{
						LNGHX.OutletStreams.Add(item3);
					}
					List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list3 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, LNGHX);
					bool flag = false;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.METHOD))
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					if (list3 != null)
					{
						foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item4 in list3)
						{
							objColl.Add(item4);
							if (flag)
							{
								item4.FluidPackage = cell.METHOD;
							}
							item4.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
						}
					}
					list2.Clear();
					foreach (string outletStream in LNGHX.OutletStreams)
					{
						list2.Add(outletStream);
					}
				}
				LNGHX.HotSidesOutletStreams[i] = list2;
				if (cell.DUTY != null)
				{
					LNGHX.HotSideDuties.Add(i, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, cell.DUTY));
				}
				if (cell.TEMPERATURE != null)
				{
					LNGHX.HotSideTemperatures.Add(i, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, cell.TEMPERATURE));
				}
				if (cell.CELL != null)
				{
					LNGHX.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "CELL"));
				}
				if (cell.DP != null && cell.DP.Value != 0.0)
				{
					LNGHX.HotSideDeltaPressures.Add(i, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, cell.DP));
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.METHOD))
				{
					LNGHX.HotSideMethods.Add(i, HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(cell.METHOD));
				}
				if (cell.NUMBER != 0 && !_HCellNumbers.ContainsKey(cell.NUMBER))
				{
					_HCellNumbers.Add(cell.NUMBER, i);
				}
			}
		}
		if (COLDS == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < COLDS.Count; j++)
		{
			Cell cell2 = COLDS[j];
			if (cell2.FEED != null)
			{
				LNGHX.ColdSidesInletStreams.Add(j, null);
				List<string> list4 = new List<string>();
				foreach (string item5 in cell2.FEED)
				{
					list4.Add(item5);
				}
				if (list4.Count > 1)
				{
					LNGHX.InletStreams.Clear();
					foreach (string item6 in list4)
					{
						LNGHX.InletStreams.Add(item6);
					}
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(LNGHX);
					if (hYSYSMixer2 != null)
					{
						objColl.Add(hYSYSMixer2);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.METHOD))
						{
							hYSYSMixer2.FluidPackage = cell2.METHOD;
						}
						hYSYSMixer2.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
					}
					list4.Clear();
					foreach (string inletStream2 in LNGHX.InletStreams)
					{
						list4.Add(inletStream2);
					}
				}
				LNGHX.ColdSidesInletStreams[j] = list4;
			}
			LNGHX.ColdSidesOutletStreams.Add(j, null);
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			List<string> list5 = new List<string>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.V))
			{
				list5.Add(cell2.V);
				dictionary2.Add("V", cell2.V);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.L))
			{
				list5.Add(cell2.L);
				dictionary2.Add("L", cell2.L);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.M))
			{
				list5.Add(cell2.M);
				dictionary2.Add("M", cell2.M);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.W))
			{
				list5.Add(cell2.W);
				dictionary2.Add("W", cell2.W);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.S))
			{
				list5.Add(cell2.S);
				dictionary2.Add("S", cell2.S);
			}
			if (list5.Count > 1)
			{
				LNGHX.OutletStreams.Clear();
				foreach (string item7 in list5)
				{
					LNGHX.OutletStreams.Add(item7);
				}
				List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list6 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary2, LNGHX);
				bool flag2 = false;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.METHOD))
				{
					flag2 = true;
				}
				if (list6 != null)
				{
					foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item8 in list6)
					{
						objColl.Add(item8);
						if (flag2)
						{
							item8.FluidPackage = cell2.METHOD;
						}
						item8.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
					}
				}
				list5.Clear();
				foreach (string outletStream2 in LNGHX.OutletStreams)
				{
					list5.Add(outletStream2);
				}
			}
			LNGHX.ColdSidesOutletStreams[j] = list5;
			if (cell2.DUTY != null)
			{
				LNGHX.ColdSideDuties.Add(j, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, cell2.DUTY));
			}
			if (cell2.TEMPERATURE != null)
			{
				LNGHX.ColdSideTemperatures.Add(j, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, cell2.TEMPERATURE));
			}
			if (cell2.CELL != null)
			{
				LNGHX.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "CELL"));
			}
			if (cell2.DP != null && cell2.DP.Value != 0.0)
			{
				LNGHX.ColdSideDeltaPressures.Add(j, UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, cell2.DP));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell2.METHOD))
			{
				LNGHX.ColdSideMethods.Add(j, HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(cell2.METHOD));
			}
			if (cell2.NUMBER != 0 && !_CCellNumbers.ContainsKey(cell2.NUMBER))
			{
				_CCellNumbers.Add(cell2.NUMBER, j);
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetInletAndOutletStreams(HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger LNGHX)
	{
		LNGHX.InletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (int key in LNGHX.HotSidesInletStreams.Keys)
		{
			foreach (string item in LNGHX.HotSidesInletStreams[key])
			{
				LNGHX.InletStreams.Add(item);
			}
		}
		foreach (int key2 in LNGHX.ColdSidesInletStreams.Keys)
		{
			foreach (string item2 in LNGHX.ColdSidesInletStreams[key2])
			{
				LNGHX.InletStreams.Add(item2);
			}
		}
		LNGHX.OutletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (int key3 in LNGHX.HotSidesOutletStreams.Keys)
		{
			foreach (string item3 in LNGHX.HotSidesOutletStreams[key3])
			{
				LNGHX.OutletStreams.Add(item3);
			}
		}
		foreach (int key4 in LNGHX.ColdSidesOutletStreams.Keys)
		{
			foreach (string item4 in LNGHX.ColdSidesOutletStreams[key4])
			{
				LNGHX.OutletStreams.Add(item4);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHCurves(HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger LNGHX, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl)
	{
		P2HCURVE p2HCURVE = HYSYSObjectHelper.RetrieveHCurveObject(LNGHX.UID);
		if (p2HCURVE == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < p2HCURVE.UnitCurves.Count; i++)
		{
			if (string.Compare(p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i].UID, LNGHX.UID, ignoreCase: true) != 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			P2HCURVE.UOCurve uOCurve = p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i];
			int key = int.Parse(uOCurve.ConnectSide);
			int num = -1;
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (_CCellNumbers.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (_HCellNumbers.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				num = _HCellNumbers[key];
				HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = HYSYSObjectHelper.AddHeaterToUO(LNGHX.UID, LNGHX.HotSidesInletStreams[num][0], LNGHX.HotSidesOutletStreams[num][0]);
				if (hYSYSHeatExchanger != null)
				{
					p2HCURVE.LinkedIDs.Remove(LNGHX.UID);
					p2HCURVE.LinkedIDs.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID);
					uOCurve.UID = hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID;
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.HcurvePlotPoint = uOCurve.POINTS;
					if (LNGHX.HotSideMethods.ContainsKey(num) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(LNGHX.HotSideMethods[num]))
					{
						hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideMethod = LNGHX.HotSideMethods[num];
					}
					objColl.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				continue;
			}
			num = _CCellNumbers[key];
			HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.AddHeaterToUO(LNGHX.UID, LNGHX.ColdSidesInletStreams[num][0], LNGHX.ColdSidesOutletStreams[num][0]);
			if (hYSYSHeatExchanger2 != null)
			{
				p2HCURVE.LinkedIDs.Remove(LNGHX.UID);
				p2HCURVE.LinkedIDs.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger2.UID);
				uOCurve.UID = hYSYSHeatExchanger2.UID;
				hYSYSHeatExchanger2.HcurvePlotPoint = uOCurve.POINTS;
				if (LNGHX.ColdSideMethods.ContainsKey(num) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(LNGHX.ColdSideMethods[num]))
				{
					hYSYSHeatExchanger2.ColdSideMethod = LNGHX.ColdSideMethods[num];
				}
				objColl.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger2);
				hYSYSHeatExchanger2.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
		}
	}
}
public class P2RigorousHeatExchanger : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class TubeConfiguration : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _LENGTH = new DoubleValue("FT", 20.0);

		private DoubleValue _OD = new DoubleValue("IN", 0.75);

		private DoubleValue _ID = new DoubleValue("IN", 0.584);

		private string _NUMBER = "254";

		private string _PASS = "2";

		private string _PATTERN = "90";

		private DoubleValue _PITCH = new DoubleValue("IN", 1.0);

		private string _MATERIAL = "CS";

		private DoubleValue _FOUL = new DoubleValue("HFF/B", 0.002);

		private DoubleValue _LAYER = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _HSCALE = new DoubleValue("", 1.0);

		private DoubleValue _AROUGH = new DoubleValue("IN", 0.0018);

		private DoubleValue _DPSCALE = new DoubleValue("", 1.0);

		public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

		public string V { get; set; }

		public string L { get; set; }

		public string W { get; set; }

		public string S { get; set; }

		public string M { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue LENGTH
		{
			get
			{
				return _LENGTH;
			}
			set
			{
				_LENGTH = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue OD
		{
			get
			{
				return _OD;
			}
			set
			{
				_OD = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue ID
		{
			get
			{
				return _ID;
			}
			set
			{
				_ID = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue THICKNESS { get; set; }

		public string BWG { get; set; }

		public string NUMBER
		{
			get
			{
				return _NUMBER;
			}
			set
			{
				_NUMBER = value;
			}
		}

		public TubeConfiguration(string sSource)
		{
			FEED = new List<string>();
			List<string> propertyList = new List<string>
			{
				"FEED=",
				"V=",
				"L=",
				"W=",
				"S=",
				"M=",
				"LENG",
				"OD",
				"OD ",
				"OD=",
				"ID",
				"ID ",
				"ID=",
				"THIC",
				"THICKNESS=",
				"THICKNESS(",
				"THICKNESS ",
				"THICK=",
				"THICK(",
				"THICK ",
				"BWG",
				"NUM",
				CUnitOperation.METHOD,
				"METHOD=",
				"METHOD ",
				"PASS=",
				"PASS ",
				"PATT",
				"PITC",
				"MATERIAL=",
				"MATERIAL ",
				"MATE=",
				"MATE ",
				"LABE",
				"LABEL=",
				"LABEL ",
				"DEN",
				"DENS ",
				"DENS=",
				"COND",
				"FOUL=",
				"FOUL ",
				"LAYE",
				"HI",
				"HSCA",
				"AROU",
				"AROUGHNESS=",
				"RROU",
				"RROUGHNESS=",
				"DPUN",
				"DPSC",
				"DPSH",
				"DPSCALE=",
				"DPSHELL=",
				"DPSCALE ",
				"DPSHELL "
			};
			string text = sSource.ToUpper();
			text = text.Replace("PROD=", "M=");
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(propertyList, text))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("FEED") || !strProperty.ToUpper().Contains('='))
			{
				ParseFeed(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("V="))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				V = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("V");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("L="))
			{
				string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				L = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("L");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("W="))
			{
				string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				W = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("W");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("S="))
			{
				string strName4 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				S = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("S");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("M="))
			{
				string strName5 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				M = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("LENG"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					LENGTH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("OD"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					OD = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					bODSpecified = true;
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("ID"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					ID = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					bIDSpecified = true;
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("THIC"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					THICKNESS = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					bThicknessSpecified = true;
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("BWG"))
			{
				BWG = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				bThicknessSpecified = true;
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("NUM"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					NUMBER = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("PASS"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					PASS = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("PATT"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					PATTERN = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("PITC"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					PITCH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("MATE"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					MATERIAL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("LABE"))
			{
				LABEL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DEN"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					DENSITY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("COND"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					CONDUCTIVITY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("FOUL"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					FOUL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("LAYE"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					LAYER = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HI"))
			{
				HI = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HSCA"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					HSCALE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("AROU"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					AROUGH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("RROU"))
			{
				RROUGH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DPSH"))
			{
				DPSHELL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DPUN"))
			{
				DPUNIT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DPSC"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					DPSCALE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
		}

		private void ParseFeed(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
				if (array2.Length != 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						string strName = array2[j];
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						FEED.Add(strName);
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class FinDefinition
	{
		private string _NUMBER = "19";

		private DoubleValue _HEIGHT = new DoubleValue("IN", 0.0625);

		private DoubleValue _THICKNESS = new DoubleValue("IN", 0.5);

		private DoubleValue _ROOT = new DoubleValue("IN", 0.625);

		public string NUMBER
		{
			get
			{
				return _NUMBER;
			}
			set
			{
				_NUMBER = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue AREA { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue HEIGHT
		{
			get
			{
				return _HEIGHT;
			}
			set
			{
				_HEIGHT = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue THICKNESS
		{
			get
			{
				return _THICKNESS;
			}
			set
			{
				_THICKNESS = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue ROOT
		{
			get
			{
				return _ROOT;
			}
			set
			{
				_ROOT = value;
			}
		}

		public bool bLowFinTube { get; set; }

		public FinDefinition(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string> { "NUM", "AREA", "HEIG", "THIC", "ROOT" }, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("NUM"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					NUMBER = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					bLowFinTube = true;
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("AREA"))
			{
				AREA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				bLowFinTube = true;
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HEIG"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					HEIGHT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					bLowFinTube = true;
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("THIC"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					THICKNESS = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					bLowFinTube = true;
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("ROOT") && strProperty.Contains('='))
			{
				ROOT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				bLowFinTube = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class TubeNozzle
	{
		public DoubleValue InletNozzleID { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue OutletNozzleID { get; set; }

		public bool bIgnored { get; set; }

		public TubeNozzle(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string> { "ID", "NONE" }, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith("ID"))
			{
				string unit = string.Empty;
				string str = strProperty;
				string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, '=');
				if (text.Contains('('))
				{
					unit = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text);
				}
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(str);
				if (array.Length != 0)
				{
					InletNozzleID = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[0].Trim()));
				}
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					OutletNozzleID = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[1].Trim()));
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("NONE"))
			{
				bIgnored = true;
			}
		}
	}

			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(propertyList, text))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("FEED") || !strProperty.ToUpper().Contains('='))
			{
				ParseFeed(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("V="))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				V = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("V");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("L="))
			{
				string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				L = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("L");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("W="))
			{
				string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				W = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("W");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("S="))
			{
				string strName4 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				S = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("S");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("M="))
			{
				string strName5 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				M = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("ID"))
			{
				if (!strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					return;
				}
				ID = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ID.Unit))
				{
					switch ((P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Title] as P2Title).DefaultDimensional)
					{
					case Dimensional.English:
						ID.Unit = "IN";
						break;
					case Dimensional.Metric:
					case Dimensional.SI:
						ID.Unit = "MM";
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("SERI"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					SERIES = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("PARA"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					PARALLEL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("SEAL"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					SEALS = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("MATE"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					MATERIAL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("LABE"))
			{
				LABEL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DEN"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					DENSITY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("FOUL"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					FOUL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("LAYE"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					LAYER = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HO"))
			{
				HO = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HSCA"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					HSCALE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DPSH"))
			{
				DPSHELL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DPUN"))
			{
				DPUNIT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("DPSC"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					DPSCALE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
		}

		public void SetDefaultID(bool bFinTube)
		{
			if (_ID == null)
			{
				if (bFinTube)
				{
					_ID = new DoubleValue("IN", 15.0);
				}
				else
				{
					_ID = new DoubleValue("IN", 23.0);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ParseFeed(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
				if (array2.Length != 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						string strName = array2[j];
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						FEED.Add(strName);
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class BaffleDefinition
	{
		private string _SEGMENT = "SINGLE";

		private DoubleValue _CUT = new DoubleValue("", 20.0);

		private DoubleValue _THICKNESS = new DoubleValue("IN", 0.1875);

		private DoubleValue _SPACING;

		private DoubleValue _OUTSPACING;

		public string SEGMENT
		{
			get
			{
				return _SEGMENT;
			}
			set
			{
				_SEGMENT = value;
			}
		}

		public bool bIgnored { get; set; }

		public string NTIW { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue CUT
		{
			get
			{
				return _CUT;
			}
			set
			{
				_CUT = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue NFAR { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue THICKNESS
		{
			get
			{
				return _THICKNESS;
			}
			set
			{
				_THICKNESS = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue SHEET { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue SPACING
		{
			get
			{
				return _SPACING;
			}
			set
			{
				_SPACING = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue INSPACING { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue OUTSPACING
		{
			get
			{
				return _OUTSPACING;
			}
			set
			{
				_OUTSPACING = value;
			}
		}

		public BaffleDefinition()
		{
		}

		public BaffleDefinition(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				"SEGM", "NTIW", "CUT", "NFAR", "THIC", "SHEE", "SPAC=", "SPAC ", "SPACING=", "SPACING ",
				"SPAC(", "SPACING(", "INSP=", "INSP ", "INSPACING=", "INSP(", "INSPACING(", "INSPACING ", "OUTS=", "OUTS ",
				"OUTSPACING=", "OUTSPACING ", "OUTS(", "OUTSPACING(", "NONE"
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("SEGM"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					SEGMENT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("NTIW"))
			{
				NTIW = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("CUT"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					CUT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
					if (CUT.Value < 1.0)
					{
						CUT.Value *= 100.0;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("NFAR"))
			{
				NFAR = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("THIC"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					THICKNESS = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("SHEE"))
			{
				SHEET = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("SPAC"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					SPACING = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("INSP"))
			{
				INSPACING = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("OUTS"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					OUTSPACING = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("NONE"))
			{
				bIgnored = true;
			}
		}

		public void SetDefaultSpacingData(DoubleValue dVal)
		{
			if (_SPACING == null)
			{
				_SPACING = new DoubleValue(dVal.Unit, dVal.Value * 0.2);
			}
		}

		public void SetDefaultOutSpacingData(bool bFinTube)
		{
			if (_OUTSPACING == null)
			{
				if (bFinTube)
				{
					_OUTSPACING = new DoubleValue("IN", 3.0);
				}
				else
				{
					_OUTSPACING = new DoubleValue("IN", 5.0);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class ShellNozzle
	{
		private string _TYPE = "CONVENTIONAL";

		public bool bIgnored { get; set; }

		public string TYPE
		{
			get
			{
				return _TYPE;
			}
			set
			{
				_TYPE = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue InletID { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue OutletID { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue InletLENGTH { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue OutletLENGTH { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue InletAREA { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue OutletAREA { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue InletCLEARANCE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue OutletCLEARANCE { get; set; }

		public ShellNozzle(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string> { "TYPE", "ID", "LENG", "AREA", "CLEA", "NONE" }, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strPropName)
		{
			if (strPropName.ToUpper().StartsWith("NONE"))
			{
				bIgnored = true;
				return;
			}
			if (strPropName.ToUpper().StartsWith("TYPE"))
			{
				if (strPropName.Contains('='))
				{
					TYPE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strPropName).Trim();
				}
				return;
			}
			string unit = string.Empty;
			string str = strPropName;
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(ref str, '=');
			if (text.ToUpper().Contains("("))
			{
				unit = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(text);
			}
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(str);
			if (array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("ID"))
			{
				InletID = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[0].ToString().Trim()));
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					OutletID = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[1].ToString().Trim()));
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("LENG"))
			{
				InletLENGTH = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[0].ToString().Trim()));
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					OutletLENGTH = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[1].ToString().Trim()));
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("AREA"))
			{
				InletAREA = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[0].ToString().Trim()));
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					OutletAREA = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[1].ToString().Trim()));
				}
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().StartsWith("CLEA"))
			{
				InletCLEARANCE = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[0].ToString().Trim()));
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					OutletCLEARANCE = new DoubleValue(unit, double.Parse(array[1].ToString().Trim()));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class AttachedConfiguration : PropertyItem
	{
		public string COLUMN { get; set; }

		public string TYPE { get; set; }

		public string TRAY { get; set; }

		public string FTRAY { get; set; }

		public string PHASE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue RATE { get; set; }

		public AttachedConfiguration(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.COLUMN,
				CUnitOperation.TYPE,
				CUnitOperation.TRAY,
				"FTRA",
				CUnitOperation.PHASE,
				CUnitOperation.RATE
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith("COLUMN"))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: false);
				strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: false);
				COLUMN = strName;
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("TYPE"))
			{
				TYPE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("TRAY"))
			{
				TRAY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("FTRAY"))
			{
				FTRAY = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("PHASE"))
			{
				PHASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("RATE"))
			{
				RATE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

				SetValue(item.Content);
			}

		{
			if (strProperty.Contains('='))
			{
				if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TEMA") || strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TYPE"))

					ORIENTATION = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);

				else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("FLOW"))
				{
					FLOW = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("UEST"))
				{
					UESTIMATE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("USCA"))
				{
					USCALE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class OperatingSpecification

		{

			{
				DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);

			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("STEM"))
			{
				STEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);

	public string PLOT { get; set; }

	public TubeConfiguration TUBES { get; set; }

	public FinDefinition FINS { get; set; }

	public TubeNozzle TNOZZLE { get; set; }

	public ShellConfiguration SHELL { get; set; }

	public BaffleDefinition BAFFLE { get; set; }

	public ShellNozzle SNOZZLE { get; set; }

	public AttachedConfiguration ATTACH { get; set; }

	public CalType CalOptionTYPE { get; set; }

	public SimulationOption HXCALC { get; set; }

	public OperatingSpecification OPERATION { get; set; }

	public OutputReport PRINT { get; set; }

	public string DATA { get; set; }

	public bool IsFinTube { get; set; }

	public P2RigorousHeatExchanger()
	{
	}

	public P2RigorousHeatExchanger(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetData(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.RIGOROUSHEATEXCHANGER + base.UID, this);
			if (FINS != null)
			{
				IsFinTube = true;
			}
			if (SHELL == null)
			{
				SHELL = new ShellConfiguration();
			}
			SHELL.SetDefaultID(IsFinTube);
			if (BAFFLE == null)
			{
				BAFFLE = new BaffleDefinition();
			}
			if (HXCALC == null)
			{
				HXCALC = new SimulationOption();
			}
			if (CalOptionTYPE == null)
			{
				CalOptionTYPE = new CalType();
			}
			BAFFLE.SetDefaultSpacingData(SHELL.ID);
			BAFFLE.SetDefaultOutSpacingData(IsFinTube);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "HXRIG", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetData(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string str = obj.Name.ToUpper();
		string strSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (PropertyPair item2 in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
		{
			CUnitOperation.UID,
			CUnitOperation.NAME,
			CUnitOperation.PLOT
		}, strSource))
		{
			string text = item2.Content.ToString().ToUpper();
			if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UID))
			{
				base.UID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				NameHelper.NotesName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
				base.UID = NameHelper.ConvertName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NAME))
			{
				base.Name = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLOT))
			{
				PLOT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
		}
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			str = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TUBES))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				TUBES = new TubeConfiguration(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FINS))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				FINS = new FinDefinition(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TNOZZLE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				TNOZZLE = new TubeNozzle(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SHELL) || str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SHEL))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				SHELL = new ShellConfiguration(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BAFFLE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				BAFFLE = new BaffleDefinition(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SNOZZLE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				SNOZZLE = new ShellNozzle(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ATTACH))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				ATTACH = new AttachedConfiguration(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TYPE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				CalOptionTYPE = new CalType(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HXCALC))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				HXCALC = new SimulationOption(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				OPERATION = new OperatingSpecification(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRINT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				PRINT = new OutputReport(empty);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DATA))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				DATA = empty;
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "HX", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (str.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(str, ' ');
				base.METHOD = empty;
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger();
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.MapForHXRIG = true;
		hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger;
		try
		{
			list.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.Name = base.Name;
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			if (TUBES != null)
			{
				ConvertTubeConfig(hYSYSHeatExchanger, list);
			}
			if (FINS != null)
			{
				ConvertFin(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (TNOZZLE != null)
			{
				ConvertTubeNozzle(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (SHELL != null)
			{
				ConvertShellConfig(hYSYSHeatExchanger, list);
			}
			if (BAFFLE != null)
			{
				ConvertBaffle(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (SNOZZLE != null)
			{
				ConvertShellNozzle(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (CalOptionTYPE != null)
			{
				ConvertCalculationType(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (HXCALC != null)
			{
				ConvertHXCalc(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				ConvertOperation(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetInletAndOutletStreams();
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.SeparateDefines();
			}
			ConvertHCurves(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "HXRIG", base.UID));
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}

	private void ConvertTubeConfig(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl)
	{
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		if (TUBES.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < TUBES.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Add(TUBES.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (TUBES.V != null && TUBES.V.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(TUBES.V);
			dictionary.Add("V", TUBES.V);
		}
		if (TUBES.L != null && TUBES.L.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(TUBES.L);
			dictionary.Add("L", TUBES.L);
		}
		if (TUBES.W != null && TUBES.W.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(TUBES.W);
			dictionary.Add("W", TUBES.W);
		}
		if (TUBES.S != null && TUBES.S.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(TUBES.S);
			dictionary.Add("S", TUBES.S);
		}
		if (TUBES.M != null && TUBES.M.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(TUBES.M);
			dictionary.Add("M", TUBES.M);
		}
		if (TUBES.METHOD != null && TUBES.METHOD.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(TUBES.METHOD);
		}
		if (TUBES.DPSHELL != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TSDPSHELL = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, TUBES.DPSHELL);
		}
		if (TUBES.DPUNIT != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TSDPUNIT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, TUBES.DPUNIT);
		}
		if (TUBES.DPSCALE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TSPDAdjustScaleNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (TUBES.FEED != null && TUBES.FEED.Count > 1)
		{
			heatExchanger.InletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string coldSideInletStream in heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.InletStreams.Add(coldSideInletStream);
			}
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(heatExchanger);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				objColl.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
			heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string inletStream in heatExchanger.InletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Add(inletStream);
			}
		}
		if (TUBES.AddedPropertyList != null && TUBES.AddedPropertyList.Count > 1)
		{
			heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string coldSideOutletStream in heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Add(coldSideOutletStream);
			}
			List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, heatExchanger);
			if (list != null)
			{
				foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item in list)
				{
					objColl.Add(item);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod))
					{
						item.FluidPackage = heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod;
					}
					item.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
				}
			}
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string outletStream in heatExchanger.OutletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(outletStream);
			}
		}
		if (TUBES.LENGTH != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeLength = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, TUBES.LENGTH);
		}
		if (TUBES.OD != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeOD = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, TUBES.OD);
		}
		if (TUBES.ID != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeID = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, TUBES.ID);
		}
		if (TUBES.THICKNESS != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, TUBES.THICKNESS);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBES.BWG))
		{
			ConvertBWG(TUBES.BWG, heatExchanger);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBES.NUMBER) && int.TryParse(TUBES.NUMBER, out var result))
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeNumberPerShell = result;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBES.PASS) && int.TryParse(TUBES.PASS, out var result2))
		{
			heatExchanger.TubePasses = result2;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBES.PATTERN))
		{
			if (TUBES.PATTERN == "30")
			{
				heatExchanger.TubeLayout = TubeLayoutEnum.Triangular;
			}
			else if (TUBES.PATTERN == "45")
			{
				heatExchanger.TubeLayout = TubeLayoutEnum.SquareRotated;
			}
			else if (TUBES.PATTERN == "60")
			{
				heatExchanger.TubeLayout = TubeLayoutEnum.TriangularRotated;
			}
			else if (TUBES.PATTERN == "90")
			{
				heatExchanger.TubeLayout = TubeLayoutEnum.Square;
			}
		}
		if (TUBES.PITCH != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubePitch = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, TUBES.PITCH);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBES.MATERIAL) && TUBES.MATERIAL != "CS")
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TubeMaterialNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TUBES.LABEL))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TubeMaterialLabelNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (TUBES.DENSITY != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeMaterialDensity = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, TUBES.DENSITY);
		}
		if (TUBES.CONDUCTIVITY != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeMaterialConductivity = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.ThermalConductivity, TUBES.CONDUCTIVITY);
		}
		if (TUBES.FOUL != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeFoul = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FoulingCoefficient, TUBES.FOUL);
		}
		if (TUBES.LAYER != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TSFoulingLayerNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (TUBES.HI != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubesideFilmCoeff = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HeatTransferCoefficient, TUBES.HI);
		}
		if (TUBES.HSCALE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TubeHSCALENotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (TUBES.AROUGH != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TubeAbsoluteRoughness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, TUBES.AROUGH);
		}
		if (TUBES.RROUGH != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TubeRROUGHNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!TUBES.bThicknessSpecified)
		{
			heatExchanger.CalculateTube = TubeDiameterCalEnum.NONE;
		}
		else
		{
			heatExchanger.CalculateTube = TubeDiameterCalEnum.ID;
		}
		if (TUBES.bIDSpecified && TUBES.bThicknessSpecified)
		{
			heatExchanger.CalculateTube = TubeDiameterCalEnum.OD;
		}
		if (TUBES.bODSpecified && TUBES.bThicknessSpecified)
		{
			heatExchanger.CalculateTube = TubeDiameterCalEnum.ID;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertBWG(string BWG, HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		switch (BWG)
		{
		case "00000":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 12.7);
			break;
		case "0000":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 11.532);
			break;
		case "000":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 10.795);
			break;
		case "00":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 9.652);
			break;
		case "0":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 8.639);
			break;
		case "1":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 7.62);
			break;
		case "2":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 7.214);
			break;
		case "3":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 6.579);
			break;
		case "4":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 6.045);
			break;
		case "5":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 5.588);
			break;
		case "6":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 5.156);
			break;
		case "7":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 4.572);
			break;
		case "8":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 4.191);
			break;
		case "9":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 3.759);
			break;
		case "10":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 3.404);
			break;

			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 3.048);

			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 2.769);

			break;
		case "14":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 2.108);
			break;
		case "15":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 1.829);
			break;
		case "16":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 1.651);
			break;
		case "17":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 1.473);
			break;
		case "18":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 1.245);
			break;
		case "19":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 1.067);
			break;
		case "20":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.839);
			break;
		case "21":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.813);
			break;
		case "22":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.711);
			break;
