			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.635);
			break;
		case "24":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.559);
			break;
		case "25":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.508);
			break;
		case "26":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.457);
			break;
		case "27":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.406);
			break;
		case "28":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.356);
			break;
		case "29":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.33);
			break;
		case "30":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.305);
			break;
		case "31":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.254);
			break;
		case "32":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.229);
			break;
		case "33":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.203);
			break;
		case "34":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.178);
			break;
		case "35":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.127);
			break;
		case "36":
			heatExchanger.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue("mm", 0.102);
			break;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertFin(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (FINS.bLowFinTube)
		{
			heatExchanger.bLowFinTube = true;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(FINS.NUMBER) && int.TryParse(FINS.NUMBER, out var result))
		{
			heatExchanger.FinNumber = result;
		}
		if (FINS.AREA != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("FinTubeTAreaNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (FINS.HEIGHT != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.FinHeight = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, FINS.HEIGHT);
		}
		if (FINS.THICKNESS != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.FinThickness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, FINS.THICKNESS);
		}
		if (FINS.ROOT != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.FinRootDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, FINS.ROOT);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertTubeNozzle(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (TNOZZLE.InletNozzleID != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TSNozzleInsideDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, TNOZZLE.InletNozzleID);
		}
		if (TNOZZLE.OutletNozzleID != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.TSNozzleOutsideDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, TNOZZLE.OutletNozzleID);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertShellConfig(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl)
	{
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		if (SHELL.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < SHELL.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Add(SHELL.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (SHELL.V != null && SHELL.V.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(SHELL.V);
			dictionary.Add("V", SHELL.V);
		}
		if (SHELL.L != null && SHELL.L.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(SHELL.L);
			dictionary.Add("L", SHELL.L);
		}
		if (SHELL.W != null && SHELL.W.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(SHELL.W);
			dictionary.Add("W", SHELL.W);
		}
		if (SHELL.S != null && SHELL.S.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(SHELL.S);
			dictionary.Add("S", SHELL.S);
		}
		if (SHELL.M != null && SHELL.M.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(SHELL.M);
			dictionary.Add("M", SHELL.M);
		}
		if (SHELL.METHOD != null && SHELL.METHOD.Length > 0)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideMethod = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(SHELL.METHOD);
		}
		if (SHELL.DPSHELL != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.SSDPSHELL = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, SHELL.DPSHELL);
		}
		if (SHELL.DPUNIT != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.SSDPUNIT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, SHELL.DPUNIT);
		}
		if (SHELL.DPSCALE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ShellDPSCALENotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SHELL.FEED != null && SHELL.FEED.Count > 1)
		{
			heatExchanger.InletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string hotSideInletStream in heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.InletStreams.Add(hotSideInletStream);
			}
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(heatExchanger);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				objColl.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(heatExchanger.HotSideMethod))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = heatExchanger.HotSideMethod;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
			heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string inletStream in heatExchanger.InletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Add(inletStream);
			}
		}
		if (SHELL != null && SHELL.AddedPropertyList.Count > 1)
		{
			heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string hotSideOutletStream in heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Add(hotSideOutletStream);
			}
			List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, heatExchanger);
			if (list != null)
			{
				foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item in list)
				{
					objColl.Add(item);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(heatExchanger.HotSideMethod))
					{
						item.FluidPackage = heatExchanger.HotSideMethod;
					}
					item.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
				}
			}
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string outletStream in heatExchanger.OutletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(outletStream);
			}
		}
		if (SHELL.ID != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellInnerDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, SHELL.ID);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SHELL.SERIES) && int.TryParse(SHELL.SERIES, out var result))
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellInSeries = result;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SHELL.PARALLEL) && int.TryParse(SHELL.PARALLEL, out var result2))
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellInParallel = result2;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SHELL.SEALS))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ShellSEALSNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SHELL.MATERIAL))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ShellMaterialNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SHELL.LABEL))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ShellMLabelNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SHELL.DENSITY != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ShellMDensityNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SHELL.FOUL != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellFouling = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FoulingCoefficient, SHELL.FOUL);
		}
		if (SHELL.LAYER != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ShellFoulingLayerNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SHELL.HO != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellsideFilmCoeff = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HeatTransferCoefficient, SHELL.HO);
		}
		if (SHELL.HSCALE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ShellHSCALENotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertBaffle(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(BAFFLE.SEGMENT))
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellBaffleType = BAFFLE.SEGMENT;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(BAFFLE.NTIW))
		{
			heatExchanger.NoTubeInWindow = true;
		}
		if (BAFFLE.CUT != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellBaffleCut = new DoubleValue("", BAFFLE.CUT.Value);
		}
		if (BAFFLE.NFAR != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("BaffleNFARNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (BAFFLE.THICKNESS != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellBaffleThickness = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, BAFFLE.THICKNESS);
		}
		if (BAFFLE.SHEET != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("BaffleSheetNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (BAFFLE.SPACING != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellBaffleSpacing = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, BAFFLE.SPACING);
		}
		if (BAFFLE.INSPACING != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellBaffleInSpacing = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, BAFFLE.INSPACING);
		}
		if (BAFFLE.OUTSPACING != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellBaffleOutSpacing = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, BAFFLE.OUTSPACING);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertShellNozzle(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SNOZZLE.TYPE))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SNozzleNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.InletID != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellNozzleInnerDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, SNOZZLE.InletID);
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.OutletID != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellNozzleOuterDiameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, SNOZZLE.OutletID);
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.InletLENGTH != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SNozzleInLenNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.OutletLENGTH != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SNozzleOutLenNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.InletAREA != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SNozzleInAreaNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.OutletAREA != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SNozzleOutAreaNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.InletCLEARANCE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SNozzleInClearNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (SNOZZLE.OutletCLEARANCE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SNozzleOutClearNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertCalculationType(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(CalOptionTYPE.TEMA))
		{
			heatExchanger.TEMAType = CalOptionTYPE.TEMA;
		}
		if (CalOptionTYPE.AREA != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EffectiveAreaNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(CalOptionTYPE.ORIENTATION))
		{
			heatExchanger.ExchangerOrientation = CalOptionTYPE.ORIENTATION;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(CalOptionTYPE.FLOW))
		{
			heatExchanger.FlowDirection = CalOptionTYPE.FLOW;
		}
		if (CalOptionTYPE.UESTIMATE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UEstimateNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (CalOptionTYPE.USCALE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UScaleNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHXCalc(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(HXCALC.DPSMETHOD))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("DPSMETHODNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(HXCALC.DPTMETHOD))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("DPTMETHODNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(HXCALC.HTCMETHOD))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HTCMETHODNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(HXCALC.MODEL))
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CALMODELNotConvert"), base.UID));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(HXCALC.HOTSIDE) && HXCALC.HOTSIDE.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("T"))
		{
			heatExchanger.bHotSideIsTube = true;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertOperation(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (OPERATION.DUTY != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, OPERATION.DUTY);
		}
		if (OPERATION.STEMP != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, OPERATION.STEMP);
		}
		if (OPERATION.TTEMP != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, OPERATION.TTEMP);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHCurves(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		P2HCURVE p2HCURVE = HYSYSObjectHelper.RetrieveHCurveObject(heatExchanger.UID);
		if (p2HCURVE == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < p2HCURVE.UnitCurves.Count; i++)
		{
			if (string.Compare(p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i].UID, heatExchanger.UID, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				P2HCURVE.UOCurve uOCurve = p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[0];
				heatExchanger.HcurvePlotPoint = uOCurve.POINTS;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
}
internal class P2SimpleHeatExchanger : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Cell : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

		public string V { get; set; }

		public string L { get; set; }

		public string W { get; set; }

		public string S { get; set; }

		public string M { get; set; }

		public string METHOD { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith("V="))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				V = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("V");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("L="))
			{
				string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				L = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("L");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("W="))
			{
				string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				W = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("W");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("S="))
			{
				string strName4 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				S = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("S");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("M="))
			{
				string strName5 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				M = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith("DP"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
		}

		public Cell(string sSource)
		{
			FEED = new List<string>();
			List<string> list = new List<string>
			{
				"FEED=",
				"V=",
				"L=",
				"W=",
				"S=",
				"M=",
				"DP",
				CUnitOperation.METHOD
			};
			string strSource = sSource.Replace(" ", "").ToUpper();
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, strSource);
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			string text = string.Empty;
			int index = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				num++;
				if (item.Name == "FEED=")
				{
					flag = true;
					index = sSource.IndexOf("FEED=");
					if (index < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref index, sSource, "FEED");
						if (num == propertySplit.Count && index > 0)
						{
							text = sSource.Substring(0, index);
							sSource = sSource.Remove(index);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					num2 = sSource.IndexOf(item.Name);
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						FindCorrectIndex(ref num2, sSource, item.Name.Replace("=", ""));
					}
					if (index > -1 && num2 > index)
					{
						text = sSource.Substring(index, num2 - index);
						sSource = sSource.Remove(index, num2 - index);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ParseFeed(text);
			}
			propertySplit.Clear();
			list.Remove("FEED=");
			propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(list, sSource.Replace(" ", ""));
			foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
			{
				SetValue(item2.Content);
			}
		}

		private void ParseFeed(string strProperty)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim());
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
				if (array2.Length != 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						string strName = array2[j];
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						FEED.Add(strName);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void FindCorrectIndex(ref int index, string sSource, string keyName)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int startIndex = 0;
			while (!flag)
			{
				index = sSource.IndexOf(keyName, startIndex);
				for (int i = index + keyName.Length; i < sSource.Length; i++)
				{
					switch (sSource[i])
					{
					case '=':
						flag = true;
						break;
					default:
						startIndex = i;
						break;
					case ' ':
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				index = -1;
			}
		}
	}

	public class HeatExchangerPerformance : PropertyItem
	{

		public DoubleValue HOCI { get; set; }

			{
				HICO = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HOCO"))
			{
				HOCO = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TMIN"))
			{
				TMIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("MITA"))
			{
				MITA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HTEM"))
			{
				HTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("CTEM"))
			{
				CTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HLFR"))
			{
				HLFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("CLFR"))
			{
				CLFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HDTB"))
			{
				HDTBB = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("CDTB"))
			{
				CDTBB = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HDTA"))
			{
				HDTAD = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("CDTA"))
			{
				CDTAD = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("UA"))
			{
				UA = ParseUtilty.GetTwoUnitsDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TOL"))
			{
				TOLERANCE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);

	public class ExchangerUtility : PropertyItem
	{
		public string UtilityName { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue InletTemperature { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue OutletTemperature { get; set; }

		public string HEATINGMEDIUM { get; set; }

		public string REFRIGERANT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue SaturatedTemperature { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue SaturatedPressure { get; set; }

		public string REFRIGENTCOMP { get; set; }

		public ExchangerUtility(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.WATER,
				CUnitOperation.TIN,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				"TIN=",
				"TIN ",
				"AIR",
				"STEA",
				"HEAT",
				"TSAT",
				"PSAT",
				"REFR",
				"COMP"
			}, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("WATE") || strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("AIR") || strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("STEA"))
			{
				string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
				UtilityName = strName;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("UTILITY");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TIN"))
			{
				InletTemperature = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TEMP"))
			{
				OutletTemperature = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TSAT"))
			{
				SaturatedTemperature = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("PSAT"))
			{
				SaturatedPressure = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("HEAT"))
			{
				string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				strName2 = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
				HEATINGMEDIUM = strName2;
				UtilityName = "HEATINGMEDIUM";
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("HEATINGMEDIUM");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("REFR"))
			{
				string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty).Trim();
				NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				strName3 = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
				REFRIGERANT = strName3;
				UtilityName = "REFRIGERANT";
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("REFRIGERANT");
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("COMP"))
			{
				REFRIGENTCOMP = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
		}
	}

	public class AttachedConfiguration : PropertyItem
	{

	public class ConfigurationOptions
	{
		private string _FlowPattern = "COUNTERCURRENT";

		public string _TPASS = "2";

		public string _SPASS = "1";

		public string FlowPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return _FlowPattern;
			}
			set
			{
				_FlowPattern = value;
			}
		}

		public string TPASS
		{
			get
			{
				return _TPASS;
			}
			set
			{
				_TPASS = value;
			}
		}

		public string SPASS
		{
			get
			{
				return _SPASS;
			}
			set
			{
				_SPASS = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue FT { get; set; }

		public TwoUnitsDoubleValue UAMAX { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue U { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue AREA { get; set; }

		public ConfigurationOptions(string sSource)
		{
			foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string> { "COCU", "COUN", "TPAS", "SPAS", "FT", "UAMA", "U=", "AREA" }, sSource))
			{
				SetValue(item.Content);
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("COCU"))
			{
				FlowPattern = "COCURRENT";
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("TPAS"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					TPASS = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty.Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("SPAS"))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains('='))
				{
					SPASS = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty.Trim());
				}
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("FT"))
			{
				FT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("UAMA"))
			{
				UAMAX = ParseUtilty.GetTwoUnitsDoubleValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("U"))
			{
				U = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
			else if (strProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("AREA"))
			{
				AREA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	private QualifierValue _Zones = new QualifierValue("OUTPUT", "5");

	public QualifierValue ZONES
	{
		get
		{
			return _Zones;
		}
		set
		{
			_Zones = value;
		}
	}

	public string PLOT { get; set; }

	public Cell HOT { get; set; }

	public Cell COLD { get; set; }

	public ExchangerUtility UTILITY { get; set; }

	public AttachedConfiguration ATTACH { get; set; }

	public ConfigurationOptions CONFIG { get; set; }

	public HeatExchangerPerformance OPERATION { get; set; }

	public P2SimpleHeatExchanger(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetData(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add("HX" + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "HX", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetData(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string name = obj.Name;
		string strSource = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (PropertyPair item2 in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
		{
			CUnitOperation.UID,
			CUnitOperation.NAME,
			CUnitOperation.ZONES,
			CUnitOperation.PLOT
		}, strSource))
		{
			string content = item2.Content;
			if (content.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UID))
			{
				base.UID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.UID);
				NameHelper.NotesName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
				base.UID = NameHelper.ConvertName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
			}
			else if (content.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NAME))
			{
				base.Name = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.NAME);
			}
			else if (content.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ZONES))
			{
				if (content.Contains('='))
				{
					ZONES = ParseUtilty.GetQualifierValue(content);
					base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.ZONES);
				}
			}
			else if (content.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLOT))
			{
				PLOT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PLOT);
			}
		}
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			name = child.Name;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HOT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				HOT = new Cell(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COLD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				COLD = new Cell(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				OPERATION = new HeatExchangerPerformance(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UTILITY))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				UTILITY = new ExchangerUtility(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ATTACH))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				ATTACH = new AttachedConfiguration(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONFIG))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				CONFIG = new ConfigurationOptions(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "HX", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		if (ATTACH != null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger();
		try
		{
			list.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.Name = base.Name;
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			if (COLD == null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.Cooler;
			}
			else if (HOT == null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater;
			}
			else
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger;
			}
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ZONES = ZONES;
			if (PLOT != null && PLOT.Length > 0)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.PLOT = PLOT;
			}
			ConvertHotAndColdCell(hYSYSHeatExchanger, list);
			if (UTILITY != null && (HOT == null || COLD == null))
			{
				ConvertUtility(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (CONFIG != null)
			{
				ConvertConfigOptions(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				ConvertOperation(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
			}
			if (base.METHOD != null)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.FluidPackage = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(base.METHOD);
			}
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetInletAndOutletStreams();
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.SeparateDefines();
			}
			ConvertHCurves(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "HX", base.UID));
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}

	private void ConvertUtility(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		if (UTILITY.UtilityName == "WATER")
		{
			heatExchanger.UTILITYTYPE = UtilityType.Water;
		}
		else if (UTILITY.UtilityName == "AIR")
		{
			heatExchanger.UTILITYTYPE = UtilityType.Air;
		}
		else if (UTILITY.UtilityName == "STEAM")
		{
			heatExchanger.UTILITYTYPE = UtilityType.Steam;
		}
		else if (UTILITY.UtilityName == "HEATINGMEDIUM")
		{
			heatExchanger.UTILITYTYPE = UtilityType.HeatingMedium;
		}
		else if (UTILITY.UtilityName == "REFRIGERANT")
		{
			heatExchanger.UTILITYTYPE = UtilityType.Refrigerant;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHotAndColdCell(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger, List<HYSYSBaseObject> objColl)
	{
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		if (HOT != null)
		{
			if (HOT.FEED != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < HOT.FEED.Count; i++)
				{
					heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Add(HOT.FEED[i]);
				}
			}
			if (HOT.V != null && HOT.V.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(HOT.V);
				dictionary.Add("V", HOT.V);
			}
			if (HOT.L != null && HOT.L.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(HOT.L);
				dictionary.Add("L", HOT.L);
			}
			if (HOT.W != null && HOT.W.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(HOT.W);
				dictionary.Add("W", HOT.W);
			}
			if (HOT.S != null && HOT.S.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(HOT.S);
				dictionary.Add("S", HOT.S);
			}
			if (HOT.M != null && HOT.M.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(HOT.M);
				dictionary.Add("M", HOT.M);
			}
			if (HOT.METHOD != null && HOT.METHOD.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideMethod = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(HOT.METHOD);
			}
			if (HOT.DP != null)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, HOT.DP);
			}
		}
		if (COLD != null)
		{
			if (COLD.FEED != null)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < COLD.FEED.Count; j++)
				{
					heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Add(COLD.FEED[j]);
				}
			}
			if (COLD.V != null && COLD.V.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(COLD.V);
				dictionary2.Add("V", COLD.V);
			}
			if (COLD.L != null && COLD.L.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(COLD.L);
				dictionary2.Add("L", COLD.L);
			}
			if (COLD.W != null && COLD.W.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(COLD.W);
				dictionary2.Add("W", COLD.W);
			}
			if (COLD.S != null && COLD.S.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(COLD.S);
				dictionary2.Add("S", COLD.S);
			}
			if (COLD.M != null && COLD.M.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(COLD.M);
				dictionary2.Add("M", COLD.M);
			}
			if (COLD.METHOD != null && COLD.METHOD.Length > 0)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(COLD.METHOD);
			}
			if (COLD.DP != null)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideDeltaPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, COLD.DP);
			}
		}
		if (HOT != null && HOT.FEED.Count > 1)
		{
			heatExchanger.InletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string hotSideInletStream in heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.InletStreams.Add(hotSideInletStream);
			}
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(heatExchanger);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				objColl.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(heatExchanger.HotSideMethod))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = heatExchanger.HotSideMethod;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
			heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string inletStream in heatExchanger.InletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Add(inletStream);
			}
		}
		if (COLD != null && COLD.FEED.Count > 1)
		{
			heatExchanger.InletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string coldSideInletStream in heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.InletStreams.Add(coldSideInletStream);
			}
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(heatExchanger);
			if (hYSYSMixer2 != null)
			{
				objColl.Add(hYSYSMixer2);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod))
				{
					hYSYSMixer2.FluidPackage = heatExchanger.ColdSideMethod;
				}
				hYSYSMixer2.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
			heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string inletStream2 in heatExchanger.InletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Add(inletStream2);
			}
		}
		if (HOT != null && HOT.AddedPropertyList.Count > 1)
		{
			heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string hotSideOutletStream in heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Add(hotSideOutletStream);
			}
			List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, heatExchanger);
			if (list != null)
			{
				foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item in list)
				{
					objColl.Add(item);
					item.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
				}
			}
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Clear();
			foreach (string outletStream in heatExchanger.OutletStreams)
			{
				heatExchanger.HotSideOutletStreams.Add(outletStream);
			}
		}
		if (COLD == null || COLD.AddedPropertyList.Count <= 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (string coldSideOutletStream in heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams)
		{
			heatExchanger.OutletStreams.Add(coldSideOutletStream);
		}
		List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary2, heatExchanger);
		if (list2 != null)
		{
			foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item2 in list2)
			{
				objColl.Add(item2);
				item2.SetCreateStreams(objColl);
			}
		}
		heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Clear();
		foreach (string outletStream2 in heatExchanger.OutletStreams)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(outletStream2);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertConfigOptions(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		ConfigurationOptions cONFIG = CONFIG;
		if (cONFIG.FlowPattern == "COCURRENT")
		{
			heatExchanger.FlowDirection = cONFIG.FlowPattern;
		}
		if (cONFIG.TPASS != null && cONFIG.TPASS.Length > 0 && int.TryParse(cONFIG.TPASS, out var result))
		{
			heatExchanger.TubePasses = result;
		}
		if (cONFIG.SPASS != null && cONFIG.SPASS.Length > 0 && int.TryParse(cONFIG.SPASS, out var result2))
		{
			heatExchanger.ShellPasses = result2;
		}
		if (cONFIG.FT != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.FTFactor = cONFIG.FT;
		}
		_ = cONFIG.UAMAX;
		if (cONFIG.U != null && cONFIG.AREA != null)
		{
			double value = cONFIG.U.Value * cONFIG.AREA.Value;
			heatExchanger.OverallUA = UnitConverter.GetHysysUA(cONFIG.U.Unit, cONFIG.AREA.Unit, value, isUA: false);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertOperation(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		HeatExchangerPerformance oPERATION = OPERATION;
		if (oPERATION.DUTY != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, oPERATION.DUTY);
		}
		else if (oPERATION.HOCI != null)
		{
			if (heatExchanger.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
			{
				heatExchanger.HOCI = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.HOCI);
			}
			else
			{
				heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HOCINotConverted"), base.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (oPERATION.HICO != null)
		{
			if (heatExchanger.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
			{
				heatExchanger.HICO = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.HICO);
			}
			else
			{
				heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HICONotConverted"), base.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (oPERATION.HOCO != null)
		{
			if (heatExchanger.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
			{
				heatExchanger.HOCO = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.HOCO);
			}
			else
			{
				heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HOCONotConverted"), base.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (oPERATION.TMIN != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("TMINNotConverted"), base.UID));
		}
		else if (oPERATION.MITA != null)
		{
			if (heatExchanger.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
			{
				heatExchanger.MITA = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.MITA);
			}
			else
			{
				heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("MITANotConverted"), base.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (oPERATION.HTEMP != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.HotSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, oPERATION.HTEMP);
		}
		else if (oPERATION.CTEMP != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.ColdSideOutletTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, oPERATION.CTEMP);
		}
		else if (oPERATION.HLFRAC != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.HLFRAC = oPERATION.HLFRAC;
		}
		else if (oPERATION.CLFRAC != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.CLFRAC = oPERATION.CLFRAC;
		}
		else if (oPERATION.HDTBB != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.HDTBB = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.HDTBB);
		}
		else if (oPERATION.CDTBB != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.CDTBB = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.CDTBB);
		}
		else if (oPERATION.HDTAD != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.HDTAD = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.HDTAD);
		}
		else if (oPERATION.CDTAD != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.CDTAD = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, oPERATION.CDTAD);
		}
		else if (oPERATION.UA != null)
		{
			if (heatExchanger.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
			{
				heatExchanger.OverallUA = UnitConverter.GetHysysUA(oPERATION.UA.FirstUnit, oPERATION.UA.SecondUnit, oPERATION.UA.Value, isUA: true);
			}
			else
			{
				heatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UANotConverted"), base.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (oPERATION.TOLERANCE != null)
		{
			heatExchanger.Tolerance = oPERATION.TOLERANCE;
		}
	}

	private void ConvertHCurves(HYSYSHeatExchanger heatExchanger)
	{
		P2HCURVE p2HCURVE = HYSYSObjectHelper.RetrieveHCurveObject(heatExchanger.UID);
		if (p2HCURVE == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < p2HCURVE.UnitCurves.Count; i++)
		{
			if (string.Compare(p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[i].UID, heatExchanger.UID, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				P2HCURVE.UOCurve uOCurve = p2HCURVE.UnitCurves[0];
				heatExchanger.HcurvePlotPoint = uOCurve.POINTS;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSObjectHelper
{
	public static HYSYSMixer HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(HYSYSUnitOperationBase hysysObj)
	{
		HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = null;
		hYSYSMixer = new HYSYSMixer();
		hYSYSMixer.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, hysysObj.UID);
		if (hysysObj.InletStreams != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < hysysObj.InletStreams.Count; i++)
			{
				hYSYSMixer.InletStreams.Add(hysysObj.InletStreams[i]);
			}
		}
		hysysObj.InletStreams.Clear();
		string item = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, hysysObj.UID);
		hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams.Add(item);
		hysysObj.InletStreams.Add(item);
		hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hysysObj.FluidPackage;
		return hYSYSMixer;
	}

	public static HYSYSMixer AddMixerToSeparator(HYSYSSeparator flash)
	{
		if (flash == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = new HYSYSMixer();
		hYSYSMixer.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, flash.UID);
		if (flash.OutletStreams != null)
		{
			hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams.Add(flash.OutletStreams[0]);
		}
		flash.OutletStreams.Clear();
		string text = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, flash.UID);
		string text2 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, flash.UID);
		flash.OutletStreams.Add(text);
		flash.OutletStreams.Add(text2);
		hYSYSMixer.InletStreams.Add(text);
		hYSYSMixer.InletStreams.Add(text2);
		flash.VapourOutletStream = text;
		flash.LiquidOutletStream = text2;
		hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = flash.FluidPackage;
		return hYSYSMixer;
	}

	public static HYSYSMixer HandleMultipleProductToMixer(HYSYSUnitOperationBase hysysObj, string MStream)
	{
		HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = null;
		hYSYSMixer = new HYSYSMixer();
		hYSYSMixer.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, hysysObj.UID);
		if (hysysObj.OutletStreams != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < hysysObj.OutletStreams.Count; i++)
			{
				if (hysysObj.OutletStreams[i] != MStream)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.InletStreams.Add(hysysObj.OutletStreams[i]);
				}
			}
		}
		hysysObj.OutletStreams.Clear();
		for (int j = 0; j < hYSYSMixer.InletStreams.Count; j++)
		{
			hysysObj.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSMixer.InletStreams[j]);
		}
		hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams.Add(MStream);
		hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hysysObj.FluidPackage;
		return hYSYSMixer;
	}

	public static HYSYSSplitter HandleMultipleProductInSamePhase(HYSYSUnitOperationBase hysysobj)
	{
		HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = null;
		try
		{
			hYSYSSplitter = new HYSYSSplitter();
			hYSYSSplitter.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, hysysobj.UID);
			if (hysysobj.OutletStreams != null && hysysobj.OutletStreams.Count > 1)
			{
				foreach (string outletStream in hysysobj.OutletStreams)
				{
					hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(outletStream);
				}
			}
			hysysobj.OutletStreams.Clear();
			string item = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, hysysobj.UID);
			hYSYSSplitter.InletStreams.Add(item);
			hysysobj.OutletStreams.Add(item);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		return hYSYSSplitter;
	}

	public static List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> FlashMultipleProduct(Dictionary<string, string> StreamCollection, HYSYSUnitOperationBase hysysObj)
	{
		if (StreamCollection == null || StreamCollection.Count < 2 || (StreamCollection.Count == 2 && StreamCollection.ContainsKey("S")))
		{
			return null;
		}
		List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list = new List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase>();
		HYSYSSeparator hYSYSSeparator = null;
		if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("S"))
		{
			string strMessage = "Unit : " + hysysObj.UID + P2Globalization.GetString("Flash_ProductSolidStream") + " " + StreamCollection["S"] + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted");
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Notes, strMessage);
			StreamCollection.Remove("S");
		}
		hYSYSSeparator = new HYSYSSeparator();
		hYSYSSeparator.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, hysysObj.UID);
		for (int i = 0; i < hysysObj.OutletStreams.Count; i++)
		{
			hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Add(hysysObj.OutletStreams[i]);
		}
		if (hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Count > 2 || (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("V") && StreamCollection.ContainsKey("W")) || (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("M") && StreamCollection.ContainsKey("W")))
		{
			hYSYSSeparator.SeparatorType = HYSYSSeparatorType.ThreePhaseSep;
			if (!StreamCollection.ContainsKey("V") && !StreamCollection.ContainsKey("M"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, hysysObj.UID);
			}
			else if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("V"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream = StreamCollection["V"];
			}
			if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("L"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream = StreamCollection["L"];
			}
			if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("W"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.HeavyLiquidtStream = StreamCollection["W"];
			}
			if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("M"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.HasMixedProduct = true;
				hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Clear();
				hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Add(StreamCollection["M"]);
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = AddMixerToSeparator(hYSYSSeparator);
				if (hYSYSMixer != null)
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				}
				if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("W"))
				{
					hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Add(StreamCollection["W"]);
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSSeparator.SeparatorType = HYSYSSeparatorType.Separator;
			if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("V"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream = StreamCollection["V"];
			}
			if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("L") && !StreamCollection.ContainsKey("W"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream = StreamCollection["L"];
			}
			else if (StreamCollection.ContainsKey("W") && !StreamCollection.ContainsKey("L"))
			{
				hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream = StreamCollection["W"];
			}
		}
		hysysObj.OutletStreams.Clear();
		string item = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, hysysObj.UID);
		hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Add(item);
		hysysObj.OutletStreams.Add(item);
		hYSYSSeparator.FluidPackage = hysysObj.FluidPackage;
		if (hYSYSSeparator != null)
		{
			list.Add(hYSYSSeparator);
		}
		return list;
	}

	public static void InsertObjectInStream(HYSYSUnitOperationBase connectedObj, string streamName, bool bInletStream, HYSYSUnitOperationBase hysysObj)
	{
		if (connectedObj != null)
		{
			string item = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, hysysObj.UID);
			if (bInletStream)
			{
				hysysObj.OutletStreams.Add(item);
				hysysObj.InletStreams.Add(streamName);
				connectedObj.InletStreams.Remove(streamName);
				connectedObj.InletStreams.Add(item);
			}
			else
			{
				hysysObj.InletStreams.Add(item);
				hysysObj.OutletStreams.Add(streamName);
				connectedObj.OutletStreams.Remove(streamName);
				connectedObj.OutletStreams.Add(item);
			}
			hysysObj.FluidPackage = connectedObj.FluidPackage;
		}
	}

	public static List<double> CheckOrder(SortedDictionary<double, double> values, bool isIncrease)
	{
		if (values == null || values.Count == 0)
		{
			return null;
		}
		List<double> list = new List<double>();
		List<double> list2 = new List<double>();
		foreach (KeyValuePair<double, double> value in values)
		{
			list2.Add(value.Key);
		}
		if (isIncrease)
		{
			for (int num = list2.Count - 1; num > 0; num--)
			{
				if (values[list2[num]] <= values[list2[num - 1]])
				{
					list.Add(list2[num]);
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			for (int num2 = list2.Count - 1; num2 > 0; num2--)
			{
				if (values[list2[num2]] >= values[list2[num2 - 1]])
				{
					list.Add(list2[num2]);
				}
			}
		}
		if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
		{
			return list;
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static HYSYSSplitter AddSplitterForFeedBlending(HYSYSUnitOperationBase hysysobj, double scalingfactor, string streamName)
	{
		HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = null;
		try
		{
			hYSYSSplitter = new HYSYSSplitter();
			hYSYSSplitter.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, hysysobj.UID);
			if (hysysobj.InletStreams != null && hysysobj.InletStreams.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSSplitter.InletStreams.Add(streamName);
				hysysobj.InletStreams.Remove(streamName);
				string text = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, hysysobj.UID);
				string item = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, hysysobj.UID);
				hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(text);
				hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(item);
				hysysobj.InletStreams.Add(text);
				Specification specification = new Specification();
				specification.SpecType = SpecificationType.STREAM;
				specification.PrimaryPropertyName = "RATE";
				specification.PrimaryID = text;
				specification.Operator = Specification.OperatorType.divide;
				specification.ReferenceName = "REFFEED";
				specification.Value = new DoubleValue("", scalingfactor);
				hYSYSSplitter.LocalSpec.Add(specification);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			hYSYSSplitter = null;
		}
		return hYSYSSplitter;
	}

	public static P2HCURVE RetrieveHCurveObject(string id)
	{
		P2HCURVE result = null;
		try
		{
			foreach (P2BaseObject g_HCurf in P2BaseObject.g_HCurves)
			{
				if (g_HCurf is P2HCURVE p2HCURVE && p2HCURVE.LinkedIDs.Contains(id))
				{
					result = p2HCURVE;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			result = null;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static HYSYSHeatExchanger AddHeaterToUO(string hysysUID, string inlet, string outlet)
	{
		HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = null;
		try
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger();
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, hysysUID);
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater;
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Add(inlet);
			hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletStreams.Add(outlet);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			hYSYSHeatExchanger = null;
		}
		return hYSYSHeatExchanger;
	}
}
public class P2Mixer : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class MixerPerformance
	{
		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public MixerPerformance()
		{
		}

		public MixerPerformance(string strSource)
		{
			if (strSource.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
			{
				if (strSource.Contains('='))
				{
					DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strSource);
				}
			}
			else if (strSource.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
			{
				PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strSource);
			}
		}
	}

	public MixerPerformance OPERATION { get; set; }

	public P2Mixer()
	{
	}

	public P2Mixer(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetData(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add("MIXER" + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "MIXER", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetData(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string name = obj.Name;
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			name = child.Name;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty));
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
					if (array2.Length != 0)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							string strName = array2[j];
							NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							base.FEED.Add(strName);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				OPERATION = new MixerPerformance(empty);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "MIXER", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(name, ' ');
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = new HYSYSMixer();
		try
		{
			list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
			hYSYSMixer.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSMixer.Name = base.Name;
			hYSYSMixer.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(base.METHOD);
			}
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
				}
			}
			if (base.PRODUCT != null)
			{
				if (base.PRODUCT.V != null && base.PRODUCT.V.Length > 0)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.V);
				}
				if (base.PRODUCT.L != null && base.PRODUCT.L.Length > 0)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.L);
				}
				if (base.PRODUCT.M != null && base.PRODUCT.M.Length > 0)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.M);
				}
				if (base.PRODUCT.S != null && base.PRODUCT.S.Length > 0)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.S);
				}
			}
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = new HYSYSValve();
				hYSYSValve.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
				HYSYSObjectHelper.InsertObjectInStream(hYSYSMixer, hYSYSMixer.OutletStreams[0], bInletStream: false, hYSYSValve);
				if (OPERATION.PRESSURE != null)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRESSURE);
				}
				else if (OPERATION.DP != null)
				{
					hYSYSMixer.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
				}
				if (hYSYSValve != null)
				{
					hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = hYSYSMixer.ATTACHED_PRESSURE;
					hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = hYSYSMixer.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP;
					hYSYSValve.FluidPackage = hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage;
					list.Add(hYSYSValve);
					hYSYSValve.SetCreateStreams(list);
					hYSYSMixer.OutletValveAdded = true;
				}
			}
			hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSMixer.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSMixer.SeparateDefines();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "MIXER", base.UID));
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}
}
public class P2UnitOperationBase : P2BaseObject
{
	public string UID { get; set; }

	public string METHOD { get; set; }

	public List<string> FEED { get; set; }

	public Product PRODUCT { get; set; }

	public string P2Paragraphy { get; set; }

	public bool IsMultipleFeed
	{
		get
		{
			if (FEED == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return FEED.Count > 1;
		}
	}

	public bool IsMultiplePRODUCT
	{
		get
		{
			if (PRODUCT == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList.Count > 1;
		}
	}

	public List<string> AddedPropertyList { get; set; }

	public object GetProperty(string propertyName)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
		{
			return null;
		}
		PropertyInfo[] properties = GetType().GetProperties();
		foreach (PropertyInfo propertyInfo in properties)
		{
			if (propertyInfo.Name.Length > 3)
			{
				if (propertyInfo.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(propertyName))
				{
					return propertyInfo.GetValue(this, null);
				}
			}
			else if (propertyInfo.Name == propertyName)
			{
				return propertyInfo.GetValue(this, null);
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	public P2UnitOperationBase()
	{
		AddedPropertyList = new List<string>();
		base.GlobalDefine = new List<DEFINE>();
		base.LocalDefine = new List<DEFINE>();
		base.GlobalSpec = new List<Specification>();
		base.LocalSpec = new List<Specification>();
		if (BaseObject._Report != null)
		{
			Report report = BaseObject._Report;
			int p2EquipmentCount = report.P2EquipmentCount + 1;
			report.P2EquipmentCount = p2EquipmentCount;
		}
	}

	public void SetHeadData(string head)
	{
		foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
		{
			CUnitOperation.UID,
			CUnitOperation.NAME
		}, head))
		{
			string content = item.Content;
			if (content.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UID))
			{
				UID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content);
				AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.UID);
				NameHelper.NotesName(UID, bIsStream: false);
				UID = NameHelper.ConvertName(UID, bIsStream: false);
			}
			else if (content.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NAME))
			{
				base.Name = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content);
				AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.NAME);
			}
		}
	}
}
public class PropertyItem
{
	public List<string> AddedPropertyList { get; set; }

	public PropertyItem()
	{
		AddedPropertyList = new List<string>();
	}

	public object GetProperty(string propertyName)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
		{
			return null;
		}
		PropertyInfo[] properties = GetType().GetProperties();
		foreach (PropertyInfo propertyInfo in properties)
		{
			if (propertyInfo.Name.Length > 3)
			{
				if (propertyInfo.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(propertyName))
				{
					return propertyInfo.GetValue(this, null);
				}
			}
			else if (propertyInfo.Name == propertyName)
			{
				return propertyInfo.GetValue(this, null);
			}
		}
		return null;
	}
}
public class Product : PropertyItem
{
	public string V { get; set; }

	public string L { get; set; }

	public string W { get; set; }

	public string S { get; set; }

	public string M { get; set; }

	protected void SetValue(string strPair)
	{
		strPair = strPair.ToUpper();
		if (strPair.ToUpper().StartsWith("V"))
		{
			string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strPair).Trim();
			NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
			V = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
			base.AddedPropertyList.Add("V");
		}
		else if (strPair.ToUpper().StartsWith("L"))
		{
			string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strPair).Trim();
			NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
			L = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
			base.AddedPropertyList.Add("L");
		}
		else if (strPair.ToUpper().StartsWith("W"))
		{
			string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strPair).Trim();
			NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
			W = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
			base.AddedPropertyList.Add("W");
		}
		else if (strPair.ToUpper().StartsWith("S"))
		{
			string strName4 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strPair).Trim();
			NameHelper.NotesName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
			S = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName4, bIsStream: true);
			base.AddedPropertyList.Add("S");
		}
		else if (strPair.ToUpper().StartsWith("M"))
		{
			string strName5 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strPair).Trim();
			NameHelper.NotesName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
			M = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName5, bIsStream: true);
			base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
		}
	}

	public Product(string sSource)
	{
		foreach (PropertyPair item in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string> { "V=", "L=", "W=", "S=", "M=" }, sSource))
		{
			SetValue(item.Content);
		}
	}
}
internal class P2Pipe : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public enum Correlations
	{
		BBM,
		BBP,
		BBMHV,
		OLIMENS,
		DEF,
		MB,
		GRAY,
		HB,
		DP1,
		DP2
	}

		public DoubleValue U { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TAMBIENT
		{
			get

				return _tambient;

			set

				_tambient = value;

		public Correlations DPCORR
		{
			get
			{
				return _dpcorr;

			set

				_dpcorr = value;
			}
