		public DoubleValue DPEST
		{
			get
			{
				return _dpest;
			}
			set
			{
				_dpest = value;
			}
		}

		public double DPTOL
		{
			get
			{
				return _dptol;
			}
			set
			{
				_dptol = value;
			}
		}

		public bool NOACCEL { get; set; }

		public bool BACK { get; set; }

		public string OPTION
		{
			get
			{
				return _option;
			}
			set
			{
				_option = value;
			}
		}

		public double KFACTOR { get; set; }

		public ConfigurationData(string source)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.DIAMETER,
				CUnitOperation.NPS,
				CUnitOperation.LENGTH,
				CUnitOperation.SEGMENTS,
				CUnitOperation.ELEVATION,
				CUnitOperation.FLOWEFF,
				CUnitOperation.FRICTION,
				CUnitOperation.AROUGH + "=",
				CUnitOperation.RROUGH + "=",
				CUnitOperation.DUTY,
				CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL,
				"U=",
				CUnitOperation.TAMBIENT,
				CUnitOperation.DPCORR,
				CUnitOperation.DPEST,
				CUnitOperation.DPTOL,
				CUnitOperation.NOACCEL,
				CUnitOperation.BACK,
				CUnitOperation.XOPTION,
				CUnitOperation.KFACTOR
			}, source.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				SetValue(array[i].ToUpper());
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DIAMETER))
			{
				DIAMETER = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Diameter");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NPS))
			{
				NPS = new Dictionary<DoubleValue, int>();
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				int value = 40;
				DoubleValue key = new DoubleValue("IN", Convert.ToDouble(array[0]));
				if (array.Length > 1)
				{
					value = Convert.ToInt32(array[1]);
				}
				NPS.Add(key, value);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("NPS");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LENGTH))
			{
				LENGTH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Length");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SEGMENTS))
			{
				SEGMENT = Convert.ToInt32(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Segment");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ELEVATION))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					ELEVATION = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Elevation");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FLOWEFF))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					FLOWEFF = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("FlowEff");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRICTION))
			{
				FRICTION = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Friction");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.AROUGH))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					AROUGH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Arough");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RROUGH))
			{
				RROUGH = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Rrough");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Duty");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL))
			{
				ISOTHERMAL = true;
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.U))
			{
				U = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.U);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TAMBIENT))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					TAMBIENT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.TAMBIENT);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPCORR))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
					string[] names = Enum.GetNames(typeof(Correlations));
					foreach (string text in names)
					{
						if (text.StartsWith(stringValue))
						{
							DPCORR = (Correlations)Enum.Parse(typeof(Correlations), text);
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.DPCORR);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPEST))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					DPEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("DPEST");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPTOL))
			{
				if (strProperty.Contains("="))
				{
					DPTOL = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("DPTOL");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NOACCEL))
			{
				NOACCEL = true;
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BACK))
			{
				BACK = true;
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.XOPTION))
			{
				OPTION = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.KFACTOR))
			{
				KFACTOR = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
			}
		}
	}

	public class LineSizingData : PropertyItem
	{
		public DoubleValue DPMAX { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue VMAX { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PMIN { get; set; }

		public List<DoubleValue> DIAMETERS { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<DoubleValue, int> NPS { get; set; }

		public LineSizingData(string source)
		{
			int num = source.IndexOf("NPS=");
			if (num != -1)
			{
				string value = source.Substring(num);
				SetValue(value);
				source = source.Substring(0, num);
			}
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(source);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				SetValue(array[i].ToUpper());
			}
		}

		private void SetValue(string strProperty)
		{
			if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DPMAX))
			{
				DPMAX = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(CUnitOperation.DPMAX);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.DPMAX);
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VMAX))
			{
				VMAX = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(CUnitOperation.VMAX);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("VMax");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PMIN))
			{
				PMIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(strProperty);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("PMin");
			}
			else if (strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DIAMETER))
			{
				DIAMETERS = new List<DoubleValue>();
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				foreach (string value in array)
				{
					DIAMETERS.Add(new DoubleValue("IN", Convert.ToDouble(value)));
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("Diameter");
			}
			else
			{
				if (!strProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NPS))
				{
					return;
				}
				NPS = new Dictionary<DoubleValue, int>();
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(strProperty));
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array[i]);
					DoubleValue key = new DoubleValue("IN", Convert.ToDouble(array2[0]));
					int value2 = 40;
					if (array2.Length > 1)
					{
						value2 = Convert.ToInt32(array2[1]);
					}
					NPS.Add(key, value2);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add("NPS");
			}
		}
	}

	public ConfigurationData CONFIGDATA { get; set; }

	public LineSizingData LINEDATA { get; set; }

	public P2Pipe(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.PIPE + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), CUnitOperation.PIPE, base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			empty = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(empty2);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string strName = array.GetValue(i).ToString();
					NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					base.FEED.Add(strName);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LINE))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				CONFIGDATA = new ConfigurationData(empty2);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIZE))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				LINEDATA = new LineSizingData(empty2);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty2, "PIPE", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty2);
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSPipe hYSYSPipe = new HYSYSPipe();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSPipe);
			hYSYSPipe.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSPipe.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSPipe.Name = base.Name;
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in base.FEED)
				{
					hYSYSPipe.InletStreams.Add(item);
				}
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			if (base.PRODUCT != null)
			{
				foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
				{
					string text = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					hYSYSPipe.OutletStreams.Add(text);
					dictionary.Add(addedProperty, text);
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSPipe.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSPipe);
				list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSPipe.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSPipe.FluidPackage;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
			if (base.IsMultiplePRODUCT)
			{
				List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, hYSYSPipe);
				bool flag = false;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSPipe.FluidPackage))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (list2 != null)
				{
					foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item2 in list2)
					{
						list.Add(item2);
						if (flag)
						{
							item2.FluidPackage = hYSYSPipe.FluidPackage;
						}
						item2.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
			}
			ConvertConfigurationData(hYSYSPipe);
			hYSYSPipe.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSPipe.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSPipe.SeparateDefines();
			}
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), CUnitOperation.PIPE, base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void ConvertConfigurationData(HYSYSPipe pipe)
	{
		if (CONFIGDATA != null)
		{
			if (CONFIGDATA.DIAMETER != null)
			{
				pipe.DIAMETER = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, CONFIGDATA.DIAMETER);
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.NPS != null)
			{
				pipe.NPS = CONFIGDATA.NPS;
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.LENGTH != null)
			{
				pipe.LENGTH = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, CONFIGDATA.LENGTH);
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.SEGMENT != 0)
			{
				pipe.SEGMENTSCOUNT = CONFIGDATA.SEGMENT;
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.ELEVATION.Value != 0.0)
			{
				pipe.ELEVATION = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, CONFIGDATA.ELEVATION);
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.AROUGH.Value != 0.0018)
			{
				pipe.ROUGHNESS = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, CONFIGDATA.AROUGH);
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.RROUGH != 0.0)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Rrough"));
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.DUTY != null)
			{
				pipe.DUTY = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, CONFIGDATA.DUTY);
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.ISOTHERMAL)
			{
				pipe.ISOTHERMAL = true;
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.U != null && CONFIGDATA.TAMBIENT != null)
			{
				pipe.HTC = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HeatTransferCoefficient, CONFIGDATA.U);
				pipe.TAMB = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, CONFIGDATA.TAMBIENT);
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.DPCORR == Correlations.BBM)
			{
				pipe.BBDPCORR = true;
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.DPEST.Value != 0.0)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "DPEST"));
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.DPTOL != 1.0)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "DPTOL"));
			}
			if (!CONFIGDATA.NOACCEL)
			{
				pipe.ACCEL = true;
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.BACK)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Back"));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(CONFIGDATA.OPTION))
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "XOption"));
			}
			if (CONFIGDATA.KFACTOR != 0.0)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "KFactor"));
			}
		}
		if (LINEDATA != null)
		{
			if (LINEDATA.DPMAX != null && CONFIGDATA.LENGTH == null)
			{
				pipe.UseDP = true;
				pipe.DP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, LINEDATA.DPMAX);
			}
			if (LINEDATA.VMAX != null)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Line Sizing Data.VMax"));
			}
			if (LINEDATA.PMIN != null)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Line Sizing Data.PMin"));
			}
			if (LINEDATA.DIAMETERS != null && LINEDATA.DIAMETERS.Count > 0)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Line Sizing Data.Diameter"));
			}
			if (LINEDATA.NPS != null && LINEDATA.NPS.Count > 0)
			{
				pipe.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Line Sizing Data.NPS"));
			}
		}
	}
}
internal class P2Pump : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class PumpOperation : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _dp = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _eff = new DoubleValue("PCT", 100.0);

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _dp;
			}
			set
			{
				_dp = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue EFF
		{
			get
			{
				return _eff;
			}
			set
			{
				_eff = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public double PRATIO { get; set; }
	}

	private PumpOperation _operation = new PumpOperation();

	public PumpOperation OPERATION
	{
		get
		{
			return _operation;
		}
		set
		{
			_operation = value;
		}
	}

	public P2Pump(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.PUMP + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), CUnitOperation.PUMP, base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			empty = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(empty2);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string strName = array.GetValue(i).ToString();
					NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					base.FEED.Add(strName);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				OPERATION = new PumpOperation();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRATIO,
					CUnitOperation.EFF
				}, empty2.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item2.Content);
				}
				string[] array2 = list.ToArray();
				foreach (string text in array2)
				{
					if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						if (text.Contains("="))
						{
							OPERATION.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
						}
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						OPERATION.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRATIO))
					{
						OPERATION.PRATIO = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text));
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EFF) && text.Contains("="))
					{
						OPERATION.EFF = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty2, "PUMP", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty2);
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSPump hYSYSPump = new HYSYSPump();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			list.Add(hYSYSPump);
			hYSYSPump.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSPump.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSPump.Name = base.Name;
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in base.FEED)
				{
					hYSYSPump.InletStreams.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (base.PRODUCT != null)
			{
				foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
				{
					string text = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					hYSYSPump.OutletStreams.Add(text);
					dictionary.Add(addedProperty, text);
				}
			}
			if (OPERATION.DP != null && OPERATION.DP.Value != 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSPump.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
			}
			if (OPERATION.PRESSURE != null)
			{
				hYSYSPump.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRESSURE);
			}
			if (OPERATION.PRESSURE == null)
			{
				hYSYSPump.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
			}
			if (OPERATION.EFF != null)
			{
				hYSYSPump.AdiabaticEfficiency = OPERATION.EFF.Value;
			}
			if (OPERATION.PRATIO != 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSPump.PRATIO = OPERATION.PRATIO;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSPump.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSPump);
				list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSPump.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSPump.FluidPackage;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
			if (base.IsMultiplePRODUCT)
			{
				List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, hYSYSPump);
				if (list2 != null)
				{
					foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item2 in list2)
					{
						list.Add(item2);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSPump.FluidPackage))
						{
							item2.FluidPackage = hYSYSPump.FluidPackage;
						}
						item2.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
			}
			hYSYSPump.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSPump.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSPump.SeparateDefines();
			}
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), CUnitOperation.PUMP, base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}
}
internal class P2ConversionReactor : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class OPERATION : PropertyItem
	{
		private bool _ISOTHERMAL = true;

		private DoubleValue _DTFEED = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _DUTY = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMAX = new DoubleValue("K", 3000.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMIN = new DoubleValue("K", 1.0);

		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public bool ISOTHERMAL
		{
			get
			{
				return _ISOTHERMAL;
			}
			set
			{
				_ISOTHERMAL = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DTFEED
		{
			get
			{
				return _DTFEED;
			}
			set
			{
				_DTFEED = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public bool ISADIABATIC { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY
		{
			get
			{
				return _DUTY;
			}
			set
			{
				_DUTY = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMAX
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMAX;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMAX = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMIN
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMIN;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMIN = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public string Phase { get; set; }
	}

	{

		private CBASIS _CBASIS;

		private string _XOPTION = "STOP";

		public RXModelType MODEL
		{
			get
			{
				return _MODEL;
			}
			set

				_MODEL = value;
			}

			{
				return _CBASIS;
			}
			set
			{
				_CBASIS = value;
			}
		}

		public bool NOHBALANCE { get; set; }

		public string XOPTION
		{
			get
			{
				return _XOPTION;
			}
			set
			{
				_XOPTION = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public class PRINT : PropertyItem
	{
		public string PATH { get; set; }
	}

	public class RXSTOIC : PropertyItem
	{
		public string RXSET { get; set; }
	}

	public class REACTION : PropertyItem
	{
		public string RXID { get; set; }

		public int BASECOMPONENT { get; set; }

		public double BASEHEAT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue C0 { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue C1 { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue C2 { get; set; }
	}

	public enum RXModelType
	{
		STOIC,
		SHIFT,
		METHANATION
	}

	public enum CBASIS
	{
		FEED,
		REACTION
	}

	public OPERATION Operation { get; set; }

	public RXCALC Rxcalc { get; set; }

	public PRINT Print { get; set; }

	public RXSTOIC Rxstoic { get; set; }

	public List<REACTION> Reactions { get; set; }

	public bool IsDTFEEDSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsDUTYSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsTMAXSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsTMINSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsDPSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsMODELSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsCBASISSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsNOHBALANCESet { get; set; }

	public bool IsXOPTIONSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsOnlyVProduct { get; set; }

	public bool IsOnlyLProduct { get; set; }

	public bool IsLSpecified { get; set; }

	public bool IsVSpecified { get; set; }

	public bool IsOnlyWProduct { get; set; }

	public string SingleProduct { get; set; }

	public P2ConversionReactor(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			Operation = new OPERATION();
			Rxcalc = new RXCALC();
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.CONVERSIONREACTOR + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "CONVERSION REACTOR", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(empty);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
					if (array2.Length != 0)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							string strName = array2[j];
							NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							base.FEED.Add(strName);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				Operation = new OPERATION();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL,
					CUnitOperation.DTFEED,
					CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
					CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC,
					CUnitOperation.DUTY,
					CUnitOperation.TMAX,
					CUnitOperation.TMIN,
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PHASE
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item2.Content);
				}
				string[] array3 = list.ToArray();
				for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
				{
					array3[k] = array3[k].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL))
					{
						Operation.ISOTHERMAL = true;
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DTFEED) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDTFEEDSet = true;
						Operation.DTFEED = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						Operation.TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC))
					{
						Operation.ISADIABATIC = true;
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDUTYSet = true;
						Operation.DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMAX) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsTMAXSet = true;
						Operation.TMAX = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMIN) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsTMINSet = true;
						Operation.TMIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDPSet = true;
						Operation.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						Operation.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHASE))
					{
						Operation.Phase = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXCALC))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.RXCALC);
				Rxcalc = new RXCALC();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit2 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.MODEL,
					CUnitOperation.CBASIS,
					CUnitOperation.NOHBALANCE,
					CUnitOperation.XOPTION
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item3 in propertySplit2)
				{
					list2.Add(item3.Content);
				}
				string[] array4 = list2.ToArray();
				for (int l = 0; l < array4.Length; l++)
				{
					array4[l] = array4[l].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MODEL) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsMODELSet = true;
						if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "STOIC")
						{
							Rxcalc.MODEL = RXModelType.STOIC;
						}
						else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "SHIFT")
						{
							Rxcalc.MODEL = RXModelType.SHIFT;
						}
						else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "METHANATION")
						{
							Rxcalc.MODEL = RXModelType.METHANATION;
						}
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CBASIS) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsCBASISSet = true;
						if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "FEED")
						{
							Rxcalc.CBASIS = CBASIS.FEED;
						}
						else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "REACTION")
						{
							Rxcalc.CBASIS = CBASIS.REACTION;
						}
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NOHBALANCE))
					{
						IsNOHBALANCESet = true;
						Rxcalc.NOHBALANCE = true;
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.XOPTION) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsXOPTIONSet = true;
						Rxcalc.XOPTION = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRINT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRINT);
				Print = new PRINT();
				Print.PATH = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC);
				Rxstoic = new RXSTOIC();
				Rxstoic.RXSET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REACTION))
			{
				bool flag = false;
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.REACTION);
				REACTION rEACTION = new REACTION();
				rEACTION.RXID = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				foreach (P2BaseObject child2 in child.Children)
				{
					if (child2.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BASE))
					{
						string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' '));
						for (int m = 0; m < array5.Length; m++)
						{
							if (array5[m].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COMPONENT))
							{
								rEACTION.BASECOMPONENT = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							else if (array5[m].StartsWith("HEAT"))
							{
								rEACTION.BASEHEAT = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
						}
					}
					if (!child2.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONVERSION))
					{
						continue;
					}
					string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' '));
					if (array6.Length >= 1)
					{
						if (array6[0].Equals("0"))
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						else
						{
							rEACTION.C0 = new DoubleValue();
							rEACTION.C0.Value = Convert.ToDouble(array6[0]);
						}
					}
					if (array6.Length >= 2)
					{
						if (array6[0].Equals("0") && array6[1].Equals("0"))
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						else
						{
							rEACTION.C1 = new DoubleValue();
							rEACTION.C1.Value = Convert.ToDouble(array6[1]);
						}
					}
					if (array6.Length == 3)
					{
						if (array6[0].Equals("0") && array6[1].Equals("0") && array6[2].Equals("0"))
						{
							flag = true;
							continue;
						}
						rEACTION.C2 = new DoubleValue();
						rEACTION.C2.Value = Convert.ToDouble(array6[2]);
					}
				}
				if (Reactions == null)
				{
					Reactions = new List<REACTION>();
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					Reactions.Add(rEACTION);
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "CONVERSION REACTOR", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CThermo.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CThermo.METHOD);
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSConversionReactor hYSYSConversionReactor = new HYSYSConversionReactor();
		hYSYSConversionReactor.UID = base.UID;
		hYSYSConversionReactor.Name = base.Name;
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		list.Add(hYSYSConversionReactor);
		hYSYSConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
		if (Operation != null)
		{
			ConvertOperationData(hYSYSConversionReactor);
		}
		if (Rxcalc != null)
		{
			ConvertRxcalcData(hYSYSConversionReactor);
		}
		if (Print != null)
		{
			hYSYSConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PRINT"));
		}
		if (Rxstoic != null)
		{
			ConvertRxstoicData(hYSYSConversionReactor);
		}
		if (Reactions != null)
		{
			ConvertReactionsData(hYSYSConversionReactor, list);
		}
		if (base.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReactor.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (base.IsMultipleFeed && (IsDTFEEDSet || (!Operation.ISADIABATIC && Operation.TEMPERATURE == null)))
		{
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSConversionReactor);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSConversionReactor.FluidPackage;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
		}
		if (base.PRODUCT != null)
		{
			foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				dictionary.Add(addedProperty, base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				switch (addedProperty)
				{
				case "V":
					hYSYSConversionReactor.V = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "L":
					hYSYSConversionReactor.L = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "M":
					hYSYSConversionReactor.M = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "W":
					hYSYSConversionReactor.W = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "S":
					hYSYSConversionReactor.S = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				}
			}
			IsOnlyVProduct = false;
			IsOnlyLProduct = false;
			IsOnlyWProduct = false;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.L) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.V) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.M) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.S) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.W))
			{
				IsOnlyWProduct = true;
				SingleProduct = hYSYSConversionReactor.W;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.L))
			{
				IsLSpecified = false;
				string item = (hYSYSConversionReactor.L = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
				hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.V))
				{
					IsOnlyVProduct = true;
					SingleProduct = hYSYSConversionReactor.V;
					hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Remove(hYSYSConversionReactor.V);
					string item2 = (hYSYSConversionReactor.V = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
					hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item2);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IsLSpecified = true;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.V))
			{
				string item3 = (hYSYSConversionReactor.V = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
				hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item3);
				if (IsLSpecified)
				{
					IsOnlyLProduct = true;
					SingleProduct = hYSYSConversionReactor.L;
					hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Remove(hYSYSConversionReactor.L);
					string item4 = (hYSYSConversionReactor.L = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
					hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item4);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IsVSpecified = true;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.W) || IsOnlyWProduct)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.M))
				{
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductToMixer(hYSYSConversionReactor, hYSYSConversionReactor.M);
					if (hYSYSMixer2 != null)
					{
						list.Add(hYSYSMixer2);
						hYSYSMixer2.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
				if ((IsOnlyVProduct || IsOnlyLProduct || IsOnlyWProduct) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SingleProduct))
				{
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer3 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductToMixer(hYSYSConversionReactor, SingleProduct);
					if (hYSYSMixer3 != null)
					{
						list.Add(hYSYSMixer3);
						hYSYSMixer3.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				HYSYSSeparator hYSYSSeparator = null;
				hYSYSSeparator = new HYSYSSeparator();
				hYSYSSeparator.SeparatorType = HYSYSSeparatorType.ThreePhaseSep;
				hYSYSSeparator.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
				hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSConversionReactor.V);
				hYSYSConversionReactor.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSConversionReactor.L);
				hYSYSSeparator.HeavyLiquidtStream = hYSYSConversionReactor.W;
				if (IsVSpecified && IsLSpecified)
				{
					string v = hYSYSConversionReactor.V;
					string l = hYSYSConversionReactor.L;
					hYSYSConversionReactor.V = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSConversionReactor.L = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream = v;
					hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream = l;
				}
				else if (IsOnlyLProduct || IsOnlyVProduct || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.M))
				{
					hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream);
					hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream);
					string mStream = string.Empty;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SingleProduct))
					{
						mStream = SingleProduct;
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.M))
					{
						mStream = hYSYSConversionReactor.M;
					}
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer4 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductToMixer(hYSYSSeparator, mStream);
					if (hYSYSMixer4 != null)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.FluidPackage))
						{
							hYSYSMixer4.FluidPackage = hYSYSConversionReactor.FluidPackage;
						}
						list.Add(hYSYSMixer4);
						hYSYSMixer4.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
				hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Add(hYSYSConversionReactor.L);
				hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Add(hYSYSConversionReactor.V);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReactor.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSSeparator.FluidPackage = hYSYSConversionReactor.FluidPackage;
				}
				list.Add(hYSYSSeparator);
				hYSYSSeparator.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
		}
		hYSYSConversionReactor.SetCreateStreams(list);
		if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
		{
			hYSYSConversionReactor.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
			hYSYSConversionReactor.SeparateDefines();
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}

	private void ConvertOperationData(HYSYSConversionReactor ConversionReactor)
	{
		if (IsDTFEEDSet || (!IsDTFEEDSet && Operation.TEMPERATURE == null && !Operation.ISADIABATIC))
		{
			ConversionReactor.AttachedDTFEED = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, Operation.DTFEED);
		}
		else if (Operation.TEMPERATURE != null)
		{
			ConversionReactor.AttachedTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, Operation.TEMPERATURE);
		}
		if (IsDUTYSet || Operation.ISADIABATIC)
		{
			ConversionReactor.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, Operation.DUTY);
		}
		if (IsTMAXSet)
		{
			ConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TMAX"));
		}
		if (IsTMINSet)
		{
			ConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TMIN"));
		}
		if (IsDPSet)
		{
			ConversionReactor.DP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, Operation.DP);
		}
		else if (Operation.PRESSURE != null)
		{
			ConversionReactor.AttachedPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, Operation.PRESSURE);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertRxcalcData(HYSYSConversionReactor ConversionReactor)
	{
		if (IsMODELSet)
		{
			ConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "MODEL"));
		}
		if (IsCBASISSet)
		{
			ConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "CBASIS"));
		}
		if (IsNOHBALANCESet)
		{
			ConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NOHBALANCE"));
		}
		if (IsXOPTIONSet)
		{
			ConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "XOPTION"));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertRxstoicData(HYSYSConversionReactor ConversionReactor)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Rxstoic.RXSET))
		{
			ConversionReactor.ReactionSet = new HYSYSReactionSet(base.UID + "_Conv_" + Rxstoic.RXSET);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertReactionsData(HYSYSConversionReactor ConversionReactor, List<HYSYSBaseObject> ObjCollection)
	{
		if (Rxcalc.MODEL == RXModelType.STOIC)
		{
			if (P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey("ReactionData"))
			{
				P2ReactionData p2ReactionData = (P2ReactionData)P2BaseObject.g_Map["ReactionData"];
				if (Reactions.Count != 0)
				{
					foreach (REACTION reaction in Reactions)
					{
						string text = base.UID + "_" + Rxstoic.RXSET + "_" + reaction.RXID;
						try
						{
							HYSYSConversionReaction hYSYSConversionReaction = p2ReactionData.CreateHYSYSConversionRX(text, p2ReactionData.P2RXSetsDic[Rxstoic.RXSET].P2RXsDic[reaction.RXID]);
							ConversionReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSConversionReaction);
							if (reaction.BASECOMPONENT != 0)
							{
								if (hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis == null)
								{
									hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis = new HYSYSConversionReaction.HYSYSConversionReactionBasis();
								}
								hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.BaseComponent = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(reaction.BASECOMPONENT);
							}
							if (reaction.BASEHEAT != 0.0)
							{
								ConversionReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "HEAT"));
							}
							if (reaction.C0 != null)
							{
								hYSYSConversionReaction.C0 = reaction.C0.Value;
							}
							else
							{
								hYSYSConversionReaction.C0 = 1.0;
							}
							if (reaction.C1 != null)
							{
								hYSYSConversionReaction.C1 = reaction.C1.Value;
							}
							if (reaction.C2 != null)
							{
								hYSYSConversionReaction.C2 = reaction.C2.Value;
							}
							if (Operation.Phase != null)
							{
								if (Operation.Phase == "V")
								{
									hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.VapourPhase;
								}
								else if (Operation.Phase == "L")
								{
									hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.LiquidPhase;
								}
								else
								{
									hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.Overall;
								}
							}
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("BuildHYSYSRxnFailed"), base.UID, text));
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else if (Rxcalc.MODEL == RXModelType.SHIFT || Rxcalc.MODEL == RXModelType.METHANATION)
		{
			ConversionReactor.ReactionSet = new HYSYSReactionSet(base.UID + "_Conv_" + Rxcalc.MODEL);
			if (Reactions.Count != 0)
			{
				foreach (REACTION reaction2 in Reactions)
				{
					try
					{
						HYSYSConversionReaction hYSYSConversionReaction2 = new HYSYSConversionReaction();
						hYSYSConversionReaction2.ID = reaction2.RXID + "_" + base.UID;
						ConversionReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSConversionReaction2);
						if (reaction2.C0 != null)
						{
							hYSYSConversionReaction2.C0 = reaction2.C0.Value;
						}
						else
						{
							hYSYSConversionReaction2.C0 = 1.0;
						}
						if (reaction2.C1 != null)
						{
							hYSYSConversionReaction2.C1 = reaction2.C1.Value;
						}
						if (reaction2.C2 != null)
						{
							hYSYSConversionReaction2.C2 = reaction2.C2.Value;
						}
						if (hYSYSConversionReaction2.ReactionBasis == null)
						{
							hYSYSConversionReaction2.ReactionBasis = new HYSYSConversionReaction.HYSYSConversionReactionBasis();
							hYSYSConversionReaction2.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase = HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.VapourPhase;
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("BuildHYSYSRxnFailed"), base.UID, reaction2.RXID));
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				HYSYSConversionReaction hYSYSConversionReaction3 = new HYSYSConversionReaction();
				hYSYSConversionReaction3.ID = Rxcalc.MODEL.ToString();
				ConversionReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSConversionReaction3);
			}
		}
		ObjCollection.Add(ConversionReactor.ReactionSet);
	}
}
internal class P2EquilibriumReactor : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class OPERATION : PropertyItem
	{
		private bool _ISOTHERMAL = true;

		private DoubleValue _DTFEED = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _DUTY = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMAX = new DoubleValue("K", 3000.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMIN = new DoubleValue("K", 1.0);

		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public bool ISOTHERMAL
		{
			get
			{
				return _ISOTHERMAL;
			}
			set
			{
				_ISOTHERMAL = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DTFEED
		{
			get
			{
				return _DTFEED;
			}
			set
			{
				_DTFEED = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public bool ISADIABATIC { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY
		{
			get
			{
				return _DUTY;
			}
			set
			{
				_DUTY = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMAX
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMAX;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMAX = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMIN
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMIN;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMIN = value;
			}
		}

		public string PHASE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }
	}

	public class RXCALC : PropertyItem
	{
		private RXModelType _MODEL;

		public RXModelType MODEL
		{
			get
			{
				return _MODEL;
			}
			set
			{
				_MODEL = value;
			}
		}

		public bool NOHBALANCE { get; set; }

		public string XOPTION { get; set; }

		public string REFS { get; set; }
	}

	public class PRINT : PropertyItem
	{
		public string PATH { get; set; }
	}

	public class RXSTOIC : PropertyItem
	{
		public string RXSET { get; set; }
	}

	public class REACTION : PropertyItem
	{
		public string RXID { get; set; }

		public int BASECOMPONENT { get; set; }

		public double A { get; set; }

		public double B { get; set; }

		public double C { get; set; }

		public double D { get; set; }

		public double E { get; set; }

		public double F { get; set; }

		public double G { get; set; }

		public double H { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue C0 { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue C1 { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue C2 { get; set; }
	}

	public enum RXModelType
	{
		STOIC,
		SHIFT,
		METHANATION
	}

	public OPERATION Operation { get; set; }

	public RXCALC Rxcalc { get; set; }

	public PRINT Print { get; set; }

	public RXSTOIC Rxstoic { get; set; }

	public List<REACTION> Reactions { get; set; }

	public bool IsDTFEEDSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsDUTYSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsTMAXSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsTMINSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsDPSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsMODELSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsNOHBALANCESet { get; set; }

	public bool IsOnlyVProduct { get; set; }

	public bool IsOnlyLProduct { get; set; }

	public bool IsOnlyWProduct { get; set; }

	public bool IsLSpecified { get; set; }

	public bool IsVSpecified { get; set; }

	public string SingleProduct { get; set; }

	public P2EquilibriumReactor(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			Operation = new OPERATION();
			Rxcalc = new RXCALC();
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.EQUILIBRIUMREACTOR + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "EQUILIBRIUM REACTOR", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(empty);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
					if (array2.Length != 0)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							string strName = array2[j];
							NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							base.FEED.Add(strName);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				Operation = new OPERATION();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL,
					CUnitOperation.DTFEED,
					CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
					CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC,
					CUnitOperation.DUTY,
					CUnitOperation.TMAX,
					CUnitOperation.TMIN,
					CUnitOperation.PHASE,
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item2.Content);
				}
				string[] array3 = list.ToArray();
				for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
				{
					array3[k] = array3[k].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL))
					{
						Operation.ISOTHERMAL = true;
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DTFEED) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDTFEEDSet = true;
						Operation.DTFEED = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						Operation.TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC))
					{
						Operation.ISADIABATIC = true;
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDUTYSet = true;
						Operation.DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMAX) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsTMAXSet = true;
						Operation.TMAX = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMIN) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsTMINSet = true;
						Operation.TMIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHASE))
					{
						Operation.PHASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDPSet = true;
						Operation.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						Operation.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXCALC))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.RXCALC);
				Rxcalc = new RXCALC();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit2 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.MODEL,
					CUnitOperation.NOHBALANCE,
					CUnitOperation.XOPTION,
					"REFS"
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item3 in propertySplit2)
				{
					list2.Add(item3.Content);
				}
				string[] array4 = list2.ToArray();
				for (int l = 0; l < array4.Length; l++)
				{
					array4[l] = array4[l].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MODEL) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsMODELSet = true;
						if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "STOIC")
						{
							Rxcalc.MODEL = RXModelType.STOIC;
						}
						else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "SHIFT")
						{
							Rxcalc.MODEL = RXModelType.SHIFT;
						}
						else if (ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]) == "METHANATION")
						{
							Rxcalc.MODEL = RXModelType.METHANATION;
						}
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NOHBALANCE))
					{
						IsNOHBALANCESet = true;
						Rxcalc.NOHBALANCE = true;
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.XOPTION))
					{
						Rxcalc.XOPTION = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]);
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith("REFS"))
					{
						Rxcalc.REFS = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRINT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRINT);
				Print = new PRINT();
				Print.PATH = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC);
				Rxstoic = new RXSTOIC();
				Rxstoic.RXSET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REACTION))
			{
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.REACTION);
				REACTION rEACTION = new REACTION();
				rEACTION.RXID = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				foreach (P2BaseObject child2 in child.Children)
				{
					if (child2.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BASE))
					{
						rEACTION.BASECOMPONENT = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, '='));
					}
					if (child2.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EQUILBRIUM))
					{
						string[] array5 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' '));
						for (int m = 0; m < array5.Length; m++)
						{
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("A"))
							{
								rEACTION.A = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("B"))
							{
								rEACTION.B = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("C"))
							{
								rEACTION.C = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("D"))
							{
								rEACTION.D = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("E"))
							{
								rEACTION.E = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("F"))
							{
								rEACTION.F = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("G"))
							{
								rEACTION.G = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
							if (array5[m].StartsWith("H"))
							{
								rEACTION.H = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
							}
						}
					}
					if (!child2.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.APPROACH))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' ').ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT))
					{
						rEACTION.DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' '));
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' ').ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRACTION))
					{
						string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child2.Name, ' ')));
						if (array6.Length >= 1)
						{
							rEACTION.C0 = new DoubleValue();
							rEACTION.C0.Value = Convert.ToDouble(array6[0]);
						}
						if (array6.Length >= 2)
						{
							rEACTION.C1 = new DoubleValue();
							rEACTION.C1.Value = Convert.ToDouble(array6[1]);
						}
						if (array6.Length == 3)
						{
							rEACTION.C2 = new DoubleValue();
							rEACTION.C2.Value = Convert.ToDouble(array6[2]);
						}
					}
				}
				if (Reactions == null)
				{
					Reactions = new List<REACTION>();
				}
				Reactions.Add(rEACTION);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "EQUILIBRIUM REACTOR", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CThermo.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CThermo.METHOD);
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSEquilibriumReactor hYSYSEquilibriumReactor = new HYSYSEquilibriumReactor();
		hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.UID = base.UID;
		hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.Name = base.Name;
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		list.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
		hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
		if (Operation != null)
		{
			ConvertOperationData(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
		}
		if (Rxcalc != null)
		{
			ConvertRxcalcData(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
		}
		if (Print != null)
		{
			hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PRINT"));
		}
		if (Rxstoic != null)
		{
			ConvertRxstoicData(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
		}
		if (Reactions != null)
		{
			ConvertReactionsData(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor, list);
		}
		if (base.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (base.IsMultipleFeed && (IsDTFEEDSet || (!Operation.ISADIABATIC && Operation.TEMPERATURE == null)))
		{
			HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
			if (hYSYSMixer != null)
			{
				list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.FluidPackage;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
		}
		if (base.PRODUCT != null)
		{
			foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				dictionary.Add(addedProperty, base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				switch (addedProperty)
				{
				case "V":
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "L":
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "M":
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.M = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "W":
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.W = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "S":
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.S = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				}
			}
			IsOnlyVProduct = false;
			IsOnlyLProduct = false;
			IsOnlyWProduct = false;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.M) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.S) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.W))
			{
				IsOnlyWProduct = true;
				SingleProduct = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.W;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L))
			{
				IsLSpecified = false;
				string item = (hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
				hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V))
				{
					IsOnlyVProduct = true;
					SingleProduct = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V;
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Remove(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V);
					string item2 = (hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item2);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IsLSpecified = true;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V))
			{
				string item3 = (hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
				hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item3);
				if (IsLSpecified)
				{
					IsOnlyLProduct = true;
					SingleProduct = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L;
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Remove(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L);
					string item4 = (hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item4);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IsVSpecified = true;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.W) || IsOnlyWProduct)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.M))
				{
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductToMixer(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor, hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.M);
					if (hYSYSMixer2 != null)
					{
						list.Add(hYSYSMixer2);
						hYSYSMixer2.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
				if ((IsOnlyVProduct || IsOnlyLProduct || IsOnlyWProduct) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SingleProduct))
				{
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer3 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductToMixer(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor, SingleProduct);
					if (hYSYSMixer3 != null)
					{
						list.Add(hYSYSMixer3);
						hYSYSMixer3.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				HYSYSSeparator hYSYSSeparator = null;
				hYSYSSeparator = new HYSYSSeparator();
				hYSYSSeparator.SeparatorType = HYSYSSeparatorType.ThreePhaseSep;
				hYSYSSeparator.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
				hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V);
				hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L);
				hYSYSSeparator.HeavyLiquidtStream = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.W;
				if (IsVSpecified && IsLSpecified)
				{
					string v = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V;
					string l = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L;
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream = v;
					hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream = l;
				}
				else if (IsOnlyLProduct || IsOnlyVProduct || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.M))
				{
					hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
					hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSSeparator.VapourOutletStream);
					hYSYSSeparator.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSSeparator.LiquidOutletStream);
					string mStream = string.Empty;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SingleProduct))
					{
						mStream = SingleProduct;
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.M))
					{
						mStream = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.M;
					}
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer4 = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleProductToMixer(hYSYSSeparator, mStream);
					if (hYSYSMixer4 != null)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.FluidPackage))
						{
							hYSYSMixer4.FluidPackage = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.FluidPackage;
						}
						list.Add(hYSYSMixer4);
						hYSYSMixer4.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
				hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.L);
				hYSYSSeparator.InletStreams.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.V);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSSeparator.FluidPackage = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.FluidPackage;
				}
				list.Add(hYSYSSeparator);
				hYSYSSeparator.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
		}
		hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.SetCreateStreams(list);
		if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
		{
			hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
			hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.SeparateDefines();
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}

	private void ConvertOperationData(HYSYSEquilibriumReactor EquilibriumReactor)
	{
		if (IsDTFEEDSet || (!IsDTFEEDSet && Operation.TEMPERATURE == null && !Operation.ISADIABATIC))
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.AttachedDTFEED = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, Operation.DTFEED);
		}
		else if (Operation.TEMPERATURE != null)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.AttachedTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, Operation.TEMPERATURE);
		}
		if (IsDUTYSet || Operation.ISADIABATIC)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, Operation.DUTY);
		}
		if (IsTMAXSet)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TMAX"));
		}
		if (IsTMINSet)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TMIN"));
		}
		if (IsDPSet)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.DP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, Operation.DP);
		}
		else if (Operation.PRESSURE != null)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.AttachedPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, Operation.PRESSURE);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertRxcalcData(HYSYSEquilibriumReactor EquilibriumReactor)
	{
		if (IsMODELSet)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "MODEL"));
		}
		if (IsNOHBALANCESet)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NOHBALANCE"));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Rxcalc.XOPTION))
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "XOPTION"));
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Rxcalc.REFS))
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "REFS"));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertRxstoicData(HYSYSEquilibriumReactor EquilibriumReactor)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Rxstoic.RXSET))
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ReactionSet = new HYSYSReactionSet(base.UID + "_Equil_" + Rxstoic.RXSET);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertReactionsData(HYSYSEquilibriumReactor EquilibriumReactor, List<HYSYSBaseObject> ObjCollection)
	{
		if (Rxcalc.MODEL == RXModelType.STOIC)
		{
			if (P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey("ReactionData"))
			{
				P2ReactionData p2ReactionData = (P2ReactionData)P2BaseObject.g_Map["ReactionData"];
				if (Reactions.Count != 0)
				{
					foreach (REACTION reaction in Reactions)
					{
						string text = base.UID + "_" + Rxstoic.RXSET + "_" + reaction.RXID;
						try
						{
							HYSYSEquilibriumReaction hYSYSEquilibriumReaction = p2ReactionData.CreateHYSYSEquilibriumRX(text, p2ReactionData.P2RXSetsDic[Rxstoic.RXSET].P2RXsDic[reaction.RXID]);
							EquilibriumReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReaction);
							if (reaction.A != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.A = reaction.A;
							}
							if (reaction.B != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.B = reaction.B;
							}
							if (reaction.C != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.C = reaction.C;
							}
							if (reaction.D != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.D = reaction.D;
							}
							if (reaction.E != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.E = reaction.E;
							}
							if (reaction.F != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.F = reaction.F;
							}
							if (reaction.G != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.G = reaction.G;
							}
							if (reaction.H != 0.0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.H = reaction.H;
							}
							if (reaction.BASECOMPONENT != 0)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.BaseComponent = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(reaction.BASECOMPONENT);
							}
							if (reaction.DT != null)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, reaction.DT);
							}
							if (reaction.C0 != null)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C0 = reaction.C0.Value;
							}
							if (reaction.C1 != null)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C1 = reaction.C1.Value;
							}
							if (reaction.C2 != null)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C2 = reaction.C2.Value;
							}
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Operation.PHASE))
							{
								if (Operation.PHASE == "V")
								{
									hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.Phase = EquilibriumReactionPhase.VapourPhase;
								}
								else if (Operation.PHASE == "L")
								{
									hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.Phase = EquilibriumReactionPhase.LiquidPhase;
								}
							}
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("BuildHYSYSRxnFailed"), base.UID, text));
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else if (Rxcalc.MODEL == RXModelType.SHIFT || Rxcalc.MODEL == RXModelType.METHANATION)
		{
			EquilibriumReactor.ReactionSet = new HYSYSReactionSet(base.UID + "_Equil_" + Rxcalc.MODEL);
			if (Reactions.Count != 0)
			{
				foreach (REACTION reaction2 in Reactions)
				{
					try
					{
						HYSYSEquilibriumReaction hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2 = new HYSYSEquilibriumReaction();
						hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.ID = reaction2.RXID + "_" + base.UID;
						EquilibriumReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2);
						if (reaction2.A != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.A = reaction2.A;
						}
						if (reaction2.B != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.B = reaction2.B;
						}
						if (reaction2.C != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.C = reaction2.C;
						}
						if (reaction2.D != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.D = reaction2.D;
						}
						if (reaction2.E != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.E = reaction2.E;
						}
						if (reaction2.F != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.F = reaction2.F;
						}
						if (reaction2.G != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.G = reaction2.G;
						}
						if (reaction2.H != 0.0)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.KeqParams.H = reaction2.H;
						}
						if (reaction2.DT != null)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.DT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, reaction2.DT);
						}
						if (reaction2.C0 != null)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.C0 = reaction2.C0.Value;
						}
						if (reaction2.C1 != null)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.C1 = reaction2.C1.Value;
						}
						if (reaction2.C2 != null)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.C2 = reaction2.C2.Value;
						}
						hYSYSEquilibriumReaction2.Phase = EquilibriumReactionPhase.VapourPhase;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("BuildHYSYSRxnFailed"), base.UID, reaction2.RXID));
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				HYSYSEquilibriumReaction hYSYSEquilibriumReaction3 = new HYSYSEquilibriumReaction();
				hYSYSEquilibriumReaction3.ID = Rxcalc.MODEL.ToString();
				EquilibriumReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReaction3);
			}
		}
		ObjCollection.Add(EquilibriumReactor.ReactionSet);
	}
}
internal class P2GibbsReactor : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class OPERATION : PropertyItem
	{
		private string _Phase = "M";

		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private bool _ISOTHERMAL = true;

		private DoubleValue _DUTY = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMAX = new DoubleValue("K", 3000.0);

		private DoubleValue _TMIN = new DoubleValue("K", 1.0);

		public string Phase
		{
			get
			{
				return _Phase;
			}
			set
			{
				_Phase = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public bool ISOTHERMAL
		{
			get
			{
				return _ISOTHERMAL;
			}
			set
			{
				_ISOTHERMAL = value;
			}
		}

		public bool ISADIABATIC { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY
		{
			get
			{
				return _DUTY;
			}
			set
			{
				_DUTY = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMAX
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMAX;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMAX = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue TMIN
		{
			get
			{
				return _TMIN;
			}
			set
			{
				_TMIN = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public class PARAMETER : PropertyItem
	{
		private int _MAXTRIALS = 50;

		private double _TOLERANCE = 0.0001;

		private double _FIBTOL = 0.01;

		private int _PROFLAG;

		private int _PHYSPROP = 1;

		public int MAXTRIALS
		{
			get
			{
				return _MAXTRIALS;
			}
			set
			{
				_MAXTRIALS = value;
			}
		}

		public double TOLERANCE
		{
			get
			{
				return _TOLERANCE;
			}
			set
			{
				_TOLERANCE = value;
			}
		}

		public double FIBTOL
		{
			get
			{
				return _FIBTOL;
			}
			set
			{
				_FIBTOL = value;
			}
		}

		public int PROFLAG
		{
			get
			{
				return _PROFLAG;
			}
			set
			{
				_PROFLAG = value;
			}
		}

		public int PHYSPROP
		{
			get
			{
				return _PHYSPROP;
			}
			set
			{
				_PHYSPROP = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public class TRIAL : PropertyItem
	{
		private int _START = 6;

		private int _FREQUENCY = 4;

		private string _INITIALPHASE = "VL";

		private double _ELIMINATE = 1E-06;

		public int START
		{
			get
			{
				return _START;
			}
			set
			{
				_START = value;
			}
		}

		public int FREQUENCY
		{
			get
			{
				return _FREQUENCY;
			}
			set
			{
				_FREQUENCY = value;
			}
		}

		public string INITIALPHASE
		{
			get
			{
				return _INITIALPHASE;
			}
			set
			{
				_INITIALPHASE = value;
			}
		}

		public double ELIMINATE
		{
			get
			{
				return _ELIMINATE;
			}
			set
			{
