				_ELIMINATE = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public class ELEMENTS : PropertyItem
	{
		public IDictionary<int, double> REACTANTS { get; set; }

		public string REACTANTSBASIS { get; set; }

		public double[] REACTANTCOMPONENTS { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, double[]> COMPONENTS { get; set; }

		public string[] NAMES { get; set; }

		public ELEMENTS()
		{
			REACTANTS = new Dictionary<int, double>();
			COMPONENTS = new Dictionary<int, double[]>();
		}
	}

	public class CONVERSION : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _APPROACH = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private string _RATEBASIS = "M";

		public DoubleValue APPROACH
		{
			get
			{
				return _APPROACH;
			}
			set
			{
				_APPROACH = value;
			}
		}

		public IDictionary<int, double> RATE { get; set; }

		public IDictionary<int, double> PERCENT { get; set; }

		public string RATEBASIS
		{
			get
			{
				return _RATEBASIS;
			}
			set
			{
				_RATEBASIS = value;
			}
		}

		public string RATEUNIT { get; set; }

		public CONVERSION()
		{
			PERCENT = new Dictionary<int, double>();
			RATE = new Dictionary<int, double>();
		}
	}

	public class RXSTOIC : PropertyItem
	{
		public string RXSET { get; set; }
	}

	public class REACTION : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _DT = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public string RXID { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DT
		{
			get
			{
				return _DT;
			}
			set
			{
				_DT = value;
			}
		}

		public TypeUnitValue EXTENT { get; set; }

		public int EXTENT_Index { get; set; }
	}

	public OPERATION Operation { get; set; }

	public PARAMETER Parameter { get; set; }

	public TRIAL Trial { get; set; }

	public ELEMENTS Elements { get; set; }

	public CONVERSION Conversion { get; set; }

	public RXSTOIC Rxstoic { get; set; }

	public List<REACTION> Reactions { get; set; }

	public bool IsPhaseSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsDPSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsDUTYSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsTMAXSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsTMINSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsMaxtrialsSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsToleranceSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsFibtolSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsProflagSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsPhyspropSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsStartSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsFrequencySet { get; set; }

	public bool IsInitialphaseSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsEliminateSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsApproachSet { get; set; }

	public bool IsDTSet { get; set; }

	public P2GibbsReactor(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			Operation = new OPERATION();
			Parameter = new PARAMETER();
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.GIBBSREACTOR + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "GIBBS REACTOR", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(empty);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpace(array[i].ToString());
					if (array2.Length != 0)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							string strName = array2[j];
							NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
							base.FEED.Add(strName);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				Operation = new OPERATION();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.PHASE,
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
					CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL,
					CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC,
					CUnitOperation.DUTY,
					CUnitOperation.TMAX,
					CUnitOperation.TMIN
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item2.Content);
				}
				string[] array3 = list.ToArray();
				for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
				{
					array3[k] = array3[k].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHASE) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						Operation.Phase = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDPSet = true;
						Operation.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						Operation.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						Operation.TEMPERATURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL))
					{
						Operation.ISOTHERMAL = true;
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC))
					{
						Operation.ISADIABATIC = true;
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsDUTYSet = true;
						Operation.DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMAX) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsTMAXSet = true;
						Operation.TMAX = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
					else if (array3[k].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TMIN) && array3[k].Contains("="))
					{
						IsTMINSet = true;
						Operation.TMIN = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array3[k]);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PARAMETER))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PARAMETER);
				Parameter = new PARAMETER();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit2 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.MAXTRIAL,
					CUnitOperation.TOLERANCE,
					CUnitOperation.FIBTOL,
					CUnitOperation.PROFLAG,
					CUnitOperation.PHYSPROP
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item3 in propertySplit2)
				{
					list2.Add(item3.Content);
				}
				string[] array4 = list2.ToArray();
				for (int l = 0; l < array4.Length; l++)
				{
					array4[l] = array4[l].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MAXTRIAL) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsMaxtrialsSet = true;
						Parameter.MAXTRIALS = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]));
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TOLERANCE) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsToleranceSet = true;
						Parameter.TOLERANCE = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]));
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FIBTOL) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsFibtolSet = true;
						Parameter.FIBTOL = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]));
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PROFLAG) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsProflagSet = true;
						Parameter.PROFLAG = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]));
					}
					if (array4[l].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PHYSPROP) && array4[l].Contains("="))
					{
						IsPhyspropSet = true;
						Parameter.PHYSPROP = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array4[l]));
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TRIAL))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.TRIAL);
				Trial = new TRIAL();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit3 = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.START,
					CUnitOperation.FREQUENCY,
					CUnitOperation.INITIALPHASE,
					CUnitOperation.ELIMINATE
				}, empty.ToUpper());
				List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item4 in propertySplit3)
				{
					list3.Add(item4.Content);
				}
				string[] array5 = list3.ToArray();
				for (int m = 0; m < array5.Length; m++)
				{
					array5[m] = array5[m].Trim().ToUpper();
					if (array5[m].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.START) && array5[m].Contains("="))
					{
						IsStartSet = true;
						Trial.START = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
					}
					if (array5[m].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FREQUENCY) && array5[m].Contains("="))
					{
						IsFrequencySet = true;
						Trial.FREQUENCY = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
					}
					if (array5[m].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.INITIALPHASE) && array5[m].Contains("="))
					{
						IsInitialphaseSet = true;
						Trial.INITIALPHASE = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]);
					}
					if (array5[m].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ELIMINATE) && array5[m].Contains("="))
					{
						IsEliminateSet = true;
						Trial.ELIMINATE = Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array5[m]));
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ELEMENTS))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.ELEMENTS);
				Elements = new ELEMENTS();
				foreach (PropertyPair item5 in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.REACTANTS,
					CUnitOperation.COMPONENTS,
					CUnitOperation.NAMES
				}, empty))
				{
					string content = item5.Content;
					if (item5.Name == CUnitOperation.REACTANTS)
					{
						string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content));
						string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(content);
						Elements.REACTANTSBASIS = parentheses;
						for (int n = 0; n < array6.Length; n++)
						{
							int key = Convert.ToInt16(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array6[n])[0]);
							double value = ((ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array6[n]).Length != 2) ? 0.0 : Convert.ToDouble(ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(array6[n])[1]));
							Elements.REACTANTS.Add(key, value);
						}
					}
					if (item5.Name == CUnitOperation.COMPONENTS)
					{
						string[] array6 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content));
						for (int num = 0; num < array6.Length; num++)
						{
							string[] array7 = array6[num].Split(',');
							int key2 = Convert.ToInt16(array7[0]);
							Elements.REACTANTCOMPONENTS = new double[array7.Length - 1];
							for (int num2 = 1; num2 < array7.Length; num2++)
							{
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array7[num2]))
								{
									Elements.REACTANTCOMPONENTS[num2 - 1] = Convert.ToDouble(array7[num2]);
								}
								else
								{
									Elements.REACTANTCOMPONENTS[num2 - 1] = 0.0;
								}
							}
							Elements.COMPONENTS.Add(key2, Elements.REACTANTCOMPONENTS);
						}
					}
					if (item5.Name == CUnitOperation.NAMES)
					{
						string[] nAMES = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content).Split(',');
						Elements.NAMES = nAMES;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONVERSION))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.CONVERSION);
				Conversion = new CONVERSION();
				foreach (PropertyPair item6 in ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.APPROACH,
					CUnitOperation.RATE,
					CUnitOperation.PERCENT
				}, empty))
				{
					string content2 = item6.Content;
					if (item6.Name == CUnitOperation.APPROACH)
					{
						IsApproachSet = true;
						if (content2.Contains("="))
						{
							Conversion.APPROACH = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(content2);
						}
					}
					if (item6.Name == CUnitOperation.RATE)
					{
						string[] array8 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content2));
						Conversion.RATEBASIS = ParseUtilty.GetStringsInBracket(content2)[0];
						Conversion.RATEUNIT = ParseUtilty.GetStringsInBracket(content2)[1];
						for (int num3 = 0; num3 < array8.Length; num3++)
						{
							int key3 = Convert.ToInt16(array8[num3].Split(',')[0]);
							double value2 = Convert.ToDouble(array8[num3].Split(',')[1]);
							Conversion.RATE.Add(key3, value2);
						}
					}
					if (item6.Name == CUnitOperation.PERCENT)
					{
						string[] array8 = ParseUtilty.CutBySlash(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(content2));
						for (int num4 = 0; num4 < array8.Length; num4++)
						{
							int key4 = Convert.ToInt16(array8[num4].Split(',')[0]);
							double value3 = Convert.ToDouble(array8[num4].Split(',')[1]);
							Conversion.PERCENT.Add(key4, value3);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.RXSTOIC);
				Rxstoic = new RXSTOIC();
				Rxstoic.RXSET = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REACTION))
			{
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.REACTION);
				REACTION rEACTION = new REACTION();
				rEACTION.RXID = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				if (child.Children.Count != 0)
				{
					if (ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Children[0].Name, ' ').ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT) && ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Children[0].Name, ' ').ToUpper().Contains("="))
					{
						rEACTION.DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' '));
					}
					else if (ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Children[0].Name, ' ').ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EXTENT))
					{
						rEACTION.EXTENT = new TypeUnitValue();
						string[] pairStringInBracket = ParseUtilty.GetPairStringInBracket(ParseUtilty.GetHead(child.Children[0].Name, '='));
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pairStringInBracket[0]))
						{
							rEACTION.EXTENT.Type = pairStringInBracket[0];
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pairStringInBracket[1]))
						{
							rEACTION.EXTENT.Unit = pairStringInBracket[1];
						}
						string[] array9 = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Children[0].Name, '='));
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array9[0]))
						{
							rEACTION.EXTENT_Index = Convert.ToInt16(array9[0]);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array9[1]))
						{
							rEACTION.EXTENT.Value = Convert.ToDouble(array9[1]);
						}
					}
				}
				if (Reactions == null)
				{
					Reactions = new List<REACTION>();
				}
				Reactions.Add(rEACTION);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty, "GIBBS REACTOR", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (child.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(CThermo.METHOD))
			{
				empty = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name, ' ');
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CThermo.METHOD);
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty.Trim());
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSGibbsReactor hYSYSGibbsReactor = new HYSYSGibbsReactor();
		hYSYSGibbsReactor.UID = base.UID;
		hYSYSGibbsReactor.Name = base.Name;
		Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
		hYSYSGibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		list.Add(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
		if (Operation != null)
		{
			ConvertOperationData(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
		}
		if (Parameter != null)
		{
			ConvertParameterData(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
		}
		if (Trial != null)
		{
			hYSYSGibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordsNotConverted"), "TRIAL"));
		}
		if (Elements != null)
		{
			ConvertElementsData(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
		}
		if (Conversion != null)
		{
			ConvertConversionData(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
		}
		if (Rxstoic != null)
		{
			ConvertRxstoicData(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
		}
		if (Reactions != null)
		{
			ConvertReactionsData(hYSYSGibbsReactor, list);
		}
		if (base.FEED != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
			{
				hYSYSGibbsReactor.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
			}
		}
		if (base.PRODUCT != null)
		{
			foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
			{
				hYSYSGibbsReactor.OutletStreams.Add(base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				dictionary.Add(addedProperty, base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString());
				switch (addedProperty)
				{
				case "V":
					hYSYSGibbsReactor.V = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "L":
					hYSYSGibbsReactor.L = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "M":
					hYSYSGibbsReactor.M = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "W":
					hYSYSGibbsReactor.W = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				case "S":
					hYSYSGibbsReactor.S = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					break;
				}
			}
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed && !Operation.ISADIABATIC && Operation.TEMPERATURE == null)
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
				if (hYSYSMixer != null)
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSGibbsReactor.FluidPackage))
					{
						hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSGibbsReactor.FluidPackage;
					}
					hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSGibbsReactor.L))
			{
				string item = (hYSYSGibbsReactor.L = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
				hYSYSGibbsReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSGibbsReactor.V))
			{
				string item2 = (hYSYSGibbsReactor.V = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
				hYSYSGibbsReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item2);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSGibbsReactor.W))
			{
				string item3 = (hYSYSGibbsReactor.W = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true));
				hYSYSGibbsReactor.OutletStreams.Add(item3);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSGibbsReactor.M))
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 = null;
				hYSYSMixer2 = new HYSYSMixer();
				hYSYSMixer2.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
				hYSYSMixer2.InletStreams.Add(hYSYSGibbsReactor.L);
				hYSYSMixer2.InletStreams.Add(hYSYSGibbsReactor.V);
				hYSYSMixer2.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSGibbsReactor.M);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSGibbsReactor.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSMixer2.FluidPackage = hYSYSGibbsReactor.FluidPackage;
				}
				list.Add(hYSYSMixer2);
				hYSYSMixer2.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
		}
		hYSYSGibbsReactor.SetCreateStreams(list);
		if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
		{
			hYSYSGibbsReactor.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
			hYSYSGibbsReactor.SeparateDefines();
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}

	private void ConvertOperationData(HYSYSGibbsReactor GibbsReactor)
	{
		if (IsPhaseSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PHASE"));
		}
		if (Operation.TEMPERATURE != null && !Operation.ISADIABATIC)
		{
			GibbsReactor.AttachedTemperature = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, Operation.TEMPERATURE);
		}
		if (Operation.ISADIABATIC)
		{
			GibbsReactor.IsAdiabatic = true;
		}
		if (IsDPSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.DP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, Operation.DP);
		}
		else if (Operation.PRESSURE != null)
		{
			GibbsReactor.AttachedPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, Operation.PRESSURE);
		}
		if (IsDUTYSet || Operation.ISADIABATIC)
		{
			GibbsReactor.Duty = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, Operation.DUTY);
		}
		if (IsTMAXSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TMAX"));
		}
		if (IsTMINSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TMIN"));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertParameterData(HYSYSGibbsReactor GibbsReactor)
	{
		if (IsMaxtrialsSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.MaxIteration = Parameter.MAXTRIALS;
		}
		if (IsToleranceSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.Tolerance = Parameter.TOLERANCE;
		}
		if (IsFibtolSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FIBTOL"));
		}
		if (IsProflagSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PROFLAG"));
		}
		if (IsPhyspropSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "PHYSPROP"));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertElementsData(HYSYSGibbsReactor GibbsReactor)
	{
		if (Elements.REACTANTS.ToArray().Length != 0)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int[] array = new int[P2Component.GetAllComponents().ToArray().Length];
			for (int i = 1; i <= P2Component.GetAllComponents().ToArray().Length; i++)
			{
				if (!Elements.REACTANTS.Keys.Contains(i))
				{
					array[num] = i;
					num++;
				}
			}
			GibbsReactor.InertComponents = new int[num];
			for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
			{
				GibbsReactor.InertComponents[j] = array[j];
			}
		}
		if (Elements.COMPONENTS.ToArray().Length != 0)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "COMPONENTS"));
		}
		if (Elements.NAMES != null)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NAMES"));
		}
	}

	private void ConvertConversionData(HYSYSGibbsReactor GibbsReactor)
	{
		if (IsApproachSet)
		{
			GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "CONVERSION APPROACH"));
		}
		if (Conversion.RATE.Count != 0)
		{
			if (Conversion.RATEBASIS != "M")
			{
				GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "RATE(W)"));
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (int key in Conversion.RATE.Keys)
				{
					DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue();
					doubleValue.Value = Conversion.RATE[key];
					doubleValue.Unit = Conversion.RATEUNIT;
					TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = new TypeUnitValue();
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit, "M");
					doubleValue.Value = typeUnitValue.Value;
					doubleValue.Unit = typeUnitValue.Unit;
					GibbsReactor.CompFixedSpec.Add(key, doubleValue);
				}
			}
		}
		if (Conversion.PERCENT.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (int key2 in Conversion.PERCENT.Keys)
		{
			GibbsReactor.CompFracSpec.Add(key2, 1.0 - Conversion.PERCENT[key2] / 100.0);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertRxstoicData(HYSYSGibbsReactor GibbsReactor)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Rxstoic.RXSET))
		{
			GibbsReactor.ReactionSet = new HYSYSReactionSet(base.UID + "_Gibbs_" + Rxstoic.RXSET);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertReactionsData(HYSYSGibbsReactor GibbsReactor, List<HYSYSBaseObject> ObjCollection)
	{
		if (P2BaseObject.g_Map.ContainsKey("ReactionData"))
		{
			P2ReactionData p2ReactionData = (P2ReactionData)P2BaseObject.g_Map["ReactionData"];
			if (Reactions.Count != 0)
			{
				foreach (REACTION reaction in Reactions)
				{
					string text = base.UID + "_" + Rxstoic.RXSET + "_" + reaction.RXID;
					try
					{
						HYSYSEquilibriumReaction hYSYSEquilibriumReaction = p2ReactionData.CreateHYSYSEquilibriumRX(text, p2ReactionData.P2RXSetsDic[Rxstoic.RXSET].P2RXsDic[reaction.RXID]);
						GibbsReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReaction);
						if (IsApproachSet && Conversion.APPROACH != null)
						{
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT = new DoubleValue();
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, Conversion.APPROACH);
						}
						if (reaction.DT.Value != 0.0)
						{
							if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT == null)
							{
								hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT = new DoubleValue();
							}
							hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, reaction.DT);
						}
						if (reaction.EXTENT != null)
						{
							GibbsReactor.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "EXTENT"));
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("BuildHYSYSRxnFailed"), base.UID, text));
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (GibbsReactor.ReactionSet != null)
		{
			ObjCollection.Add(GibbsReactor.ReactionSet);
		}
	}
}
public class P2Reset : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public TransferBasisEnum CONST { get; set; }

	public new string METHOD { get; set; }

	public new string PRODUCT { get; set; }

	public new string FEED { get; set; }

	public P2Reset(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.RESET + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "RESET", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "RESET", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			try
			{
				base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
				string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
				if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					FEED = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					PRODUCT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CONSTANT))
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					text = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
					if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
					{
						CONST = TransferBasisEnum.TP;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ENTH))
					{
						CONST = TransferBasisEnum.PH;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VFRAC))
					{
						CONST = TransferBasisEnum.VFP;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEW))
					{
						CONST = TransferBasisEnum.DEWP;
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.BUBBLE))
					{
						CONST = TransferBasisEnum.BUBBLEP;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		try
		{
			HYSYSCutter hYSYSCutter = new HYSYSCutter();
			list.Add(hYSYSCutter);
			hYSYSCutter.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSCutter.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSCutter.InletStreams.Add(FEED);
			hYSYSCutter.OutletStreams.Add(PRODUCT);
			hYSYSCutter.TransferBasis = CONST;
			hYSYSCutter.FluidPackage = METHOD;
			hYSYSCutter.SetCreateStreams(list);
		}
		catch
		{
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}
}
internal class P2Splitter : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class SplitterOperation : PropertyItem
	{
		private string _option = "FILL";

		private DoubleValue _DP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _DP;
			}
			set
			{
				_DP = value;
			}
		}

		public string OPTION
		{
			get
			{
				return _option;
			}
			set
			{
				_option = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public class SplitterProduct : PropertyItem
	{
		public List<string> V { get; set; }

		public List<string> L { get; set; }

		public List<string> M { get; set; }

		public SplitterProduct(string strSource)
		{
			string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(strSource);
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				if (text.StartsWith("V="))
				{
					string strName = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text).Trim();
					NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					if (V == null)
					{
						V = new List<string>();
					}
					V.Add(NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true));
					if (!base.AddedPropertyList.Contains("V"))
					{
						base.AddedPropertyList.Add("V");
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("L="))
				{
					string strName2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text).Trim();
					NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
					if (L == null)
					{
						L = new List<string>();
					}
					L.Add(NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true));
					if (!base.AddedPropertyList.Contains("L"))
					{
						base.AddedPropertyList.Add("L");
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("M="))
				{
					string strName3 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text).Trim();
					NameHelper.NotesName(strName3, bIsStream: true);
					if (M == null)
					{
						M = new List<string>();
					}
					M.Add(NameHelper.ConvertName(strName3, bIsStream: true));
					if (!base.AddedPropertyList.Contains("M"))
					{
						base.AddedPropertyList.Add("M");
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private SplitterOperation _operation = new SplitterOperation();

	public SplitterOperation OPERATION
	{
		get
		{
			return _operation;
		}
		set
		{
			_operation = value;
		}
	}

	public SplitterProduct SPLITTERPRODUCT { get; set; }

	public P2Splitter(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.SPLITTER + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "SPLITTER", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			empty = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(empty2);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string strName = array.GetValue(i).ToString();
					NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					base.FEED.Add(strName);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				SPLITTERPRODUCT = new SplitterProduct(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPECIFICATION))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				Specification item = new Specification(empty2, CUnitOperation.SPLITTER, base.UID);
				base.GlobalSpec.Add(item);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				OPERATION = new SplitterOperation();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.OPTION
				}, empty2.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item3 in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item3.Content);
				}
				string[] array2 = list.ToArray();
				foreach (string text in array2)
				{
					if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						if (text.Contains("="))
						{
							OPERATION.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
						}
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						OPERATION.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPTION) && text.Contains("="))
					{
						OPERATION.OPTION = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item2 = new DEFINE(empty2, "SPLITTER", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item2);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty2);
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			bool flag = false;
			HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = new HYSYSSplitter();
			hYSYSSplitter.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSSplitter.Name = base.Name;
			hYSYSSplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			if (OPERATION != null)
			{
				if ((OPERATION.DP != null && OPERATION.DP.Value != 0.0) || OPERATION.PRESSURE != null)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (OPERATION.OPTION != null && OPERATION.OPTION.Length > 0)
				{
					hYSYSSplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Option"));
				}
			}
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.FEED.Count; i++)
				{
					hYSYSSplitter.InletStreams.Add(base.FEED[i]);
				}
			}
			if (SPLITTERPRODUCT != null)
			{
				foreach (string addedProperty in SPLITTERPRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
				{
					foreach (string item in SPLITTERPRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty) as List<string>)
					{
						hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSSplitter.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSSplitter);
				if (hYSYSMixer != null)
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSSplitter.FluidPackage))
					{
						hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSSplitter.FluidPackage;
					}
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = new HYSYSValve();
				hYSYSValve.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
				HYSYSObjectHelper.InsertObjectInStream(hYSYSSplitter, hYSYSSplitter.InletStreams[0], bInletStream: true, hYSYSValve);
				if (OPERATION.PRESSURE != null)
				{
					hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRESSURE);
				}
				else
				{
					hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
				}
				list.Add(hYSYSValve);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSSplitter.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSValve.FluidPackage = hYSYSSplitter.FluidPackage;
				}
				hYSYSValve.SetCreateStreams(list);
				hYSYSSplitter.InletValveAdded = true;
			}
			if (base.GlobalSpec.Count > 0)
			{
				RetrieveLocalSpecs(hYSYSSplitter);
			}
			list.Add(hYSYSSplitter);
			hYSYSSplitter.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSSplitter.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSSplitter.SeparateDefines();
			}
			array = Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			array = null;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "SPLITTER", base.UID));
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void RetrieveLocalSpecs(HYSYSSplitter splitter)
	{
		foreach (Specification item in base.GlobalSpec)
		{
			item.SetGlobalProperty(splitter.UID, splitter.InletStreams, splitter.OutletStreams);
			if (!item.bGlobalSpec)
			{
				splitter.LocalSpec.Add(item);
			}
			else
			{
				splitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Global specification \"" + item.P2SpecExpression + "\""));
			}
		}
	}
}
public class UnitConverter
{
	private static bool isNeedOutPutException;

	private static UnitTypes UnitTypesData { get; set; }

	static UnitConverter()
	{
		isNeedOutPutException = true;
		if (UnitTypesData == null)
		{
			UnitTypesData = P2DataMapResultInputer.LoadP2DataMapResult();
		}
	}

	private static TypeUnitValue GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double valueIn, string unitKey, string unitClassType, out string p2RetrunKey)
	{
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey))
		{
			unitKey = unitKey.ToUpper();
			unitKey = HandleUnitWithSlashTime(unitKey);
			if (unitKey.Contains("MOLE"))
			{
				unitKey = unitKey.Replace("MOLE", "MOL");
			}
		}
		P2Title obj = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Title] as P2Title;
		IList<StringValue> specialDefine = obj.SpecialDefine;
		Dimensional defaultDimensional = obj.DefaultDimensional;
		p2RetrunKey = string.Empty;
		TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = new TypeUnitValue();
		typeUnitValue.Type = unitClassType;
		if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature)
		{
			unitClass = UnitClassEnum.Temperature;
		}
		try
		{
			string unitClassKey = UOM.GetUnitClassKey(unitClass, unitClassType);
			UnitType unitType = UnitTypesData.GetUnitType(defaultDimensional, unitClassKey);
			if (specialDefine == null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey) && unitClass == UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity && (defaultDimensional == Dimensional.Metric || defaultDimensional == Dimensional.SI))
			{
				unitClassKey = UOM.GetUnitClassKey(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, string.Empty);
				unitType = UnitTypesData.GetUnitType(defaultDimensional, unitClassKey);
				unitClass = UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity;
			}
			if (specialDefine != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey) && specialDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (StringValue item in specialDefine)
				{
					string empty = string.Empty;
					empty = ((unitClassKey.Length < 4) ? unitClassKey.ToUpper() : unitClassKey.Substring(0, 4).ToUpper());
					if (item.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith(empty))
					{
						if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity && item.Value.ToUpper().StartsWith("DENS") && item.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("XDEN"))
						{
							unitClassKey = UOM.GetUnitClassKey(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, string.Empty);
							unitType = UnitTypesData.GetUnitType(defaultDimensional, unitClassKey);
							unitClass = UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity;
							foreach (StringValue item2 in specialDefine)
							{
								if (item2.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("LDEN"))
								{
									unitKey = item2.Value;
									break;
								}
							}
						}
						else if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.MolarEnthalpy)
						{
							foreach (StringValue item3 in specialDefine)
							{
								if (item3.Name.ToUpper().StartsWith("ENTH"))
								{
									unitKey = item3.Value;
									break;
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							unitKey = item.Value;
						}
						break;
					}
					if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.DropPressure && item.Name.ToUpper() == "PRES")
					{
						unitKey = item.Value;
					}
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey))
			{
				unitKey = GetDerivedKey(unitClass, unitClassType);
			}
			unitKey = CheckUnitDividedByTime(unitClass, unitKey);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey))
			{
				typeUnitValue.Unit = unitType.DefaultUnit.HYSYSKey;
				p2RetrunKey = unitType.DefaultUnit.Symbol;
				switch (unitClassKey)
				{
				case "DUTY":
				{
					DoubleValue defaultDutyUnit = GetDefaultDutyUnit();
					typeUnitValue.Unit = unitType.Get(defaultDutyUnit.Unit).HYSYSKey;
					p2RetrunKey = unitType.Get(defaultDutyUnit.Unit).Symbol;
					typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unitType.Get(defaultDutyUnit.Unit).HYSYSMutiCoef * defaultDutyUnit.Value * 1000000.0;
					break;
				}
				case "MOLARENTHALPY":
					typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unitType.DefaultUnit.HYSYSMutiCoef * 1000.0;
					break;
				case "VELO":
				{
					DoubleValue defultVelocity = GetDefultVelocity();
					typeUnitValue.Unit = unitType.Get(defultVelocity.Unit).HYSYSKey;
					p2RetrunKey = unitType.Get(defultVelocity.Unit).Symbol;
					typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unitType.Get(defultVelocity.Unit).HYSYSMutiCoef * defultVelocity.Value;
					break;
				}
				default:
					typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unitType.DefaultUnit.HYSYSMutiCoef;
					break;
				}
			}
			else if (unitType.Units.ContainsKey(unitKey))
			{
				typeUnitValue.Unit = unitType.Get(unitKey).HYSYSKey;
				p2RetrunKey = unitType.Get(unitKey).Symbol;
				if (unitClassKey == "DUTY")
				{
					typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unitType.Get(unitKey).HYSYSMutiCoef * 1000000.0;
				}
				else if (unitClassKey == "MOLARENTHALPY")
				{
					typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unitType.DefaultUnit.HYSYSMutiCoef * 1000.0;
				}
				else
				{
					typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unitType.Get(unitKey).HYSYSMutiCoef;
				}
			}
			else if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.MoleLiquidVolume)
			{
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, Unit> unit in unitType.Units)
				{
					if (unit.Value.Symbol.StartsWith(unitKey))
					{
						typeUnitValue.Unit = unit.Value.HYSYSKey;
						typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn * unit.Value.HYSYSMutiCoef;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Fraction && string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey))
			{
				typeUnitValue.Unit = string.Empty;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			if (isNeedOutPutException)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UnitNotConverted"), unitClass.ToString(), valueIn, unitKey, ex.Message));
			}
			typeUnitValue.Value = valueIn;
			typeUnitValue.Unit = string.Empty;
		}
		return typeUnitValue;
	}

	public static TypeUnitValue GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double valueIn, string unitKey, string unitClassType)
	{
		isNeedOutPutException = true;
		string p2RetrunKey;
		if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Rate)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClassType))
			{
				unitClassType = "M";
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClassType) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey))
			{
				isNeedOutPutException = false;
				TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, "M", out p2RetrunKey);
				if (hysysValue == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
				{
					hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, "WT", out p2RetrunKey);
					if (hysysValue == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
					{
						hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, "V", out p2RetrunKey);
						if (hysysValue != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
						{
							unitClassType = "V";
						}
					}
					else if (hysysValue != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
					{
						unitClassType = "WT";
					}
				}
				else if (hysysValue != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
				{
					unitClassType = "M";
				}
			}
		}
		isNeedOutPutException = true;
		return GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, unitClassType, out p2RetrunKey);
	}

	public static string GetHysysRateType(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double valueIn, string unitKey, string unitClassType)
	{
		isNeedOutPutException = true;
		if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Rate)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClassType))
			{
				unitClassType = "M";
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClassType) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitKey))
			{
				isNeedOutPutException = false;
				TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, "M", out var p2RetrunKey);
				if (hysysValue == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
				{
					hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, "WT", out p2RetrunKey);
					if (hysysValue == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
					{
						hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, "V", out p2RetrunKey);
						if (hysysValue != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
						{
							unitClassType = "V";
						}
					}
					else if (hysysValue != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
					{
						unitClassType = "WT";
					}
				}
				else if (hysysValue != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hysysValue.Unit))
				{
					unitClassType = "M";
				}
			}
		}
		return unitClassType;
	}

	private static string CheckUnitDividedByTime(UnitClassEnum unitClass, string unit)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unit))
		{
			return unit;
		}
		unit = unit.ToUpper();
		if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Rate || unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Velocity || unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Work || unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Duty)
		{
			if (unit.IndexOf("/HR") > -1)
			{
				unit = unit.Replace("/HR", "/H");
			}
			else if (unit.IndexOf("/SEC") > -1)
			{
				unit = unit.Replace("/SEC", "/S");
			}
			else if (unit.IndexOf("/YR") > -1)
			{
				unit = unit.Replace("/YR", "/Y");
			}
			else if (unit.IndexOf("/MIN") > -1)
			{
				unit = unit.Replace("/MIN", "/M");
			}
			else if (unit.IndexOf("/WK") > -1)
			{
				unit = unit.Replace("/WK", "/W");
			}
			else if (unit.IndexOf("/DAY") > -1)
			{
				unit = unit.Replace("/DAY", "/D");
			}
		}
		return unit;
	}

	private static DoubleValue GetDefaultDutyUnit()
	{
		string basedUnitKey = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Heat);
		string basedUnitKey2 = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Time);
		DoubleValue p2Value = GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Heat, 1.0, basedUnitKey, "KJ");
		DoubleValue p2Value2 = GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Time, 1.0, basedUnitKey2, "HR");
		return new DoubleValue
		{
			Unit = "KJ/H",
			Value = p2Value.Value / p2Value2.Value
		};
	}

	public static TypeUnitValue GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum unitClass, TypeUnitValue val)
	{
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, val.Value, val.Unit, val.Type);
		hysysValue.Name = val.Name;
		return hysysValue;
	}

	private static string HandleUnitWithSlashTime(string unitSring)
	{
		unitSring = unitSring.Trim().Replace(" ", "");
		foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item in new Dictionary<string, string>
		{
			{ "/SECOND", "/S" },
			{ "/MINUTE", "/M" },
			{ "/HOUR", "/H" },
			{ "/DAY", "/D" },
			{ "/WEEK", "/W" },
			{ "/MONTH", "/MON" },
			{ "/YEAR", "/Y" }
		})
		{
			int num = unitSring.IndexOf(item.Key);
			if (num > 0 && num + item.Key.Length == unitSring.Length)
			{
				unitSring = unitSring.Substring(0, num) + item.Value;
				break;
			}
		}
		return unitSring;
	}

	public static DoubleValue GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double valueIn, string unitKey)
	{
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, string.Empty);
		return new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit, hysysValue.Value);
	}

	public static DoubleValue GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum unitClass, DoubleValue doubleValue)
	{
		if (doubleValue == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit, string.Empty);
		return new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit, hysysValue.Value);
	}

	public static DoubleValue GetHysysActGasVRate(DoubleValue doubleValue)
	{
		if (doubleValue == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit, "GV");
		return GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HYSYS_ACT_GAS_VFLOW, new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit.ToUpper(), hysysValue.Value));
	}

	public static TypeUnitValue GetHysysStdGasVRate(DoubleValue doubleValue, string unitClassType)
	{
		if (doubleValue == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClassType))
		{
			return null;
		}
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit, unitClassType);
		switch (unitClassType)
		{
		case "G":
		case "GV":
		case "L":
		case "LV":
		{
			DoubleValue hysysValue2 = GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HYSYS_VOL_TO_STD_GAS_VFLOW, new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit.ToUpper(), hysysValue.Value));
			hysysValue.Value = hysysValue2.Value;
			hysysValue.Unit = hysysValue2.Unit;
			break;
		}
		}
		return hysysValue;
	}

	public static DoubleValue GetHysysMassDensityValue(DoubleValue doubleValue)
	{
		if (doubleValue == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity, doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit, string.Empty);
		if (hysysValue.Unit == "API_60")
		{
			hysysValue.Unit = "API";
		}
		return new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit, hysysValue.Value);
	}

	public static DoubleValues GetHysysMassDensityValues(DoubleValues doubleValues)
	{
		if (doubleValues == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		DoubleValues hysysValues = GetHysysValues(UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity, doubleValues.Values, doubleValues.Unit, string.Empty);
		if (hysysValues.Unit == "API_60")
		{
			hysysValues.Unit = "API";
		}
		return hysysValues;
	}

	public static DoubleValues GetHysysValues(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double[] valueIn, string unitKey, string unitClassType)
	{
		DoubleValues doubleValues = new DoubleValues();
		try
		{
			TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, 1.0, unitKey, unitClassType);
			doubleValues.Unit = hysysValue.Unit;
			List<double> list = new List<double>();
			if (valueIn != null && valueIn.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < valueIn.Length; i++)
				{
					list.Add(valueIn[i] * hysysValue.Value);
				}
			}
			doubleValues.Values = list.ToArray();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			doubleValues.Values = valueIn;
			doubleValues.Unit = unitKey;
		}
		return doubleValues;
	}

	public static DoubleValues GetHysysValues(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double[] valueIn, string unitKey)
	{
		return GetHysysValues(unitClass, valueIn, unitKey, string.Empty);
	}

	public static DoubleValues GetHysysValues(UnitClassEnum unitClass, DoubleValues doubleValues)
	{
		if (doubleValues == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		return GetHysysValues(unitClass, doubleValues.Values, doubleValues.Unit, string.Empty);
	}

	public static string GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum unitClass, string unitKey)
	{
		return GetHysysValue(unitClass, 1.0, unitKey).Unit;
	}

	public static string GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum unitClass, string unitKey, string unitBasis)
	{
		return GetHysysValue(unitClass, 1.0, unitKey, unitBasis).Unit;
	}

	private static string GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum unitClass)
	{
		P2Title obj = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Title] as P2Title;
		IList<StringValue> specialDefine = obj.SpecialDefine;
		Dimensional defaultDimensional = obj.DefaultDimensional;
		string text = string.Empty;
		string unitClassKey = UOM.GetUnitClassKey(unitClass, string.Empty);
		UnitType unitType = UnitTypesData.GetUnitType(defaultDimensional, unitClassKey);
		foreach (StringValue item in specialDefine)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			empty = ((unitClassKey.Length < 4) ? unitClassKey.ToUpper() : unitClassKey.Substring(0, 4).ToUpper());
			if (item.Name.StartsWith(empty))
			{
				text = item.Value;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
		{
			text = unitType.DefaultUnit.Symbol;
		}
		return text;
	}

	private static UnitType GetUnitTypeByClass(UnitClassEnum unitClass)
	{
		P2Title obj = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Title] as P2Title;
		_ = obj.SpecialDefine;
		Dimensional defaultDimensional = obj.DefaultDimensional;
		_ = string.Empty;
		string unitClassKey = UOM.GetUnitClassKey(unitClass, string.Empty);
		return UnitTypesData.GetUnitType(defaultDimensional, unitClassKey);
	}

	private static string GetDerivedKey(UnitClassEnum unitClass, string unitClassType)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		string empty2 = string.Empty;
		string empty3 = string.Empty;
		switch (unitClass)
		{
		case UnitClassEnum.Area:
			return empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Length) + "2";
		case UnitClassEnum.Rate:
			empty2 = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Time);
			empty2 = ((!empty2.StartsWith("MON")) ? empty2[0].ToString() : "MON");
			switch (unitClassType)
			{
			case "M":
				empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Mass);
				switch (empty)
				{
				case "TONM":
					empty = "TMM";
					break;
				case "KTONM":
					empty = "KTMM";
					break;
				case "TON":
					empty = "TSM";
					break;
				case "TONL":
					empty = "TLM";
					break;
				case "G":
				case "GR":
					empty = "GM";
					break;
				default:
					empty += "M";
					break;
				}
				return empty + "/" + empty2;
			case "WT":
			case "W":
				empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Mass);
				switch (empty)
				{
				case "TONM":
					empty = "TM";
					break;
				case "KTONM":
					empty = "KTM";
					break;
				case "TON":
					empty = "TS";
					break;
				case "TONL":
					empty = "TL";
					break;
				case "G":
				case "GR":
					empty = "G";
					break;
				}
				return empty + "/" + empty2;
			case "LV":
			case "V":
				empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume);
				return empty + "/" + empty2;
			case "GV":
			case "G":
				empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.VaporVolume);
				return empty + "/" + empty2;
			}
			break;
		case UnitClassEnum.UA:
			empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.HeatTransferCoefficient);
			empty2 = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Length) + "2";
			return empty + empty2;
		case UnitClassEnum.VolumEnthalpy:
			empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Heat);
			empty2 = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume);
			return empty + "/" + empty2;
		case UnitClassEnum.HeatCapacity:
		{
			empty = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Heat);
			empty2 = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Mass);
			empty3 = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Temperature);
			UnitType unitTypeByClass = GetUnitTypeByClass(UnitClassEnum.HeatCapacity);
			IDictionary<string, Unit> units = unitTypeByClass.GetUnits();
			string text = string.Empty;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, Unit> item in units)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty3) && item.Key.IndexOf(empty) > -1 && item.Key.IndexOf(empty2) > -1 && item.Key.IndexOf(empty3) > -1)
				{
					text = item.Key;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = unitTypeByClass.DefaultUnit.Symbol;
			}
			return text;
		}
		}
		return string.Empty;
	}

	private static DoubleValue GetDefultVelocity()
	{
		string basedUnitKey = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Length);
		string basedUnitKey2 = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.Time);
		DoubleValue p2Value = GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Length, 1.0, basedUnitKey, "M");
		DoubleValue p2Value2 = GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Time, 1.0, basedUnitKey2, "SEC");
		return new DoubleValue
		{
			Unit = "M/S",
			Value = p2Value.Value / p2Value2.Value
		};
	}

	public static DoubleValue GetHysysUA(string heatTransferCoefficientKey, string areaKey, double value, bool isUA)
	{
		DoubleValue result = null;
		string text = string.Empty;
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(heatTransferCoefficientKey))
		{
			heatTransferCoefficientKey = GetBasedUnitKey(UnitClassEnum.HeatTransferCoefficient);
		}
		if (!isUA)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(areaKey))
			{
				areaKey = GetDerivedKey(UnitClassEnum.Area, string.Empty);
			}
			text = heatTransferCoefficientKey + areaKey;
		}
		else
		{
			switch (heatTransferCoefficientKey)
			{
			case "KW/MK":
				text = "KW/K";
				break;
			case "KC/H":
				text = "KC/HC";
				break;
			case "KJ/H":
				text = "KJ/HK";
				break;
			case "BTU/H":
				text = "BTU/HF";
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
		{
			result = GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.UA, value, text);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static string GetP2GlobalUnit(UnitClassEnum unitClass)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClass.ToString()))
		{
			return string.Empty;
		}
		GetHysysValue(unitClass, 1.0, string.Empty, string.Empty, out var p2RetrunKey);
		return p2RetrunKey;
	}

	public static string GetP2GlobalUnit(UnitClassEnum unitClass, string rateClassType)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClass.ToString()))
		{
			return string.Empty;
		}
		GetHysysValue(unitClass, 1.0, string.Empty, rateClassType, out var p2RetrunKey);
		return p2RetrunKey;
	}

	public static DoubleValue GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double val, string initialUnit, string targetUnit, string rateType)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitClass.ToString()))
		{
			return null;
		}
		string empty = string.Empty;
		empty = ((unitClass != UnitClassEnum.Rate) ? GetP2GlobalUnit(unitClass) : GetP2GlobalUnit(unitClass, rateType));
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetUnit))
		{
			targetUnit = GetP2GlobalUnit(unitClass, rateType);
		}
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = GetHysysValue(unitClass, 1.0, initialUnit, rateType);
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue2 = GetHysysValue(unitClass, 1.0, targetUnit, rateType);
		if (hysysValue == null || hysysValue2 == null)
		{
			return new DoubleValue(empty, val);
		}
		double offsetValue;
		DoubleValue unitCoefficient = HYSYSUOMMap.GetUnitCoefficient(unitClass, hysysValue.Unit, rateType, out offsetValue);
		double offsetValue2;
		DoubleValue unitCoefficient2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetUnitCoefficient(unitClass, hysysValue2.Unit, rateType, out offsetValue2);
		if (unitCoefficient == null || unitCoefficient2 == null)
		{
			return new DoubleValue(empty, val);
		}
		DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue();
		doubleValue.Unit = targetUnit;
		if (unitClass == UnitClassEnum.Temperature)
		{
			doubleValue.Value = HYSYSUOMMap.GetTempConvertedValue(val, hysysValue.Unit, hysysValue2.Unit);
		}
		else
		{
			doubleValue.Value = (val - offsetValue) * unitCoefficient2.Value / unitCoefficient.Value + offsetValue2;
		}
		return doubleValue;
	}

	public static DoubleValue GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum unitClass, double val, string initialUnit, string targetUnit)
	{
		return GetP2Value(unitClass, val, initialUnit, targetUnit, string.Empty);
	}

	public static DoubleValue CovertGVToMV(DoubleValue valin)
	{
		string unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, "GV").Unit;
		DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, valin, unit, "GV");
		return new DoubleValue("kgmole/s", convertedValue.Value * 0.0422115);
	}
}
public class P2BVLEUtility : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class EVALUATE
	{
		private double _XVALUES1;

		private double _XVALUES2 = 1.0;

		private int _POINTS = 21;

		public int COMPi { get; set; }

		public int COMPj { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TEST { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PEST { get; set; }

		public double XVALUES1
		{
			get
			{
				return _XVALUES1;
			}
			set
			{
				_XVALUES1 = value;
			}
		}

		public double XVALUES2
		{
			get
			{
				return _XVALUES2;
			}
			set
			{
				_XVALUES2 = value;
			}
		}

		public int POINTS
		{
			get
			{
				return _POINTS;
			}
			set
			{
				_POINTS = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DELX { get; set; }

		public bool DEW { get; set; }

		public string PLOT { get; set; }
	}

	public List<EVALUATE> EVALUATEs { get; set; }

	public P2BVLEUtility(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.UTILITY + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "BVLE", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "BVLE", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			if (EVALUATEs == null)
			{
				EVALUATEs = new List<EVALUATE>();
			}
			string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
			if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EVALUATE))
				{
					continue;
				}
				EVALUATE eVALUATE = new EVALUATE();
				EVALUATEs.Add(eVALUATE);
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.COMPRESSOR,
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
					CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE,
					CUnitOperation.PEST,
					CUnitOperation.XVALUES,
					CUnitOperation.POINTS,
					CUnitOperation.DELX,
					CUnitOperation.DEW,
					CUnitOperation.PLOT
				}, text.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item.Content);
				}
				string[] array = list.ToArray();
				if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					try
					{
						if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COMPONENT))
						{
							string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]));
							if (array2 != null && array2.Length == 2)
							{
								eVALUATE.COMPi = int.Parse(array2[0].Trim());
								eVALUATE.COMPj = int.Parse(array2[1].Trim());
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
						{
							eVALUATE.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
						{
							eVALUATE.TEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
						{
							eVALUATE.TEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PEST))
						{
							eVALUATE.PEST = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.XVALUES))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]));
								if (array3 != null && array3.Length == 2)
								{
									eVALUATE.XVALUES1 = int.Parse(array3[0].Trim());
									eVALUATE.XVALUES2 = int.Parse(array3[1].Trim());
								}
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POINTS))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								eVALUATE.POINTS = (int)ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DELX))
						{
							eVALUATE.DELX = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEW))
						{
							eVALUATE.DEW = true;
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLOT))
						{
							eVALUATE.PLOT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						base.bParsingError = true;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		if (EVALUATEs != null && EVALUATEs.Count > 0)
		{
			int num = -1;
			foreach (EVALUATE eVALUATE in EVALUATEs)
			{
				num++;
				try
				{
					HYSYSPropertyTableUtility hYSYSPropertyTableUtility = new HYSYSPropertyTableUtility();
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(eVALUATE.PLOT) && (eVALUATE.PLOT.StartsWith("KVAL") || eVALUATE.PLOT.StartsWith("ALL")))
					{
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Kvalue = true;
						string item;
						if (eVALUATE.PLOT.StartsWith("ALL"))
						{
							item = $"Only KVALUE Plot was converted.";
							hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						}
						list.Add(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility);
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.UID = base.UID + "_" + num;
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.COMPi = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(eVALUATE.COMPi);
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.COMPj = P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(eVALUATE.COMPj);
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.PRESSURE);
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, eVALUATE.TEMP);
						item = $"POINTS={eVALUATE.POINTS} was not converted.";
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						if (eVALUATE.DEW)
						{
							item = $"DEW was not converted.";
							hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						}
						if (eVALUATE.PEST != null)
						{
							DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.PEST);
							item = $"PEST={hysysValue.Value} {hysysValue.Unit} was not converted.";
							hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						}
						item = $"XVALUES {eVALUATE.XVALUES1} {eVALUATE.XVALUES2} were not converted.";
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						item = $"POINTS = {eVALUATE.POINTS} was ignored.";
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						if (eVALUATE.DELX != null)
						{
							item = $"DELX={eVALUATE.DELX} was not converted.";
							hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						}
						if (eVALUATE.TEST != null)
						{
							DoubleValue hysysValue2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, eVALUATE.TEST);
							item = $"TEST={hysysValue2.Value} {hysysValue2.Unit} was not converted.";
							hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						string item = $"BVLE {base.UID} EVALUATE COMP {eVALUATE.COMPi} {eVALUATE.COMPj}  Plot {eVALUATE.PLOT} were not converted.";
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Warning, item);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "BVLE", base.UID));
				}
			}
		}
		return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
	}
}
public class P2DEPRESSUREUtility : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class Vessel
	{
		private double _VOLFACTOR = 1.0;

		public DoubleValue VOLUME { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue WEIGHT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue CP { get; set; }

		public TypeUnitValue HOLDUP { get; set; }

		public bool IsSPHERICAL { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DIAMETER { get; set; }

		public double VOLFACTOR
		{
			get
			{
				return _VOLFACTOR;
			}
			set
			{
				_VOLFACTOR = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue LIQHEIGHT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue HORIZONTAL { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue VERTICAL { get; set; }

		public Vessel(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
			SetValue(text.Trim());
		}

		public void SetValue(string sSource)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.VOLUME,
				CUnitOperation.WEIGHT,
				CUnitOperation.CP,
				CUnitOperation.HOLDUP,
				CUnitOperation.SPHERICAL,
				CUnitOperation.DIAMETER,
				CUnitOperation.VOLFACTOR,
				CUnitOperation.LIQHEIGHT,
				CUnitOperation.HORIZONTAL,
				CUnitOperation.VERTICAL,
				CUnitOperation.CHARGE
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array != null && array.Length == 1)
			{
				array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array[0]);
			}
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VOLUME))
					{
						VOLUME = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WEIGHT))
					{
						WEIGHT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CP))
					{
						CP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HOLDUP))
					{
						HOLDUP = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(array[i]);
						if (HOLDUP != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(HOLDUP.Type))
						{
							HOLDUP.Type = "M";
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SPHERICAL))
					{
						IsSPHERICAL = true;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DIAMETER))
					{
						DIAMETER = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CHARGE))
					{
						HOLDUP = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(array[i]);
						if (HOLDUP != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(HOLDUP.Type))
						{
							HOLDUP.Type = "M";
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VOLFACTOR))
					{
						VOLFACTOR = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LIQHEIGHT))
					{
						LIQHEIGHT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HORIZONTAL))
					{
						HORIZONTAL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VERTICAL))
					{
						VERTICAL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Operation
	{

		private double _VTOL = 1E-05;

		public Operation(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
			SetValue(text.Trim());
		}

		public void SetValue(string sSource)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.PFINAL,
				CUnitOperation.FINALTIME,
				CUnitOperation.DTIME,
				CUnitOperation.STEPTIME,
				CUnitOperation.ISENTROPIC,
				CUnitOperation.RELIEFTIME,
				CUnitOperation.PRELIEF,
				CUnitOperation.VTOL,
				CUnitOperation.XOPTION,
				CUnitOperation.MAXSTEPS,
				CUnitOperation.POPT,
				CUnitOperation.CSTEP,
				CUnitOperation.CSIZE
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PFINAL))
					{
						PFINAL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FINALTIME))
					{
						FINALTIME = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DTIME))
					{
						DTIME = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STEPTIME))
					{
						STEPTIME = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISENTROPIC))
					{
						ISENTROPIC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RELIEFTIME))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							RELIEFTIME = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRELIEF))
					{
						PRELIEF = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VTOL))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							VTOL = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.XOPTION))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							XOPTION = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MAXSTEPS))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							MAXSTEPS = (int)ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POPT))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							POPT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSTEP))
					{
						if (array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							CSTEP = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CSIZE) && array[i].Contains('='))
					{
						CSIZE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}

				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class Heat
	{
		private string _MODEL = "USER";

		private double _QFAC = 1.0;

		private double _AFAC = 1.0;

		private double _HTCFAC = 1.0;

		public string HeatP2String { get; set; }

		public string MODEL
		{
			get
			{
				return _MODEL;
			}
			set

		}

		public List<double> DATA { get; set; }

		public double QFAC
		{
			get

			set

		public DoubleValue HTC { get; set; }
