		public Heat(P2BaseObject obj)
		{
			string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ');
			SetValue(text.Trim());
			HeatP2String = obj.Name;
		}

		public void SetValue(string sSource)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.MODEL,
				CUnitOperation.DATA,
				CUnitOperation.QFAC,
				CUnitOperation.IWAREA,
				CUnitOperation.AFAC,
				CUnitOperation.HTAREA,
				CUnitOperation.HTCFAC,
				CUnitOperation.HTC,
				CUnitOperation.HTCVAP,
				CUnitOperation.HTCLIQ
			}, sSource.ToUpper());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MODEL))
					{
						MODEL = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
					}
					else
					{
						if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DATA))
						{
							string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringValue))
							{
								continue;
							}
							List<string> list2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(stringValue).ToList();
							DATA = new List<double>();
							foreach (string item2 in list2)
							{
								double.TryParse(item2, out var result);
								DATA.Add(result);
							}
							continue;
						}
						if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.QFAC))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								QFAC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.IWAREA))
						{
							IWAREA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.AFAC))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								AFAC = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(array[i]).Value;
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTAREA))
						{
							HTAREA = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTCFAC))
						{
							HTCFAC = ParseUtilty.GetTypeUnitValue(array[i]).Value;
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTC))
						{
							HTC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTCVAP))
						{
							HTCVAP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HTCLIQ))
						{
							HTCLIQ = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public string MAKEUP { get; set; }

	public Vessel VESSEL { get; set; }

	public Operation OPERATION { get; set; }

	public Reliefvalve RELIEFVALVE { get; set; }

	public Heat HEAT { get; set; }

	public List<string> PLOT { get; set; }

	public string PRINT { get; set; }

	public List<DEFINE> DEFINEs { get; set; }

	public List<string> DEFINEstrs { get; set; }

	public new List<string> PRODUCT { get; set; }

	public P2DEPRESSUREUtility()
	{
	}

	public P2DEPRESSUREUtility(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HeatCapacity, new DoubleValue("", 1.0));
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.UTILITY + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "DEPRESSURE", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "DEPRESSURE", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			try
			{
				base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
				string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
				if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MAKEUP))
				{
					text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					MAKEUP = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
				{
					base.FEED = new List<string>();
					string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' '));
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						string strName = array[i].Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
						base.FEED.Add(strName);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
				{
					PRODUCT = new List<string>();
					string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' '));
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						string strName2 = array2[j].Trim();
						NameHelper.NotesName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
						strName2 = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName2, bIsStream: true);
						PRODUCT.Add(strName2);
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VESSEL))
				{
					VESSEL = new Vessel(child);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
				{
					OPERATION = new Operation(child);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RELIEFVALVE))
				{
					RELIEFVALVE = new Reliefvalve(child);
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.HEAT))
				{
					HEAT = new Heat(child);
				}
				if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRINT))
				{
					PRINT = text;
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PLOT))
				{
					string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(text);
					if (array3 != null)
					{
						PLOT = array3.ToList();
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
				{
					string defineExpress = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
					if (DEFINEs == null)
					{
						DEFINEs = new List<DEFINE>();
					}
					DEFINE item = new DEFINE(defineExpress, "DEPRESSURE", base.UID);
					DEFINEs.Add(item);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				base.bParsingError = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		try
		{
			bool flag = true;
			HYSYSDerpessureUtility hYSYSDerpessureUtility = new HYSYSDerpessureUtility();
			hYSYSDerpessureUtility.UID = base.UID + "_" + base.Name;
			hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Feed = base.FEED[0];
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Utility_OnlyStreamInletStream"), base.FEED[0]));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(MAKEUP))
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.MakeUpStringName = MAKEUP;
			}
			if (PRODUCT != null && PRODUCT.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("PRODUCT {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (VESSEL != null)
			{
				if (VESSEL.VERTICAL != null)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Height = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, VESSEL.VERTICAL);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, VESSEL.DIAMETER);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter.Value = hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter.Value * VESSEL.VOLFACTOR;
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.IsVertical = true;
				}
				else if (VESSEL.HORIZONTAL != null)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Height = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, VESSEL.HORIZONTAL);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, VESSEL.DIAMETER);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter.Value = hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter.Value * VESSEL.VOLFACTOR;
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.IsVertical = false;
				}
				else if (VESSEL.VOLUME != null)
				{
					string strMessage = string.Format("DEPRESSURE {0} {1}", base.UID, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, strMessage);
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (VESSEL.IsSPHERICAL)
				{
					string strMessage2 = string.Format("DEPRESSURE {0} {1}", base.UID, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, strMessage2);
					flag = false;
				}
				if (VESSEL.LIQHEIGHT != null)
				{
					DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, VESSEL.LIQHEIGHT);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("LIQHEIGHT ={0}{1}  {2}", hysysValue.Value, hysysValue.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (VESSEL.CP != null)
				{
					DoubleValue hysysValue2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, VESSEL.CP);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("CP ={0}{1} {2}", hysysValue2.Value, hysysValue2.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (VESSEL.WEIGHT != null)
				{
					DoubleValue hysysValue3 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Mass, VESSEL.WEIGHT);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("WEIGHT ={0}{1}  {2}", hysysValue3.Value, hysysValue3.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = null;
			if (OPERATION.PFINAL != null)
			{
				DoubleValue hysysValue4 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PFINAL);
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION PFINAL ={0}{1} {2}", hysysValue4.Value, hysysValue4.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (OPERATION.DTIME != null)
			{
				DoubleValue hysysValue5 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Time, OPERATION.DTIME);
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION DTIME ={0}{1} {2}", hysysValue5.Value, hysysValue5.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (OPERATION.STEPTIME != null)
			{
				DoubleValue hysysValue6 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Time, OPERATION.STEPTIME);
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.TimeStepSize = hysysValue6;
			}
			if (OPERATION.FINALTIME != null)
			{
				DoubleValue hysysValue7 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Time, OPERATION.FINALTIME);
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.DepressuringTime = hysysValue7;
			}
			if (OPERATION.ISENTROPIC != null)
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION ISENTROPIC  {0}", P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (OPERATION.RELIEFTIME != null)
			{
				DoubleValue hysysValue8 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Time, OPERATION.RELIEFTIME);
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION RELIEFTIME ={0}{1} {2}", hysysValue8.Value, hysysValue8.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (OPERATION.PRELIEF != null)
			{
				DoubleValue hysysValue9 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRELIEF);
				hYSYSStream = new HYSYSStream();
				hYSYSStream.Pressure = hysysValue9;
				hYSYSStream.RefStream = base.FEED[0];
				hYSYSStream.Stream = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, "_Ref_" + base.FEED[0]);
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Feed = hYSYSStream.Stream;
			}
			if (OPERATION.VTOL != 0.0)
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION VTOL ={0} {1}", OPERATION.VTOL, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION XOPTION ={0} {1}", OPERATION.XOPTION, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			if (OPERATION.MAXSTEPS != 0)
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION MAXSTEPS ={0} {1}", OPERATION.MAXSTEPS, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(OPERATION.CSTEP))
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION CSTEP ={0} {1}", OPERATION.CSTEP, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(OPERATION.POPT))
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION POPT ={0} {1}", OPERATION.POPT, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("OPERATION CSIZE ={0} {1}", OPERATION.CSIZE, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			if (RELIEFVALVE != null)
			{
				if (RELIEFVALVE.MODEL.StartsWith("SUPE"))
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Equation = HYSYSDerpessureUtility.VapourFlowEquation.Supersonic;
				}
				if (RELIEFVALVE.MODEL.StartsWith("SUBS"))
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Equation = HYSYSDerpessureUtility.VapourFlowEquation.Subsonic;
				}
				if (RELIEFVALVE.MODEL.StartsWith("USER"))
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Equation = HYSYSDerpessureUtility.VapourFlowEquation.Masoneilan;
				}
				if (RELIEFVALVE.MODEL.StartsWith("CONS"))
				{
					string strMessage3 = string.Format("DEPRESSURE {0}  {1}", base.UID, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, strMessage3);
					flag = false;
				}
				if (RELIEFVALVE.CONSTANT != 0.0)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cd = 1.0;
					DoubleValue p2Value = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Mass, 1.0, "", "KG");
					DoubleValue p2Value2 = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Time, 1.0, "", "SEC");
					DoubleValue p2Value3 = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, 1.0, "", "KPA");
					DoubleValue p2Value4 = UnitConverter.GetP2Value(UnitClassEnum.LiquidVolume, 1.0, "", "M3");
					double num = Math.Pow(p2Value.Value * p2Value4.Value / p2Value3.Value, 0.5) / p2Value2.Value * 3600.0 / Math.Pow(1000.0, 0.5);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Area = new DoubleValue("mm2", RELIEFVALVE.CONSTANT * num);
				}
				if (RELIEFVALVE.CF != 0.0)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cf = new DoubleValue("", RELIEFVALVE.CF);
				}
				if (RELIEFVALVE.CV != 0.0)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cv = new DoubleValue("", RELIEFVALVE.CV);
				}
				if (RELIEFVALVE.PBACK != null)
				{
					DoubleValue hysysValue10 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Length, RELIEFVALVE.PBACK);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.BackPressure = hysysValue10;
				}
			}
			if (HEAT != null)
			{
				if (HEAT.MODEL.StartsWith("USER"))
				{
					if (HEAT.DATA != null && HEAT.DATA.Count == 6 && HEAT.DATA[5] == 0.0)
					{
						hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.Mode = HYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatOperatingMode.Fire;
					}
					else
					{
						string strMessage4 = string.Format("DEPRESSURE {0} {1}", base.UID, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, strMessage4);
						flag = false;
					}
				}
				else if (HEAT.MODEL.StartsWith("API2") || HEAT.MODEL.StartsWith("APIS") || HEAT.MODEL.StartsWith("RPSC") || HEAT.MODEL.StartsWith("ISOT"))
				{
					string strMessage5 = string.Format("DEPRESSURE {0}  {1}", base.UID, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObjects, WarningLevel.Notes, strMessage5);
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (HEAT.MODEL.StartsWith("RP52"))
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.Mode = HYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatOperatingMode.FireAPI521;
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.FireDataC.Add(21000.0);
				}
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.FireDataC = HEAT.DATA;
				if (HEAT.IWAREA != null)
				{
					DoubleValue hysysValue11 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Area, HEAT.IWAREA);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT IWAREA ={0}{1} {2}", hysysValue11.Value, hysysValue11.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (HEAT.AFAC != 0.0)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT AFAC ={0} {1}", HEAT.AFAC, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (HEAT.QFAC != 0.0)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT AFAC ={0} {1}", HEAT.QFAC, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (HEAT.HTAREA != null)
				{
					DoubleValue hysysValue12 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Area, HEAT.HTAREA);
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT HTAREA ={0}{1} {2}", hysysValue12.Value, hysysValue12.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (HEAT.HTCFAC != 0.0)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT HTCFAC ={0} {1}", HEAT.HTCFAC, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (HEAT.HTC != null)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT HTC ={0} {1}", HEAT.HTC, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (HEAT.HTCVAP != null)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT HTCVAP ={0} {1}", HEAT.HTCVAP, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
				if (HEAT.HTCLIQ != null)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("HEAT HTCLIQ ={0} {1}", HEAT.HTCLIQ, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PRINT))
			{
				hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("PRINT {0}  {1}", PRINT, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
			if (DEFINEstrs != null && DEFINEstrs.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string dEFINEstr in DEFINEstrs)
				{
					hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format("PRINT {0}  {1}", dEFINEstr, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				list.Add(hYSYSDerpessureUtility);
				if (hYSYSStream != null)
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSStream);
				}
			}
		}
		catch
		{
		}
		return list.ToArray();
	}
}
public class P2HCURVE : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public enum CurveType
	{
		ISOTHERMAL,
		ADIABATIC,
		BUBP,
		BUBT,
		DEWP,
		DEWT
	}

	public class StreamCurve
	{
		private DoubleValue _dp = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private DoubleValue _dt = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		private int _points = 11;

		public CurveType Type { get; set; }

		public string StreamID { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _dp;
			}
			set
			{
				_dp = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DT
		{
			get
			{
				return _dt;
			}
			set
			{
				_dt = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValues TEMPERATURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValues PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DUTY { get; set; }

		public int POINTS
		{
			get
			{
				return _points;
			}
			set
			{
				_points = value;
			}
		}

		public string SourceString { get; set; }

		public string GAMM { get; set; }

		public string KPRI { get; set; }

		public StreamCurve(string source)
		{
			SourceString = source;
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				CUnitOperation.STREAM,
				CUnitOperation.DT,
				CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE,
				"DP",
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.POINTS,
				CUnitOperation.DUTY,
				"GAMM",
				"KPRI"
			}, source.ToUpper().Trim());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STREAM))
				{
					StreamID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.Trim());
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT))
				{
					if (text.Contains("="))
					{
						DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text.Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("GAMM"))
				{
					GAMM = text.Trim();
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("KPRI"))
				{
					KPRI = text.Trim();
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY))
				{
					DUTY = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text.Trim());
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
				{
					string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.Trim());
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringValue))
					{
						string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(stringValue);
						TEMPERATURE = new DoubleValues();
						TEMPERATURE.Values = new double[array2.Length];
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							TEMPERATURE.Values[j] = double.Parse(array2[j]);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("DP"))
				{
					if (text.Contains("="))
					{
						DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text.Trim());
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
				{
					string stringValue2 = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.Trim());
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringValue2))
					{
						string[] array3 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(stringValue2);
						PRESSURE = new DoubleValues();
						PRESSURE.Values = new double[array3.Length];
						for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
						{
							PRESSURE.Values[k] = double.Parse(array3[k]);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POINTS) && text.Contains("="))
				{
					POINTS = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.Trim()));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class UOCurve
	{
		public CurveType Type { get; set; }

		public string UID { get; set; }

		public string ConnectSide { get; set; }

		public DoubleValues PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public int POINTS { get; set; }

		public UOCurve(string source)
		{
			List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
			{
				"HX",
				"HXRI",
				"LNGH",
				"AIRC",
				"COLU",
				"SIDE",
				"FLAS",
				"HOT",
				"COLD",
				"SHEL",
				"TUBE",
				"CELL",
				"AIR",
				"HEAT",
				"COOL",
				CUnitOperation.PRESSURE,
				CUnitOperation.POINTS,
				"GAMM",
				"KPRI"
			}, source.ToUpper().Trim());
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
			{
				list.Add(item.Content);
			}
			string[] array = list.ToArray();
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				if (text.StartsWith("HX") || text.StartsWith("HXRI") || text.StartsWith("LNGH") || text.StartsWith("AIRC") || text.StartsWith("COLU") || text.StartsWith("SIDE") || text.StartsWith("FLAS"))
				{
					UID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text).Trim();
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("HOT") || text.StartsWith("COLD") || text.StartsWith("SHEL") || text.StartsWith("TUBE") || text.StartsWith("CELL") || text.StartsWith("AIR") || text.StartsWith("HEAT") || text.StartsWith("COOL"))
				{
					ConnectSide = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text).Trim();
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
				{
					string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.Trim());
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringValue))
					{
						string[] array2 = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(stringValue);
						PRESSURE = new DoubleValues();
						PRESSURE.Values = new double[array2.Length];
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							PRESSURE.Values[j] = double.Parse(array2[j]);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POINTS))
				{
					POINTS = int.Parse(ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text.Trim()));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public List<StreamCurve> StreamCurves { get; set; }

	public List<string> LinkedIDs { get; set; }

	public List<UOCurve> UnitCurves { get; set; }

	public string HeadString { get; set; }

	public P2HCURVE(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_HCurves.Add(this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "HCURVE", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		HeadString = obj.Name;
		string text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
		{
			CUnitOperation.UID,
			CUnitOperation.NAME,
			CUnitOperation.PLOT
		}, text.ToUpper());
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
		{
			list.Add(item.Content);
		}
		string[] array = list.ToArray();
		foreach (string text2 in array)
		{
			if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.UID))
			{
				base.UID = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				NameHelper.NotesName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
				base.UID = NameHelper.ConvertName(base.UID, bIsStream: false);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.UID);
			}
			else if (text2.ToUpper().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.NAME))
			{
				base.Name = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.NAME);
			}
		}
		LinkedIDs = new List<string>();
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			string text3 = child.Name.ToUpper();
			if (text3.StartsWith("\0"))
			{
				continue;
			}
			string head = ParseUtilty.GetHead(text3, ' ');
			string text4 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text3, ' ');
			if (head.StartsWith("PROP") || head.StartsWith("METH"))
			{
				continue;
			}
			CurveType type = CurveType.ISOTHERMAL;
			GetCurveType(head, out type);
			if (text4.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STREAM))
			{
				StreamCurve streamCurve = new StreamCurve(text4);
				streamCurve.Type = type;
				if (!LinkedIDs.Contains(streamCurve.StreamID))
				{
					LinkedIDs.Add(streamCurve.StreamID);
				}
				if (StreamCurves == null)
				{
					StreamCurves = new List<StreamCurve>();
				}
				StreamCurves.Add(streamCurve);
			}
			else
			{
				UOCurve uOCurve = new UOCurve(text4);
				uOCurve.Type = type;
				if (!LinkedIDs.Contains(uOCurve.UID))
				{
					LinkedIDs.Add(uOCurve.UID);
				}
				if (UnitCurves == null)
				{
					UnitCurves = new List<UOCurve>();
				}
				UnitCurves.Add(uOCurve);
			}
		}
	}

	private void GetCurveType(string head, out CurveType type)
	{
		if (head.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ISOTHERMAL))
		{
			type = CurveType.ISOTHERMAL;
		}
		else if (head.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ADIABATIC))
		{
			type = CurveType.ADIABATIC;
		}
		else if (head.StartsWith("BUBT"))
		{
			type = CurveType.BUBT;
		}
		else if (head.StartsWith("BUBP"))
		{
			type = CurveType.BUBP;
		}
		else if (head.StartsWith("DEWT"))
		{
			type = CurveType.DEWT;
		}
		else if (head.StartsWith("DEWP"))
		{
			type = CurveType.DEWP;
		}
		else
		{
			type = CurveType.ISOTHERMAL;
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		if (StreamCurves == null || StreamCurves.Count == 0)
		{
			return null;
		}
		try
		{
			int num = 1;
			foreach (StreamCurve streamCurf in StreamCurves)
			{
				if ((streamCurf.Type == CurveType.ADIABATIC && streamCurf.DUTY != null) || (streamCurf.DT != null && streamCurf.DT.Value == 0.0 && streamCurf.DP != null && streamCurf.DP.Value == 0.0 && streamCurf.TEMPERATURE == null && streamCurf.PRESSURE == null))
				{
					string strMessage = string.Format("HCURVE UID={0}, {1} {2} {3}", base.UID, streamCurf.Type.ToString(), streamCurf.SourceString, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, strMessage);
					continue;
				}
				HYSYSPropertyTableUtility hYSYSPropertyTableUtility = new HYSYSPropertyTableUtility();
				list.Add(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility);
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.UID = base.UID + "_Prop" + num;
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsHCurve = true;
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(HeadString);
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(streamCurf.Type.ToString() + " " + streamCurf.SourceString);
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs = UnitConverter.GetHysysValues(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, streamCurf.TEMPERATURE);
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs = UnitConverter.GetHysysValues(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, streamCurf.PRESSURE);
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, streamCurf.DT);
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, streamCurf.DP);
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points = streamCurf.POINTS;
				hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.StreamID = streamCurf.StreamID;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(streamCurf.GAMM))
				{
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(streamCurf.GAMM + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(streamCurf.KPRI))
				{
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(streamCurf.KPRI + " " + P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
				}
				if (streamCurf.Type == CurveType.DEWT)
				{
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubT = true;
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.VaporFraction = new DoubleValue("", 1.0);
				}
				else if (streamCurf.Type == CurveType.BUBT)
				{
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubT = true;
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.VaporFraction = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
				}
				else if (streamCurf.Type == CurveType.BUBP)
				{
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubP = true;
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.VaporFraction = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
				}
				else if (streamCurf.Type == CurveType.DEWP)
				{
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubP = true;
					hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.VaporFraction = new DoubleValue("", 1.0);
				}
				num++;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "HCURVE", base.UID));
		}
		return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
	}
}
public class P2HydrateUtility : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class EVALUATE
	{
		private DoubleValue _IPRESSURE = new DoubleValue("ATM", 1.0);

		private DoubleValue _MAXPRESSURE = new DoubleValue("ATM", 100.0);

		public string STREAM { get; set; }

		public int POINTS { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue INHIBITOR { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue IPRESSURE
		{
			get
			{
				return _IPRESSURE;
			}
			set
			{
				_IPRESSURE = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue MAXPRESSURE
		{
			get
			{
				return _MAXPRESSURE;
			}
			set
			{
				_MAXPRESSURE = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue DP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue PESTIMATE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue ITEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DT { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue MAXTEMP { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue TESTIMATE { get; set; }
	}

	public List<EVALUATE> EVALUATEs { get; set; }

	public P2HydrateUtility(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.UTILITY + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "HYDRATE", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "HYDRATE", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			if (EVALUATEs == null)
			{
				EVALUATEs = new List<EVALUATE>();
			}
			string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
			if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EVALUATE))
				{
					continue;
				}
				EVALUATE eVALUATE = new EVALUATE();
				EVALUATEs.Add(eVALUATE);
				List<string> propertyList = new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.STREAM,
					CUnitOperation.POINTS,
					CUnitOperation.INHIBITOR,
					CUnitOperation.IPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.MAXPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PESTIMATE,
					CUnitOperation.ITEMP,
					CUnitOperation.DT,
					CUnitOperation.MAXTEMP,
					CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE
				};
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ');
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(propertyList, text.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item.Content);
				}
				string[] array = list.ToArray();
				if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					try
					{
						if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STREAM))
						{
							eVALUATE.STREAM = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POINTS))
						{
							eVALUATE.POINTS = (int)ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.INHIBITOR))
						{
							eVALUATE.INHIBITOR = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.IPRESSURE))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								eVALUATE.IPRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MAXPRESSURE))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								eVALUATE.MAXPRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
						{
							eVALUATE.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PESTIMATE))
						{
							eVALUATE.PESTIMATE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.ITEMP))
						{
							eVALUATE.ITEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DT))
						{
							eVALUATE.DT = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.MAXTEMP))
						{
							eVALUATE.MAXTEMP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TESTIMATE))
						{
							eVALUATE.TESTIMATE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						base.bParsingError = true;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		if (EVALUATEs != null && EVALUATEs.Count > 0)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (EVALUATE eVALUATE in EVALUATEs)
			{
				num++;
				try
				{
					HYSYSHydrateUtility hYSYSHydrateUtility = new HYSYSHydrateUtility();
					list.Add(hYSYSHydrateUtility);
					hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
					hYSYSHydrateUtility.StreamName = eVALUATE.STREAM;
					hYSYSHydrateUtility.UID = base.UID + "_" + eVALUATE.STREAM + num;
					HYSYSPhaseEnvelope hYSYSPhaseEnvelope = new HYSYSPhaseEnvelope();
					list.Add(hYSYSPhaseEnvelope);
					hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.UID = base.UID + "_" + eVALUATE.STREAM + "HyPlot";
					hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.StreamName = eVALUATE.STREAM;
					hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.IsHydrate = true;
					hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Utility_ForHydrateConversion"), hYSYSHydrateUtility.UID));
					hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Utility_PhaseForHydrate"), hYSYSHydrateUtility.UID));
					string item = string.Format("POINTS={0} {1}.", eVALUATE.POINTS, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
					hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					if (eVALUATE.IPRESSURE != null)
					{
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.InitPressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.IPRESSURE);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.DP != null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.DP);
						item = string.Format("DP={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue.Value, hysysValue.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.INHIBITOR != null)
					{
						item = string.Format("INHIBITOR({0})={1} {2}", eVALUATE.INHIBITOR.Unit, eVALUATE.INHIBITOR.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.MAXPRESSURE != null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.MAXPRESSURE);
						item = string.Format("MAXPRESSURE={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue2.Value, hysysValue2.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.PESTIMATE != null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue3 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.PESTIMATE);
						item = string.Format("PESTIMATE={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue3.Value, hysysValue3.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.ITEMP != null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue4 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, eVALUATE.ITEMP);
						item = string.Format("ITEMP={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue4.Value, hysysValue4.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.DT != null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue5 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, eVALUATE.DT);
						item = string.Format("DT={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue5.Value, hysysValue5.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.MAXTEMP != null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue6 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, eVALUATE.MAXTEMP);
						item = string.Format("MAXTEMP={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue6.Value, hysysValue6.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					if (eVALUATE.TESTIMATE != null)
					{
						DoubleValue hysysValue7 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, eVALUATE.TESTIMATE);
						item = string.Format("TESTIMATE={0} {1} {1}", hysysValue7.Value, hysysValue7.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
						hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "HYDRATE", base.UID));
				}
			}
		}
		return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
	}
}
public class P2PhaseEnvelopeUtility : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class EVALUATE
	{
		private DoubleValue _IPRESSURE = new DoubleValue("", 147.0);

		private string _IPLOT = "ON";

		private string _VERSION = "V5";

		private DoubleValue _PSTART;

		public string STREAM { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue LFRAC { get; set; }

		public int POINTS { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP { get; set; }

		public string StrSource { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue IPRESSURE
		{
			get
			{
				return _IPRESSURE;
			}
			set
			{
				_IPRESSURE = value;
			}
		}

		public string IPLOT
		{
			get
			{
				return _IPLOT;
			}
			set
			{
				_IPLOT = value;
			}
		}

		public int CPLOT { get; set; }

		public string VERSION
		{
			get
			{
				return _VERSION;
			}
			set
			{
				_VERSION = value;
			}
		}

		public DoubleValue PSTART
		{
			get
			{
				if (_PSTART == null)
				{
					if (_VERSION == "V5")
					{
						return new DoubleValue("ATM", 1.0);
					}
					if (_VERSION == "V4")
					{
						return new DoubleValue("ATM", 10.0);
					}
				}
				return _PSTART;
			}
			set
			{
				_PSTART = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public List<EVALUATE> EVALUATEs { get; set; }

	public P2PhaseEnvelopeUtility(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.UTILITY + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "PHASE", base.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			if (base.bMappingError)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "PHASE", base.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			if (EVALUATEs == null)
			{
				EVALUATEs = new List<EVALUATE>();
			}
			string text = child.Name.ToUpper();
			if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(text);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.EVALUATE))
				{
					continue;
				}
				EVALUATE eVALUATE = new EVALUATE();
				EVALUATEs.Add(eVALUATE);
				List<string> propertyList = new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.STREAM,
					CUnitOperation.LFRAC,
					CUnitOperation.POINTS,
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.IPLOT,
					CUnitOperation.CPLOT,
					CUnitOperation.VERSION,
					CUnitOperation.IPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PSTART
				};
				eVALUATE.StrSource = text;
				text = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(text, ' ');
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(propertyList, text.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item.Content);
				}
				string[] array = list.ToArray();
				if (array != null && array.Length == 1)
				{
					array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(array[0]);
				}
				if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					try
					{
						if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STREAM))
						{
							eVALUATE.STREAM = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.LFRAC))
						{
							eVALUATE.LFRAC = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.POINTS))
						{
							eVALUATE.POINTS = (int)ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
						{
							eVALUATE.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.IPRESSURE))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								eVALUATE.IPRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.IPLOT))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								eVALUATE.IPLOT = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.CPLOT))
						{
							eVALUATE.CPLOT = (int)ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]).Value;
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.VERSION))
						{
							if (array[i].Contains('='))
							{
								eVALUATE.VERSION = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(array[i]);
							}
						}
						else if (array[i].StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PSTART) && array[i].Contains('='))
						{
							eVALUATE.PSTART = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(array[i]);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						base.bParsingError = true;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
		if (EVALUATEs != null && EVALUATEs.Count > 0)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (EVALUATE eVALUATE in EVALUATEs)
			{
				num++;
				try
				{
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (HYSYSBaseObject item10 in list)
					{
						if (item10 is HYSYSPhaseEnvelope hYSYSPhaseEnvelope && hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.StreamName == eVALUATE.STREAM)
						{
							string item = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Utility_OnlyOneEvalueConverted"), eVALUATE.STREAM, eVALUATE.StrSource);
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						HYSYSPhaseEnvelope hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2 = new HYSYSPhaseEnvelope();
						list.Add(hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2);
						base.P2Paragraphy += eVALUATE.StrSource;
						hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
						hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.StreamName = eVALUATE.STREAM;
						hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.UID = base.UID + "_" + eVALUATE.STREAM + num;
						if (eVALUATE.POINTS > 100)
						{
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.Points = eVALUATE.POINTS;
						}
						else
						{
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.Points = 100;
							string item2 = string.Format("POINTS={0} {1}", eVALUATE.POINTS, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
						}
						if (eVALUATE.LFRAC != null)
						{
							string item3 = string.Format("LFRAC={0} {1}", eVALUATE.LFRAC.Value, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item3);
						}
						if (eVALUATE.DP != null)
						{
							DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.DP);
							string item4 = string.Format("DP={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue.Value, hysysValue.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item4);
						}
						if (eVALUATE.IPRESSURE != null)
						{
							DoubleValue hysysValue2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.IPRESSURE);
							string item5 = string.Format("IPRESSURE={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue2.Value, hysysValue2.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item5);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(eVALUATE.IPLOT))
						{
							string item6 = string.Format("IPLOT={0} {1}", eVALUATE.IPLOT, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item6);
						}
						if (eVALUATE.CPLOT != 0)
						{
							string item7 = string.Format("CPLOT={0} {1}", eVALUATE.CPLOT, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item7);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(eVALUATE.VERSION))
						{
							string item8 = string.Format("VERSION={0} {1}", eVALUATE.VERSION, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item8);
						}
						if (eVALUATE.PSTART != null)
						{
							DoubleValue hysysValue3 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, eVALUATE.PSTART);
							string item9 = string.Format("PSTART={0} {1} {2}", hysysValue3.Value, hysysValue3.Unit, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted"));
							hYSYSPhaseEnvelope2.ConvertNotes.Add(item9);
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "PHASE", base.UID));
				}
			}
		}
		return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
	}
}
public class P2Valve : P2UnitOperationBase
{
	public class ValveOperation : PropertyItem
	{
		private DoubleValue _dp = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);

		public DoubleValue PRESSURE { get; set; }

		public DoubleValue DP
		{
			get
			{
				return _dp;
			}
			set
			{
				_dp = value;
			}
		}
	}

	public ValveOperation OPERATION { get; set; }

	public P2Valve(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		try
		{
			SetValue(obj);
			P2BaseObject.g_Map.Add(CUnitOperation.VALVE + base.UID, this);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ParseUO"), "VALVE", base.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetValue(P2BaseObject obj)
	{
		string headData = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(obj.Name, ' ').Trim();
		SetHeadData(headData);
		base.P2Paragraphy = obj.ToString() + "\n";
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in obj.Children)
		{
			base.P2Paragraphy = base.P2Paragraphy + child.ToString() + "\n";
			empty = child.Name.ToUpper();
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FEED))
			{
				base.FEED = new List<string>();
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(empty2);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string strName = array.GetValue(i).ToString();
					NameHelper.NotesName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					strName = NameHelper.ConvertName(strName, bIsStream: true);
					base.FEED.Add(strName);
				}
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.FEED);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.PRODUCT = new Product(empty2);
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.PRODUCT);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.OPERATION))
			{
				base.AddedPropertyList.Add(CUnitOperation.OPERATION);
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				OPERATION = new ValveOperation();
				List<PropertyPair> propertySplit = ParseUtilty.GetPropertySplit(new List<string>
				{
					CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE,
					CUnitOperation.PRESSURE
				}, empty2.ToUpper());
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (PropertyPair item2 in propertySplit)
				{
					list.Add(item2.Content);
				}
				string[] array2 = list.ToArray();
				foreach (string text in array2)
				{
					if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DLTAPRESSURE))
					{
						OPERATION.DP = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE))
					{
						OPERATION.PRESSURE = ParseUtilty.GetDoubleValue(text);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DEFINE))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(child.Name.ToUpper(), ' ');
				DEFINE item = new DEFINE(empty2, "VALVE", base.UID);
				base.GlobalDefine.Add(item);
			}
			else if (empty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.METHOD))
			{
				empty2 = ParseUtilty.RemoveHead(empty, ' ').Trim();
				base.METHOD = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(empty2);
			}
		}
	}

	public override HYSYSBaseObject[] Convert2HYSYSObj()
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = null;
		try
		{
			HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = new HYSYSValve();
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSValve);
			hYSYSValve.ConvertNotes.Add(base.P2Paragraphy);
			hYSYSValve.UID = base.UID;
			hYSYSValve.Name = base.Name;
			if (base.FEED != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in base.FEED)
				{
					hYSYSValve.InletStreams.Add(item);
				}
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			if (base.PRODUCT != null)
			{
				foreach (string addedProperty in base.PRODUCT.AddedPropertyList)
				{
					string text = base.PRODUCT.GetProperty(addedProperty).ToString();
					hYSYSValve.OutletStreams.Add(text);
					dictionary.Add(addedProperty, text);
				}
			}
			if (OPERATION != null && OPERATION.DP != null)
			{
				hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, OPERATION.DP);
			}
			if (OPERATION != null && OPERATION.PRESSURE != null)
			{
				hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, OPERATION.PRESSURE);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.METHOD))
			{
				hYSYSValve.FluidPackage = base.METHOD;
			}
			if (base.IsMultipleFeed)
			{
				HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = HYSYSObjectHelper.HandleMultipleFeedToMixer(hYSYSValve);
				list.Add(hYSYSMixer);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSValve.FluidPackage))
				{
					hYSYSMixer.FluidPackage = hYSYSValve.FluidPackage;
				}
				hYSYSMixer.SetCreateStreams(list);
			}
			if (base.IsMultiplePRODUCT)
			{
				List<HYSYSUnitOperationBase> list2 = HYSYSObjectHelper.FlashMultipleProduct(dictionary, hYSYSValve);
				bool flag = false;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSValve.FluidPackage))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (list2 != null)
				{
					foreach (HYSYSUnitOperationBase item2 in list2)
					{
						list.Add(item2);
						if (flag)
						{
							item2.FluidPackage = hYSYSValve.FluidPackage;
						}
						item2.SetCreateStreams(list);
					}
				}
			}
			hYSYSValve.SetCreateStreams(list);
			if (base.GlobalDefine.Count > 0)
			{
				hYSYSValve.GlobalDefine = base.GlobalDefine;
				hYSYSValve.SeparateDefines();
			}
			return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertUO"), "VALVE", base.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}
}
public class VisualBaseNode : P2BaseObject, INotifyPropertyChanged
{
	public enum ConvertResult
	{
		CR_PASSED,
		CR_PARTIGNORED,
		CR_IGNORED
	}

	private ConvertResult m_ConvertResult;

	private string m_Message;

	public ConvertResult _ConvertResult
	{
		get
		{
			return m_ConvertResult;
		}
		set
		{
			SetOwnConvertResult(value);
			SetChildrenResult(value);
		}
	}

	public string Message
	{
		get
		{
			return m_Message;
		}
		set
		{
			m_Message = value;
			SetChildrenMessage(m_Message);
		}
	}

	public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

	private void SetChildrenResult(ConvertResult cr)
	{
		foreach (VisualBaseNode child in base.Children)
		{
			child._ConvertResult = cr;
			child.SetChildrenResult(cr);
		}
	}

	public void SetOwnConvertResult(ConvertResult cr)
	{
		m_ConvertResult = cr;
		if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs("_ConvertResult"));
		}
	}

	public VisualBaseNode()
	{
		_ConvertResult = ConvertResult.CR_IGNORED;
	}

	private void SetChildrenMessage(string msg)
	{
		foreach (VisualBaseNode child in base.Children)
		{
			child.Message = msg;
			child.SetChildrenMessage(msg);
		}
	}
}
